module Core (
	clk,
	rst,
	rstStart,
	nextMemReadSerial,
	nextMemWriteSerial,
	memReadData,
	memReadDataReady,
	memReadSerial,
	memAccessResponse,
	memAccessReadBusy,
	memAccessWriteBusy,
	reqExternalInterrupt,
	externalInterruptCode,
	debugRegister,
	lastCommittedPC,
	memAccessAddr,
	memAccessWriteData,
	memAccessRE,
	memAccessWE,
	serialWE,
	serialWriteData
);
	input wire clk;
	input wire rst;
	input wire rstStart;
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM = 2;
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_MEM_ACCESS_SERIAL_BIT_SIZE = 2;
	input wire [1:0] nextMemReadSerial;
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_MEM_WRITE_SERIAL_BIT_SIZE = 1;
	input wire [0:0] nextMemWriteSerial;
	localparam MemoryTypes_MEMORY_ENTRY_BIT_NUM = 64;
	input wire [63:0] memReadData;
	input wire memReadDataReady;
	input wire [1:0] memReadSerial;
	input wire [1:0] memAccessResponse;
	input wire memAccessReadBusy;
	input wire memAccessWriteBusy;
	input wire reqExternalInterrupt;
	localparam BasicTypes_RSD_EXTERNAL_INTERRUPT_CODE_WIDTH = 5;
	input wire [4:0] externalInterruptCode;
	output wire debugRegister;
	localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
	output reg [18:0] lastCommittedPC;
	localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_WIDTH = 22;
	localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH = 20;
	output wire [21:0] memAccessAddr;
	output wire [63:0] memAccessWriteData;
	output wire memAccessRE;
	output wire memAccessWE;
	output wire serialWE;
	localparam IO_UnitTypes_SERIAL_OUTPUT_WIDTH = 32;
	output wire [31:0] serialWriteData;
	generate
		if (1) begin : debugIF
			wire clk;
			wire rst;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			reg [18:0] lastCommittedPC;
			wire debugRegister;
		end
	endgenerate
	assign debugIF.clk = clk;
	assign debugIF.rst = rst;
	generate
		if (1) begin : hwCounterIF
			wire clk;
			wire rst;
			localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			localparam DebugTypes_HARDWARE_COUNTER_TYPE_BIT_SIZE = 3;
			reg [2:0] hardwareCounterType [0:0];
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
			reg [31:0] hardwareCounterData [0:0];
			localparam BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			reg loadMiss [0:1];
			localparam BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 1;
			reg [BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE:0] commitNum;
			reg refetchThisPC;
			reg refetchNextPC;
			reg refetchBrTarget;
		end
	endgenerate
	assign hwCounterIF.clk = clk;
	assign hwCounterIF.rst = rst;
	assign debugRegister = debugIF.debugRegister;
	always @(posedge clk) lastCommittedPC <= debugIF.lastCommittedPC;
	generate
		if (1) begin : hwCounter
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
			reg [31:0] countCycle;
			always @(posedge Core.hwCounterIF.clk)
				if (Core.hwCounterIF.rst)
					countCycle <= 0;
				else
					countCycle <= countCycle + 1;
			localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
					Core.hwCounterIF.hardwareCounterData[i] = countCycle;
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : ctrlIF
			wire clk;
			wire rst;
			reg [1:0] npStage;
			reg [1:0] ifStage;
			reg [1:0] pdStage;
			reg [1:0] idStage;
			reg [1:0] rnStage;
			reg [1:0] dsStage;
			reg [1:0] scStage;
			reg [1:0] isStage;
			reg [1:0] backEnd;
			reg [1:0] cmStage;
			reg ifStageEmpty;
			reg pdStageEmpty;
			reg idStageEmpty;
			reg rnStageEmpty;
			reg activeListEmpty;
			reg wholePipelineEmpty;
			reg npStageSendBubbleLower;
			reg npStageSendBubbleLowerForInterrupt;
			reg ifStageSendBubbleLower;
			reg idStageStallUpper;
			reg rnStageFlushUpper;
			reg rnStageSendBubbleLower;
			reg cmStageFlushUpper;
			reg isStageStallUpper;
			reg stallByDecodeStage;
		end
	endgenerate
	assign ctrlIF.clk = clk;
	assign ctrlIF.rst = rst;
	generate
		if (1) begin : npStageIF
			wire clk;
			wire rst;
			wire rstStart;
			reg pcWE;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			wire [18:0] pcOut;
			reg [18:0] pcIn;
			reg [18:0] predNextPC;
			localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_BRANCH_GLOBAL_HISTORY_BIT_WIDTH = 10;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH = 2;
			reg [109:0] brResult;
			reg [18:0] interruptAddrIn;
			reg interruptAddrWE;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_WIDTH = 22;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH = 20;
			reg [21:0] icNextReadAddrIn;
			localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
			reg [39:0] nextStage;

			reg [31:0] dataInA [0:0];
			reg [31:0] dataInB [0:0];
			wire [31:0] mulDataOut [0:0];
			reg mulGetUpper [0:0];
			reg [1:0] mulCode [0:0];
			wire [31:0] divDataOut [0:0];
			reg [1:0] divCode [0:0];
			reg divReset [0:0];
			reg divReq [0:0];
			reg divReserved [0:0];
			reg divFinished [0:0];
			reg divBusy [0:0];
			reg divFree [0:0];
			reg divAcquire [0:0];
			reg divRelease [0:0];
			wire divResetFromMI_Stage [0:0];
			wire divResetFromMR_Stage [0:0];
			wire divResetFromMT_Stage [0:0];
			reg divResetFromCI_Stage [0:0];
		end
	endgenerate
	assign mulDivUnitIF.clk = clk;
	assign mulDivUnitIF.rst = rst;
	generate
		if (1) begin : cacheFlushManagerIF
			wire clk;
			wire rst;
			reg cacheFlushReq;
			reg cacheFlushComplete;
		end
	endgenerate
	assign cacheFlushManagerIF.clk = clk;
	assign cacheFlushManagerIF.rst = rst;
	generate
		if (1) begin : controller
			reg [1:0] npStage;
			reg [1:0] ifStage;
			reg [1:0] pdStage;
			reg [1:0] idStage;
			reg [1:0] rnStage;
			reg [1:0] dsStage;
			reg [1:0] scStage;
			reg [1:0] isStage;
			reg [1:0] backEnd;
			reg [1:0] cmStage;
			reg stallByDecodeStage;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			always @(*) begin
				stallByDecodeStage = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				if (Core.ctrlIF.rst) begin
					npStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE};
					ifStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE};
					pdStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE};
					idStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE};
					rnStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE};
					dsStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE};
				end
				else begin
					npStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_FALSE};
					ifStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_FALSE};
					pdStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_FALSE};
					idStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_FALSE};
					rnStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_FALSE};
					dsStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_FALSE};
					if (Core.ctrlIF.cmStageFlushUpper) begin
						npStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_FALSE};
						ifStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE};
						pdStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE};
						idStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE};
						rnStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE};
						dsStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE};
					end
					else if (Core.ctrlIF.rnStageSendBubbleLower) begin
						npStage = {BasicTypes_TRUE, BasicTypes_FALSE};
						ifStage = {BasicTypes_TRUE, BasicTypes_FALSE};
						pdStage = {BasicTypes_TRUE, BasicTypes_FALSE};
						idStage = {BasicTypes_TRUE, BasicTypes_FALSE};
						rnStage = {BasicTypes_TRUE, BasicTypes_TRUE};
					end
					else if (Core.ctrlIF.rnStageFlushUpper) begin
						npStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_FALSE};
						ifStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE};
						pdStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE};
						idStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE};
					end
					else if (Core.ctrlIF.idStageStallUpper) begin
						npStage = {BasicTypes_TRUE, BasicTypes_FALSE};
						ifStage = {BasicTypes_TRUE, BasicTypes_FALSE};
						pdStage = {BasicTypes_TRUE, BasicTypes_FALSE};
						idStage = {BasicTypes_TRUE, BasicTypes_FALSE};
						stallByDecodeStage = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					end
					else if (Core.ctrlIF.ifStageSendBubbleLower) begin
						npStage = {BasicTypes_TRUE, BasicTypes_TRUE};
						ifStage = {BasicTypes_TRUE, BasicTypes_TRUE};
					end
					else if (Core.ctrlIF.npStageSendBubbleLower || Core.ctrlIF.npStageSendBubbleLowerForInterrupt)
						npStage = {BasicTypes_TRUE, BasicTypes_TRUE};
				end
				Core.ctrlIF.npStage = npStage;
				Core.ctrlIF.ifStage = ifStage;
				Core.ctrlIF.pdStage = pdStage;
				Core.ctrlIF.idStage = idStage;
				Core.ctrlIF.rnStage = rnStage;
				Core.ctrlIF.dsStage = dsStage;
				Core.ctrlIF.stallByDecodeStage = stallByDecodeStage;
				if (Core.ctrlIF.rst) begin
					scStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE};
					isStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE};
					backEnd = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE};
					cmStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE};
				end
				else if (Core.ctrlIF.isStageStallUpper) begin
					scStage = {BasicTypes_TRUE, BasicTypes_FALSE};
					isStage = {BasicTypes_TRUE, BasicTypes_FALSE};
					backEnd = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_FALSE};
					cmStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_FALSE};
				end
				else begin
					scStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_FALSE};
					isStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_FALSE};
					backEnd = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_FALSE};
					cmStage = {BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_FALSE};
				end
				Core.ctrlIF.scStage = scStage;
				Core.ctrlIF.isStage = isStage;
				Core.ctrlIF.backEnd = backEnd;
				Core.ctrlIF.cmStage = cmStage;
				Core.ctrlIF.wholePipelineEmpty = ((((npStage[0] && Core.ctrlIF.ifStageEmpty) && Core.ctrlIF.pdStageEmpty) && Core.ctrlIF.idStageEmpty) && Core.ctrlIF.rnStageEmpty) && Core.ctrlIF.activeListEmpty;
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : memoryAccessController
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM = 2;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_MEM_ACCESS_SERIAL_BIT_SIZE = 2;
			wire [1:0] nextMemReadSerial;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_MEM_WRITE_SERIAL_BIT_SIZE = 1;
			wire [0:0] nextMemWriteSerial;
			localparam MemoryTypes_MEMORY_ENTRY_BIT_NUM = 64;
			wire [63:0] memReadData;
			wire memReadDataReady;
			wire [1:0] memReadSerial;
			wire [1:0] memAccessResponse;
			wire memAccessReadBusy;
			wire memAccessWriteBusy;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_WIDTH = 22;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH = 20;
			reg [21:0] memAccessAddr;
			reg [63:0] memAccessWriteData;
			reg memAccessRE;
			reg memAccessWE;
			reg icAck;
			reg dcAck;
			reg [1:0] reqSerial;
			reg [1:0] nextReqSerial;
			reg [1:0] resultSerial;
			reg [1:0] nextResultSerial;
			always @(posedge Core.cacheSystemIF.clk)
				if (Core.cacheSystemIF.rst) begin
					reqSerial <= 1'sb0;
					resultSerial <= 1'sb0;
				end
				else begin
					reqSerial <= nextReqSerial;
					resultSerial <= nextResultSerial;
				end
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			always @(*) begin
				icAck = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				dcAck = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				if (!memAccessReadBusy && Core.cacheSystemIF.icMemAccessReq[22])
					icAck = BasicTypes_TRUE;
				else if ((!memAccessReadBusy && Core.cacheSystemIF.dcMemAccessReq[87]) && (Core.cacheSystemIF.dcMemAccessReq[86] == BasicTypes_FALSE))
					dcAck = BasicTypes_TRUE;
				else if ((!memAccessWriteBusy && Core.cacheSystemIF.dcMemAccessReq[87]) && (Core.cacheSystemIF.dcMemAccessReq[86] == BasicTypes_TRUE))
					dcAck = BasicTypes_TRUE;
				if (icAck) begin
					memAccessAddr = Core.cacheSystemIF.icMemAccessReq[21-:22];
					memAccessWriteData = 1'sb0;
					memAccessRE = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					memAccessWE = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else if (dcAck) begin
					memAccessAddr = Core.cacheSystemIF.dcMemAccessReq[85-:22];
					memAccessWriteData = Core.cacheSystemIF.dcMemAccessReq[63-:64];
					memAccessRE = (Core.cacheSystemIF.dcMemAccessReq[86] ? BasicTypes_FALSE : BasicTypes_TRUE);
					memAccessWE = (Core.cacheSystemIF.dcMemAccessReq[86] ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
				end
				else begin
					memAccessAddr = 1'sb0;
					memAccessWriteData = 1'sb0;
					memAccessRE = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					memAccessWE = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				Core.cacheSystemIF.icMemAccessReqAck[3] = icAck;
				Core.cacheSystemIF.icMemAccessReqAck[2-:2] = nextMemReadSerial;
				Core.cacheSystemIF.dcMemAccessReqAck[3] = dcAck;
				Core.cacheSystemIF.dcMemAccessReqAck[2-:2] = nextMemReadSerial;
				Core.cacheSystemIF.dcMemAccessReqAck[0-:1] = nextMemWriteSerial;
			end
			always @(*) begin
				Core.cacheSystemIF.icMemAccessResult[66] = memReadDataReady;
				Core.cacheSystemIF.icMemAccessResult[65-:2] = memReadSerial;
				Core.cacheSystemIF.icMemAccessResult[63-:64] = memReadData;
				Core.cacheSystemIF.dcMemAccessResult[66] = memReadDataReady;
				Core.cacheSystemIF.dcMemAccessResult[65-:2] = memReadSerial;
				Core.cacheSystemIF.dcMemAccessResult[63-:64] = memReadData;
				Core.cacheSystemIF.dcMemAccessResponse = memAccessResponse;
			end
			always @(*) begin
				nextReqSerial = reqSerial;
				nextResultSerial = resultSerial;
				if (icAck || (dcAck && (Core.cacheSystemIF.dcMemAccessReq[86] == BasicTypes_FALSE)))
					nextReqSerial = reqSerial + 1;
				if (memReadDataReady)
					nextResultSerial = resultSerial + 1;
			end
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM = 8;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_LINE_BIT_WIDTH = 64;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM = 8;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BIT_WIDTH = 64;
		end
	endgenerate
	assign memAccessAddr = memoryAccessController.memAccessAddr;
	assign memAccessWriteData = memoryAccessController.memAccessWriteData;
	assign memAccessRE = memoryAccessController.memAccessRE;
	assign memAccessWE = memoryAccessController.memAccessWE;
	assign memoryAccessController.memAccessReadBusy = memAccessReadBusy;
	assign memoryAccessController.memAccessWriteBusy = memAccessWriteBusy;
	assign memoryAccessController.nextMemReadSerial = nextMemReadSerial;
	assign memoryAccessController.nextMemWriteSerial = nextMemWriteSerial;
	assign memoryAccessController.memReadDataReady = memReadDataReady;
	assign memoryAccessController.memReadData = memReadData;
	assign memoryAccessController.memReadSerial = memReadSerial;
	assign memoryAccessController.memAccessResponse = memAccessResponse;
	generate
		if (1) begin : npStage
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			reg [18:0] predNextPC;
			localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
			reg [39:0] nextStage;
			reg stall;
			reg clear;
			reg regStall;
			reg beginStall;
			reg writePC_FromOuter;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			always @(posedge Core.npStageIF.clk)
				if (Core.npStageIF.rst)
					regStall <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
				else
					regStall <= stall;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			always @(*) begin
				stall = Core.ctrlIF.npStage[1];
				clear = Core.ctrlIF.npStage[0];
				Core.ctrlIF.npStageSendBubbleLower = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				beginStall = !regStall && stall;
				if ((Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase || Core.recoveryManagerIF.recoverFromRename) || Core.npStageIF.interruptAddrWE)
					writePC_FromOuter = BasicTypes_TRUE;
				else
					writePC_FromOuter = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				Core.npStageIF.pcWE = ((writePC_FromOuter || !stall) || beginStall) && !Core.npStageIF.rst;
			end
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_2
				reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;
				_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
				if (Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase)
					predNextPC = Core.recoveryManagerIF.recoveredPC_FromRwCommit;
				else if (Core.recoveryManagerIF.recoverFromRename)
					predNextPC = Core.recoveryManagerIF.recoveredPC_FromRename;
				else begin
					predNextPC = Core.npStageIF.pcOut;
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_3
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
								_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
								if (((!regStall && Core.ifStageIF.fetchStageIsValid[i]) && Core.ifStageIF.btbHit[i]) && Core.ifStageIF.brPredTaken[i]) begin
									predNextPC = Core.ifStageIF.btbOut[(1 - i) * 19+:19];
									_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
								end
							end
						if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
							_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
					end
				end
				if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00)
					Core.npStageIF.predNextPC = predNextPC;
			end
			localparam BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH = 8;
			localparam BasicTypes_INSN_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM = 8;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 3;
			function automatic StepOverCacheLine;
				input reg [18:0] pc1;
				input reg [18:0] pc2;
				StepOverCacheLine = pc1[CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] != pc2[CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_4
				reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;
				_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
				if (Core.npStageIF.interruptAddrWE)
					Core.npStageIF.pcIn = Core.npStageIF.interruptAddrIn;
				else if (beginStall)
					Core.npStageIF.pcIn = predNextPC;
				else begin
					Core.npStageIF.pcIn = predNextPC + 8;
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_5
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 1; i < BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
								_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
								if (StepOverCacheLine(predNextPC, predNextPC + (i * BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH))) begin
									Core.npStageIF.pcIn = predNextPC + (i * BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH);
									_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
								end
							end
						if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
							_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
					end
				end
				if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00) begin
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_6
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							begin
								nextStage[((1 - i) * 20) + 18-:MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH] = predNextPC + (i * BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH);
								if ((Core.npStageIF.interruptAddrWE || clear) || StepOverCacheLine(predNextPC, nextStage[((1 - i) * 20) + 18-:MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH]))
									nextStage[((1 - i) * 20) + 19] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
								else
									nextStage[((1 - i) * 20) + 19] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
							end
					end
					Core.npStageIF.nextStage = nextStage;
				end
			end
			localparam BasicTypes_ADDR_WIDTH = 32;
			reg [31:0] fetchAddr;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_TAG = 13;
			function automatic [31:0] MemoryMapTypes_ToAddrFromPC;
				input reg [18:0] pc;
				MemoryMapTypes_ToAddrFromPC = {pc[18], {MemoryMapTypes_PC_TAG {1'b0}}, pc[17:0]};
			endfunction
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_0_ADDR_BIT_WIDTH = 16;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_0_BEGIN = 32'h00001000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_0_END = 32'h00010000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_1_ADDR_BIT_WIDTH = 18;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_1_BEGIN = 32'h80000000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_1_END = 32'h80040000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SERIAL_OUTPUT = 32'h40002000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_BASE = 32'h40000000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_BEGIN = MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_BASE;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_END = 1073741840;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_ADDR_BIT_WIDTH = 19;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_BEGIN = 32'h80040000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_END = 32'h80050000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_SECTION_0_BASE = 20'h00000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_SECTION_1_BASE = 20'h10000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_SERIAL_OUTPUT = 20'h02000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_TIMER_BASE = 20'h00000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_TIMER_ZONE_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_BASE = 20'h10000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_WIDTH = 22;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH = 20;
			function automatic [21:0] MemoryMapTypes_ToPhyAddrFromLogical;
				input reg [31:0] logAddr;
				reg [21:0] phyAddr;
				begin
					if (logAddr == MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SERIAL_OUTPUT) begin
						phyAddr[21] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						phyAddr[20] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						phyAddr[19-:MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH] = MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_SERIAL_OUTPUT;
					end
					else if ((MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_BEGIN <= logAddr) && (logAddr < MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_END)) begin
						phyAddr[21] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						phyAddr[20] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						phyAddr[19-:MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH] = MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_TIMER_BASE + logAddr[3:0];
					end
					else if ((MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_BEGIN <= logAddr) && (logAddr < MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_END)) begin
						phyAddr[21] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						phyAddr[20] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						phyAddr[19-:MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH] = MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_BASE + logAddr[18:0];
					end
					else if ((MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_0_BEGIN <= logAddr) && (logAddr < MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_0_END)) begin
						phyAddr[21] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						phyAddr[20] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						phyAddr[19-:MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH] = MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_SECTION_0_BASE + logAddr[MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_0_ADDR_BIT_WIDTH:0];
					end
					else if ((MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_1_BEGIN <= logAddr) && (logAddr < MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_1_END)) begin
						phyAddr[21] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						phyAddr[20] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						phyAddr[19-:MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH] = MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_SECTION_1_BASE + logAddr[17:0];
					end
					else begin
						phyAddr[21] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						phyAddr[20] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						phyAddr[19-:MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH] = 32'hcccccccc;
					end
					MemoryMapTypes_ToPhyAddrFromLogical = phyAddr;
				end
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin
				if (Core.ifStageIF.fetchStageIsValid[0] && stall)
					fetchAddr = MemoryMapTypes_ToAddrFromPC(Core.ifStageIF.fetchStagePC[0]);
				else
					fetchAddr = MemoryMapTypes_ToAddrFromPC(predNextPC);
				Core.npStageIF.icNextReadAddrIn = MemoryMapTypes_ToPhyAddrFromLogical(fetchAddr);
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : pc
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_INSN_RESET_VECTOR = 32'h00001000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			FlipFlopWE #(
				.FF_WIDTH(MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH),
				.RESET_VALUE(MemoryMapTypes_INSN_RESET_VECTOR)
			) body(
				.out(Core.npStageIF.pcOut),
				.in(Core.npStageIF.pcIn),
				.we(Core.npStageIF.pcWE),
				.clk(Core.npStageIF.clk),
				.rst(Core.npStageIF.rst)
			);
		end
		if (1) begin : btb
			localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			reg [0:1] btbWE;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_BTB_ENTRY_NUM = 1024;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_BTB_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 10;
			reg [19:0] btbWA;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_BTB_CONTENTS_ADDR_WIDTH = 13;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_BTB_TAG_WIDTH = 4;
			reg [37:0] btbWV;
			localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
			reg [19:0] btbRA;
			wire [37:0] btbRV;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			reg [37:0] btbOut;
			reg [0:1] btbHit;
			reg [0:1] readIsCondBr;
			reg [18:0] pcIn;
			reg [37:0] tagReg;
			reg [37:0] nextTagReg;
			reg pushBtbQueue;
			reg popBtbQueue;
			wire full;
			wire empty;
			localparam BasicTypes_ADDR_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_BTB_QUEUE_SIZE = 32;
			reg [50:0] btbQueue [0:31];
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_BTB_QUEUE_SIZE_BIT_WIDTH = 5;
			wire [4:0] headPtr;
			wire [4:0] tailPtr;
			reg updateBtb;
			BlockMultiBankRAM #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(FetchUnitTypes_BTB_ENTRY_NUM),
				.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(19),
				.READ_NUM(BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH),
				.WRITE_NUM(BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH)
			) btbEntryArray(
				.clk(Core.npStageIF.clk),
				.we(btbWE),
				.wa(btbWA),
				.wv(btbWV),
				.ra(btbRA),
				.rv(btbRV)
			);
			QueuePointer #(.SIZE(FetchUnitTypes_BTB_QUEUE_SIZE)) btbQueuePointer(
				.clk(Core.npStageIF.clk),
				.rst(Core.npStageIF.rst),
				.push(pushBtbQueue),
				.pop(popBtbQueue),
				.full(full),
				.empty(empty),
				.headPtr(headPtr),
				.tailPtr(tailPtr)
			);
			reg [9:0] resetIndex;
			always @(posedge Core.npStageIF.clk) begin
				if (Core.npStageIF.rstStart)
					resetIndex <= 0;
				else
					resetIndex <= resetIndex + 1;
				if (Core.npStageIF.rst)
					tagReg <= 1'sb0;
				else
					tagReg <= nextTagReg;
			end
			always @(posedge Core.npStageIF.clk)
				if (pushBtbQueue) begin
					btbQueue[headPtr][50-:32] <= btbWA[0+:FetchUnitTypes_BTB_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
					btbQueue[headPtr][18-:19] <= btbWV[0+:19];
				end
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH = 8;
			localparam BasicTypes_INSN_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			localparam BasicTypes_INSN_ADDR_BIT_WIDTH = 2;
			function automatic [12:0] FetchUnitTypes_ToBTB_Addr;
				input reg [18:0] addr;
				FetchUnitTypes_ToBTB_Addr = addr[14:BasicTypes_INSN_ADDR_BIT_WIDTH];
			endfunction
			function automatic [9:0] FetchUnitTypes_ToBTB_Index;
				input reg [18:0] addr;
				FetchUnitTypes_ToBTB_Index = addr[11:BasicTypes_INSN_ADDR_BIT_WIDTH];
			endfunction
			function automatic [3:0] FetchUnitTypes_ToBTB_Tag;
				input reg [18:0] addr;
				FetchUnitTypes_ToBTB_Tag = addr[15:12];
			endfunction
			function automatic [18:0] FetchUnitTypes_ToRawAddrFromBTB_Addr;
				input reg [12:0] addr;
				input reg [18:0] pc;
				FetchUnitTypes_ToRawAddrFromBTB_Addr = {pc[18:15], addr[12:0], 2'b00};
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin
				pcIn = Core.npStageIF.predNextPC;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_7
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							btbRA[(1 - i) * FetchUnitTypes_BTB_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:FetchUnitTypes_BTB_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = FetchUnitTypes_ToBTB_Index(pcIn + (i * BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH));
							nextTagReg[i * MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH+:MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH] = pcIn + (i * BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH);
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_8
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							btbHit[i] = btbRV[((1 - i) * 19) + 18] && (btbRV[((1 - i) * 19) + 17-:4] == FetchUnitTypes_ToBTB_Tag(tagReg[i * MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH+:MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH]));
							btbOut[(1 - i) * MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH+:MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH] = FetchUnitTypes_ToRawAddrFromBTB_Addr(btbRV[((1 - i) * 19) + 13-:13], tagReg[i * MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH+:MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH]);
							readIsCondBr[i] = btbRV[((1 - i) * 19) + 0];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_9
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						btbWE[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				updateBtb = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				pushBtbQueue = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_10
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							if (updateBtb)
								pushBtbQueue = Core.npStageIF.brResult[((1 - i) * 55) + 12] && Core.npStageIF.brResult[((1 - i) * 55) + 16];
							else begin
								btbWE[i] = Core.npStageIF.brResult[((1 - i) * 55) + 12] && Core.npStageIF.brResult[((1 - i) * 55) + 16];
								updateBtb = updateBtb | btbWE[i];
							end
							btbWA[(1 - i) * FetchUnitTypes_BTB_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:FetchUnitTypes_BTB_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = FetchUnitTypes_ToBTB_Index(Core.npStageIF.brResult[((1 - i) * 55) + 54-:19]);
							btbWV[((1 - i) * 19) + 17-:4] = FetchUnitTypes_ToBTB_Tag(Core.npStageIF.brResult[((1 - i) * 55) + 54-:19]);
							btbWV[((1 - i) * 19) + 13-:13] = FetchUnitTypes_ToBTB_Addr(Core.npStageIF.brResult[((1 - i) * 55) + 35-:19]);
							btbWV[((1 - i) * 19) + 18] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
							btbWV[((1 - i) * 19) + 0] = Core.npStageIF.brResult[((1 - i) * 55) + 14];
						end
				end
				if (!empty && !updateBtb) begin
					popBtbQueue = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					btbWE[0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					btbWA[FetchUnitTypes_BTB_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:FetchUnitTypes_BTB_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = btbQueue[tailPtr][50-:32];
					btbWV[19+:19] = btbQueue[tailPtr][18-:19];
				end
				else
					popBtbQueue = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				if (Core.npStageIF.rst) begin
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_11
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							begin
								btbWE[i] = (i == 0 ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
								btbWA[(1 - i) * FetchUnitTypes_BTB_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:FetchUnitTypes_BTB_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = resetIndex;
								btbWV[((1 - i) * 19) + 17-:4] = 0;
								btbWV[((1 - i) * 19) + 13-:13] = 0;
								btbWV[((1 - i) * 19) + 18] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							end
					end
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_12
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							btbRA[(1 - i) * FetchUnitTypes_BTB_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:FetchUnitTypes_BTB_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = i;
					end
					pushBtbQueue = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					popBtbQueue = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				Core.ifStageIF.readIsCondBr = readIsCondBr;
				Core.ifStageIF.btbOut = btbOut;
				Core.ifStageIF.btbHit = btbHit;
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : brPred
			if (1) begin : predictor
				reg stall;
				reg clear;
				localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
				reg [18:0] pcIn;
				localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
				reg [0:1] brPredTaken;
				reg updateHistory [0:1];
				localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
				reg [0:1] phtWE;
				localparam FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_NUM = 2048;
				localparam FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 11;
				reg [21:0] phtWA;
				localparam FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH = 2;
				reg [3:0] phtWV;
				reg [1:0] phtPrevValue [0:1];
				reg [21:0] phtRA;
				wire [3:0] phtRV;
				localparam FetchUnitTypes_BRANCH_GLOBAL_HISTORY_BIT_WIDTH = 10;
				reg [9:0] nextBrGlobalHistory;
				reg [9:0] regBrGlobalHistory;
				reg [19:0] brGlobalHistory;
				reg mispred;
				reg pushPhtQueue;
				reg popPhtQueue;
				wire full;
				wire empty;
				localparam FetchUnitTypes_PHT_QUEUE_SIZE = 32;
				localparam BasicTypes_ADDR_WIDTH = 32;
				reg [33:0] phtQueue [0:31];
				localparam FetchUnitTypes_PHT_QUEUE_SIZE_BIT_WIDTH = 5;
				wire [4:0] headPtr;
				wire [4:0] tailPtr;
				reg updatePht;
				BlockMultiBankRAM #(
					.ENTRY_NUM(FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_NUM),
					.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH),
					.READ_NUM(BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH),
					.WRITE_NUM(BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH)
				) pht(
					.clk(Core.npStageIF.clk),
					.we(phtWE),
					.wa(phtWA),
					.wv(phtWV),
					.ra(phtRA),
					.rv(phtRV)
				);
				QueuePointer #(.SIZE(FetchUnitTypes_PHT_QUEUE_SIZE)) phtQueuePointer(
					.clk(Core.npStageIF.clk),
					.rst(Core.npStageIF.rst),
					.push(pushPhtQueue),
					.pop(popPhtQueue),
					.full(full),
					.empty(empty),
					.headPtr(headPtr),
					.tailPtr(tailPtr)
				);
				reg [10:0] resetIndex;
				always @(posedge Core.npStageIF.clk)
					if (Core.npStageIF.rstStart)
						resetIndex <= 0;
					else
						resetIndex <= resetIndex + 1;
				always @(posedge Core.npStageIF.clk) begin
					if (Core.npStageIF.rst)
						regBrGlobalHistory <= 1'sb0;
					else
						regBrGlobalHistory <= nextBrGlobalHistory;
					if (pushPhtQueue) begin
						phtQueue[headPtr][33-:32] <= phtWA[0+:FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
						phtQueue[headPtr][1-:FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH] <= phtWV[0+:FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH];
					end
				end
				localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
				localparam BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH = 8;
				localparam BasicTypes_INSN_WIDTH = 32;
				localparam BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH = 4;
				localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
				localparam FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_MAX = 3;
				localparam BasicTypes_INSN_ADDR_BIT_WIDTH = 2;
				function automatic [10:0] ToPHT_Index_Global;
					input reg [31:0] addr;
					input reg [9:0] gh;
					reg [10:0] phtIndex;
					begin
						phtIndex = addr[12:BasicTypes_INSN_ADDR_BIT_WIDTH];
						phtIndex[10:1] = phtIndex[10:1] ^ gh;
						ToPHT_Index_Global = phtIndex;
					end
				endfunction
				always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_13
					reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;
					_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
					stall = Core.ctrlIF.ifStage[1];
					clear = Core.ctrlIF.ifStage[0];
					pcIn = Core.npStageIF.predNextPC;
					nextBrGlobalHistory = regBrGlobalHistory;
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_14
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							begin
								brPredTaken[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
								brGlobalHistory[(1 - i) * FetchUnitTypes_BRANCH_GLOBAL_HISTORY_BIT_WIDTH+:FetchUnitTypes_BRANCH_GLOBAL_HISTORY_BIT_WIDTH] = regBrGlobalHistory;
								updateHistory[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							end
					end
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_15
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
								_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
								brPredTaken[i] = phtRV[((1 - i) * FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH) + 1] && Core.ifStageIF.btbHit[i];
								updateHistory[i] = (Core.ifStageIF.btbHit[i] && Core.ifStageIF.readIsCondBr[i]) && Core.ifStageIF.updateBrHistory[i];
								if (updateHistory[i]) begin
									nextBrGlobalHistory = (nextBrGlobalHistory << 1) | brPredTaken[i];
									if (brPredTaken[i])
										_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
								end
							end
						if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
							_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
					end
					if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00) begin
						Core.ifStageIF.phtPrevValue = phtRV;
						Core.ifStageIF.brPredTaken = brPredTaken;
						Core.ifStageIF.brGlobalHistory = brGlobalHistory;
						begin : sv2v_autoblock_16
							reg signed [31:0] i;
							for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
								begin
									phtWE[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
									phtWV[(1 - i) * FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH+:FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
									phtPrevValue[i] = Core.npStageIF.brResult[((1 - i) * 55) + 1-:2];
									phtWA[(1 - i) * FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = ToPHT_Index_Global(Core.npStageIF.brResult[((1 - i) * 55) + 54-:19], Core.npStageIF.brResult[((1 - i) * 55) + 11-:10]);
								end
						end
						updatePht = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						pushPhtQueue = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						begin : sv2v_autoblock_17
							reg signed [31:0] i;
							for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
								begin
									if (updatePht)
										pushPhtQueue = Core.npStageIF.brResult[((1 - i) * 55) + 12];
									else begin
										phtWE[i] = Core.npStageIF.brResult[((1 - i) * 55) + 12];
										updatePht = updatePht | phtWE[i];
									end
									mispred = Core.npStageIF.brResult[((1 - i) * 55) + 13] && Core.npStageIF.brResult[((1 - i) * 55) + 12];
									if (Core.npStageIF.brResult[((1 - i) * 55) + 16])
										phtWV[(1 - i) * FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH+:FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH] = (phtPrevValue[i] == FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_MAX ? FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_MAX : phtPrevValue[i] + 1);
									else
										phtWV[(1 - i) * FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH+:FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH] = (phtPrevValue[i] == 0 ? 0 : phtPrevValue[i] - 1);
									if (mispred)
										if (Core.npStageIF.brResult[((1 - i) * 55) + 14])
											nextBrGlobalHistory = (Core.npStageIF.brResult[((1 - i) * 55) + 11-:10] << 1) | Core.npStageIF.brResult[((1 - i) * 55) + 16];
										else
											nextBrGlobalHistory = Core.npStageIF.brResult[((1 - i) * 55) + 11-:10];
								end
						end
						begin : sv2v_autoblock_18
							reg signed [31:0] i;
							for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
								phtRA[(1 - i) * FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = ToPHT_Index_Global(pcIn + (i * BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH), nextBrGlobalHistory);
						end
						if (!empty && !updatePht) begin
							popPhtQueue = BasicTypes_TRUE;
							phtWE[0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
							phtWA[FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = phtQueue[tailPtr][33-:32];
							phtWV[FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH+:FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH] = phtQueue[tailPtr][1-:FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH];
						end
						else
							popPhtQueue = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						if (Core.npStageIF.rst) begin
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_19
								reg signed [31:0] i;
								for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
									begin
										phtWE[i] = (i == 0 ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
										phtWA[(1 - i) * FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = resetIndex;
										phtWV[(1 - i) * FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH+:FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH] = 2;
									end
							end
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_20
								reg signed [31:0] i;
								for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
									phtRA[(1 - i) * FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = i;
							end
							pushPhtQueue = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							popPhtQueue = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : ifStage
			reg stall;
			reg clear;
			reg empty;
			reg regStall;
			reg beginStall;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			always @(posedge Core.ifStageIF.clk)
				if (Core.ifStageIF.rst)
					regStall <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
				else
					regStall <= stall;
			localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			reg [39:0] pipeReg;
			localparam BasicTypes_INSN_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_BRANCH_GLOBAL_HISTORY_BIT_WIDTH = 10;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH = 2;
			reg [167:0] nextStage;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			always @(*) begin
				stall = Core.ctrlIF.ifStage[1];
				clear = Core.ctrlIF.ifStage[0];
				Core.ctrlIF.ifStageSendBubbleLower = pipeReg[39] && !Core.ifStageIF.icReadHit[0];
				empty = BasicTypes_TRUE;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_21
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						if (pipeReg[((1 - i) * 20) + 19])
							empty = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				Core.ctrlIF.ifStageEmpty = empty;
				beginStall = !regStall && stall;
			end
			reg isFlushed [0:1];
			always @(posedge Core.ifStageIF.clk)
				if (Core.ifStageIF.rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_22
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						pipeReg[(1 - i) * 20+:20] <= 1'sb0;
				end
				else if (!stall)
					pipeReg <= Core.npStageIF.nextStage;
				else begin : sv2v_autoblock_23
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						if (isFlushed[i])
							pipeReg[((1 - i) * 20) + 19] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
			reg [63:0] brPred;
			reg [63:0] regBrPred;
			always @(posedge Core.ifStageIF.clk)
				if (Core.ifStageIF.rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_24
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						regBrPred[(1 - i) * 32+:32] <= 1'sb0;
				end
				else if (beginStall)
					regBrPred <= brPred;
			localparam BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH = 8;
			localparam BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH = 4;
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_25
				reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;
				_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_26
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							brPred[((1 - i) * 32) + 31-:19] = (Core.ifStageIF.brPredTaken[i] ? Core.ifStageIF.btbOut[(1 - i) * 19+:19] : pipeReg[((1 - i) * 20) + 18-:MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH] + BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH);
							brPred[((1 - i) * 32) + 12] = Core.ifStageIF.brPredTaken[i];
							brPred[((1 - i) * 32) + 11-:10] = Core.ifStageIF.brGlobalHistory[(1 - i) * 10+:10];
							brPred[((1 - i) * 32) + 1-:FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH] = Core.ifStageIF.phtPrevValue[(1 - i) * 2+:2];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_27
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
							_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
							isFlushed[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							if ((!regStall && pipeReg[((1 - i) * 20) + 19]) && brPred[((1 - i) * 32) + 12]) begin
								begin : sv2v_autoblock_28
									reg signed [31:0] j;
									for (j = i + 1; j < BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH; j = j + 1)
										isFlushed[j] = pipeReg[((1 - j) * 20) + 19];
								end
								_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
							end
						end
					if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
						_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
				end
				if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00) begin : sv2v_autoblock_29
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						Core.ifStageIF.updateBrHistory[i] = !regStall && pipeReg[((1 - i) * 20) + 19];
				end
			end
			localparam BasicTypes_ADDR_WIDTH = 32;
			reg [31:0] fetchAddrOut;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_TAG = 13;
			function automatic [31:0] MemoryMapTypes_ToAddrFromPC;
				input reg [18:0] pc;
				MemoryMapTypes_ToAddrFromPC = {pc[18], {MemoryMapTypes_PC_TAG {1'b0}}, pc[17:0]};
			endfunction
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_0_ADDR_BIT_WIDTH = 16;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_0_BEGIN = 32'h00001000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_0_END = 32'h00010000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_1_ADDR_BIT_WIDTH = 18;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_1_BEGIN = 32'h80000000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_1_END = 32'h80040000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SERIAL_OUTPUT = 32'h40002000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_BASE = 32'h40000000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_BEGIN = MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_BASE;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_END = 1073741840;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_ADDR_BIT_WIDTH = 19;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_BEGIN = 32'h80040000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_END = 32'h80050000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_SECTION_0_BASE = 20'h00000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_SECTION_1_BASE = 20'h10000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_SERIAL_OUTPUT = 20'h02000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_TIMER_BASE = 20'h00000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_TIMER_ZONE_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_BASE = 20'h10000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_WIDTH = 22;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH = 20;
			function automatic [21:0] MemoryMapTypes_ToPhyAddrFromLogical;
				input reg [31:0] logAddr;
				reg [21:0] phyAddr;
				begin
					if (logAddr == MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SERIAL_OUTPUT) begin
						phyAddr[21] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						phyAddr[20] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						phyAddr[19-:MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH] = MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_SERIAL_OUTPUT;
					end
					else if ((MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_BEGIN <= logAddr) && (logAddr < MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_END)) begin
						phyAddr[21] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						phyAddr[20] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						phyAddr[19-:MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH] = MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_TIMER_BASE + logAddr[3:0];
					end
					else if ((MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_BEGIN <= logAddr) && (logAddr < MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_END)) begin
						phyAddr[21] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						phyAddr[20] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						phyAddr[19-:MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH] = MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_BASE + logAddr[18:0];
					end
					else if ((MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_0_BEGIN <= logAddr) && (logAddr < MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_0_END)) begin
						phyAddr[21] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						phyAddr[20] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						phyAddr[19-:MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH] = MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_SECTION_0_BASE + logAddr[MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_0_ADDR_BIT_WIDTH:0];
					end
					else if ((MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_1_BEGIN <= logAddr) && (logAddr < MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_1_END)) begin
						phyAddr[21] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						phyAddr[20] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						phyAddr[19-:MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH] = MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_SECTION_1_BASE + logAddr[17:0];
					end
					else begin
						phyAddr[21] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						phyAddr[20] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						phyAddr[19-:MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH] = 32'hcccccccc;
					end
					MemoryMapTypes_ToPhyAddrFromLogical = phyAddr;
				end
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin
				Core.ifStageIF.icRE = pipeReg[39];
				fetchAddrOut = MemoryMapTypes_ToAddrFromPC(pipeReg[38-:MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH]);
				Core.ifStageIF.icReadAddrIn = MemoryMapTypes_ToPhyAddrFromLogical(fetchAddrOut);
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_30
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							Core.ifStageIF.fetchStageIsValid[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 20) + 19];
							Core.ifStageIF.fetchStagePC[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 20) + 18-:MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH];
						end
				end
			end
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_31
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							if (((stall || clear) || Core.ifStageIF.rst) || isFlushed[i])
								nextStage[((1 - i) * 84) + 83] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							else
								nextStage[((1 - i) * 84) + 83] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 20) + 19];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 84) + 50-:19] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 20) + 18-:MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 84) + 31-:32] = (regStall ? regBrPred[(1 - i) * 32+:32] : brPred[(1 - i) * 32+:32]);
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 84) + 82-:32] = (pipeReg[((1 - i) * 20) + 19] ? Core.ifStageIF.icReadDataOut[i] : {32 {1'sb0}});
						end
				end
				Core.ifStageIF.nextStage = nextStage;
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : iCache
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM = 8;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 3;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_WAY_NUM = 2;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH = 8;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_WIDTH = 22;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_TAG_BIT_WIDTH = 11;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH = 20;
			function automatic [21:0] GetFullPhyAddr;
				input reg [7:0] index;
				input reg [10:0] tag;
				GetFullPhyAddr = {tag, index, {CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM_BIT_WIDTH {1'b0}}};
			endfunction
			function automatic [7:0] GetICacheIndex;
				input reg [21:0] addr;
				GetICacheIndex = addr[10:CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
			endfunction
			function automatic [10:0] GetICacheTag;
				input reg [21:0] addr;
				GetICacheTag = addr[21:11];
			endfunction
			localparam BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH = 8;
			localparam BasicTypes_INSN_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam BasicTypes_INSN_ADDR_BIT_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_LINE_INSN_NUM = 2;
			function automatic [0:0] GetICacheLineInsnIndex;
				input reg [21:0] addr;
				GetICacheLineInsnIndex = addr[2:BasicTypes_INSN_ADDR_BIT_WIDTH];
			endfunction
			function automatic [1:0] UpdateNRUState;
				input reg [1:0] NRUState;
				input reg [0:0] way;
				if ((NRUState | (1 << way)) == 3)
					UpdateNRUState = 1 << way;
				else
					UpdateNRUState = NRUState | (1 << way);
			endfunction
			function automatic [1:0] DecideWayToEvictByNRUState;
				input reg [1:0] NRUState;
				DecideWayToEvictByNRUState = (NRUState | (NRUState + 1)) ^ NRUState;
			endfunction
			reg [2:0] regPhase;
			reg [2:0] nextPhase;
			always @(posedge Core.npStageIF.clk)
				if (Core.npStageIF.rst)
					regPhase <= 3'd0;
				else
					regPhase <= nextPhase;
			reg regFlushStart;
			reg nextFlushStart;
			reg regFlush;
			reg nextFlush;
			reg regFlushReqAck;
			reg nextFlushReqAck;
			reg flushComplete;
			wire valid [0:1];
			reg hit;
			wire [1:0] hitArray;
			reg [0:0] hitWay;
			reg we [0:1];
			reg [7:0] readIndex;
			reg [7:0] writeIndex;
			reg [7:0] nextReadIndex;
			reg [10:0] readTag;
			reg [10:0] writeTag;
			wire [63:0] readLineInsnList [0:1];
			genvar i;
			for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_WAY_NUM; i = i + 1) begin : genblk1
				ICacheArray array(
					.clk(Core.npStageIF.clk),
					.rst((Core.npStageIF.rst ? Core.npStageIF.rst : regFlush)),
					.rstStart((Core.npStageIF.rstStart ? Core.npStageIF.rstStart : regFlushStart)),
					.we(we[i]),
					.writeIndex(writeIndex),
					.writeTag(writeTag),
					.writeLineData(Core.cacheSystemIF.icMemAccessResult[63-:64]),
					.readIndex(nextReadIndex),
					.readTag(readTag),
					.hit(hitArray[i]),
					.valid(valid[i]),
					.readLineData(readLineInsnList[i])
				);
			end
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_32
				reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;
				_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
				readIndex = GetICacheIndex(Core.ifStageIF.icReadAddrIn);
				readTag = GetICacheTag(Core.ifStageIF.icReadAddrIn);
				nextReadIndex = GetICacheIndex(Core.npStageIF.icNextReadAddrIn);
				hit = |hitArray;
				hitWay = 1'sb0;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_33
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_WAY_NUM; i = i + 1)
						if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
							_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
							if (hitArray[i]) begin
								hitWay = i;
								_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
							end
						end
					if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
						_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
				end
			end
			reg [1:0] updatedNRUState;
			wire [1:0] readNRUState;
			reg [1:0] wayToEvictOneHot;
			reg [0:0] wayToEvict;
			reg nruStateWE;
			wire [7:0] rstIndex;
			ICacheNRUStateArray nruStateArray(
				.clk(Core.npStageIF.clk),
				.rst((Core.npStageIF.rst ? Core.npStageIF.rst : regFlush)),
				.rstStart((Core.npStageIF.rstStart ? Core.npStageIF.rstStart : regFlushStart)),
				.writeIndex(readIndex),
				.writeWay(hitWay),
				.writeHit(nruStateWE),
				.writeNRUState(updatedNRUState),
				.readIndex(nextReadIndex),
				.readNRUState(readNRUState),
				.rstIndex(rstIndex)
			);
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_34
				reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;
				_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
				updatedNRUState = UpdateNRUState(readNRUState, hitWay);
				wayToEvictOneHot = DecideWayToEvictByNRUState(readNRUState);
				wayToEvict = 1'sb0;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_35
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_WAY_NUM; i = i + 1)
						if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
							_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
							if (wayToEvictOneHot[i]) begin
								wayToEvict = i;
								_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
							end
						end
					if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
						_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
				end
			end
			localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
			reg [0:0] wordPtr [0:1];
			ICacheHitLogic iCacheHitLogic(
				.hitIn((hit && (regPhase == 3'd0)) && Core.ifStageIF.icRE),
				.headWordPtr(wordPtr[0]),
				.hitOut(Core.ifStageIF.icReadHit)
			);
			always @(*) begin
				wordPtr[0] = GetICacheLineInsnIndex(Core.ifStageIF.icReadAddrIn);
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_36
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 1; i < BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						wordPtr[i] = wordPtr[0] + i;
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_37
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						Core.ifStageIF.icReadDataOut[i] = readLineInsnList[hitWay][wordPtr[i] * BasicTypes_INSN_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_INSN_WIDTH];
				end
			end
			reg regMissValid;
			reg nextMissValid;
			reg [7:0] regMissIndex;
			reg [7:0] nextMissIndex;
			reg [10:0] regMissTag;
			reg [10:0] nextMissTag;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM = 2;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_MEM_ACCESS_SERIAL_BIT_SIZE = 2;
			reg [1:0] regSerial;
			reg [1:0] nextSerial;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_38
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_WAY_NUM; i = i + 1)
						we[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				nruStateWE = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				nextPhase = regPhase;
				nextMissValid = regMissValid;
				nextMissIndex = regMissIndex;
				nextMissTag = regMissTag;
				nextSerial = regSerial;
				nextFlushStart = regFlushStart;
				nextFlush = regFlush;
				nextFlushReqAck = regFlushReqAck;
				flushComplete = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				case (regPhase)
					default: nextPhase = 3'd0;
					3'd0:
						if (Core.cacheSystemIF.icFlushReq) begin
							nextPhase = 3'd4;
							nextFlushStart = BasicTypes_TRUE;
							nextFlush = BasicTypes_TRUE;
							nextFlushReqAck = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						end
						else if (Core.ifStageIF.icRE && !hit) begin
							nextPhase = 3'd1;
							nextMissValid = BasicTypes_TRUE;
							nextMissIndex = readIndex;
							nextMissTag = readTag;
							nextFlushReqAck = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						end
						else if (Core.ifStageIF.icRE)
							nruStateWE = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					3'd1:
						if (Core.cacheSystemIF.icMemAccessReqAck[3]) begin
							nextPhase = 3'd2;
							nextMissValid = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							nextSerial = Core.cacheSystemIF.icMemAccessReqAck[2-:2];
						end
					3'd2:
						if (Core.cacheSystemIF.icMemAccessResult[66] && (Core.cacheSystemIF.icMemAccessResult[65-:2] == regSerial)) begin
							nextPhase = 3'd3;
							we[wayToEvict] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						end
					3'd3: begin
						nextFlushReqAck = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						nextPhase = 3'd0;
					end
					3'd4: begin
						nextFlushStart = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						nextMissTag = 1'sb0;
						nextPhase = 3'd5;
					end
					3'd5:
						if (&rstIndex) begin
							nextFlush = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							nextPhase = 3'd6;
						end
					3'd6: begin
						flushComplete = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						if (Core.cacheSystemIF.flushComplete) begin
							nextFlushReqAck = BasicTypes_TRUE;
							nextPhase = 3'd0;
						end
					end
				endcase
				writeIndex = regMissIndex;
				writeTag = regMissTag;
				Core.cacheSystemIF.icMemAccessReq[22] = regMissValid;
				Core.cacheSystemIF.icMemAccessReq[21-:22] = GetFullPhyAddr(regMissIndex, regMissTag);
				Core.cacheSystemIF.icFlushReqAck = regFlushReqAck;
				Core.cacheSystemIF.icFlushComplete = flushComplete;
			end
			always @(posedge Core.npStageIF.clk)
				if (Core.npStageIF.rst) begin
					regMissValid <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
					regMissIndex <= 1'sb0;
					regMissTag <= 1'sb0;
					regSerial <= 1'sb0;
				end
				else begin
					regMissValid <= nextMissValid;
					regMissIndex <= nextMissIndex;
					regMissTag <= nextMissTag;
					regSerial <= nextSerial;
				end
			always @(posedge Core.npStageIF.clk)
				if (Core.npStageIF.rst) begin
					regFlush <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
					regFlushStart <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
					regFlushReqAck <= BasicTypes_TRUE;
				end
				else begin
					regFlush <= nextFlush;
					regFlushStart <= nextFlushStart;
					regFlushReqAck <= nextFlushReqAck;
				end
		end
		if (1) begin : pdStage
			localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;
			localparam BasicTypes_INSN_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_BRANCH_GLOBAL_HISTORY_BIT_WIDTH = 10;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			reg [167:0] pipeReg;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			always @(posedge Core.pdStageIF.clk)
				if (Core.pdStageIF.rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_39
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						pipeReg[((1 - i) * 84) + 83] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else if (!Core.ctrlIF.pdStage[1])
					pipeReg <= Core.ifStageIF.nextStage;
			localparam BasicTypes_ADDR_WIDTH = 32;
			reg [31:0] pc [0:1];
			reg illegalPC [0:1];
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_0_BEGIN = 32'h00001000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_0_END = 32'h00010000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_1_BEGIN = 32'h80000000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_1_END = 32'h80040000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SERIAL_OUTPUT = 32'h40002000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_BASE = 32'h40000000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_BEGIN = MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_BASE;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_END = 1073741840;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_BEGIN = 32'h80040000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_END = 32'h80050000;
			function automatic [1:0] MemoryMapTypes_GetMemoryMapType;
				input reg [31:0] addr;
				if (addr == MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SERIAL_OUTPUT)
					MemoryMapTypes_GetMemoryMapType = 2'b01;
				else if ((MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_BEGIN <= addr) && (addr < MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_END))
					MemoryMapTypes_GetMemoryMapType = 2'b01;
				else if ((MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_BEGIN <= addr) && (addr < MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_END))
					MemoryMapTypes_GetMemoryMapType = 2'b00;
				else if ((MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_0_BEGIN <= addr) && (addr < MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_0_END))
					MemoryMapTypes_GetMemoryMapType = 2'b00;
				else if ((MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_1_BEGIN <= addr) && (addr < MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_1_END))
					MemoryMapTypes_GetMemoryMapType = 2'b00;
				else
					MemoryMapTypes_GetMemoryMapType = 2'b10;
			endfunction
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_TAG = 13;
			function automatic [31:0] MemoryMapTypes_ToAddrFromPC;
				input reg [18:0] pc;
				MemoryMapTypes_ToAddrFromPC = {pc[18], {MemoryMapTypes_PC_TAG {1'b0}}, pc[17:0]};
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_40
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
					begin
						pc[i] = MemoryMapTypes_ToAddrFromPC(pipeReg[((1 - i) * 84) + 50-:19]);
						illegalPC[i] = (MemoryMapTypes_GetMemoryMapType(pc[i]) == 2'b10 ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
					end
			end
			localparam MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM = 3;
			localparam MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_INDEX_BITS = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_LREG_NUM = 32;
			localparam BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 5;
			localparam OpFormatTypes_BR_DISP_WIDTH = 20;
			localparam BasicTypes_RISCV_SHIFTER_WIDTH = 30;
			localparam OpFormatTypes_CSR_IMM_WIDTH = 5;
			wire [425:0] microOps;
			wire [9:0] insnInfo;
			genvar i;
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1) begin : genblk1
				Decoder decoder(
					.insn(pipeReg[((1 - i) * 84) + 82-:32]),
					.insnInfo(insnInfo[i * 5+:5]),
					.microOps(microOps[71 * (i * 3)+:213]),
					.illegalPC(illegalPC[i])
				);
			end
			reg stall;
			reg clear;
			reg empty;
			reg [603:0] nextStage;
			always @(*) begin
				stall = Core.ctrlIF.pdStage[1];
				clear = Core.ctrlIF.pdStage[0];
				empty = BasicTypes_TRUE;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_41
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 302) + 301] = ((stall || clear) || Core.pdStageIF.rst ? BasicTypes_FALSE : pipeReg[((1 - i) * 84) + 83]);
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 302) + 300-:32] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 84) + 82-:32];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 302) + 268-:19] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 84) + 50-:19];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 302) + 249-:32] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 84) + 31-:32];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 302) + 4-:5] = insnInfo[i * 5+:5];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 302) + 217-:213] = microOps[71 * (i * 3)+:213];
							if (pipeReg[((1 - i) * 84) + 83])
								empty = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						end
				end
				Core.pdStageIF.nextStage = nextStage;
				Core.ctrlIF.pdStageEmpty = empty;
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : idStage
			localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;
			localparam BasicTypes_INSN_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_BRANCH_GLOBAL_HISTORY_BIT_WIDTH = 10;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			localparam MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM = 3;
			localparam MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_INDEX_BITS = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_LREG_NUM = 32;
			localparam BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 5;
			localparam OpFormatTypes_BR_DISP_WIDTH = 20;
			localparam BasicTypes_RISCV_SHIFTER_WIDTH = 30;
			localparam OpFormatTypes_CSR_IMM_WIDTH = 5;
			reg [603:0] pipeReg;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			always @(posedge Core.idStageIF.clk)
				if (Core.idStageIF.rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_42
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						pipeReg[((1 - i) * 302) + 301] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else if (!Core.ctrlIF.idStage[1])
					pipeReg <= Core.pdStageIF.nextStage;
			reg stall;
			reg clear;
			reg empty;
			reg [245:0] nextStage;
			localparam MicroOpTypes_ALL_DECODED_MICRO_OP_WIDTH = 6;
			reg [425:0] microOps;
			reg [9:0] insnInfo;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_43
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_44
								reg signed [31:0] j;
								for (j = 0; j < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; j = j + 1)
									microOps[((i * MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM) + j) * 71+:71] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 302) + (5 + (j * 71))+:71];
							end
							insnInfo[i * 5+:5] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 302) + 4-:5];
						end
				end
				empty = BasicTypes_TRUE;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_45
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						if (pipeReg[((1 - i) * 302) + 301])
							empty = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				Core.ctrlIF.idStageEmpty = empty;
			end
			reg initiate;
			reg complete;
			reg [63:0] isfIn;
			wire flush;
			reg [0:1] insnValidIn;
			wire [0:1] insnValidOut;
			wire [0:1] insnFlush;
			wire [63:0] brPredOut;
			reg [63:0] brPredIn;
			reg [37:0] pcIn;
			wire [18:0] recoveredPC;
			DecodedBranchResolver decodeStageBranchResolver(
				.clk(Core.idStageIF.clk),
				.rst(Core.idStageIF.rst),
				.stall(Core.ctrlIF.idStage[1] && !Core.ctrlIF.stallByDecodeStage),
				.decodeComplete(complete),
				.insnValidIn(insnValidIn),
				.isf(isfIn),
				.brPredIn(brPredIn),
				.pc(pcIn),
				.insnInfo(insnInfo),
				.insnValidOut(insnValidOut),
				.insnFlushed(insnFlush),
				.flush(flush),
				.brPredOut(brPredOut),
				.recoveredPC(recoveredPC)
			);
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_46
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							insnValidIn[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 302) + 301];
							isfIn[i * 32+:32] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 302) + 300-:32];
							pcIn[(1 - i) * MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH+:MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 302) + 268-:19];
							brPredIn[i * 32+:32] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 302) + 249-:32];
						end
				end
				Core.idStageIF.nextFlush = (complete && flush) && !clear;
				Core.idStageIF.nextRecoveredPC = recoveredPC;
			end
			reg [5:0] remainingValidMOps;
			reg [5:0] nextValidMOps;
			reg [5:0] curValidMOps;
			wire [5:0] pickedValidMOps;
			reg [5:0] serializedMOps;
			localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 1;
			localparam BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE;
			localparam MicroOpTypes_ALL_DECODED_MICRO_OP_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 3;
			wire [5:0] mopPickedIndex;
			wire [0:1] mopPicked;
			reg [0:0] orgPickedInsnLane;
			always @(posedge Core.idStageIF.clk)
				if (Core.idStageIF.rst || Core.ctrlIF.idStage[0]) begin
					remainingValidMOps <= 0;
					initiate <= BasicTypes_TRUE;
				end
				else if (!(Core.ctrlIF.idStage[1] && !Core.ctrlIF.stallByDecodeStage)) begin
					initiate <= complete;
					remainingValidMOps <= nextValidMOps;
				end
			function automatic [0:0] ToInsnLane;
				input reg [2:0] mopLane;
				reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;
				begin
					_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_47
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
								_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
								begin : sv2v_autoblock_48
									reg signed [31:0] j;
									begin : sv2v_autoblock_49
										reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump_1;
										_sv2v_jump_1 = _sv2v_jump;
										for (j = 0; j < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; j = j + 1)
											if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
												_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
												if (mopLane == ((i * MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM) + j)) begin
													ToInsnLane = i;
													_sv2v_jump = 2'b11;
												end
											end
										if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
											_sv2v_jump = _sv2v_jump_1;
									end
								end
							end
						if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
							_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
					end
					if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00) begin
						ToInsnLane = 0;
						_sv2v_jump = 2'b11;
					end
				end
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin
				stall = Core.ctrlIF.idStage[1];
				clear = Core.ctrlIF.idStage[0];
				if (initiate) begin : sv2v_autoblock_50
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_ALL_DECODED_MICRO_OP_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						curValidMOps[i] = microOps[(i * 71) + 4];
				end
				else
					curValidMOps = remainingValidMOps;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_51
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_ALL_DECODED_MICRO_OP_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						serializedMOps[i] = microOps[i * 71];
				end
			end
			MicroOpPicker picker(
				.req(curValidMOps),
				.serialize(serializedMOps),
				.picked(mopPicked),
				.pickedIndex(mopPickedIndex),
				.next(pickedValidMOps)
			);
			always @(*) begin
				nextValidMOps = pickedValidMOps;
				complete = BasicTypes_TRUE;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_52
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_53
								reg signed [31:0] j;
								for (j = 0; j < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; j = j + 1)
									if (nextValidMOps[(i * MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM) + j] && insnValidIn[i])
										complete = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							end
							if (!insnValidOut[i]) begin : sv2v_autoblock_54
								reg signed [31:0] j;
								for (j = 0; j < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; j = j + 1)
									nextValidMOps[(i * MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM) + j] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							end
						end
				end
				Core.ctrlIF.idStageStallUpper = !complete;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_55
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							orgPickedInsnLane = ToInsnLane(mopPickedIndex[(1 - i) * MicroOpTypes_ALL_DECODED_MICRO_OP_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE+:MicroOpTypes_ALL_DECODED_MICRO_OP_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE]);
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 123) + 121-:71] = microOps[mopPickedIndex[(1 - i) * MicroOpTypes_ALL_DECODED_MICRO_OP_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE+:MicroOpTypes_ALL_DECODED_MICRO_OP_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE] * 71+:71];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 123) + 122] = (insnValidOut[orgPickedInsnLane] && mopPicked[i]) && !clear;
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 123) + 50-:19] = pipeReg[((1 - orgPickedInsnLane) * 302) + 268-:19];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 123) + 31-:32] = brPredOut[(1 - orgPickedInsnLane) * 32+:32];
						end
				end
				Core.idStageIF.nextStage = nextStage;
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : rnStage
			localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_BRANCH_GLOBAL_HISTORY_BIT_WIDTH = 10;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			localparam MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_INDEX_BITS = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_LREG_NUM = 32;
			localparam BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 5;
			localparam OpFormatTypes_BR_DISP_WIDTH = 20;
			localparam BasicTypes_RISCV_SHIFTER_WIDTH = 30;
			localparam OpFormatTypes_CSR_IMM_WIDTH = 5;
			reg [245:0] pipeReg;
			reg regFlush;
			reg [18:0] regRecoveredPC;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			always @(posedge Core.rnStageIF.clk)
				if (Core.rnStageIF.rst) begin
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_56
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							pipeReg[((1 - i) * 123) + 122] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
					end
					regFlush <= 1'sb0;
					regRecoveredPC <= 1'sb0;
				end
				else if (!Core.ctrlIF.rnStage[1]) begin
					pipeReg <= Core.idStageIF.nextStage;
					regFlush <= Core.idStageIF.nextFlush;
					regRecoveredPC <= Core.idStageIF.nextRecoveredPC;
				end
			always @(*) begin
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.recoverFromRename = regFlush;
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.recoveredPC_FromRename = regRecoveredPC;
				Core.ctrlIF.rnStageFlushUpper = regFlush;
			end
			reg stall;
			reg clear;
			reg empty;
			wire serialize;
			reg [1:0] valid;
			reg update [0:1];
			reg [141:0] opInfo;
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM = 64;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM = BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			reg [93:0] alEntry;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM = 64;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			reg [361:0] nextStage;
			reg isLoad [0:1];
			reg isStore [0:1];
			reg isBranch [0:1];
			reg activeListEmpty;
			reg storeQueueEmpty;
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_57
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							valid[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 123) + 122];
							opInfo[i * 71+:71] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 123) + 121-:71];
						end
				end
				Core.ctrlIF.rnStageSendBubbleLower = (|valid && (((!Core.renameLogicIF.allocatable || !Core.schedulerIF.allocatable) || !Core.activeListIF.allocatable) || !Core.loadStoreUnitIF.allocatable)) || serialize;
				stall = Core.ctrlIF.rnStage[1];
				clear = Core.ctrlIF.rnStage[0];
				activeListEmpty = Core.activeListIF.validEntryNum == 0;
				storeQueueEmpty = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.storeQueueEmpty;
			end
			RenameStageSerializer serializer(
				.clk(Core.rnStageIF.clk),
				.rst(Core.rnStageIF.rst),
				.stall(stall),
				.clear(clear),
				.activeListEmpty(activeListEmpty),
				.storeQueueEmpty(storeQueueEmpty),
				.opInfo(opInfo),
				.valid(valid),
				.serialize(serialize)
			);
			reg isEnv [0:1];
			localparam BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_58
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						update[i] = (valid[i] && !stall) && !clear;
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_59
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							isLoad[i] = (opInfo[(i * 71) + 67-:2] == 2'b10) && (opInfo[(i * 71) + 65-:3] == 3'b000);
							isStore[i] = (opInfo[(i * 71) + 67-:2] == 2'b10) && (opInfo[(i * 71) + 65-:3] == 3'b001);
							isEnv[i] = (opInfo[(i * 71) + 67-:2] == 2'b10) && (opInfo[(i * 71) + 65-:3] == 3'b110);
							isBranch[i] = (opInfo[(i * 71) + 67-:2] == 2'b00) && |{opInfo[(i * 71) + 65-:3] == 3'b010, opInfo[(i * 71) + 65-:3] == 3'b011};
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_60
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							Core.renameLogicIF.updateRMT[i] = update[i];
							Core.renameLogicIF.logSrcRegA[i] = (isBranch[i] ? opInfo[(i * 71) + 57-:5] : opInfo[(i * 71) + 57-:5]);
							Core.renameLogicIF.logSrcRegB[i] = (isBranch[i] ? opInfo[(i * 71) + 52-:5] : opInfo[(i * 71) + 52-:5]);
							Core.renameLogicIF.logDstReg[i] = (isBranch[i] ? opInfo[(i * 71) + 62-:5] : opInfo[(i * 71) + 62-:5]);
							Core.renameLogicIF.readRegA[i] = opInfo[(i * 71) + 12-:2] == 2'b00;
							Core.renameLogicIF.readRegB[i] = opInfo[(i * 71) + 10-:2] == 2'b00;
							Core.renameLogicIF.writeReg[i] = opInfo[(i * 71) + 8];
							Core.renameLogicIF.watWriteRegFromPipeReg[i] = opInfo[(i * 71) + 8] && update[i];
							Core.renameLogicIF.watWriteIssueQueuePtrFromPipeReg[i] = Core.schedulerIF.allocatedPtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_61
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 181) + 57-:6] = Core.renameLogicIF.phySrcRegA[(1 - i) * 6+:6];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 181) + 51-:6] = Core.renameLogicIF.phySrcRegB[(1 - i) * 6+:6];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 181) + 45-:6] = Core.renameLogicIF.phyDstReg[(1 - i) * 6+:6];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 181) + 39-:6] = Core.renameLogicIF.phyPrevDstReg[(1 - i) * 6+:6];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_62
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 181) + 33-:4] = Core.renameLogicIF.srcIssueQueuePtrRegA[(1 - i) * 4+:4];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 181) + 29-:4] = Core.renameLogicIF.srcIssueQueuePtrRegB[(1 - i) * 4+:4];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_63
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							Core.activeListIF.pushTail[i] = update[i];
							alEntry[((1 - i) * 47) + 46-:19] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 123) + 50-:19];
							alEntry[((1 - i) * 47) + 9-:6] = nextStage[((1 - i) * 181) + 39-:6];
							alEntry[((1 - i) * 47) + 15-:6] = nextStage[((1 - i) * 181) + 45-:6];
							alEntry[((1 - i) * 47) + 27-:5] = opInfo[(i * 71) + 62-:5];
							alEntry[((1 - i) * 47) + 22] = opInfo[(i * 71) + 8];
							alEntry[((1 - i) * 47) + 21] = isLoad[i];
							alEntry[((1 - i) * 47) + 20] = isStore[i];
							alEntry[((1 - i) * 47) + 19] = isBranch[i];
							alEntry[((1 - i) * 47) + 18] = opInfo[(i * 71) + 44];
							alEntry[((1 - i) * 47) + 16] = opInfo[(i * 71) + 7] || opInfo[(i * 71) + 6];
							alEntry[((1 - i) * 47) + 17] = opInfo[(i * 71) + 3];
							alEntry[((1 - i) * 47) + 3-:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.renameLogicIF.prevDependIssueQueuePtr[i];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 181) + 21-:6] = Core.activeListIF.pushedTailPtr[(1 - i) * 6+:6];
						end
				end
				Core.activeListIF.pushedTailData = alEntry;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_64
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							Core.schedulerIF.allocate[i] = update[i];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 181) + 25-:4] = Core.schedulerIF.allocatedPtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_65
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							Core.loadStoreUnitIF.allocateLoadQueue[i] = update[i] && isLoad[i];
							Core.loadStoreUnitIF.allocateStoreQueue[i] = update[i] && isStore[i];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 181) + 15-:4] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.allocatedLoadQueuePtr[i];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 181) + 11-:4] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.allocatedStoreQueuePtr[i];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_66
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						Core.rnStageIF.pc[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 123) + 50-:19];
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_67
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 181) + 180] = ((stall || clear) || Core.rnStageIF.rst ? BasicTypes_FALSE : valid[i]);
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 181) + 108-:19] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 123) + 50-:19];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 181) + 89-:32] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 123) + 31-:32];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 181) + 179-:71] = opInfo[i * 71+:71];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 181) + 7-:4] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.allocatedLoadQueuePtr[i];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 181) + 3-:4] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.allocatedStoreQueuePtr[i];
						end
				end
				Core.rnStageIF.nextStage = nextStage;
				empty = BasicTypes_TRUE;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_68
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						if (pipeReg[((1 - i) * 123) + 122])
							empty = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				Core.ctrlIF.rnStageEmpty = empty;
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : renameLogic
			localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;
			reg allocatePhyReg [0:1];
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM = 64;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM = BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			reg [11:0] allocatedPhyRegNum;
			reg [0:1] allocatePhyScalarReg;
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			wire [11:0] allocatedPhyScalarRegNum;
			localparam BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH = 2;
			reg [0:1] releasePhyScalarReg;
			reg [11:0] releasedPhyScalarRegNum;
			localparam BasicTypes_LSCALAR_NUM = 32;
			localparam RenameLogicTypes_SCALAR_FREE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM = 32;
			localparam RenameLogicTypes_SCALAR_FREE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 5;
			wire [RenameLogicTypes_SCALAR_FREE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH:0] scalarFreeListCount;
			localparam BasicTypes_LREG_NUM = 32;
			localparam BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 5;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			reg [93:0] alReadData;
			MultiWidthFreeList #(
				.SIZE(RenameLogicTypes_SCALAR_FREE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM),
				.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH),
				.PUSH_WIDTH(BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH),
				.POP_WIDTH(BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH),
				.INITIAL_LENGTH(RenameLogicTypes_SCALAR_FREE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM)
			) scalarFreeList(
				.clk(Core.renameLogicIF.clk),
				.rst(Core.renameLogicIF.rst),
				.rstStart(Core.renameLogicIF.rstStart),
				.count(scalarFreeListCount),
				.pop(allocatePhyScalarReg),
				.poppedData(allocatedPhyScalarRegNum),
				.push(releasePhyScalarReg),
				.pushedData(releasedPhyScalarRegNum)
			);
			reg [4:0] rmtRecoveryIndex;
			reg [BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH:0] rmtRecoveryCount;
			reg inRecoveryRMT;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam RenameLogicTypes_RECOVERY_FROM_RRMT = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			always @(posedge Core.renameLogicIF.clk)
				if (Core.renameLogicIF.rst) begin
					rmtRecoveryCount <= 0;
					rmtRecoveryIndex <= 0;
				end
				else if (Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase) begin
					rmtRecoveryCount <= BasicTypes_LREG_NUM;
					rmtRecoveryIndex <= 0;
				end
				else begin
					if (rmtRecoveryCount > BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH)
						rmtRecoveryCount <= rmtRecoveryCount - BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH;
					else
						rmtRecoveryCount <= 0;
					if (!RenameLogicTypes_RECOVERY_FROM_RRMT)
						rmtRecoveryIndex <= rmtRecoveryIndex + BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH;
					else
						rmtRecoveryIndex <= rmtRecoveryIndex + BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH;
				end
			reg [1:0] rmtWriteReg;
			reg [11:0] rmtWriteReg_PhyRegNum;
			reg [9:0] rmtWriteReg_LogRegNum;
			reg [0:1] watWriteReg;
			reg [9:0] watWriteLogRegNum;
			reg [7:0] watWriteIssueQueuePtr;
			reg [9:0] retRMT_ReadReg_LogRegNum;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_69
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						retRMT_ReadReg_LogRegNum[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_70
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							rmtWriteReg[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							rmtWriteReg_PhyRegNum[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
							rmtWriteReg_LogRegNum[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_71
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						allocatedPhyRegNum[((1 - i) * BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH) + 5-:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = allocatedPhyScalarRegNum[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
				end
				Core.renameLogicIF.phyDstReg = allocatedPhyRegNum;
				inRecoveryRMT = (RenameLogicTypes_RECOVERY_FROM_RRMT ? rmtRecoveryCount != 0 : Core.recoveryManagerIF.inRecoveryAL);
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.renameLogicRecoveryRMT = inRecoveryRMT;
				alReadData = Core.activeListIF.readData;
				Core.renameLogicIF.allocatable = !inRecoveryRMT && (scalarFreeListCount >= BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH);
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_72
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							allocatePhyReg[i] = Core.renameLogicIF.updateRMT[i] && Core.renameLogicIF.writeReg[i];
							allocatePhyScalarReg[i] = allocatePhyReg[i];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_73
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
