							releasePhyScalarReg[i] = Core.renameLogicIF.releaseReg[i];
							releasedPhyScalarRegNum[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.renameLogicIF.phyReleasedReg[i][5-:6];
						end
				end
				if (|Core.renameLogicIF.updateRMT) begin
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_74
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							begin
								rmtWriteReg[i] = Core.renameLogicIF.updateRMT[i] && Core.renameLogicIF.writeReg[i];
								rmtWriteReg_PhyRegNum[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = allocatedPhyRegNum[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
								rmtWriteReg_LogRegNum[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.renameLogicIF.logDstReg[i];
							end
					end
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_75
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							rmtWriteReg[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					end
				end
				else if (inRecoveryRMT)
					if (RenameLogicTypes_RECOVERY_FROM_RRMT) begin
						begin : sv2v_autoblock_76
							reg signed [31:0] i;
							for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
								begin
									rmtWriteReg[i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
									rmtWriteReg_PhyRegNum[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.renameLogicIF.retRMT_ReadReg_PhyRegNum[i];
									rmtWriteReg_LogRegNum[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = rmtRecoveryIndex + i;
									retRMT_ReadReg_LogRegNum[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = rmtRecoveryIndex + i;
								end
						end
						begin : sv2v_autoblock_77
							reg signed [31:0] i;
							for (i = BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
								begin
									rmtWriteReg[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
									rmtWriteReg_PhyRegNum[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
									rmtWriteReg_LogRegNum[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
								end
						end
					end
					else begin : sv2v_autoblock_78
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							begin
								rmtWriteReg[i] = (i < Core.activeListIF.popTailNum ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
								rmtWriteReg_PhyRegNum[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = alReadData[((1 - i) * 47) + 9-:6];
								rmtWriteReg_LogRegNum[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = alReadData[((1 - i) * 47) + 27-:5];
							end
					end
				if (!inRecoveryRMT) begin
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_79
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							begin
								watWriteReg[i] = Core.renameLogicIF.watWriteRegFromPipeReg[i];
								watWriteLogRegNum[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.renameLogicIF.logDstReg[i];
								watWriteIssueQueuePtr[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.renameLogicIF.watWriteIssueQueuePtrFromPipeReg[i];
							end
					end
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_80
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							begin
								watWriteReg[i] = 1'sb0;
								watWriteLogRegNum[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
								watWriteIssueQueuePtr[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
							end
					end
				end
				else begin : sv2v_autoblock_81
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							watWriteReg[i] = (i < Core.activeListIF.popTailNum ? alReadData[((1 - i) * 47) + 22] : BasicTypes_FALSE);
							watWriteLogRegNum[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = alReadData[((1 - i) * 47) + 27-:5];
							watWriteIssueQueuePtr[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = alReadData[((1 - i) * 47) + 3-:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
						end
				end
				Core.renameLogicIF.rmtWriteReg = rmtWriteReg;
				Core.renameLogicIF.rmtWriteReg_PhyRegNum = rmtWriteReg_PhyRegNum;
				Core.renameLogicIF.rmtWriteReg_LogRegNum = rmtWriteReg_LogRegNum;
				Core.renameLogicIF.watWriteReg = watWriteReg;
				Core.renameLogicIF.watWriteLogRegNum = watWriteLogRegNum;
				Core.renameLogicIF.watWriteIssueQueuePtr = watWriteIssueQueuePtr;
				Core.renameLogicIF.retRMT_ReadReg_LogRegNum = retRMT_ReadReg_LogRegNum;
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : renameLogicCommitter
			reg [1:0] phase;
			reg [1:0] nextPhase;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM = 64;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			reg [SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH:0] recoveryCount;
			reg [SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH:0] nextRecoveryCount;
			always @(posedge Core.renameLogicIF.clk) begin
				phase <= nextPhase;
				recoveryCount <= nextRecoveryCount;
			end
			localparam BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH = 2;
			always @(*)
				if (Core.renameLogicIF.rst) begin
					nextPhase = 2'd0;
					nextRecoveryCount = 0;
				end
				else if (Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase) begin
					nextPhase = 2'd1;
					nextRecoveryCount = 0;
				end
				else if (phase == 2'd1) begin
					nextPhase = 2'd2;
					nextRecoveryCount = Core.activeListIF.recoveryEntryNum;
				end
				else if (phase == 2'd2) begin
					nextPhase = (recoveryCount == 0 ? 2'd0 : 2'd2);
					nextRecoveryCount = (recoveryCount > BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH ? recoveryCount - BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH : 0);
				end
				else begin
					nextPhase = phase;
					nextRecoveryCount = recoveryCount;
				end
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM = 64;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM = BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			reg [13:0] regReleasedReg;
			reg [13:0] nextReleasedReg;
			always @(posedge Core.renameLogicIF.clk)
				if (Core.renameLogicIF.rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_82
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						regReleasedReg[(1 - i) * 7+:7] <= 1'sb0;
				end
				else
					regReleasedReg <= nextReleasedReg;
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_83
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
					begin
						Core.renameLogicIF.releaseReg[i] = regReleasedReg[((1 - i) * 7) + 6];
						Core.renameLogicIF.phyReleasedReg[i] = regReleasedReg[((1 - i) * 7) + 5-:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
					end
			end
			localparam BasicTypes_LREG_NUM = 32;
			localparam BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 5;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			reg [93:0] alReadData;
			localparam BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 1;
			reg [BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE:0] releaseNum;
			reg [BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE:0] flushNum;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			localparam RenameLogicTypes_RECOVERY_FROM_RRMT = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			always @(*) begin
				alReadData = Core.activeListIF.readData;
				if (phase == 2'd0) begin
					Core.activeListIF.popTailNum = 0;
					if (Core.renameLogicIF.commit)
						Core.activeListIF.popHeadNum = Core.renameLogicIF.commitNum;
					else
						Core.activeListIF.popHeadNum = 0;
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_84
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							begin
								if (Core.renameLogicIF.commit && (i < Core.renameLogicIF.commitNum))
									nextReleasedReg[((1 - i) * 7) + 6] = alReadData[((1 - i) * 47) + 22];
								else
									nextReleasedReg[((1 - i) * 7) + 6] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
								nextReleasedReg[((1 - i) * 7) + 5-:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = alReadData[((1 - i) * 47) + 9-:6];
							end
					end
					Core.recoveryManagerIF.inRecoveryAL = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					flushNum = 0;
				end
				else if (phase == 2'd1) begin
					Core.activeListIF.popHeadNum = 0;
					Core.activeListIF.popTailNum = 0;
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_85
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							begin
								nextReleasedReg[((1 - i) * 7) + 6] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
								nextReleasedReg[((1 - i) * 7) + 5-:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 0;
							end
					end
					Core.recoveryManagerIF.inRecoveryAL = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					flushNum = 0;
				end
				else if (RenameLogicTypes_RECOVERY_FROM_RRMT) begin
					releaseNum = BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH;
					if (releaseNum > recoveryCount)
						releaseNum = recoveryCount;
					flushNum = releaseNum;
					Core.activeListIF.popHeadNum = releaseNum;
					Core.activeListIF.popTailNum = 0;
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_86
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							begin
								if (i < releaseNum)
									nextReleasedReg[((1 - i) * 7) + 6] = alReadData[((1 - i) * 47) + 22];
								else
									nextReleasedReg[((1 - i) * 7) + 6] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
								nextReleasedReg[((1 - i) * 7) + 5-:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = alReadData[((1 - i) * 47) + 15-:6];
							end
					end
					Core.recoveryManagerIF.inRecoveryAL = BasicTypes_TRUE;
				end
				else begin
					releaseNum = BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH;
					if (releaseNum > recoveryCount)
						releaseNum = recoveryCount;
					flushNum = releaseNum;
					Core.activeListIF.popHeadNum = 0;
					Core.activeListIF.popTailNum = releaseNum;
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_87
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							begin
								if (i < releaseNum)
									nextReleasedReg[((1 - i) * 7) + 6] = alReadData[((1 - i) * 47) + 22];
								else
									nextReleasedReg[((1 - i) * 7) + 6] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
								nextReleasedReg[((1 - i) * 7) + 5-:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = alReadData[((1 - i) * 47) + 15-:6];
							end
					end
					Core.recoveryManagerIF.inRecoveryAL = BasicTypes_TRUE;
				end
				Core.renameLogicIF.flushNum = flushNum;
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : activeList
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM = 64;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			wire [5:0] headPtr;
			localparam BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH = 2;
			reg [11:0] headPtrList;
			wire [5:0] tailPtr;
			reg [11:0] tailPtrList;
			reg [11:0] readPtrList;
			localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;
			reg [11:0] pushedTailPtr;
			wire [SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH:0] count;
			reg [1:0] pushNum;
			BiTailMultiWidthQueuePointer #(
				.SIZE(SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM),
				.INITIAL_HEAD_PTR(0),
				.INITIAL_TAIL_PTR(0),
				.INITIAL_COUNT(0),
				.PUSH_WIDTH(BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH),
				.POP_WIDTH(BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH)
			) activeListPointer(
				.clk(Core.activeListIF.clk),
				.rst(Core.activeListIF.rst),
				.popHead(Core.activeListIF.popHeadNum > 0),
				.popHeadCount(Core.activeListIF.popHeadNum),
				.pushTail(pushNum > 0),
				.pushTailCount(pushNum),
				.popTail(Core.activeListIF.popTailNum > 0),
				.popTailCount(Core.activeListIF.popTailNum),
				.count(count),
				.headPtr(headPtr),
				.tailPtr(tailPtr)
			);
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			localparam RenameLogicTypes_RECOVERY_FROM_ACTIVE_LIST = BasicTypes_TRUE;
			always @(*) begin
				Core.activeListIF.allocatable = (count <= 62 ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
				Core.activeListIF.validEntryNum = count;
				pushNum = 0;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_88
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							if ((tailPtr + pushNum) >= SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM)
								pushedTailPtr[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = (tailPtr + pushNum) - SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM;
							else
								pushedTailPtr[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = tailPtr + pushNum;
							pushNum = pushNum + (Core.activeListIF.pushTail[i] ? 1 : 0);
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_89
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						if ((headPtr + i) < SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM)
							headPtrList[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = headPtr + i;
						else
							headPtrList[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = (headPtr + i) - SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM;
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_90
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						if (((tailPtr - 1) - i) >= SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM)
							tailPtrList[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = ((tailPtr - 1) - i) + SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM;
						else
							tailPtrList[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = (tailPtr - 1) - i;
				end
				if (RenameLogicTypes_RECOVERY_FROM_ACTIVE_LIST && (Core.activeListIF.popTailNum > 0))
					readPtrList = tailPtrList;
				else
					readPtrList = headPtrList;
				Core.activeListIF.detectedFlushRangeTailPtr = tailPtr + pushNum;
				Core.activeListIF.pushedTailPtr = pushedTailPtr;
				Core.ctrlIF.activeListEmpty = count == 0;
			end
			reg [0:1] pushTail;
			localparam BasicTypes_LREG_NUM = 32;
			localparam BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 5;
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM = 64;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM = BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			reg [93:0] pushedTailData;
			wire [93:0] readData;
			DistributedMultiBankRAM #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM),
				.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(47),
				.READ_NUM(BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH),
				.WRITE_NUM(BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH)
			) activeList(
				.clk(Core.activeListIF.clk),
				.we(pushTail),
				.wa(pushedTailPtr),
				.wv(pushedTailData),
				.ra(readPtrList),
				.rv(readData)
			);
			always @(*) begin
				pushTail = Core.activeListIF.pushTail;
				pushedTailData = Core.activeListIF.pushedTailData;
				Core.activeListIF.readData = readData;
			end
			localparam BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_ISSUE_WIDTH = 5;
			reg we [0:4];
			localparam BasicTypes_ADDR_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			reg [70:0] writeData [0:4];
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_91
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							we[i] = Core.activeListIF.intWrite[i];
							writeData[i] = Core.activeListIF.intWriteData[i];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_92
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							we[i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH] = Core.activeListIF.complexWrite[i];
							writeData[i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH] = Core.activeListIF.complexWriteData[i];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_93
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							we[(i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH] = Core.activeListIF.memWrite[i];
							writeData[(i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH] = Core.activeListIF.memWriteData[i];
						end
				end
			end
			reg [SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH:0] oldestAge;
			reg [SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH:0] writeAge [0:4];
			reg exceptionDetected;
			reg [2:0] refetchType;
			localparam BasicTypes_ISSUE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 3;
			reg [2:0] exceptionIndex;
			reg [69:0] recoveryReg;
			reg [69:0] nextRecoveryReg;
			reg [SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH:0] recoveryEntryNum;
			reg [SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH:0] nextRecoveryEntryNum;
			reg [5:0] flushRangeHeadPtr;
			reg [5:0] flushRangeTailPtr;
			always @(posedge Core.activeListIF.clk)
				if (Core.activeListIF.rst || (Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase && !exceptionDetected))
					recoveryReg <= 1'sb0;
				else
					recoveryReg <= nextRecoveryReg;
			always @(posedge Core.activeListIF.clk)
				if (Core.activeListIF.rst)
					recoveryEntryNum <= 1'sb0;
				else if (Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase)
					recoveryEntryNum <= nextRecoveryEntryNum;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_TAG = 13;
			function automatic [31:0] MemoryMapTypes_ToAddrFromPC;
				input reg [18:0] pc;
				MemoryMapTypes_ToAddrFromPC = {pc[18], {MemoryMapTypes_PC_TAG {1'b0}}, pc[17:0]};
			endfunction
			function automatic [SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH:0] SchedulerTypes_ActiveListPtrToAge;
				input reg [5:0] ptr;
				input reg [5:0] head;
				reg [SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH:0] age;
				begin
					age = ptr;
					if (ptr < head)
						SchedulerTypes_ActiveListPtrToAge = age + SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM;
					else
						SchedulerTypes_ActiveListPtrToAge = age;
				end
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin
				nextRecoveryReg = recoveryReg;
				oldestAge = SchedulerTypes_ActiveListPtrToAge(recoveryReg[18-:6], headPtr);
				exceptionIndex = 1'sb0;
				exceptionDetected = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				refetchType = 3'b000;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_94
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							writeAge[i] = SchedulerTypes_ActiveListPtrToAge(writeData[i][70-:6], headPtr);
							if (we[i] && !(|{writeData[i][56-:4] == 4'b0000, writeData[i][56-:4] == 4'b0001}))
								if (!nextRecoveryReg[4] || (writeAge[i] < oldestAge)) begin
									oldestAge = writeAge[i];
									nextRecoveryReg[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
									nextRecoveryReg[18-:6] = writeData[i][70-:6];
									nextRecoveryReg[12-:4] = writeData[i][64-:4];
									nextRecoveryReg[8-:4] = writeData[i][60-:4];
									nextRecoveryReg[69-:19] = writeData[i][52-:19];
									nextRecoveryReg[50-:32] = writeData[i][33-:32];
									nextRecoveryReg[3-:4] = writeData[i][56-:4];
									exceptionDetected = |{writeData[i][56-:4] == 4'b0011, writeData[i][56-:4] == 4'b0010};
									exceptionIndex = i;
									if (writeData[i][56-:4] == 4'b0011)
										refetchType = (writeData[i][1] ? 3'b011 : (writeData[i][0] ? 3'b010 : 3'b001));
									else
										refetchType = 3'b000;
								end
							if (exceptionDetected && !Core.recoveryManagerIF.unableToStartRecovery)
								nextRecoveryReg[3-:4] = 4'b0001;
						end
				end
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.recoveredPC_FromCommitStage = MemoryMapTypes_ToAddrFromPC(recoveryReg[69-:19]);
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.recoveredPC_FromRwStage = MemoryMapTypes_ToAddrFromPC(nextRecoveryReg[69-:19]);
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.faultingDataAddr = recoveryReg[50-:32];
				Core.activeListIF.loadQueueRecoveryTailPtr = recoveryReg[12-:4];
				Core.activeListIF.storeQueueRecoveryTailPtr = recoveryReg[8-:4];
				flushRangeHeadPtr = Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeHeadPtr;
				flushRangeTailPtr = Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeTailPtr;
				nextRecoveryEntryNum = (flushRangeTailPtr >= flushRangeHeadPtr ? flushRangeTailPtr - flushRangeHeadPtr : (SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM + flushRangeTailPtr) - flushRangeHeadPtr);
				Core.activeListIF.recoveryEntryNum = recoveryEntryNum;
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.exceptionDetectedInRwStage = exceptionDetected && !Core.recoveryManagerIF.unableToStartRecovery;
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.refetchTypeFromRwStage = refetchType;
				Core.activeListIF.exceptionOpPtr = nextRecoveryReg[18-:6];
			end
			localparam EXEC_STATE_WRITE_NUM = 7;
			reg [0:6] esWE;
			reg [41:0] esWA;
			reg [11:0] esRA;
			reg [0:6] esWV;
			wire [0:1] esRV;
			reg [7:0] headExecState;
			DistributedMultiPortRAM #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM),
				.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(1),
				.READ_NUM(BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH),
				.WRITE_NUM(EXEC_STATE_WRITE_NUM)
			) execState(
				.clk(Core.activeListIF.clk),
				.we(esWE),
				.wa(esWA),
				.wv(esWV),
				.ra(esRA),
				.rv(esRV)
			);
			always @(*) begin
				if (Core.activeListIF.rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_95
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < EXEC_STATE_WRITE_NUM; i = i + 1)
						begin
							esWE[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							esWA[(6 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = i;
							esWV[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						end
				end
				else begin
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_96
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							begin
								esWE[i] = Core.activeListIF.pushTail[i];
								esWA[(6 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = pushedTailPtr[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
								esWV[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							end
					end
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_97
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							begin
								esWE[i + BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH] = we[i];
								esWA[(6 - (i + BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH)) * SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = writeData[i][70-:6];
								esWV[i + BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH] = writeData[i][56-:4] != 4'b0000;
							end
					end
				end
				esRA = headPtrList;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_98
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						if (recoveryReg[4] && (esRA[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] == recoveryReg[18-:6]))
							headExecState[(1 - i) * 4+:4] = recoveryReg[3-:4];
						else if (esRV[i])
							headExecState[(1 - i) * 4+:4] = 4'b0001;
						else
							headExecState[(1 - i) * 4+:4] = 4'b0000;
				end
				Core.activeListIF.headExecState = headExecState;
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : rmt_wat
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM = 64;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM = BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;
			reg [11:0] phySrcRegA;
			reg [11:0] phySrcRegB;
			reg [11:0] phyPrevDstReg;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			reg [7:0] srcIssueQueuePtrRegA;
			reg [7:0] srcIssueQueuePtrRegB;
			localparam RenameLogicTypes_RMT_ENTRY_BIT_SIZE = BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH;
			localparam BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH = 2;
			reg [0:1] rmtWE;
			localparam BasicTypes_LREG_NUM = 32;
			localparam BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 5;
			reg [9:0] rmtWA;
			reg [19:0] rmtWV;
			reg [29:0] rmtRA;
			wire [59:0] rmtRV;
			DistributedMultiPortRAM #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(BasicTypes_LREG_NUM),
				.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(10),
				.READ_NUM(6),
				.WRITE_NUM(BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH)
			) regRMT(
				.clk(Core.renameLogicIF.clk),
				.we(rmtWE),
				.wa(rmtWA),
				.wv(rmtWV),
				.ra(rmtRA),
				.rv(rmtRV)
			);
			reg [4:0] rstWriteLogRegNum [0:1];
			reg [5:0] rstWritePhyRegNum [0:1];
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_99
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						if (!Core.renameLogicIF.rst) begin
							rmtWE[i] = Core.renameLogicIF.rmtWriteReg[i];
							rmtWA[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.renameLogicIF.rmtWriteReg_LogRegNum[(1 - i) * 5+:5];
							rmtWV[((1 - i) * 10) + 9-:6] = Core.renameLogicIF.rmtWriteReg_PhyRegNum[((1 - i) * 6) + 5-:6];
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_100
								reg signed [31:0] j;
								for (j = 0; j < i; j = j + 1)
									if (rmtWE[i] && (rmtWA[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] == rmtWA[(1 - j) * BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]))
										rmtWE[j] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							end
							rmtWV[((1 - i) * 10) + 3-:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.renameLogicIF.watWriteIssueQueuePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4];
						end
						else begin
							rmtWE[i] = (i == 0 ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
							rmtWA[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = rstWriteLogRegNum[i];
							rmtWV[((1 - i) * 10) + 9-:6] = rstWritePhyRegNum[i];
							rmtWV[((1 - i) * 10) + 3-:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_101
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							rmtRA[(5 - (3 * i)) * BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.renameLogicIF.logSrcRegA[i];
							rmtRA[(5 - ((3 * i) + 1)) * BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.renameLogicIF.logSrcRegB[i];
							rmtRA[(5 - ((3 * i) + 2)) * BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.renameLogicIF.logDstReg[i];
							phySrcRegA[((1 - i) * BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH) + 5-:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = rmtRV[((5 - (3 * i)) * 10) + 9-:6];
							phySrcRegB[((1 - i) * BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH) + 5-:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = rmtRV[((5 - ((3 * i) + 1)) * 10) + 9-:6];
							phyPrevDstReg[((1 - i) * BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH) + 5-:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = rmtRV[((5 - ((3 * i) + 2)) * 10) + 9-:6];
							srcIssueQueuePtrRegA[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = rmtRV[((5 - (3 * i)) * 10) + 3-:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
							srcIssueQueuePtrRegB[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = rmtRV[((5 - ((3 * i) + 1)) * 10) + 3-:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
							Core.renameLogicIF.prevDependIssueQueuePtr[i] = rmtRV[((5 - ((3 * i) + 2)) * 10) + 3-:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_102
								reg signed [31:0] j;
								for (j = 0; j < i; j = j + 1)
									if (Core.renameLogicIF.rmtWriteReg[j]) begin
										if (Core.renameLogicIF.logSrcRegA[i] == Core.renameLogicIF.logDstReg[j]) begin
											phySrcRegA[((1 - i) * BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH) + 5-:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.renameLogicIF.rmtWriteReg_PhyRegNum[((1 - j) * 6) + 5-:6];
											srcIssueQueuePtrRegA[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.renameLogicIF.watWriteIssueQueuePtr[(1 - j) * 4+:4];
										end
										if (Core.renameLogicIF.logSrcRegB[i] == Core.renameLogicIF.logDstReg[j]) begin
											phySrcRegB[((1 - i) * BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH) + 5-:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.renameLogicIF.rmtWriteReg_PhyRegNum[((1 - j) * 6) + 5-:6];
											srcIssueQueuePtrRegB[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.renameLogicIF.watWriteIssueQueuePtr[(1 - j) * 4+:4];
										end
										if (Core.renameLogicIF.logDstReg[i] == Core.renameLogicIF.logDstReg[j])
											phyPrevDstReg[((1 - i) * BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH) + 5-:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.renameLogicIF.rmtWriteReg_PhyRegNum[((1 - j) * 6) + 5-:6];
									end
							end
						end
				end
				Core.renameLogicIF.phySrcRegA = phySrcRegA;
				Core.renameLogicIF.phySrcRegB = phySrcRegB;
				Core.renameLogicIF.phyPrevDstReg = phyPrevDstReg;
				Core.renameLogicIF.srcIssueQueuePtrRegA = srcIssueQueuePtrRegA;
				Core.renameLogicIF.srcIssueQueuePtrRegB = srcIssueQueuePtrRegB;
			end
			always @(posedge Core.renameLogicIF.clk) begin : sv2v_autoblock_103
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
					if (Core.renameLogicIF.rstStart)
						rstWriteLogRegNum[i] <= 0;
					else
						rstWriteLogRegNum[i] <= rstWriteLogRegNum[i] + 1;
			end
			localparam BasicTypes_LSCALAR_NUM = 32;
			localparam RenameLogicTypes_SCALAR_FREE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM = 32;
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_104
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
					rstWritePhyRegNum[i] = rstWriteLogRegNum[i][4-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] + RenameLogicTypes_SCALAR_FREE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM;
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : retirementRMT
			localparam BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH = 2;
			reg [0:1] we;
			localparam BasicTypes_LREG_NUM = 32;
			localparam BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 5;
			reg [9:0] writeLogRegNum;
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM = 64;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM = BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam RenameLogicTypes_RMT_ENTRY_BIT_SIZE = BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH;
			reg [11:0] writePhyRegNum;
			reg [4:0] rstWriteLogRegNum [0:1];
			reg [5:0] rstWritePhyRegNum [0:1];
			localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;
			reg [9:0] readLogRegNum;
			wire [11:0] readPhyRegNum;
			localparam RenameLogicTypes_RMT_ENTRY_NUM = BasicTypes_LREG_NUM;
			DistributedMultiPortRAM #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(RenameLogicTypes_RMT_ENTRY_NUM),
				.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(RenameLogicTypes_RMT_ENTRY_BIT_SIZE),
				.READ_NUM(BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH),
				.WRITE_NUM(BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH)
			) regRMT(
				.clk(Core.renameLogicIF.clk),
				.we(we),
				.wa(writeLogRegNum),
				.wv(writePhyRegNum),
				.ra(readLogRegNum),
				.rv(readPhyRegNum)
			);
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_105
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						if (!Core.renameLogicIF.rst) begin
							writeLogRegNum[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.renameLogicIF.retRMT_WriteReg_LogRegNum[i];
							writePhyRegNum[(1 - i) * 6+:6] = Core.renameLogicIF.retRMT_WriteReg_PhyRegNum[i][5-:6];
							we[i] = Core.renameLogicIF.retRMT_WriteReg[i];
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_106
								reg signed [31:0] j;
								for (j = 0; j < i; j = j + 1)
									if (we[i] && (writeLogRegNum[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] == writeLogRegNum[(1 - j) * BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]))
										we[j] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							end
						end
						else begin
							writeLogRegNum[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = rstWriteLogRegNum[i];
							writePhyRegNum[(1 - i) * 6+:6] = rstWritePhyRegNum[i];
							we[i] = (i == 0 ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_107
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							readLogRegNum[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.renameLogicIF.retRMT_ReadReg_LogRegNum[(1 - i) * 5+:5];
							Core.renameLogicIF.retRMT_ReadReg_PhyRegNum[i][5-:6] = readPhyRegNum[(1 - i) * 6+:6];
						end
				end
			end
			always @(posedge Core.renameLogicIF.clk) begin : sv2v_autoblock_108
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
					if (Core.renameLogicIF.rstStart)
						rstWriteLogRegNum[i] <= 0;
					else
						rstWriteLogRegNum[i] <= rstWriteLogRegNum[i] + 1;
			end
			localparam BasicTypes_LSCALAR_NUM = 32;
			localparam RenameLogicTypes_SCALAR_FREE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM = 32;
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_109
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
					rstWritePhyRegNum[i] = rstWriteLogRegNum[i][4-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] + RenameLogicTypes_SCALAR_FREE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM;
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : memoryDependencyPredictor
			localparam BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			reg [0:0] mdtWE;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_MDT_ENTRY_NUM = 1024;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_MDT_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 10;
			reg [9:0] mdtWA;
			reg [0:0] mdtWV;
			localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;
			reg [19:0] mdtRA;
			wire [1:0] mdtRV;
			reg [0:1] prediction;
			BlockMultiBankRAM #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(SchedulerTypes_MDT_ENTRY_NUM),
				.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(1),
				.READ_NUM(BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH),
				.WRITE_NUM(BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH)
			) mdt(
				.clk(Core.rnStageIF.clk),
				.we(mdtWE),
				.wa(mdtWA),
				.wv(mdtWV),
				.ra(mdtRA),
				.rv(mdtRV)
			);
			reg [9:0] resetIndex;
			always @(posedge Core.rnStageIF.clk)
				if (Core.rnStageIF.rstStart)
					resetIndex <= 0;
				else
					resetIndex <= resetIndex + 1;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH = 8;
			localparam BasicTypes_INSN_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			localparam BasicTypes_INSN_ADDR_BIT_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			function automatic [9:0] SchedulerTypes_ToMDT_Index;
				input reg [18:0] addr;
				SchedulerTypes_ToMDT_Index = addr[11:BasicTypes_INSN_ADDR_BIT_WIDTH];
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_110
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						mdtRA[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_MDT_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_MDT_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = SchedulerTypes_ToMDT_Index(Core.rnStageIF.pc[0] + (i * BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH));
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_111
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						prediction[i] = mdtRV[1 - i];
				end
				Core.rnStageIF.memDependencyPred = prediction;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_112
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							mdtWE[i] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.memAccessOrderViolation[i];
							mdtWA[i * SchedulerTypes_MDT_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_MDT_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = SchedulerTypes_ToMDT_Index(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.conflictLoadPC[i * 19+:19]);
							mdtWV[i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						end
				end
				if (Core.rnStageIF.rst) begin
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_113
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							begin
								mdtWE[i] = i == 0;
								mdtWA[i * SchedulerTypes_MDT_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_MDT_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = resetIndex;
								mdtWV[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							end
					end
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_114
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							mdtRA[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_MDT_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_MDT_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = i;
					end
				end
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : dsStage
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_BRANCH_GLOBAL_HISTORY_BIT_WIDTH = 10;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			localparam OpFormatTypes_BR_DISP_WIDTH = 20;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_BR_SUB_INFO_BIT_WIDTH = 56;
			localparam BasicTypes_RISCV_SHIFTER_WIDTH = 30;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_INT_SUB_INFO_BIT_WIDTH = 39;
			localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM = 64;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM = BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_INDEX_BITS = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_LREG_NUM = 32;
			localparam BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 5;
			localparam OpFormatTypes_CSR_IMM_WIDTH = 5;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM = 64;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			reg [361:0] pipeReg;
			always @(posedge Core.ctrlIF.clk)
				if (Core.ctrlIF.rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_115
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						pipeReg[(1 - i) * 181+:181] <= 1'sb0;
				end
				else if (!Core.ctrlIF.dsStage[1])
					pipeReg <= Core.rnStageIF.nextStage;
				else
					pipeReg <= pipeReg;
			reg stall;
			reg clear;
			reg update [0:1];
			reg [70:0] opInfo [0:1];
			localparam SchedulerTypes_INT_SUB_INFO_PADDING_BIT_WIDTH = 17;
			reg [113:0] intEntry [0:1];
			reg [57:0] complexEntry [0:1];
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM = 2;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 1;
			reg [100:0] memEntry [0:1];
			reg [33:0] schedulerEntry [0:1];
			reg [11:0] opSrc [0:1];
			reg [6:0] opDst [0:1];
			reg [55:0] intSubInfo [0:1];
			reg [55:0] brSubInfo [0:1];
			reg [2:0] mulSubInfo [0:1];
			reg [2:0] divSubInfo [0:1];
			always @(*) begin
				stall = Core.ctrlIF.dsStage[1];
				clear = Core.ctrlIF.dsStage[0];
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_116
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							update[i] = (!stall && !clear) && pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 180];
							opInfo[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 179-:71];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_117
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							opSrc[i][11-:6] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 57-:6];
							opSrc[i][5-:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 51-:6];
							opDst[i][6] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 117];
							opDst[i][5-:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 45-:6];
							intEntry[i][57-:3] = opInfo[i][65-:3];
							intEntry[i][54-:3] = opInfo[i][70-:3];
							intEntry[i][37-:12] = opSrc[i];
							intEntry[i][25-:7] = opDst[i];
							intEntry[i][51-:6] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 21-:6];
							intEntry[i][45-:4] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 7-:4];
							intEntry[i][41-:4] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 3-:4];
							intEntry[i][18-:19] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 108-:19];
							intSubInfo[i][55-:2] = opInfo[i][12-:2];
							intSubInfo[i][53-:2] = opInfo[i][10-:2];
							intSubInfo[i][46-:30] = opInfo[i][42-:BasicTypes_RISCV_SHIFTER_WIDTH];
							intSubInfo[i][47] = opInfo[i][43];
							intSubInfo[i][51-:4] = opInfo[i][47-:4];
							intSubInfo[i][16-:SchedulerTypes_INT_SUB_INFO_PADDING_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
							brSubInfo[i][19-:OpFormatTypes_BR_DISP_WIDTH] = opInfo[i][32-:OpFormatTypes_BR_DISP_WIDTH];
							brSubInfo[i][51-:32] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 89-:32];
							brSubInfo[i][55-:2] = opInfo[i][12-:2];
							brSubInfo[i][53-:2] = opInfo[i][10-:2];
							if ((intEntry[i][57-:3] == 3'b010) || (intEntry[i][57-:3] == 3'b011))
								intEntry[i][113-:56] = brSubInfo[i];
							else
								intEntry[i][113-:56] = intSubInfo[i];
							complexEntry[i][54-:3] = opInfo[i][65-:3];
							complexEntry[i][37-:12] = opSrc[i];
							complexEntry[i][25-:7] = opDst[i];
							complexEntry[i][51-:6] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 21-:6];
							complexEntry[i][18-:19] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 108-:19];
							complexEntry[i][45-:4] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 7-:4];
							complexEntry[i][41-:4] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 3-:4];
							mulSubInfo[i][2] = opInfo[i][47];
							mulSubInfo[i][1-:2] = opInfo[i][46-:2];
							divSubInfo[i][2] = 1'sb0;
							divSubInfo[i][1-:2] = opInfo[i][44-:2];
							if (complexEntry[i][54-:3] == 3'b000)
								complexEntry[i][57-:3] = mulSubInfo[i];
							else
								complexEntry[i][57-:3] = divSubInfo[i];
							memEntry[i][100-:3] = opInfo[i][65-:3];
							memEntry[i][97-:3] = opInfo[i][70-:3];
							memEntry[i][94-:2] = opInfo[i][12-:2];
							memEntry[i][92-:2] = opInfo[i][10-:2];
							memEntry[i][90-:12] = opInfo[i][24-:12];
							memEntry[i][78] = opInfo[i][47];
							memEntry[i][77] = opInfo[i][46];
							memEntry[i][76-:3] = opInfo[i][45-:3];
							memEntry[i][73-:8] = opInfo[i][42-:8];
							memEntry[i][65-:3] = opInfo[i][47-:3];
							memEntry[i][62] = opInfo[i][46];
							memEntry[i][61-:4] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 15-:4];
							memEntry[i][57-:4] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 11-:4];
							memEntry[i][53] = 0;
							memEntry[i][52-:CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
							memEntry[i][37-:12] = opSrc[i];
							memEntry[i][25-:7] = opDst[i];
							memEntry[i][51-:6] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 21-:6];
							memEntry[i][45-:4] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 7-:4];
							memEntry[i][41-:4] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 3-:4];
							memEntry[i][18-:19] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 108-:19];
							schedulerEntry[i][33-:2] = opInfo[i][67-:2];
							schedulerEntry[i][31-:3] = opInfo[i][65-:3];
							schedulerEntry[i][28] = opInfo[i][12-:2] == 2'b00;
							schedulerEntry[i][27] = opInfo[i][10-:2] == 2'b00;
							schedulerEntry[i][26-:12] = opSrc[i];
							schedulerEntry[i][14-:7] = opDst[i];
							schedulerEntry[i][7-:4] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 33-:4];
							schedulerEntry[i][3-:4] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 29-:4];
							Core.schedulerIF.write[i] = update[i];
							Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 25-:4];
							Core.schedulerIF.writeAL_Ptr[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 21-:6];
							Core.schedulerIF.intWriteData[(1 - i) * 114+:114] = intEntry[i];
							Core.schedulerIF.complexWriteData[(1 - i) * 58+:58] = complexEntry[i];
							Core.schedulerIF.memWriteData[(1 - i) * 101+:101] = memEntry[i];
							Core.schedulerIF.writeSchedulerData[i] = schedulerEntry[i];
							Core.schedulerIF.allocated[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 181) + 180];
							Core.schedulerIF.memDependencyPred[i] = Core.rnStageIF.memDependencyPred[i];
						end
				end
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : scStage
			reg stall;
			reg clear;
			localparam BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_ISSUE_WIDTH = 5;
			reg flush [0:4];
			reg valid [0:4];
			reg update [0:4];
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			reg [4:0] nextStage [0:4];
			reg [3:0] issueQueuePtr [0:4];
			reg [15:0] flushIQ_Entry;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			always @(*) begin
				stall = Core.ctrlIF.scStage[1];
				clear = Core.ctrlIF.scStage[0];
				Core.schedulerIF.stall = stall;
				flushIQ_Entry = Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushIQ_Entry;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_118
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							valid[i] = Core.schedulerIF.selected[i];
							issueQueuePtr[i] = Core.schedulerIF.selectedPtr[i];
							flush[i] = flushIQ_Entry[issueQueuePtr[i]];
							update[i] = ((!stall && !clear) && valid[i]) && !flush[i];
							nextStage[i][4] = (((stall || clear) || Core.scStageIF.rst) || flush[i] ? BasicTypes_FALSE : valid[i]);
							nextStage[i][3-:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.schedulerIF.selectedPtr[i];
							if (i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH)
								Core.scStageIF.intNextStage[(1 - i) * 5+:5] = nextStage[i];
							else if (i < 3)
								Core.scStageIF.complexNextStage[(i - BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) * 5+:5] = nextStage[i];
							else
								Core.scStageIF.memNextStage[(1 - ((i - BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) - BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH)) * 5+:5] = nextStage[i];
						end
				end
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : issueQueue
			localparam BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_ISSUE_WIDTH = 5;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_RETURN_INDEX_WIDTH = 2;
			reg [0:6] releaseEntry;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			reg [27:0] releasePtr;
			wire [SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH:0] freeListCount;
			reg freeListReset;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_RESET_CYCLE = 3;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_RESET_CYCLE_BIT_SIZE = 2;
			reg [1:0] freeListResetCycleCount;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM = 64;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			reg [5:0] alPtrReg [0:15];
			reg [15:0] flush;
			reg [15:0] prevFlushAtRecovery;
			reg issueQueueReturnIndex;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_RETURN_INDEX_CYCLE = 8;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_RETURN_INDEX_CYCLE_BIT_SIZE = 3;
			reg [2:0] issueQueueReturnIndexCycleCount;
			reg [3:0] returnIndexOffset;
			localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;
			reg [7:0] writePtr;
			reg [19:0] selectedPtr;
			localparam BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;
			MultiWidthFreeList #(
				.SIZE(SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM),
				.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH),
				.PUSH_WIDTH(7),
				.POP_WIDTH(BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH),
				.INITIAL_LENGTH(SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM)
			) issueQueueFreeList(
				.clk(Core.schedulerIF.clk),
				.rst(Core.schedulerIF.rst || freeListReset),
				.rstStart(Core.schedulerIF.rstStart),
				.count(freeListCount),
				.pop(Core.schedulerIF.allocate),
				.poppedData(Core.schedulerIF.allocatedPtr),
				.push(releaseEntry),
				.pushedData(releasePtr)
			);
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			always @(*) begin
				Core.schedulerIF.allocatable = (!freeListReset && (freeListCount >= BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH) ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_119
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							releasePtr[(6 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.releasePtr[i];
							releaseEntry[i] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.releaseEntry[i];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_120
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_RETURN_INDEX_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						if (issueQueueReturnIndex && prevFlushAtRecovery[returnIndexOffset + i]) begin
							releaseEntry[BasicTypes_ISSUE_WIDTH + i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
							releasePtr[(6 - (BasicTypes_ISSUE_WIDTH + i)) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = returnIndexOffset + i;
						end
						else begin
							releaseEntry[BasicTypes_ISSUE_WIDTH + i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							releasePtr[(6 - (BasicTypes_ISSUE_WIDTH + i)) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = returnIndexOffset + i;
						end
				end
			end
			always @(posedge Core.schedulerIF.clk)
				if (Core.schedulerIF.rst) begin
					freeListReset <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
					freeListResetCycleCount <= 0;
				end
				else if (Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase && !Core.recoveryManagerIF.recoveryFromRwStage) begin
					freeListReset <= BasicTypes_TRUE;
					freeListResetCycleCount <= 0;
				end
				else if (freeListResetCycleCount == 2) begin
					freeListReset <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
					freeListResetCycleCount <= 0;
				end
				else begin
					freeListReset <= freeListReset;
					freeListResetCycleCount <= freeListResetCycleCount + 1;
				end
			reg [7:0] intIssuePtr;
			reg [7:0] memIssuePtr;
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM = 64;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM = BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_BRANCH_GLOBAL_HISTORY_BIT_WIDTH = 10;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam OpFormatTypes_BR_DISP_WIDTH = 20;
			localparam BasicTypes_RISCV_SHIFTER_WIDTH = 30;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_BR_SUB_INFO_BIT_WIDTH = 56;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_INT_SUB_INFO_BIT_WIDTH = 39;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_INT_SUB_INFO_PADDING_BIT_WIDTH = 17;
			wire [227:0] intIssuedData;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM = 2;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 1;
			localparam OpFormatTypes_CSR_IMM_WIDTH = 5;
			wire [201:0] memIssuedData;
			reg [3:0] complexIssuePtr;
			wire [57:0] complexIssuedData;
			DistributedMultiPortRAM #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM),
				.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(114),
				.READ_NUM(BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH),
				.WRITE_NUM(BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH)
			) intPayloadRAM(
				.clk(Core.schedulerIF.clk),
				.we(Core.schedulerIF.write),
				.wa(Core.schedulerIF.writePtr),
				.wv(Core.schedulerIF.intWriteData),
				.ra(intIssuePtr),
				.rv(intIssuedData)
			);
			DistributedMultiPortRAM #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM),
				.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(58),
				.READ_NUM(BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH),
				.WRITE_NUM(BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH)
			) complexPayloadRAM(
				.clk(Core.schedulerIF.clk),
				.we(Core.schedulerIF.write),
				.wa(Core.schedulerIF.writePtr),
				.wv(Core.schedulerIF.complexWriteData),
				.ra(complexIssuePtr),
				.rv(complexIssuedData)
			);
			DistributedMultiPortRAM #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM),
				.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(101),
				.READ_NUM(BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH),
				.WRITE_NUM(BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH)
			) memPayloadRAM(
				.clk(Core.schedulerIF.clk),
				.we(Core.schedulerIF.write),
				.wa(Core.schedulerIF.writePtr),
				.wv(Core.schedulerIF.memWriteData),
				.ra(memIssuePtr),
				.rv(memIssuedData)
			);
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_121
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							intIssuePtr[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.schedulerIF.intIssuePtr[i];
							Core.schedulerIF.intIssuedData[i] = intIssuedData[(1 - i) * 114+:114];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_122
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							complexIssuePtr[i * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.schedulerIF.complexIssuePtr[i];
							Core.schedulerIF.complexIssuedData[i] = complexIssuedData[i * 58+:58];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_123
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							memIssuePtr[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.schedulerIF.memIssuePtr[i];
							Core.schedulerIF.memIssuedData[i] = memIssuedData[(1 - i) * 101+:101];
						end
				end
			end
			always @(posedge Core.schedulerIF.clk) begin : sv2v_autoblock_124
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
					if (Core.schedulerIF.write[i])
						alPtrReg[writePtr[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]] <= Core.schedulerIF.writeAL_Ptr[i];
			end
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_125
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
					Core.recoveryManagerIF.selectedActiveListPtr[i] = alPtrReg[selectedPtr[(4 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]];
			end
			always @(*) begin
				writePtr = Core.schedulerIF.writePtr;
				selectedPtr = Core.recoveryManagerIF.selectedPtr;
			end
			function automatic PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector;
				input reg detectRange;
				input reg [5:0] headPtr;
				input reg [5:0] tailPtr;
				input reg [5:0] opPtr;
				if (!detectRange)
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr >= headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr < headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr > tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else if ((opPtr < headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_126
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)
						flush[i] = PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector(Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase && Core.recoveryManagerIF.notIssued[i], Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeHeadPtr, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeTailPtr, alPtrReg[i]);
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_127
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						if (Core.schedulerIF.allocated[i])
							flush[writePtr[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]] = PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector(Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeHeadPtr, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeTailPtr, Core.schedulerIF.writeAL_Ptr[i]);
				end
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushIQ_Entry = flush;
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.issueQueueReturnIndex = issueQueueReturnIndex || freeListReset;
			end
			always @(posedge Core.schedulerIF.clk)
				if (Core.schedulerIF.rst) begin
					prevFlushAtRecovery <= 0;
					issueQueueReturnIndex <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
					issueQueueReturnIndexCycleCount <= 0;
				end
				else if (Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase && Core.recoveryManagerIF.recoveryFromRwStage) begin
					prevFlushAtRecovery <= flush;
					issueQueueReturnIndex <= BasicTypes_TRUE;
					issueQueueReturnIndexCycleCount <= 0;
				end
				else if (issueQueueReturnIndexCycleCount == 7) begin
					issueQueueReturnIndex <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
					issueQueueReturnIndexCycleCount <= 0;
				end
				else begin
					issueQueueReturnIndex <= issueQueueReturnIndex;
					issueQueueReturnIndexCycleCount <= issueQueueReturnIndexCycleCount + 1;
				end
			always @(posedge Core.schedulerIF.clk)
				if (Core.schedulerIF.rst || (Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase && Core.recoveryManagerIF.recoveryFromRwStage))
					returnIndexOffset <= 0;
				else if (returnIndexOffset >= 14)
					returnIndexOffset <= 0;
				else
					returnIndexOffset <= returnIndexOffset + SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_RETURN_INDEX_WIDTH;
		end
		if (1) begin : replayQueue
			localparam REPLAY_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 20;
			localparam REPLAY_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 5;
			localparam BasicTypes_COMPLEX_EXEC_STAGE_DEPTH = 3;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_COMPLEX_LATENCY = 5;
			localparam REPLAY_QUEUE_MAX_INTERVAL = SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_COMPLEX_LATENCY;
			localparam REPLAY_QUEUE_MAX_INTERVAL_BIT_WIDTH = 3;
			localparam BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_SUBSET_BIT_WIDTH = 5;
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM = 64;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM = BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM = 64;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_BRANCH_GLOBAL_HISTORY_BIT_WIDTH = 10;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam OpFormatTypes_BR_DISP_WIDTH = 20;
			localparam BasicTypes_RISCV_SHIFTER_WIDTH = 30;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_BR_SUB_INFO_BIT_WIDTH = 56;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_INT_SUB_INFO_BIT_WIDTH = 39;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_INT_SUB_INFO_PADDING_BIT_WIDTH = 17;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM = 2;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 1;
			localparam OpFormatTypes_CSR_IMM_WIDTH = 5;
			reg [507:0] recordData;
			wire [507:0] replayEntryOut;
			wire [4:0] headPtr;
			wire [4:0] tailPtr;
			wire full;
			wire empty;
			reg pushEntry;
			reg popEntry;
			reg almostFull;
			wire [REPLAY_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH:0] count;
			QueuePointerWithEntryCount #(
				.SIZE(REPLAY_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM),
				.INITIAL_HEAD_PTR(0),
				.INITIAL_TAIL_PTR(0),
				.INITIAL_COUNT(0)
			) pointer(
				.clk(Core.schedulerIF.clk),
				.rst(Core.schedulerIF.rst),
				.push(pushEntry),
				.pop(popEntry),
				.full(full),
				.empty(empty),
				.headPtr(headPtr),
				.tailPtr(tailPtr),
				.count(count)
			);
			DistributedDualPortRAM #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(32),
				.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(508)
			) replayQueue(
				.clk(Core.schedulerIF.clk),
				.we(pushEntry),
				.wa(tailPtr),
				.wv(recordData),
				.ra(headPtr),
				.rv(replayEntryOut)
			);
			reg recoveryFromCmStage;
			reg recoveryFromRwStage;
			reg replayEntryValidIn;
			reg replayEntryValidOut;
			reg noValidInst;
			reg [REPLAY_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH:0] validInstCount;
			reg [REPLAY_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH:0] validInstCountNext;
			reg [2:0] intervalIn;
			reg [2:0] nextIntervalIn;
			reg [2:0] intervalCount;
			reg [2:0] nextIntervalCount;
			reg [REPLAY_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH:0] canBeFlushedEntryCount;
			reg [5:0] flushRangeHeadPtr;
			reg [5:0] flushRangeTailPtr;
			reg flushInt [0:1];
			reg flushMem [0:1];
			reg flushComplex [0:0];
			reg [507:0] replayEntryReg;
			reg [507:0] nextReplayEntry;
			reg replayReg;
			reg nextReplay;
			reg [1:0] mshrNotReady;
			reg [1:0] mshrAddrSubsetMatch;
			reg [1:0] targetMSHRValid;
			reg [0:0] mshrID [0:1];
			reg mshrValid [0:1];
			reg [4:0] mshrPhase [0:1];
			reg [4:0] mshrAddrSubset [0:1];
			reg mshrMakeMSHRCanBeInvalidByReplayQueue [0:1];
			always @(*) begin
				recoveryFromRwStage = Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase && Core.recoveryManagerIF.recoveryFromRwStage;
				recoveryFromCmStage = Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase && !Core.recoveryManagerIF.recoveryFromRwStage;
			end
			always @(posedge Core.schedulerIF.clk)
				if (Core.schedulerIF.rst) begin
					replayEntryReg[507-:2] <= 1'sb0;
					replayEntryReg[277] <= 1'sb0;
					replayEntryReg[218-:2] <= 1'sb0;
					replayReg <= 1'sb0;
					intervalIn <= 1'sb0;
					intervalCount <= 1'sb0;
				end
				else begin
					replayEntryReg <= nextReplayEntry;
					replayReg <= nextReplay;
					intervalIn <= nextIntervalIn;
					intervalCount <= nextIntervalCount;
				end
			always @(posedge Core.schedulerIF.clk)
				if (Core.schedulerIF.rst)
					validInstCount <= 0;
				else
					validInstCount <= validInstCountNext;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			always @(*) begin
				noValidInst = (validInstCount == 0 ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
				validInstCountNext = validInstCount;
				if (pushEntry && replayEntryValidIn)
					validInstCountNext = validInstCountNext + 1;
				if (popEntry && replayEntryValidOut)
					validInstCountNext = validInstCountNext - 1;
			end
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_128
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; i = i + 1)
						begin
							mshrValid[i] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.mshrValid[i];
							mshrPhase[i] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.mshrPhase[i];
							mshrAddrSubset[i] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.mshrAddrSubset[i];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_129
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						mshrID[i] = replayEntryOut[15 + ((i * 101) + 52)-:CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
				end
			end
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_130
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
					if (Core.schedulerIF.rst)
						mshrNotReady[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					else
						mshrNotReady[i] = (mshrPhase[mshrID[i]] < 5'd17 ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
			end
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_131
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
					if (Core.schedulerIF.rst)
						targetMSHRValid[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					else
						targetMSHRValid[i] = (mshrValid[mshrID[i]] ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
			end
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_132
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
					if (Core.schedulerIF.rst)
						mshrAddrSubsetMatch[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					else
						mshrAddrSubsetMatch[i] = (mshrAddrSubset[mshrID[i]] == replayEntryOut[3 + (i * CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_SUBSET_BIT_WIDTH)+:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_SUBSET_BIT_WIDTH] ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
			end
			always @(*) begin
				nextIntervalIn = intervalIn;
				if (pushEntry)
					nextIntervalIn = 1'sb0;
				else if (intervalIn < REPLAY_QUEUE_MAX_INTERVAL)
					nextIntervalIn = nextIntervalIn + 1;
			end
			always @(*) begin
				nextIntervalCount = intervalCount;
				if (popEntry)
					nextIntervalCount = 1'sb0;
				else if (intervalCount < REPLAY_QUEUE_MAX_INTERVAL)
					nextIntervalCount = nextIntervalCount + 1;
			end
			function automatic PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector;
				input reg detectRange;
				input reg [5:0] headPtr;
				input reg [5:0] tailPtr;
				input reg [5:0] opPtr;
				if (!detectRange)
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr >= headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr < headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr > tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else if ((opPtr < headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			endfunction
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_MEM_LATENCY = 3;
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_133
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							recordData[506 + i] = Core.schedulerIF.intRecordEntry[i];
							recordData[278 + (i * 114)+:114] = Core.schedulerIF.intRecordData[i];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_134
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							recordData[277 + i] = Core.schedulerIF.complexRecordEntry[i];
							recordData[219 + (i * 58)+:58] = Core.schedulerIF.complexRecordData[i];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_135
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							recordData[217 + i] = Core.schedulerIF.memRecordEntry[i];
							recordData[15 + (i * 101)+:101] = Core.schedulerIF.memRecordData[i];
							recordData[13 + i] = Core.schedulerIF.memRecordAddrHit[i];
							recordData[3 + (i * CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_SUBSET_BIT_WIDTH)+:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_SUBSET_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.schedulerIF.memRecordAddrSubset[i];
						end
				end
				recordData[2-:3] = intervalIn;
				if (Core.schedulerIF.rst) begin
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_136
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							recordData[506 + i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					end
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_137
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							recordData[277 + i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					end
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_138
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							recordData[217 + i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					end
					recordData[2-:3] = 1'sb0;
				end
				if (Core.schedulerIF.rst)
					replayEntryValidIn = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				else begin
					replayEntryValidIn = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_139
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							if (recordData[506 + i])
								replayEntryValidIn = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					end
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_140
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							if (recordData[277 + i])
								replayEntryValidIn = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					end
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_141
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							if (recordData[217 + i])
								replayEntryValidIn = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					end
				end
				if (Core.schedulerIF.rst)
					replayEntryValidOut = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				else begin
					replayEntryValidOut = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_142
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							if (replayEntryOut[506 + i])
								replayEntryValidOut = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					end
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_143
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							if (replayEntryOut[277 + i])
								replayEntryValidOut = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					end
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_144
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							if (replayEntryOut[217 + i])
								replayEntryValidOut = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					end
				end
				if (Core.schedulerIF.rst)
					pushEntry = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				else if (full)
					pushEntry = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				else if (~replayEntryValidIn && noValidInst)
					pushEntry = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				else begin
					pushEntry = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_145
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							if (recordData[506 + i])
								pushEntry = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					end
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_146
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							if (recordData[277 + i])
								pushEntry = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					end
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_147
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							if (recordData[217 + i])
								pushEntry = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					end
				end
				if (Core.schedulerIF.rst)
					popEntry = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				else if (empty)
					popEntry = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				else if (intervalCount < replayEntryOut[2-:3])
					popEntry = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				else if (~replayEntryValidOut)
					popEntry = BasicTypes_TRUE;
				else begin
					popEntry = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_148
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							if (((replayEntryOut[217 + i] && (replayEntryOut[15 + ((i * 101) + 100)-:3] == 3'b000)) && replayEntryOut[15 + ((i * 101) + 53)]) && mshrNotReady[i])
								popEntry = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					end
					if (((replayEntryOut[217] && (replayEntryOut[115-:3] == 3'b101)) && replayEntryOut[77]) && !Core.cacheFlushManagerIF.cacheFlushComplete)
						popEntry = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_149
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							if (((((replayEntryOut[217 + i] && (replayEntryOut[15 + ((i * 101) + 100)-:3] == 3'b000)) && replayEntryOut[13 + i]) && targetMSHRValid[i]) && mshrAddrSubsetMatch[i]) && mshrNotReady[i])
								popEntry = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					end
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_150
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							if ((replayEntryOut[277 + i] && (replayEntryOut[219 + ((i * 58) + 54)-:3] == 3'b001)) && Core.mulDivUnitIF.divBusy[i])
								popEntry = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					end
				end
				if (count >= 17)
					almostFull = BasicTypes_TRUE;
				else
					almostFull = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_151
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							nextReplayEntry[506 + i] = popEntry && replayEntryOut[506 + i];
							nextReplayEntry[278 + (i * 114)+:114] = replayEntryOut[278 + (i * 114)+:114];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_152
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							nextReplayEntry[277 + i] = popEntry && replayEntryOut[277 + i];
							nextReplayEntry[219 + (i * 58)+:58] = replayEntryOut[219 + (i * 58)+:58];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_153
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							nextReplayEntry[217 + i] = popEntry && replayEntryOut[217 + i];
							nextReplayEntry[15 + (i * 101)+:101] = replayEntryOut[15 + (i * 101)+:101];
							nextReplayEntry[13 + i] = replayEntryOut[13 + i];
							nextReplayEntry[3 + (i * CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_SUBSET_BIT_WIDTH)+:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_SUBSET_BIT_WIDTH] = replayEntryOut[3 + (i * CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_SUBSET_BIT_WIDTH)+:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_SUBSET_BIT_WIDTH];
						end
				end
				nextReplayEntry[2-:3] = replayEntryOut[2-:3];
				nextReplay = (popEntry && replayEntryValidOut ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_154
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							flushInt[i] = PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector(canBeFlushedEntryCount != 0, flushRangeHeadPtr, flushRangeTailPtr, replayEntryReg[278 + ((i * 114) + 51)-:6]);
							Core.schedulerIF.intReplayEntry[i] = replayEntryReg[506 + i] && !flushInt[i];
							Core.schedulerIF.intReplayData[i] = replayEntryReg[278 + (i * 114)+:114];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_155
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							flushComplex[i] = PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector(canBeFlushedEntryCount != 0, flushRangeHeadPtr, flushRangeTailPtr, replayEntryReg[219 + ((i * 58) + 51)-:6]);
							Core.schedulerIF.complexReplayEntry[i] = replayEntryReg[277 + i] && !flushComplex[i];
							Core.schedulerIF.complexReplayData[i] = replayEntryReg[219 + (i * 58)+:58];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_156
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							flushMem[i] = PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector(canBeFlushedEntryCount != 0, flushRangeHeadPtr, flushRangeTailPtr, replayEntryReg[15 + ((i * 101) + 51)-:6]);
							Core.schedulerIF.memReplayEntry[i] = replayEntryReg[217 + i] && !flushMem[i];
							Core.schedulerIF.memReplayData[i] = replayEntryReg[15 + (i * 101)+:101];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_157
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; i = i + 1)
						mshrMakeMSHRCanBeInvalidByReplayQueue[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_158
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						if ((replayEntryReg[217 + i] && replayEntryReg[15 + ((i * 101) + 53)]) && flushMem[i])
							mshrMakeMSHRCanBeInvalidByReplayQueue[replayEntryReg[15 + ((i * 101) + 52)-:CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_159
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; i = i + 1)
						Core.loadStoreUnitIF.makeMSHRCanBeInvalidByReplayQueue[i] = mshrMakeMSHRCanBeInvalidByReplayQueue[i];
				end
				Core.ctrlIF.isStageStallUpper = replayReg | almostFull;
				Core.schedulerIF.replay = replayReg;
			end
			always @(posedge Core.schedulerIF.clk)
				if (Core.schedulerIF.rst)
					canBeFlushedEntryCount <= 0;
				else if (recoveryFromRwStage || recoveryFromCmStage) begin
					canBeFlushedEntryCount <= count;
					flushRangeHeadPtr <= Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeHeadPtr;
					flushRangeTailPtr <= Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeTailPtr;
				end
				else if ((canBeFlushedEntryCount > 0) && Core.schedulerIF.replay)
					canBeFlushedEntryCount <= canBeFlushedEntryCount - 1;
			always @(*) Core.recoveryManagerIF.replayQueueFlushedOpExist = canBeFlushedEntryCount != 0;
		end
		if (1) begin : scheduler
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			reg [15:0] notIssued;
			reg [15:0] isInt;
			reg [15:0] isComplex;
			reg [15:0] isDiv;
			reg [15:0] isLoad;
			reg [15:0] isStore;
			reg [15:0] flushIQ_Entry;
			reg [15:0] selectedVector;
			localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;
			reg [0:1] dispatchStore;
			reg [0:1] dispatchLoad;
			reg canIssueDiv;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			always @(posedge Core.schedulerIF.clk)
				if (Core.schedulerIF.rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_160
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)
						notIssued[i] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else begin
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_161
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							if (Core.schedulerIF.write[i]) begin
								notIssued[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_TRUE;
								if (Core.schedulerIF.writeSchedulerData[i][33-:2] == 2'b10) begin
									if (Core.schedulerIF.writeSchedulerData[i][31-:3] == 3'b001) begin
										isLoad[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
										isStore[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_TRUE;
										isComplex[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
										isInt[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
										isDiv[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
									end
									else begin
										isDiv[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
										isComplex[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
										isLoad[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_TRUE;
										isStore[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
										isInt[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
									end
								end
								else if (Core.schedulerIF.writeSchedulerData[i][33-:2] == 2'b01) begin
									if (Core.schedulerIF.writeSchedulerData[i][31-:3] == 3'b001) begin
										isLoad[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
										isStore[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
										isComplex[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_TRUE;
										isInt[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
										isDiv[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_TRUE;
									end
									else begin
										isLoad[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
										isStore[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
										isComplex[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_TRUE;
										isInt[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
										isDiv[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
									end
								end
								else begin
									isLoad[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
									isStore[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
									isComplex[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
									isInt[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_TRUE;
									isDiv[Core.schedulerIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
								end
							end
					end
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_162
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)
							if (selectedVector[i] || flushIQ_Entry[i])
								notIssued[i] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
					end
				end
			localparam BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_ISSUE_WIDTH = 5;
			localparam BasicTypes_MULDIV_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_163
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						if (Core.schedulerIF.write[i]) begin
							if (Core.schedulerIF.writeSchedulerData[i][33-:2] == 2'b10) begin
								if (Core.schedulerIF.writeSchedulerData[i][31-:3] == 3'b001) begin
									dispatchStore[i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
									dispatchLoad[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
								end
								else begin
									dispatchStore[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
									dispatchLoad[i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
								end
							end
							else begin
								dispatchStore[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
								dispatchLoad[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							end
						end
						else begin
							dispatchStore[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							dispatchLoad[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						end
				end
				Core.wakeupSelectIF.dispatchStore = dispatchStore;
				Core.wakeupSelectIF.dispatchLoad = dispatchLoad;
				Core.wakeupSelectIF.notIssued = notIssued;
				Core.wakeupSelectIF.stall = Core.schedulerIF.stall;
				Core.wakeupSelectIF.write = Core.schedulerIF.write;
				Core.wakeupSelectIF.writePtr = Core.schedulerIF.writePtr;
				Core.wakeupSelectIF.memDependencyPred = Core.schedulerIF.memDependencyPred;
				flushIQ_Entry = Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushIQ_Entry;
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.notIssued = notIssued;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_164
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.writeSrcTag[i][10] = Core.schedulerIF.writeSchedulerData[i][28];
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.writeSrcTag[i][17] = Core.schedulerIF.writeSchedulerData[i][27];
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.writeSrcTag[i][16-:6] = Core.schedulerIF.writeSchedulerData[i][26-:6];
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.writeSrcTag[i][23-:6] = Core.schedulerIF.writeSchedulerData[i][20-:6];
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.writeDstTag[i][0] = Core.schedulerIF.writeSchedulerData[i][14];
