							Core.wakeupSelectIF.writeDstTag[i][6-:6] = Core.schedulerIF.writeSchedulerData[i][13-:6];
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.writeSrcTag[i][0] = Core.schedulerIF.writeSchedulerData[i][28];
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.writeSrcTag[i][5] = Core.schedulerIF.writeSchedulerData[i][27];
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.writeSrcTag[i][4-:4] = Core.schedulerIF.writeSchedulerData[i][7-:4];
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.writeSrcTag[i][9-:4] = Core.schedulerIF.writeSchedulerData[i][3-:4];
						end
				end
				canIssueDiv = BasicTypes_TRUE;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_165
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MULDIV_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						if (!Core.mulDivUnitIF.divFree[i])
							canIssueDiv = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_166
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)
						begin
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.intIssueReq[i] = notIssued[i] && isInt[i];
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.loadIssueReq[i] = notIssued[i] && isLoad[i];
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.complexIssueReq[i] = (notIssued[i] && isComplex[i]) && (!isDiv[i] || (isDiv[i] && canIssueDiv));
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.storeIssueReq[i] = notIssued[i] && isStore[i];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_167
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							Core.schedulerIF.selected[i] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.selected[i] && !Core.schedulerIF.stall;
							Core.schedulerIF.selectedPtr[i] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.selectedPtr[(4 - i) * 4+:4];
						end
				end
				selectedVector = 1'sb0;
				if (!Core.schedulerIF.stall) begin : sv2v_autoblock_168
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						selectedVector = selectedVector | Core.wakeupSelectIF.selectedVector[(4 - i) * 16+:16];
				end
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : wakeupPipelineRegister
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM = 64;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_INT_LATENCY = 1;
			reg [26:0] intPipeReg [0:1][0:0];
			localparam BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_MEM_LATENCY = 3;
			reg [26:0] memPipeReg [0:1][0:2];
			reg [26:0] nextIntPipeReg [0:1];
			reg [26:0] nextMemPipeReg [0:1];
			localparam BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			localparam BasicTypes_COMPLEX_EXEC_STAGE_DEPTH = 3;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_COMPLEX_LATENCY = 5;
			reg [26:0] complexPipeReg [0:0][0:4];
			reg [26:0] nextComplexPipeReg [0:0];
			reg [3:0] canBeFlushedRegCountComplex;
			reg flushComplex [0:0];
			reg [3:0] complexSelectedPtr [0:0];
			reg [0:0] canBeFlushedRegCountInt;
			reg [2:0] canBeFlushedRegCountMem;
			reg [5:0] flushRangeHeadPtr;
			reg [5:0] flushRangeTailPtr;
			reg flushInt [0:1];
			localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			reg flushMem [0:0];
			reg [3:0] intSelectedPtr [0:1];
			reg [3:0] memSelectedPtr [0:1];
			reg [15:0] flushIQ_Entry;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			always @(posedge Core.wakeupSelectIF.clk)
				if (Core.wakeupSelectIF.rst || (Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase && !Core.recoveryManagerIF.recoveryFromRwStage)) begin
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_169
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_170
								reg signed [31:0] j;
								for (j = 0; j < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_INT_LATENCY; j = j + 1)
									intPipeReg[i][j][26] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
							end
					end
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_171
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_172
								reg signed [31:0] j;
								for (j = 0; j < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_COMPLEX_LATENCY; j = j + 1)
									complexPipeReg[i][j][26] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
							end
					end
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_173
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_174
								reg signed [31:0] j;
								for (j = 0; j < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_MEM_LATENCY; j = j + 1)
									memPipeReg[i][j][26] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
							end
					end
				end
				else if (!Core.wakeupSelectIF.stall) begin
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_175
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							begin
								begin : sv2v_autoblock_176
									reg signed [31:0] j;
									for (j = 1; j < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_INT_LATENCY; j = j + 1)
										intPipeReg[i][j - 1] <= intPipeReg[i][j];
								end
								intPipeReg[i][0] <= nextIntPipeReg[i];
							end
					end
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_177
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							begin
								begin : sv2v_autoblock_178
									reg signed [31:0] j;
									for (j = 1; j < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_COMPLEX_LATENCY; j = j + 1)
										complexPipeReg[i][j - 1] <= complexPipeReg[i][j];
								end
								complexPipeReg[i][4] <= nextComplexPipeReg[i];
							end
					end
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_179
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							begin
								begin : sv2v_autoblock_180
									reg signed [31:0] j;
									for (j = 1; j < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_MEM_LATENCY; j = j + 1)
										memPipeReg[i][j - 1] <= memPipeReg[i][j];
								end
								memPipeReg[i][2] <= nextMemPipeReg[i];
							end
					end
				end
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			function automatic PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector;
				input reg detectRange;
				input reg [5:0] headPtr;
				input reg [5:0] tailPtr;
				input reg [5:0] opPtr;
				if (!detectRange)
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr >= headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr < headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr > tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else if ((opPtr < headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			endfunction
			localparam SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH = 4;
			always @(*) begin
				flushIQ_Entry = Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushIQ_Entry;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_181
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							intSelectedPtr[i] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.selectedPtr[(4 - i) * 4+:4];
							nextIntPipeReg[i][26] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.selected[i] && !flushIQ_Entry[intSelectedPtr[i]];
							nextIntPipeReg[i][25-:4] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.selectedPtr[(4 - i) * 4+:4];
							nextIntPipeReg[i][21-:16] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.selectedVector[(4 - i) * 16+:16];
							nextIntPipeReg[i][5-:SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.recoveryManagerIF.selectedActiveListPtr[i];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_182
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							complexSelectedPtr[i] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.selectedPtr[(4 - (i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH)) * 4+:4];
							nextComplexPipeReg[i][26] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.selected[i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH] && !flushIQ_Entry[complexSelectedPtr[i]];
							nextComplexPipeReg[i][25-:4] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.selectedPtr[(4 - (i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH)) * 4+:4];
							nextComplexPipeReg[i][21-:16] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.selectedVector[(4 - (i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH)) * 16+:16];
							nextComplexPipeReg[i][5-:SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.recoveryManagerIF.selectedActiveListPtr[i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_183
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							memSelectedPtr[i] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.selectedPtr[(4 - ((i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH)) * 4+:4];
							nextMemPipeReg[i][26] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.selected[(i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH] && !flushIQ_Entry[memSelectedPtr[i]];
							nextMemPipeReg[i][25-:4] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.selectedPtr[(4 - ((i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH)) * 4+:4];
							nextMemPipeReg[i][21-:16] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.selectedVector[(4 - ((i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH)) * 16+:16];
							nextMemPipeReg[i][5-:SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.recoveryManagerIF.selectedActiveListPtr[(i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_184
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							flushInt[i] = PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector(canBeFlushedRegCountInt != 0, flushRangeHeadPtr, flushRangeTailPtr, intPipeReg[i][0][5-:SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]);
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.wakeup[i] = intPipeReg[i][0][26] && !flushInt[i];
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.wakeupPtr[i] = intPipeReg[i][0][25-:4];
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.wakeupVector[i] = intPipeReg[i][0][21-:16];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_185
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							flushComplex[i] = PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector(canBeFlushedRegCountComplex != 0, flushRangeHeadPtr, flushRangeTailPtr, complexPipeReg[i][0][5-:SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]);
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.wakeup[i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH] = complexPipeReg[i][0][26] && !flushComplex[i];
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.wakeupPtr[i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH] = complexPipeReg[i][0][25-:4];
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.wakeupVector[i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH] = complexPipeReg[i][0][21-:16];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_186
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							flushMem[i] = PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector(canBeFlushedRegCountMem != 0, flushRangeHeadPtr, flushRangeTailPtr, memPipeReg[i][0][5-:SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]);
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.wakeup[(i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH] = memPipeReg[i][0][26] && !flushMem[i];
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.wakeupPtr[(i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH] = memPipeReg[i][0][25-:4];
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.wakeupVector[(i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH] = memPipeReg[i][0][21-:16];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_187
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < 1; i = i + 1)
						begin
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.wakeupPtr[i + SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH] = memPipeReg[i + BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH][0][25-:4];
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.wakeupVector[i + SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH] = memPipeReg[i + BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH][0][21-:16];
						end
				end
			end
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_188
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.releaseEntry[i] = intPipeReg[i][0][26] && !Core.wakeupSelectIF.stall;
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.releasePtr[i] = intPipeReg[i][0][25-:4];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_189
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.releaseEntry[i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH] = complexPipeReg[i][0][26] && !Core.wakeupSelectIF.stall;
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.releasePtr[i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH] = complexPipeReg[i][0][25-:4];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_190
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.releaseEntry[(i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH] = memPipeReg[i][0][26] && !Core.wakeupSelectIF.stall;
							Core.wakeupSelectIF.releasePtr[(i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH] = memPipeReg[i][0][25-:4];
						end
				end
			end
			always @(posedge Core.wakeupSelectIF.clk)
				if (Core.wakeupSelectIF.rst) begin
					canBeFlushedRegCountInt <= 0;
					canBeFlushedRegCountComplex <= 0;
					canBeFlushedRegCountMem <= 0;
					flushRangeHeadPtr <= 0;
					flushRangeTailPtr <= 0;
				end
				else if (Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase && Core.recoveryManagerIF.recoveryFromRwStage) begin
					canBeFlushedRegCountInt <= SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_INT_LATENCY;
					canBeFlushedRegCountComplex <= SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_COMPLEX_LATENCY;
					canBeFlushedRegCountMem <= SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_MEM_LATENCY;
					flushRangeHeadPtr <= Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeHeadPtr;
					flushRangeTailPtr <= Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeTailPtr;
				end
				else begin
					if ((canBeFlushedRegCountInt > 0) && !Core.wakeupSelectIF.stall)
						canBeFlushedRegCountInt <= canBeFlushedRegCountInt - 1;
					if ((canBeFlushedRegCountComplex > 0) && !Core.wakeupSelectIF.stall)
						canBeFlushedRegCountComplex <= canBeFlushedRegCountComplex - 1;
					if ((canBeFlushedRegCountMem > 0) && !Core.wakeupSelectIF.stall)
						canBeFlushedRegCountMem <= canBeFlushedRegCountMem - 1;
				end
			always @(*) Core.recoveryManagerIF.wakeupPipelineRegFlushedOpExist = ((canBeFlushedRegCountInt != 0) || (canBeFlushedRegCountComplex != 0)) || (canBeFlushedRegCountMem != 0);
		end
		if (1) begin : destinationRAM
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM = 64;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM = BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;
			reg [0:1] write;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			reg [7:0] writePtr;
			reg [13:0] writeData;
			localparam BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH = 4;
			reg [15:0] readPtr;
			wire [27:0] readData;
			DistributedMultiPortRAM #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM),
				.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(7),
				.READ_NUM(SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH),
				.WRITE_NUM(BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH)
			) dstRAM(
				.clk(Core.wakeupSelectIF.clk),
				.we(write),
				.wa(writePtr),
				.wv(writeData),
				.ra(readPtr),
				.rv(readData)
			);
			reg [3:0] rstIndex;
			always @(posedge Core.wakeupSelectIF.clk)
				if (Core.wakeupSelectIF.rstStart || (rstIndex >= 15))
					rstIndex <= 0;
				else
					rstIndex <= rstIndex + 1;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_191
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							write[i] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.write[i];
							writePtr[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4];
							writeData[((1 - i) * 7) + 6-:7] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.writeDstTag[i][6-:7];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_192
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						Core.wakeupSelectIF.wakeupDstTag[i][6-:7] = readData[((3 - i) * 7) + 6-:7];
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_193
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						readPtr[(3 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.wakeupPtr[i];
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_194
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						readPtr[(3 - (i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH)) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.wakeupPtr[i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH];
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_195
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						readPtr[(3 - ((i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH)) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.wakeupPtr[(i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH];
				end
				if (Core.wakeupSelectIF.rst) begin
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_196
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							write[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					end
					write[0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					writePtr[SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = rstIndex;
					writeData[7+:7] = 1'sb0;
				end
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : wakeupLogic
			localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;
			localparam MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_SOURCE_REG_NUM = 2;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_SRC_REG_NUM = MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_SOURCE_REG_NUM;
			reg [3:0] dispatchedSrcRegValid;
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM = 64;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM = BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			reg [23:0] dispatchedSrcRegNum;
			reg [0:1] dispatchedDstRegValid;
			reg [11:0] dispatchedDstRegNum;
			localparam BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH = 4;
			reg [0:3] wakeupDstRegValid;
			reg [23:0] wakeupDstRegNum;
			wire [3:0] dispatchedSrcRegReady;
			localparam BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			reg [79:0] wakeupDstVector;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			reg [15:0] dispatchedSrcRegPtr;
			wire [0:15] opMatrixReady;
			reg [31:0] dependStoreBitVector;
			reg [15:0] storeBitVector;
			reg [15:0] storeBitVectorReg;
			reg [0:1] dispatchStore;
			reg [0:1] dispatchLoad;
			reg [15:0] notIssued;
			reg [0:1] memDependencyPred;
			ReadyBitTable #(
				.SRC_OP_NUM(SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_SRC_REG_NUM),
				.REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH(BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH),
				.ENTRY_NUM(BasicTypes_PREG_NUM)
			) regReadyBitTbl(
				.clk(Core.wakeupSelectIF.clk),
				.rst(Core.wakeupSelectIF.rst),
				.rstStart(Core.wakeupSelectIF.rstStart),
				.stall(Core.wakeupSelectIF.stall),
				.wakeup(Core.wakeupSelectIF.wakeup),
				.wakeupDstValid(wakeupDstRegValid),
				.wakeupDstRegNum(wakeupDstRegNum),
				.dispatch(Core.wakeupSelectIF.write),
				.dispatchedDstValid(dispatchedDstRegValid),
				.dispatchedDstRegNum(dispatchedDstRegNum),
				.dispatchedSrcValid(dispatchedSrcRegValid),
				.dispatchedSrcRegNum(dispatchedSrcRegNum),
				.dispatchedSrcReady(dispatchedSrcRegReady)
			);
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_197
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							dispatchedDstRegValid[i] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.writeDstTag[i][0];
							dispatchedDstRegNum[(1 - i) * BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.writeDstTag[i][6-:6];
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_198
								reg signed [31:0] j;
								for (j = 0; j < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_SRC_REG_NUM; j = j + 1)
									begin
										dispatchedSrcRegNum[(((1 - i) * 2) + (1 - j)) * 6+:6] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.writeSrcTag[i][10 + ((j * 7) + 6)-:6];
										dispatchedSrcRegValid[((1 - i) * 2) + (1 - j)] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.writeSrcTag[i][10 + (j * 7)];
									end
							end
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_199
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							wakeupDstRegValid[i] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.wakeupDstTag[i][0];
							wakeupDstRegNum[(3 - i) * BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.wakeupDstTag[i][6-:6];
						end
				end
			end
			ProducerMatrix producerMatrix(
				.clk(Core.wakeupSelectIF.clk),
				.stall(Core.wakeupSelectIF.stall),
				.dispatch(Core.wakeupSelectIF.write),
				.dispatchedSrcRegReady(dispatchedSrcRegReady),
				.dispatchedSrcRegPtr(dispatchedSrcRegPtr),
				.dependStoreBitVector(dependStoreBitVector),
				.dispatchPtr(Core.wakeupSelectIF.writePtr),
				.wakeupDstVector(wakeupDstVector),
				.opReady(opMatrixReady)
			);
			always @(posedge Core.wakeupSelectIF.clk)
				if (Core.wakeupSelectIF.rst)
					storeBitVectorReg <= 0;
				else
					storeBitVectorReg <= storeBitVector;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			always @(*) begin
				dispatchStore = Core.wakeupSelectIF.dispatchStore;
				dispatchLoad = Core.wakeupSelectIF.dispatchLoad;
				notIssued = Core.wakeupSelectIF.notIssued;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_200
					reg signed [31:0] w;
					for (w = 0; w < 5; w = w + 1)
						wakeupDstVector[(4 - w) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.wakeupVector[w];
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_201
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin : sv2v_autoblock_202
							reg signed [31:0] j;
							for (j = 0; j < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_SRC_REG_NUM; j = j + 1)
								dispatchedSrcRegPtr[((i * 2) + j) * 4+:4] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.writeSrcTag[i][0 + ((j * 5) + 4)-:4];
						end
				end
				storeBitVector = storeBitVectorReg & notIssued;
				memDependencyPred = Core.wakeupSelectIF.memDependencyPred;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_203
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						if (dispatchStore[i]) begin
							dependStoreBitVector[(1 - i) * 16+:16] = 0;
							storeBitVector[Core.wakeupSelectIF.writePtr[(1 - i) * 4+:4]] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						end
						else if (dispatchLoad[i] && memDependencyPred[i])
							dependStoreBitVector[(1 - i) * 16+:16] = storeBitVector;
						else
							dependStoreBitVector[(1 - i) * 16+:16] = 0;
				end
			end
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_204
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)
					Core.wakeupSelectIF.opReady[i] = opMatrixReady[i];
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : selectLogic
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			reg [15:0] intRequest;
			wire [15:0] intGrant;
			localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			wire [0:1] intSelected;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			wire [7:0] intSelectedPtr;
			reg [15:0] compRequest;
			wire [15:0] compGrant;
			localparam BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			wire [0:0] compSelected;
			wire [3:0] compSelectedPtr;
			reg [15:0] loadRequest;
			reg [15:0] storeRequest;
			wire [15:0] loadGrant;
			wire [15:0] storeGrant;
			localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			wire [0:0] loadSelected;
			localparam BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			wire [0:0] storeSelected;
			wire [3:0] loadSelectedPtr;
			wire [3:0] storeSelectedPtr;
			localparam BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_ISSUE_WIDTH = 5;
			reg [0:4] portSelected;
			reg [19:0] portSelectedPtr;
			reg [79:0] portSelectedVector;
			reg [0:4] recoverySelected;
			reg [19:0] recoverySelectedPtr;
			InterleavedPicker #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM),
				.GRANT_NUM(BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH)
			) intPicker(
				.req(intRequest),
				.grant(intGrant),
				.grantPtr(intSelectedPtr),
				.granted(intSelected)
			);
			Picker #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM),
				.GRANT_NUM(BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH)
			) compPicker(
				.req(compRequest),
				.grant(compGrant),
				.grantPtr(compSelectedPtr),
				.granted(compSelected)
			);
			Picker #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM),
				.GRANT_NUM(BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH)
			) loadPicker(
				.req(loadRequest),
				.grant(loadGrant),
				.grantPtr(loadSelectedPtr),
				.granted(loadSelected)
			);
			Picker #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM),
				.GRANT_NUM(BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH)
			) storePicker(
				.req(storeRequest),
				.grant(storeGrant),
				.grantPtr(storeSelectedPtr),
				.granted(storeSelected)
			);
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_205
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)
						begin
							intRequest[i] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.opReady[i] && Core.wakeupSelectIF.intIssueReq[i];
							loadRequest[i] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.opReady[i] && Core.wakeupSelectIF.loadIssueReq[i];
							storeRequest[i] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.opReady[i] && Core.wakeupSelectIF.storeIssueReq[i];
							compRequest[i] = Core.wakeupSelectIF.opReady[i] && Core.wakeupSelectIF.complexIssueReq[i];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_206
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							portSelected[i] = intSelected[i];
							portSelectedPtr[(4 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = intSelectedPtr[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
							portSelectedVector[(4 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM] = intGrant;
							recoverySelected[i] = intSelected[i];
							recoverySelectedPtr[(4 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = intSelectedPtr[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_207
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							portSelected[i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH] = compSelected[i];
							portSelectedPtr[(4 - (i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH)) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = compSelectedPtr[i * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
							portSelectedVector[(4 - (i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH)) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM] = compGrant;
							recoverySelected[i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH] = compSelected[i];
							recoverySelectedPtr[(4 - (i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH)) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = compSelectedPtr[i * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_208
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							portSelected[(i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH] = loadSelected[i];
							portSelectedPtr[(4 - ((i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH)) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = loadSelectedPtr[i * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
							portSelectedVector[(4 - ((i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH)) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM] = loadGrant;
							recoverySelected[(i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH] = loadSelected[i];
							recoverySelectedPtr[(4 - ((i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH)) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = loadSelectedPtr[i * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_209
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							portSelected[((i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH] = storeSelected[i];
							portSelectedPtr[(4 - (((i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH)) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = storeSelectedPtr[i * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
							portSelectedVector[(4 - (((i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH)) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM] = storeGrant;
							recoverySelected[((i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH] = storeSelected[i];
							recoverySelectedPtr[(4 - (((i + BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH) + BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH)) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = storeSelectedPtr[i * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
						end
				end
				Core.wakeupSelectIF.selected = portSelected;
				Core.wakeupSelectIF.selectedPtr = portSelectedPtr;
				Core.wakeupSelectIF.selectedVector = portSelectedVector;
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.selected = recoverySelected;
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.selectedPtr = recoverySelectedPtr;
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : intIsStage
			localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			reg [9:0] pipeReg;
			reg [9:0] nextPipeReg;
			always @(posedge Core.intIsStageIF.clk)
				if (Core.intIsStageIF.rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_210
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						pipeReg[(1 - i) * 5+:5] <= 1'sb0;
				end
				else if (!Core.ctrlIF.isStage[1])
					pipeReg <= Core.scStageIF.intNextStage;
				else
					pipeReg <= nextPipeReg;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM = 64;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			function automatic PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector;
				input reg detectRange;
				input reg [5:0] headPtr;
				input reg [5:0] tailPtr;
				input reg [5:0] opPtr;
				if (!detectRange)
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr >= headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr < headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr > tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else if ((opPtr < headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_211
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
					begin
						if (Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase)
							nextPipeReg[((1 - i) * 5) + 4] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 5) + 4] && !PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector(Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeHeadPtr, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeTailPtr, Core.schedulerIF.intIssuedData[i][51-:6]);
						else
							nextPipeReg[((1 - i) * 5) + 4] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 5) + 4];
						nextPipeReg[((1 - i) * 5) + 3-:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 5) + 3-:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
					end
			end
			reg stall;
			reg clear;
			reg flush [0:1];
			reg valid [0:1];
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM = 64;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM = BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_BRANCH_GLOBAL_HISTORY_BIT_WIDTH = 10;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam OpFormatTypes_BR_DISP_WIDTH = 20;
			localparam BasicTypes_RISCV_SHIFTER_WIDTH = 30;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_BR_SUB_INFO_BIT_WIDTH = 56;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_INT_SUB_INFO_BIT_WIDTH = 39;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_INT_SUB_INFO_PADDING_BIT_WIDTH = 17;
			reg [229:0] nextStage;
			reg [113:0] issuedData [0:1];
			reg [3:0] issueQueuePtr [0:1];
			always @(*) begin
				stall = Core.ctrlIF.isStage[1];
				clear = Core.ctrlIF.isStage[0];
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_212
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							if (Core.schedulerIF.replay) begin
								issuedData[i] = Core.schedulerIF.intReplayData[i];
								valid[i] = Core.schedulerIF.intReplayEntry[i];
							end
							else begin
								issuedData[i] = Core.schedulerIF.intIssuedData[i];
								valid[i] = !stall && pipeReg[((1 - i) * 5) + 4];
							end
							issueQueuePtr[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 5) + 3-:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
							flush[i] = PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector(Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeHeadPtr, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeTailPtr, issuedData[i][51-:6]);
							Core.schedulerIF.intIssue[i] = (!clear && valid[i]) && !flush[i];
							Core.schedulerIF.intIssuePtr[i] = issueQueuePtr[i];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 115) + 114] = ((clear || Core.intIsStageIF.rst) || flush[i] ? BasicTypes_FALSE : valid[i]);
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 115) + 113-:114] = issuedData[i];
						end
				end
				Core.intIsStageIF.nextStage = nextStage;
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : intRrStage
			localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM = 64;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM = BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM = 64;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_BRANCH_GLOBAL_HISTORY_BIT_WIDTH = 10;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam OpFormatTypes_BR_DISP_WIDTH = 20;
			localparam BasicTypes_RISCV_SHIFTER_WIDTH = 30;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_BR_SUB_INFO_BIT_WIDTH = 56;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_INT_SUB_INFO_BIT_WIDTH = 39;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_INT_SUB_INFO_PADDING_BIT_WIDTH = 17;
			reg [229:0] pipeReg;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			always @(posedge Core.intRrStageIF.clk)
				if (Core.intRrStageIF.rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_213
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						pipeReg[((1 - i) * 115) + 114] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else if (!Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[1])
					pipeReg <= Core.intIsStageIF.nextStage;
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
			reg [31:0] immOut [0:1];
			localparam BasicTypes_ADDR_WIDTH = 32;
			reg [31:0] pc [0:1];
			reg [32:0] operandA [0:1];
			reg [32:0] operandB [0:1];
			reg stall;
			reg clear;
			reg flush [0:1];
			reg [113:0] iqData [0:1];
			reg [55:0] intSubInfo [0:1];
			reg [55:0] brSubInfo [0:1];
			reg [11:0] opSrc [0:1];
			reg [6:0] opDst [0:1];
			localparam BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 1;
			localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 1;
			localparam BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 1;
			reg [385:0] nextStage;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_TAG = 13;
			function automatic [31:0] MemoryMapTypes_ToAddrFromPC;
				input reg [18:0] pc;
				MemoryMapTypes_ToAddrFromPC = {pc[18], {MemoryMapTypes_PC_TAG {1'b0}}, pc[17:0]};
			endfunction
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			function automatic PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector;
				input reg detectRange;
				input reg [5:0] headPtr;
				input reg [5:0] tailPtr;
				input reg [5:0] opPtr;
				if (!detectRange)
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr >= headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr < headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr > tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else if ((opPtr < headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			endfunction
			function automatic [31:0] RISCV_OpImm;
				input reg [29:0] intOperandImm;
				reg [31:0] result;
				begin
					case (intOperandImm[1-:2])
						default: result = {{12 {intOperandImm[21]}}, intOperandImm[21-:20]};
						2'b11: result = {intOperandImm[21-:20], 12'h000};
					endcase
					RISCV_OpImm = result;
				end
			endfunction
			function automatic [31:0] SelectOperandIntReg;
				input reg [1:0] opType;
				input reg [31:0] regV;
				input reg [31:0] immV;
				input reg [31:0] pcV;
				case (opType)
					default: SelectOperandIntReg = regV;
					2'b01: SelectOperandIntReg = immV;
					2'b10: SelectOperandIntReg = pcV;
				endcase
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin
				stall = Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[1];
				clear = Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[0];
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_214
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							iqData[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 115) + 113-:114];
							intSubInfo[i] = iqData[i][113-:56];
							brSubInfo[i] = iqData[i][113-:56];
							opSrc[i] = iqData[i][37-:12];
							opDst[i] = iqData[i][25-:7];
							pc[i] = MemoryMapTypes_ToAddrFromPC(iqData[i][18-:19]);
							Core.registerFileIF.intSrcRegNumA[i] = opSrc[i][11-:6];
							Core.registerFileIF.intSrcRegNumB[i] = opSrc[i][5-:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
							Core.bypassNetworkIF.intPhySrcRegNumA[i] = opSrc[i][11-:6];
							Core.bypassNetworkIF.intPhySrcRegNumB[i] = opSrc[i][5-:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
							Core.bypassNetworkIF.intWriteReg[i] = opDst[i][6] & pipeReg[((1 - i) * 115) + 114];
							Core.bypassNetworkIF.intPhyDstRegNum[i] = opDst[i][5-:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
							Core.bypassNetworkIF.intReadRegA[i] = (intSubInfo[i][55-:2] == 2'b00) || (brSubInfo[i][55-:2] == 2'b00);
							Core.bypassNetworkIF.intReadRegB[i] = (intSubInfo[i][53-:2] == 2'b00) || (brSubInfo[i][55-:2] == 2'b00);
							immOut[i] = RISCV_OpImm(intSubInfo[i][46-:30]);
							operandA[i][31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] = SelectOperandIntReg(intSubInfo[i][55-:2], Core.registerFileIF.intSrcRegDataA[i][31-:32], immOut[i], pc[i]);
							operandB[i][31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] = SelectOperandIntReg(intSubInfo[i][53-:2], Core.registerFileIF.intSrcRegDataB[i][31-:32], immOut[i], pc[i]);
							operandA[i][32] = (intSubInfo[i][55-:2] != 2'b00) || Core.registerFileIF.intSrcRegDataA[i][32];
							operandB[i][32] = (intSubInfo[i][53-:2] != 2'b00) || Core.registerFileIF.intSrcRegDataB[i][32];
							flush[i] = PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector(Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeHeadPtr, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeTailPtr, iqData[i][51-:6]);
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 193) + 192] = (((stall || clear) || Core.intRrStageIF.rst) || flush[i] ? BasicTypes_FALSE : pipeReg[((1 - i) * 115) + 114]);
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 193) + 77-:33] = operandA[i];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 193) + 44-:33] = operandB[i];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 193) + 191-:114] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 115) + 113-:114];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 193) + 11-:12] = Core.bypassNetworkIF.intCtrlOut[(1 - i) * 12+:12];
						end
				end
				Core.intRrStageIF.nextStage = nextStage;
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : intExStage
			localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 1;
			localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 1;
			localparam BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 1;
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM = 64;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM = BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM = 64;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_BRANCH_GLOBAL_HISTORY_BIT_WIDTH = 10;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam OpFormatTypes_BR_DISP_WIDTH = 20;
			localparam BasicTypes_RISCV_SHIFTER_WIDTH = 30;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_BR_SUB_INFO_BIT_WIDTH = 56;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_INT_SUB_INFO_BIT_WIDTH = 39;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_INT_SUB_INFO_PADDING_BIT_WIDTH = 17;
			reg [385:0] pipeReg;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			always @(posedge Core.intExStageIF.clk)
				if (Core.intExStageIF.rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_215
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						pipeReg[((1 - i) * 193) + 192] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else if (!Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[1])
					pipeReg <= Core.intRrStageIF.nextStage;
			reg stall;
			reg clear;
			reg flush [0:1];
			reg [113:0] iqData [0:1];
			reg [55:0] intOpInfo [0:1];
			reg [31:0] bPred [0:1];
			localparam BasicTypes_ADDR_WIDTH = 32;
			reg [31:0] pc [0:1];
			reg [32:0] fuOpA [0:1];
			reg [32:0] fuOpB [0:1];
			reg [32:0] dataOut [0:1];
			reg isCondEnabled [0:1];
			wire [31:0] aluDataOut [0:1];
			reg [3:0] aluCode [0:1];
			genvar i;
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1) begin : BlockALU
				IntALU intALU(
					.aluCode(aluCode[i]),
					.fuOpA_In(fuOpA[i][31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH]),
					.fuOpB_In(fuOpB[i][31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH]),
					.aluDataOut(aluDataOut[i])
				);
			end
			reg shiftOperandType [0:1];
			reg [29:0] shiftImmIn [0:1];
			wire [31:0] shiftDataOut [0:1];
			wire shiftCarryOut [0:1];
			localparam BasicTypes_SHIFT_AMOUNT_BIT_SIZE = 5;
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1) begin : BlockShifter
				Shifter shifter(
					.shiftOperandType(shiftOperandType[i]),
					.shiftType(shiftImmIn[i][24-:2]),
					.immShiftAmount(shiftImmIn[i][29-:5]),
					.regShiftAmount(fuOpB[i][4:0]),
					.dataIn(fuOpA[i][31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH]),
					.carryIn(1'b0),
					.dataOut(shiftDataOut[i]),
					.carryOut(shiftCarryOut[i])
				);
			end
			reg isBranch [0:1];
			reg isJump [0:1];
			reg brTaken [0:1];
			reg [54:0] brResult [0:1];
			reg predMiss [0:1];
			reg regValid [0:1];
			reg [55:0] intSubInfo [0:1];
			reg [55:0] brSubInfo [0:1];
			localparam BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH = 8;
			localparam BasicTypes_INSN_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			function automatic IsConditionEnabledInt;
				input reg [2:0] cond;
				input reg [31:0] opA;
				input reg [31:0] opB;
				reg ce;
				reg signed [31:0] signedOpA;
				reg signed [31:0] signedOpB;
				begin
					signedOpA = opA;
					signedOpB = opB;
					case (cond)
						3'b000: ce = opA == opB;
						3'b001: ce = opA != opB;
						3'b100: ce = signedOpA < signedOpB;
						3'b110: ce = opA < opB;
						3'b101: ce = signedOpA >= signedOpB;
						3'b111: ce = opA >= opB;
						3'b010: ce = 1;
						default: ce = 1;
					endcase
					IsConditionEnabledInt = ce;
				end
			endfunction
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_TAG = 13;
			function automatic [31:0] MemoryMapTypes_ToAddrFromPC;
				input reg [18:0] pc;
				MemoryMapTypes_ToAddrFromPC = {pc[18], {MemoryMapTypes_PC_TAG {1'b0}}, pc[17:0]};
			endfunction
			function automatic [18:0] MemoryMapTypes_ToPC_FromAddr;
				input reg [31:0] addr;
				MemoryMapTypes_ToPC_FromAddr = {addr[31], addr[17:0]};
			endfunction
			function automatic [31:0] OpFormatTypes_ExtendJALR_Target;
				input reg [19:0] brDisp;
				OpFormatTypes_ExtendJALR_Target = {{12 {brDisp[19]}}, brDisp};
			endfunction
			function automatic [31:0] OpFormatTypes_AddJALR_TargetOffset;
				input reg [31:0] data;
				input reg [19:0] disp;
				reg [31:0] target;
				begin
					target = data + OpFormatTypes_ExtendJALR_Target(disp);
					target[0] = 1'b0;
					OpFormatTypes_AddJALR_TargetOffset = target;
				end
			endfunction
			function automatic [31:0] OpFormatTypes_ExtendBranchDisplacement;
				input reg [19:0] brDisp;
				OpFormatTypes_ExtendBranchDisplacement = {{11 {brDisp[19]}}, brDisp, 1'b0};
			endfunction
			localparam OpFormatTypes_PC_OPERAND_OFFSET = 4;
			function automatic PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector;
				input reg detectRange;
				input reg [5:0] headPtr;
				input reg [5:0] tailPtr;
				input reg [5:0] opPtr;
				if (!detectRange)
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr >= headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr < headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr > tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else if ((opPtr < headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin
				stall = Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[1];
				clear = Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[0];
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_216
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							iqData[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 193) + 191-:114];
							intOpInfo[i] = iqData[i][113-:56];
							intSubInfo[i] = intOpInfo[i][55-:56];
							brSubInfo[i] = intOpInfo[i][55-:56];
							pc[i] = MemoryMapTypes_ToAddrFromPC(iqData[i][18-:19]);
							flush[i] = PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector(Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeHeadPtr, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeTailPtr, iqData[i][51-:6]);
							fuOpA[i] = (pipeReg[((1 - i) * 193) + 11] ? Core.bypassNetworkIF.intSrcRegDataOutA[i] : pipeReg[((1 - i) * 193) + 77-:33]);
							fuOpB[i] = (pipeReg[((1 - i) * 193) + 5] ? Core.bypassNetworkIF.intSrcRegDataOutB[i] : pipeReg[((1 - i) * 193) + 44-:33]);
							isCondEnabled[i] = IsConditionEnabledInt(iqData[i][54-:3], fuOpA[i][31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH], fuOpB[i][31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH]);
							shiftOperandType[i] = intSubInfo[i][47];
							shiftImmIn[i] = intSubInfo[i][46-:30];
							aluCode[i] = intSubInfo[i][51-:4];
							case (iqData[i][57-:3])
								3'b000: dataOut[i][31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] = aluDataOut[i];
								3'b001: dataOut[i][31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] = shiftDataOut[i];
								3'b010, 3'b011: dataOut[i][31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] = pc[i] + OpFormatTypes_PC_OPERAND_OFFSET;
								default: dataOut[i][31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] = (isCondEnabled[i] ? fuOpA[i][31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] : fuOpB[i][31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH]);
							endcase
							regValid[i] = ((intSubInfo[i][55-:2] != 2'b00) || fuOpA[i][32]) && ((intSubInfo[i][53-:2] != 2'b00) || fuOpB[i][32]);
							dataOut[i][32] = regValid[i];
							Core.bypassNetworkIF.intCtrlIn[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 193) + 11-:12];
							Core.bypassNetworkIF.intDstRegDataOut[i] = dataOut[i];
							bPred[i] = brSubInfo[i][51-:32];
							isBranch[i] = |{iqData[i][57-:3] == 3'b010, iqData[i][57-:3] == 3'b011};
							isJump[i] = ((iqData[i][57-:3] == 3'b010) && (iqData[i][54-:3] == 3'b010)) || (iqData[i][57-:3] == 3'b011);
							brTaken[i] = (pipeReg[((1 - i) * 193) + 192] && isBranch[i]) && isCondEnabled[i];
							brResult[i][14] = !isJump[i];
							brResult[i][54-:19] = MemoryMapTypes_ToPC_FromAddr(pc[i]);
							if (brTaken[i])
								brResult[i][35-:19] = MemoryMapTypes_ToPC_FromAddr((iqData[i][57-:3] == 3'b010 ? pc[i] + OpFormatTypes_ExtendBranchDisplacement(brSubInfo[i][19-:OpFormatTypes_BR_DISP_WIDTH]) : OpFormatTypes_AddJALR_TargetOffset(fuOpA[i][31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH], brSubInfo[i][19-:OpFormatTypes_BR_DISP_WIDTH])));
							else
								brResult[i][35-:19] = MemoryMapTypes_ToPC_FromAddr(pc[i] + BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH);
							brResult[i][16] = brTaken[i];
							brResult[i][15] = bPred[i][12];
							brResult[i][12] = (isBranch[i] && pipeReg[((1 - i) * 193) + 192]) && regValid[i];
							brResult[i][11-:10] = bPred[i][11-:10];
							brResult[i][1-:FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH] = bPred[i][1-:FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH];
							predMiss[i] = (pipeReg[((1 - i) * 193) + 192] && isBranch[i]) && ((bPred[i][12] != brTaken[i]) || ((brTaken[i] == BasicTypes_TRUE) && (bPred[i][31-:19] != brResult[i][35-:19])));
							brResult[i][13] = predMiss[i];
						end
				end
			end
			reg [407:0] nextStage;
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_217
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 204) + 202-:114] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 193) + 191-:114];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 204) + 203] = (((stall || clear) || Core.intExStageIF.rst) || flush[i] ? BasicTypes_FALSE : pipeReg[((1 - i) * 193) + 192]);
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 204) + 88-:33] = dataOut[i];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 204) + 54-:55] = brResult[i];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 204) + 55] = predMiss[i];
						end
				end
				Core.intExStageIF.nextStage = nextStage;
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : intRwStage
			localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_BRANCH_GLOBAL_HISTORY_BIT_WIDTH = 10;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM = 64;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM = BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM = 64;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam OpFormatTypes_BR_DISP_WIDTH = 20;
			localparam BasicTypes_RISCV_SHIFTER_WIDTH = 30;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_BR_SUB_INFO_BIT_WIDTH = 56;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_INT_SUB_INFO_BIT_WIDTH = 39;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_INT_SUB_INFO_PADDING_BIT_WIDTH = 17;
			reg [407:0] pipeReg;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			always @(posedge Core.ctrlIF.clk)
				if (Core.ctrlIF.rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_218
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						pipeReg[((1 - i) * 204) + 203] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else if (!Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[1])
					pipeReg <= Core.intExStageIF.nextStage;
			localparam BasicTypes_ADDR_WIDTH = 32;
			reg [70:0] alWriteData [0:1];
			reg [113:0] iqData [0:1];
			reg [109:0] brResult;
			reg stall;
			reg clear;
			reg flush [0:1];
			reg update [0:1];
			reg valid [0:1];
			reg regValid [0:1];
			localparam BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH = 8;
			localparam BasicTypes_INSN_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam BasicTypes_INSN_ADDR_BIT_WIDTH = 2;

			function automatic PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector;
				input reg detectRange;
				input reg [5:0] headPtr;
				input reg [5:0] tailPtr;
				input reg [5:0] opPtr;
				if (!detectRange)
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr >= headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr < headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr > tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else if ((opPtr < headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin
				stall = Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[1];
				clear = Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[0];

							Core.npStageIF.brResult = brResult;

								if (regValid[i])
									alWriteData[i][56-:4] = (pipeReg[((1 - i) * 204) + 55] ? 4'b0011 : 4'b0001);
								else
									alWriteData[i][56-:4] = 4'b0000;
							end
							else
								alWriteData[i][56-:4] = 4'b0000;
							if (|{alWriteData[i][56-:4] == 4'b0011, alWriteData[i][56-:4] == 4'b0001})
								if ((brResult[((1 - i) * 55) + 18-:2] != 0) && brResult[((1 - i) * 55) + 12]) begin

									alWriteData[i][33-:32] = brResult[((1 - i) * 55) + 35-:19];
								end

							Core.activeListIF.intWriteData[i] = alWriteData[i];
							Core.schedulerIF.intRecordEntry[i] = update[i] && !regValid[i];
							Core.schedulerIF.intRecordData[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 204) + 202-:114];
						end
				end
			end
		end

									begin
										iqData[i][j] = localPipeReg[(((i * 2) + (2 - j)) * 60) + 57-:58];
										flush[i][j] = PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector(Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeHeadPtr, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeTailPtr, localPipeReg[(((i * 2) + (2 - j)) * 60) + 51-:6]);
									end
							end
							fuOpA[i] = (pipeReg[(i * 139) + 11] ? Core.bypassNetworkIF.complexSrcRegDataOutA[i] : pipeReg[(i * 139) + 77-:33]);
							fuOpB[i] = (pipeReg[(i * 139) + 5] ? Core.bypassNetworkIF.complexSrcRegDataOutB[i] : pipeReg[(i * 139) + 44-:33]);
							regValid[i] = fuOpA[i][32] && fuOpB[i][32];
							dataOut[i][32] = localPipeReg[((i * 2) * 60) + 58];
							case (localPipeReg[((i * 2) * 60) + 54-:3])
								3'b001: dataOut[i][31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] = Core.mulDivUnitIF.divDataOut[i];
								default: dataOut[i][31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] = Core.mulDivUnitIF.mulDataOut[i];
							endcase
							Core.bypassNetworkIF.complexCtrlIn[i] = pipeReg[(i * 139) + 11-:12];
							Core.bypassNetworkIF.complexDstRegDataOut[i] = dataOut[i];
							Core.schedulerIF.complexRecordEntry[i] = (((!stall && !clear) && !flush[i][1]) && localPipeReg[(((i * 2) + 1) * 60) + 59]) && !localPipeReg[(((i * 2) + 1) * 60) + 58];
							Core.schedulerIF.complexRecordData[i] = localPipeReg[(((i * 2) + 1) * 60) + 57-:58];
						end
				end
			end
			reg [91:0] nextStage;
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_232
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							nextLocalPipeReg[(((i * 2) + 1) * 60) + 59] = (flush[i][0] ? BasicTypes_FALSE : pipeReg[(i * 139) + 138]);
							nextLocalPipeReg[(((i * 2) + 1) * 60) + 57-:58] = pipeReg[(i * 139) + 135-:58];
							if (isDiv[i])
								nextLocalPipeReg[(((i * 2) + 1) * 60) + 58] = pipeReg[(i * 139) + 137] && Core.mulDivUnitIF.divFinished[i];
							else
								nextLocalPipeReg[(((i * 2) + 1) * 60) + 58] = regValid[i];
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_233
								reg signed [31:0] j;
								for (j = 1; j < 2; j = j + 1)
									begin
										nextLocalPipeReg[(((i * 2) + (1 - j)) * 60) + 59] = (flush[i][j] ? BasicTypes_FALSE : localPipeReg[(((i * 2) + (2 - j)) * 60) + 59]);
										nextLocalPipeReg[(((i * 2) + (1 - j)) * 60) + 58] = localPipeReg[(((i * 2) + (2 - j)) * 60) + 58];
										nextLocalPipeReg[(((i * 2) + (1 - j)) * 60) + 57-:58] = localPipeReg[(((i * 2) + (2 - j)) * 60) + 57-:58];
									end
							end
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_234
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							nextStage[(i * 92) + 90-:58] = localPipeReg[((i * 2) * 60) + 57-:58];
							nextStage[(i * 92) + 91] = (((stall || clear) || Core.complexExStageIF.rst) || flush[i][2] ? BasicTypes_FALSE : localPipeReg[((i * 2) * 60) + 59]);
							nextStage[(i * 92) + 32-:33] = dataOut[i];
						end
				end
				Core.complexExStageIF.nextStage = nextStage;
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : complexRwStage
			localparam BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM = 64;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM = BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM = 64;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			reg [91:0] pipeReg;
			always @(posedge Core.ctrlIF.clk)
				if (Core.ctrlIF.rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_235
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						pipeReg[(i * 92) + 91] <= 1'sb0;
				end
				else if (!Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[1])
					pipeReg <= Core.complexExStageIF.nextStage;
			localparam BasicTypes_ADDR_WIDTH = 32;
			reg [70:0] alWriteData [0:0];
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_BRANCH_GLOBAL_HISTORY_BIT_WIDTH = 10;
			localparam FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam OpFormatTypes_BR_DISP_WIDTH = 20;
			localparam BasicTypes_RISCV_SHIFTER_WIDTH = 30;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_BR_SUB_INFO_BIT_WIDTH = 56;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_INT_SUB_INFO_BIT_WIDTH = 39;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_INT_SUB_INFO_PADDING_BIT_WIDTH = 17;
			reg [113:0] iqData [0:0];
			reg stall;
			reg clear;
			reg flush [0:0];
			reg update [0:0];
			reg valid [0:0];
			reg regValid [0:0];
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			function automatic PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector;
				input reg detectRange;
				input reg [5:0] headPtr;
				input reg [5:0] tailPtr;
				input reg [5:0] opPtr;
				if (!detectRange)
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr >= headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr < headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr > tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else if ((opPtr < headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin
				stall = Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[1];
				clear = Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[0];
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_236
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							iqData[i] = pipeReg[(i * 92) + 90-:58];
							regValid[i] = pipeReg[(i * 92) + 32];
							valid[i] = pipeReg[(i * 92) + 91];
							flush[i] = PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector(Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeHeadPtr, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeTailPtr, iqData[i][51-:6]);
							update[i] = ((!stall && !clear) && valid[i]) && !flush[i];
							Core.registerFileIF.complexDstRegWE[i] = update[i] && iqData[i][25];
							Core.registerFileIF.complexDstRegNum[i] = iqData[i][24-:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
							Core.registerFileIF.complexDstRegData[i] = pipeReg[(i * 92) + 32-:33];
							alWriteData[i][70-:6] = iqData[i][51-:6];
							alWriteData[i][64-:4] = iqData[i][45-:4];
							alWriteData[i][60-:4] = iqData[i][41-:4];
							alWriteData[i][70-:6] = iqData[i][51-:6];
							alWriteData[i][52-:19] = iqData[i][18-:19];
							alWriteData[i][33-:32] = 1'sb0;
							alWriteData[i][1] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							alWriteData[i][0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							if (update[i] && regValid[i])
								alWriteData[i][56-:4] = 4'b0001;
							else
								alWriteData[i][56-:4] = 4'b0000;
							Core.activeListIF.complexWrite[i] = update[i];
							Core.activeListIF.complexWriteData[i] = alWriteData[i];
						end
				end
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : mulDivUnit
			genvar i;
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam BasicTypes_MULDIV_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			localparam BasicTypes_MULDIV_STAGE_DEPTH = 3;
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MULDIV_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1) begin : BlockMulUnit
				PipelinedMultiplierUnit #(
					.BIT_WIDTH(BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH),
					.PIPELINE_DEPTH(BasicTypes_MULDIV_STAGE_DEPTH)
				) mulUnit(
					.clk(Core.mulDivUnitIF.clk),
					.stall(Core.mulDivUnitIF.stall),
					.fuOpA_In(Core.mulDivUnitIF.dataInA[i]),
					.fuOpB_In(Core.mulDivUnitIF.dataInB[i]),
					.getUpper(Core.mulDivUnitIF.mulGetUpper[i]),
					.mulCode(Core.mulDivUnitIF.mulCode[i]),
					.dataOut(Core.mulDivUnitIF.mulDataOut[i])
				);
			end
			reg [1:0] regPhase;
			reg [1:0] nextPhase;
			wire finished [0:0];
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MULDIV_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1) begin : BlockDivUnit
				DividerUnit divUnit(
					.clk(Core.mulDivUnitIF.clk),
					.rst(Core.mulDivUnitIF.rst),
					.req(Core.mulDivUnitIF.divReq[i]),
					.fuOpA_In(Core.mulDivUnitIF.dataInA[i]),
					.fuOpB_In(Core.mulDivUnitIF.dataInB[i]),
					.divCode(Core.mulDivUnitIF.divCode[i]),
					.finished(finished[i]),
					.dataOut(Core.mulDivUnitIF.divDataOut[i])
				);
			end
			always @(posedge Core.mulDivUnitIF.clk)
				if (Core.mulDivUnitIF.rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_237
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MULDIV_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						regPhase[i * 2+:2] <= 2'd0;
				end
				else
					regPhase <= nextPhase;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			always @(*) begin
				nextPhase = regPhase;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_238
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MULDIV_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							case (regPhase[i * 2+:2])
								default: nextPhase[i * 2+:2] = 2'd0;
								2'd0:
									if (Core.mulDivUnitIF.divAcquire[i])
										nextPhase[i * 2+:2] = 2'd1;
								2'd1:
									if (Core.mulDivUnitIF.divReq[i])
										nextPhase[i * 2+:2] = 2'd2;
								2'd2:
									if (finished[i])
										nextPhase[i * 2+:2] = 2'd3;
								2'd3:
									if (Core.mulDivUnitIF.divRelease[i])
										nextPhase[i * 2+:2] = 2'd0;
							endcase
							if (Core.mulDivUnitIF.divReset[i])
								nextPhase[i * 2+:2] = 2'd0;
							if (Core.mulDivUnitIF.divResetFromCI_Stage[i])
								nextPhase[i * 2+:2] = 2'd0;
							Core.mulDivUnitIF.divFree[i] = (nextPhase[i * 2+:2] == 2'd0 ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
							Core.mulDivUnitIF.divFinished[i] = (regPhase[i * 2+:2] == 2'd3 ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
							Core.mulDivUnitIF.divBusy[i] = (regPhase[i * 2+:2] == 2'd2 ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
							Core.mulDivUnitIF.divReserved[i] = (regPhase[i * 2+:2] == 2'd1 ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
						end
				end
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : memIsStage
			localparam BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			reg [9:0] pipeReg;
			reg [9:0] nextPipeReg;
			always @(posedge Core.memIsStageIF.clk)
				if (Core.memIsStageIF.rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_239
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						pipeReg[(1 - i) * 5+:5] <= 1'sb0;
				end
				else if (!Core.ctrlIF.isStage[1])
					pipeReg <= Core.scStageIF.memNextStage;
				else
					pipeReg <= nextPipeReg;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM = 64;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			function automatic PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector;
				input reg detectRange;
				input reg [5:0] headPtr;
				input reg [5:0] tailPtr;
				input reg [5:0] opPtr;
				if (!detectRange)
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr >= headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr < headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr > tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else if ((opPtr < headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_240
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
					begin
						if (Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase)
							nextPipeReg[((1 - i) * 5) + 4] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 5) + 4] && !PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector(Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeHeadPtr, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeTailPtr, Core.schedulerIF.memIssuedData[i][51-:6]);
						else
							nextPipeReg[((1 - i) * 5) + 4] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 5) + 4];
						nextPipeReg[((1 - i) * 5) + 3-:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 5) + 3-:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
					end
			end
			reg stall;
			reg clear;
			reg flush [0:1];
			reg valid [0:1];
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM = 64;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM = BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM = 2;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 1;
			localparam OpFormatTypes_CSR_IMM_WIDTH = 5;
			reg [213:0] nextStage;
			reg [100:0] issuedData [0:1];
			reg [3:0] issueQueuePtr [0:1];
			always @(*) begin
				stall = Core.ctrlIF.isStage[1];
				clear = Core.ctrlIF.isStage[0];
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_241
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							if (Core.schedulerIF.replay) begin
								issuedData[i] = Core.schedulerIF.memReplayData[i];
								valid[i] = Core.schedulerIF.memReplayEntry[i];
							end
							else begin
								issuedData[i] = Core.schedulerIF.memIssuedData[i];
								valid[i] = !stall && pipeReg[((1 - i) * 5) + 4];
							end
							issueQueuePtr[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 5) + 3-:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
							flush[i] = PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector(Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeHeadPtr, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeTailPtr, issuedData[i][51-:6]);
							Core.schedulerIF.memIssue[i] = (!clear && valid[i]) && !flush[i];
							Core.schedulerIF.memIssuePtr[i] = issueQueuePtr[i];
							nextStage[(1 - i) * 107] = Core.schedulerIF.replay;
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 107) + 4-:4] = issueQueuePtr[i];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 107) + 106] = ((clear || Core.memIsStageIF.rst) || flush[i] ? BasicTypes_FALSE : valid[i]);
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 107) + 105-:101] = issuedData[i];
						end
				end
				Core.memIsStageIF.nextStage = nextStage;
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : memRrStage
			localparam BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM = 64;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM = BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM = 64;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM = 2;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 1;
			localparam OpFormatTypes_CSR_IMM_WIDTH = 5;
			reg [213:0] pipeReg;
			always @(posedge Core.memRrStageIF.clk)
				if (Core.memRrStageIF.rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_242
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						pipeReg[((1 - i) * 107) + 106] <= 1'sb0;
				end
				else if (!Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[1])
					pipeReg <= Core.memIsStageIF.nextStage;
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
			reg [31:0] immOut [0:1];
			localparam BasicTypes_ADDR_WIDTH = 32;
			reg [31:0] pc [0:1];
			reg [32:0] operandA [0:1];
			reg [32:0] operandB [0:1];
			reg stall;
			reg clear;
			reg flush [0:1];
			reg [100:0] iqData [0:1];
			reg [48:0] memOpInfo [0:1];
			reg [11:0] opSrc [0:1];
			reg [6:0] opDst [0:1];
			localparam BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 1;
			localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 1;
			localparam BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 1;
			reg [369:0] nextStage;
			reg [0:0] mshrID;
			localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			reg makeMSHRCanBeInvalid [0:0];
			reg isLoad [0:0];
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			function automatic PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector;
				input reg detectRange;
				input reg [5:0] headPtr;
				input reg [5:0] tailPtr;
				input reg [5:0] opPtr;
				if (!detectRange)
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr >= headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr < headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr > tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else if ((opPtr < headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			endfunction
			function automatic [31:0] SelectOperand;
				input reg [1:0] opType;
				input reg [31:0] regV;
				input reg [31:0] immV;
				input reg [31:0] pcV;
				case (opType)
					default: SelectOperand = regV;
					2'b01: SelectOperand = immV;
					2'b10: SelectOperand = pcV;
				endcase
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin
				stall = Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[1];
				clear = Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[0];
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_243
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							iqData[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 107) + 105-:101];
							memOpInfo[i] = iqData[i][100-:49];
							opSrc[i] = iqData[i][37-:12];
							opDst[i] = iqData[i][25-:7];
							pc[i] = iqData[i][18-:19];
							Core.registerFileIF.memSrcRegNumA[i] = opSrc[i][11-:6];
							Core.registerFileIF.memSrcRegNumB[i] = opSrc[i][5-:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
							Core.bypassNetworkIF.memPhySrcRegNumA[i] = opSrc[i][11-:6];
							Core.bypassNetworkIF.memPhySrcRegNumB[i] = opSrc[i][5-:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
							Core.bypassNetworkIF.memWriteReg[i] = opDst[i][6] & pipeReg[((1 - i) * 107) + 106];
							Core.bypassNetworkIF.memPhyDstRegNum[i] = opDst[i][5-:BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
							Core.bypassNetworkIF.memReadRegA[i] = memOpInfo[i][42-:2] == 2'b00;
							Core.bypassNetworkIF.memReadRegB[i] = memOpInfo[i][40-:2] == 2'b00;
							immOut[i] = 1'sb0;
							operandA[i][31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] = SelectOperand(memOpInfo[i][42-:2], Core.registerFileIF.memSrcRegDataA[i][31-:32], immOut[i], pc[i]);
							operandB[i][31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] = SelectOperand(memOpInfo[i][40-:2], Core.registerFileIF.memSrcRegDataB[i][31-:32], immOut[i], pc[i]);
							operandA[i][32] = (memOpInfo[i][42-:2] != 2'b00) || Core.registerFileIF.memSrcRegDataA[i][32];
							operandB[i][32] = (memOpInfo[i][40-:2] != 2'b00) || Core.registerFileIF.memSrcRegDataB[i][32];
							flush[i] = PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector(Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeHeadPtr, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeTailPtr, iqData[i][51-:6]);
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 185) + 184] = (((stall || clear) || Core.memRrStageIF.rst) || flush[i] ? BasicTypes_FALSE : pipeReg[((1 - i) * 107) + 106]);
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 185) + 183-:101] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 107) + 105-:101];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 185) + 82-:33] = operandA[i];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 185) + 49-:33] = operandB[i];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 185) + 16-:12] = Core.bypassNetworkIF.memCtrlOut[(1 - i) * 12+:12];
							nextStage[(1 - i) * 185] = pipeReg[(1 - i) * 107] && pipeReg[((1 - i) * 107) + 106];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 185) + 4-:4] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 107) + 4-:4];
						end
				end
				Core.memRrStageIF.nextStage = nextStage;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_244
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; i = i + 1)
						Core.loadStoreUnitIF.makeMSHRCanBeInvalidByMemoryRegisterReadStage[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_245
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							isLoad[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 107) + 105-:3] == 3'b000;
							if ((pipeReg[((1 - i) * 107) + 106] && isLoad[i]) && flush[i])
								makeMSHRCanBeInvalid[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 107) + 58];
							else
								makeMSHRCanBeInvalid[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							mshrID = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 107) + 57-:CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
							if (makeMSHRCanBeInvalid[i])
								Core.loadStoreUnitIF.makeMSHRCanBeInvalidByMemoryRegisterReadStage[mshrID] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						end
				end
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : memExStage
			localparam BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 1;
			localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 1;
			localparam BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 1;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM = 64;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM = BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM = 64;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM = 2;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 1;
			localparam OpFormatTypes_CSR_IMM_WIDTH = 5;
			reg [369:0] pipeReg;
			always @(posedge Core.memExStageIF.clk)
				if (Core.memExStageIF.rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_246
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						pipeReg[((1 - i) * 185) + 184] <= 1'sb0;
				end
				else if (!Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[1])
					pipeReg <= Core.memRrStageIF.nextStage;
			reg stall;
			reg clear;
			reg flush [0:1];
			reg [100:0] iqData [0:1];
			reg [48:0] memOpInfo [0:1];
			reg [32:0] fuOpA [0:1];
			reg [32:0] fuOpB [0:1];
			reg regValid [0:1];
			localparam BasicTypes_ADDR_WIDTH = 32;
			reg [31:0] addrOut [0:1];
			reg [1:0] memMapType [0:1];
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_WIDTH = 22;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH = 20;
			reg [21:0] phyAddrOut [0:1];
			reg isUncachable [0:1];
			localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			reg makeMSHRCanBeInvalid [0:0];
			reg cacheFlushReq;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_0_BEGIN = 32'h00001000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_0_END = 32'h00010000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_1_BEGIN = 32'h80000000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_1_END = 32'h80040000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SERIAL_OUTPUT = 32'h40002000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_BASE = 32'h40000000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_BEGIN = MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_BASE;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_END = 1073741840;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_BEGIN = 32'h80040000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_END = 32'h80050000;
			function automatic [1:0] MemoryMapTypes_GetMemoryMapType;
				input reg [31:0] addr;
				if (addr == MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SERIAL_OUTPUT)
					MemoryMapTypes_GetMemoryMapType = 2'b01;
				else if ((MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_BEGIN <= addr) && (addr < MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_END))
					MemoryMapTypes_GetMemoryMapType = 2'b01;
				else if ((MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_BEGIN <= addr) && (addr < MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_END))
					MemoryMapTypes_GetMemoryMapType = 2'b00;
				else if ((MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_0_BEGIN <= addr) && (addr < MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_0_END))
					MemoryMapTypes_GetMemoryMapType = 2'b00;
