				else if ((MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_1_BEGIN <= addr) && (addr < MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_1_END))
					MemoryMapTypes_GetMemoryMapType = 2'b00;
				else
					MemoryMapTypes_GetMemoryMapType = 2'b10;
			endfunction
			function automatic MemoryMapTypes_IsPhyAddrUncachable;
				input reg [21:0] phyAddr;
				MemoryMapTypes_IsPhyAddrUncachable = phyAddr[21];
			endfunction
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_0_ADDR_BIT_WIDTH = 16;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_1_ADDR_BIT_WIDTH = 18;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_ADDR_BIT_WIDTH = 19;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_SECTION_0_BASE = 20'h00000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_SECTION_1_BASE = 20'h10000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_SERIAL_OUTPUT = 20'h02000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_TIMER_BASE = 20'h00000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_TIMER_ZONE_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_BASE = 20'h10000;
			function automatic [21:0] MemoryMapTypes_ToPhyAddrFromLogical;
				input reg [31:0] logAddr;
				reg [21:0] phyAddr;
				begin
					if (logAddr == MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SERIAL_OUTPUT) begin
						phyAddr[21] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						phyAddr[20] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						phyAddr[19-:MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH] = MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_SERIAL_OUTPUT;
					end
					else if ((MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_BEGIN <= logAddr) && (logAddr < MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_TIMER_END)) begin
						phyAddr[21] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						phyAddr[20] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						phyAddr[19-:MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH] = MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_TIMER_BASE + logAddr[3:0];
					end
					else if ((MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_BEGIN <= logAddr) && (logAddr < MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_END)) begin
						phyAddr[21] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						phyAddr[20] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						phyAddr[19-:MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH] = MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_UNCACHABLE_BASE + logAddr[18:0];
					end
					else if ((MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_0_BEGIN <= logAddr) && (logAddr < MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_0_END)) begin
						phyAddr[21] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						phyAddr[20] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						phyAddr[19-:MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH] = MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_SECTION_0_BASE + logAddr[MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_0_ADDR_BIT_WIDTH:0];
					end
					else if ((MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_1_BEGIN <= logAddr) && (logAddr < MemoryMapTypes_LOG_ADDR_SECTION_1_END)) begin
						phyAddr[21] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						phyAddr[20] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						phyAddr[19-:MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH] = MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_SECTION_1_BASE + logAddr[17:0];
					end
					else begin
						phyAddr[21] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						phyAddr[20] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						phyAddr[19-:MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH] = 32'hcccccccc;
					end
					MemoryMapTypes_ToPhyAddrFromLogical = phyAddr;
				end
			endfunction
			localparam OpFormatTypes_ADDR_OPERAND_IMM_WIDTH = 12;
			localparam OpFormatTypes_ADDR_SIGN_EXTENTION_WIDTH = 20;
			function automatic PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector;
				input reg detectRange;
				input reg [5:0] headPtr;
				input reg [5:0] tailPtr;
				input reg [5:0] opPtr;
				if (!detectRange)
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr >= headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr < headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr > tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else if ((opPtr < headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin
				stall = Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[1];
				clear = Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[0];
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_247
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						makeMSHRCanBeInvalid[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 185) + 136];
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_248
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							iqData[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 185) + 183-:101];
							flush[i] = PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector(Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeHeadPtr, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeTailPtr, iqData[i][51-:6]);
							memOpInfo[i] = iqData[i][100-:49];
							fuOpA[i] = (pipeReg[((1 - i) * 185) + 16] ? Core.bypassNetworkIF.memSrcRegDataOutA[i] : pipeReg[((1 - i) * 185) + 82-:33]);
							fuOpB[i] = (pipeReg[((1 - i) * 185) + 10] ? Core.bypassNetworkIF.memSrcRegDataOutB[i] : pipeReg[((1 - i) * 185) + 49-:33]);
							addrOut[i] = fuOpA[i][31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] + {{OpFormatTypes_ADDR_SIGN_EXTENTION_WIDTH {memOpInfo[i][38]}}, memOpInfo[i][38-:12]};
							phyAddrOut[i] = MemoryMapTypes_ToPhyAddrFromLogical(addrOut[i]);
							memMapType[i] = MemoryMapTypes_GetMemoryMapType(addrOut[i]);
							isUncachable[i] = MemoryMapTypes_IsPhyAddrUncachable(phyAddrOut[i]);
							Core.bypassNetworkIF.memCtrlIn[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 185) + 16-:12];
							regValid[i] = ((memOpInfo[i][42-:2] != 2'b00) || fuOpA[i][32]) && ((memOpInfo[i][40-:2] != 2'b00) || fuOpB[i][32]);
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_249
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcReadReq[i] = ((((!stall && !clear) && pipeReg[((1 - i) * 185) + 184]) && regValid[i]) && !flush[i]) && (memOpInfo[i][48-:3] == 3'b000);
							Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcReadAddr[i] = phyAddrOut[i];
							Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcReadUncachable[i] = isUncachable[i];
							Core.loadStoreUnitIF.makeMSHRCanBeInvalid[i] = makeMSHRCanBeInvalid[i];
						end
				end
				cacheFlushReq = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				if ((pipeReg[369] && (memOpInfo[0][48-:3] == 3'b101)) && memOpInfo[0][10]) begin
					cacheFlushReq = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					if (!Core.cacheFlushManagerIF.cacheFlushComplete)
						regValid[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				Core.cacheFlushManagerIF.cacheFlushReq = cacheFlushReq;
			end
			genvar i;
			reg isCSR;
			always @(*) begin
				isCSR = memOpInfo[0][48-:3] == 3'b100;
				Core.csrUnitIF.csrWE = pipeReg[369] && isCSR;
				Core.csrUnitIF.csrNumber = memOpInfo[0][38-:12];
				Core.csrUnitIF.csrWriteIn = (memOpInfo[0][16] ? memOpInfo[0][21-:5] : pipeReg[267-:33]);
				Core.csrUnitIF.csrCode = memOpInfo[0][15-:2];
			end
			reg [383:0] nextStage;
			localparam BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_LANE_BEGIN = BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH;
			localparam BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_250
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 192) + 190-:101] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 185) + 183-:101];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 192) + 191] = (((stall || clear) || Core.memExStageIF.rst) || flush[i] ? BasicTypes_FALSE : pipeReg[((1 - i) * 185) + 184]);
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 192) + 89] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 192) + 55-:32] = ((i == 0) && isCSR ? Core.csrUnitIF.csrReadOut : fuOpB[i][31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH]);
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 192) + 87-:32] = addrOut[i];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 192) + 88] = regValid[i];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 192) + 23-:2] = memMapType[i];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 192) + 21-:22] = phyAddrOut[i];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_251
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						if (makeMSHRCanBeInvalid[i])
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 192) + 143] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_252
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						nextStage[((1 - (i + BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_LANE_BEGIN)) * 192) + 143] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				Core.memExStageIF.nextStage = nextStage;
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : mtStage
			localparam BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_WIDTH = 22;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH = 20;
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM = 64;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM = BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM = 64;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM = 2;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 1;
			localparam OpFormatTypes_CSR_IMM_WIDTH = 5;
			reg [383:0] pipeReg;
			localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			reg [191:0] ldPipeReg [0:0];
			localparam BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			reg [191:0] stPipeReg [0:0];
			always @(posedge Core.mtStageIF.clk)
				if (Core.mtStageIF.rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_253
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						pipeReg[((1 - i) * 192) + 191] <= 1'sb0;
				end
				else if (!Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[1])
					pipeReg <= Core.memExStageIF.nextStage;
			reg stall;
			reg clear;
			reg lsuMakeMSHRCanBeInvalidByMemoryTagAccessStage [0:1];
			reg [0:0] mshrID;
			reg [100:0] ldIqData [0:0];
			reg [100:0] stIqData [0:0];
			localparam BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_LANE_BEGIN = BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH;
			always @(*) begin
				stall = Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[1];
				clear = Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[0];
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_254
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							ldPipeReg[i] = pipeReg[(1 - i) * 192+:192];
							ldIqData[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 192) + 190-:101];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_255
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							stPipeReg[i] = pipeReg[(1 - (i + BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_LANE_BEGIN)) * 192+:192];
							stIqData[i] = pipeReg[((1 - (i + BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_LANE_BEGIN)) * 192) + 190-:101];
						end
				end
			end
			reg isLoad [0:0];
			reg isCSR [0:0];
			reg isENV [0:0];
			reg ldUpdate [0:0];
			reg ldRegValid [0:0];
			reg ldFlush [0:0];
			reg isDiv [0:0];
			reg isMul [0:0];
			reg isFenceI [0:0];
			localparam BasicTypes_ADDR_WIDTH = 32;
			reg [151:0] ldNextStage [0:0];
			reg [100:0] ldRecordData [0:0];
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			function automatic OpFormatTypes_IsMisalignedAddress;
				input reg [31:0] addr;
				input reg [1:0] size;
				if ((size == 2'b00) || (size == 2'b11))
					OpFormatTypes_IsMisalignedAddress = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				else if (size == 2'b01)
					OpFormatTypes_IsMisalignedAddress = (addr[0:0] != 0 ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
				else if (size == 2'b10)
					OpFormatTypes_IsMisalignedAddress = (addr[1:0] != 0 ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
				else
					OpFormatTypes_IsMisalignedAddress = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			endfunction
			function automatic PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector;
				input reg detectRange;
				input reg [5:0] headPtr;
				input reg [5:0] tailPtr;
				input reg [5:0] opPtr;
				if (!detectRange)
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr >= headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr < headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr > tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else if ((opPtr < headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_256
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; i = i + 1)
						lsuMakeMSHRCanBeInvalidByMemoryTagAccessStage[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_257
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							ldFlush[i] = PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector(Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeHeadPtr, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeTailPtr, ldIqData[i][51-:6]);
							ldUpdate[i] = ((ldPipeReg[i][191] && !stall) && !clear) && !ldFlush[i];
							isLoad[i] = ldIqData[i][100-:3] == 3'b000;
							isCSR[i] = ldIqData[i][100-:3] == 3'b100;
							isENV[i] = ldIqData[i][100-:3] == 3'b110;
							isDiv[i] = ldIqData[i][100-:3] == 3'b011;
							isMul[i] = ldIqData[i][100-:3] == 3'b010;
							isFenceI[i] = (ldIqData[i][100-:3] == 3'b101) && ldIqData[i][62];
							Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executeLoad[i] = ldUpdate[i] && isLoad[i];
							Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadAddr[i] = ldPipeReg[i][21-:22];
							Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadPC[i] = ldIqData[i][18-:19];
							Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadMemAccessMode[i] = ldIqData[i][76-:3];
							Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedStoreQueuePtrByLoad[i] = ldIqData[i][57-:4];
							Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadQueuePtrByLoad[i] = ldIqData[i][61-:4];
							ldRecordData[i][51-:6] = ldPipeReg[i][141-:6];
							ldRecordData[i][37-:12] = ldPipeReg[i][127-:12];
							ldRecordData[i][25-:7] = ldPipeReg[i][115-:7];
							ldRecordData[i][18-:19] = ldPipeReg[i][108-:19];
							ldRecordData[i][100-:49] = ldPipeReg[i][190-:49];
							ldRecordData[i][41-:4] = ldIqData[i][41-:4];
							ldRecordData[i][45-:4] = ldIqData[i][45-:4];
							if (ldPipeReg[i][143]) begin
								ldRecordData[i][53] = ldPipeReg[i][143];
								ldRecordData[i][52-:CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = ldPipeReg[i][142-:CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
							end
							else if (i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH) begin
								ldRecordData[i][53] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.loadHasAllocatedMSHR[i];
								if (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.loadHasAllocatedMSHR[i])
									ldRecordData[i][52-:CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.loadMSHRID[i];
								else if (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.mshrAddrHit[i])
									ldRecordData[i][52-:CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.mshrAddrHitMSHRID[i];
								else
									ldRecordData[i][52-:CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = ldPipeReg[i][142-:CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
							end
							else begin
								ldRecordData[i][53] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
								ldRecordData[i][52-:CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
							end
							Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcReadCancelFromMT_Stage[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							if (isLoad[i]) begin
								if (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.storeLoadForwarded[i]) begin
									Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcReadCancelFromMT_Stage[i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
									ldRegValid[i] = (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.forwardMiss[i] ? BasicTypes_FALSE : ldPipeReg[i][88]);
								end
								else if (ldRecordData[i][53])
									ldRegValid[i] = (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.mshrReadHit[i] ? ldPipeReg[i][88] : BasicTypes_FALSE);
								else if (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.mshrReadHit[i])
									ldRegValid[i] = ldPipeReg[i][88];
								else
									ldRegValid[i] = (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcReadHit[i] ? ldPipeReg[i][88] : BasicTypes_FALSE);
							end
							else if (ldRecordData[i][53])
								ldRegValid[i] = (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.mshrReadHit[i] ? ldPipeReg[i][88] : BasicTypes_FALSE);
							else
								ldRegValid[i] = ldPipeReg[i][88];
							Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadRegValid[i] = ldRegValid[i];
							ldNextStage[i][92] = ldRegValid[i];
							ldNextStage[i][87-:32] = ldPipeReg[i][87-:32];
							ldNextStage[i][55-:2] = ldPipeReg[i][23-:2];
							ldNextStage[i][53-:22] = ldPipeReg[i][21-:22];
							ldNextStage[i][31-:32] = ldPipeReg[i][55-:32];
							ldNextStage[i][149] = isLoad[i];
							ldNextStage[i][150] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							ldNextStage[i][148] = isCSR[i];
							ldNextStage[i][147] = isDiv[i];
							ldNextStage[i][146] = isMul[i];
							ldNextStage[i][145-:7] = ldIqData[i][25-:7];
							ldNextStage[i][138-:6] = ldIqData[i][51-:6];
							ldNextStage[i][132-:4] = ldIqData[i][45-:4];
							ldNextStage[i][128-:4] = ldIqData[i][41-:4];
							ldNextStage[i][124-:32] = ldIqData[i][18-:19];
							if (!ldUpdate[i] || (ldUpdate[i] && !ldRegValid[i]))
								ldNextStage[i][91-:4] = 4'b0000;
							else if (isLoad[i]) begin
								if (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.storeLoadForwarded[i]) begin
									ldNextStage[i][91-:4] = (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.forwardMiss[i] ? 4'b0010 : 4'b0001);
									if (ldRecordData[i][53])
										lsuMakeMSHRCanBeInvalidByMemoryTagAccessStage[ldRecordData[i][52-:CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
								end
								else if (ldRecordData[i][53])
									ldNextStage[i][91-:4] = (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.mshrReadHit[i] ? 4'b0001 : 4'b0010);
								else if (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.mshrReadHit[i])
									ldNextStage[i][91-:4] = 4'b0001;
								else
									ldNextStage[i][91-:4] = (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcReadHit[i] ? 4'b0001 : 4'b0010);
							end
							else if (ldRecordData[i][53])
								ldNextStage[i][91-:4] = (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.mshrReadHit[i] ? 4'b0001 : 4'b0010);
							else if (isENV[i])
								case (ldIqData[i][65-:3])
									3'b001: ldNextStage[i][91-:4] = 4'b0101;
									3'b000: ldNextStage[i][91-:4] = 4'b0100;
									3'b010: ldNextStage[i][91-:4] = 4'b0110;
									3'b011: ldNextStage[i][91-:4] = 4'b1100;
									3'b100: ldNextStage[i][91-:4] = 4'b1101;
									default: ldNextStage[i][91-:4] = 4'b0101;
								endcase
							else if (isFenceI[i])
								ldNextStage[i][91-:4] = 4'b0011;
							else
								ldNextStage[i][91-:4] = 4'b0001;
							if (|{ldNextStage[i][91-:4] == 4'b0001, ldNextStage[i][91-:4] == 4'b0011})
								if (isLoad[i])
									if (ldPipeReg[i][23-:2] == 2'b10)
										ldNextStage[i][91-:4] = 4'b1001;
									else if (OpFormatTypes_IsMisalignedAddress(ldPipeReg[i][87-:32], ldIqData[i][75-:2]))
										ldNextStage[i][91-:4] = 4'b1000;
							ldNextStage[i][151] = (((stall || clear) || Core.mtStageIF.rst) || ldFlush[i] ? BasicTypes_FALSE : ldPipeReg[i][191]);
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_258
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; i = i + 1)
						Core.loadStoreUnitIF.makeMSHRCanBeInvalidByMemoryTagAccessStage[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_259
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							if (ldPipeReg[i][191] && isLoad[i]) begin
								if (ldFlush[i])
									lsuMakeMSHRCanBeInvalidByMemoryTagAccessStage[i] = ldRecordData[i][53];
							end
							else
								lsuMakeMSHRCanBeInvalidByMemoryTagAccessStage[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							mshrID = ldRecordData[i][52-:CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
							if (lsuMakeMSHRCanBeInvalidByMemoryTagAccessStage[i] && isLoad[i])
								Core.loadStoreUnitIF.makeMSHRCanBeInvalidByMemoryTagAccessStage[mshrID] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						end
				end
			end
			reg isStore [0:0];
			reg stUpdate [0:0];
			reg stRegValid [0:0];
			reg stFlush [0:0];
			reg [151:0] stNextStage [0:0];
			reg [100:0] stRecordData [0:0];
			reg [0:0] memAccessOrderViolation;
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_260
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
					begin
						memAccessOrderViolation[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						stFlush[i] = PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector(Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeHeadPtr, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeTailPtr, stIqData[i][51-:6]);
						stUpdate[i] = ((stPipeReg[i][191] && !stall) && !clear) && !stFlush[i];
						isStore[i] = stIqData[i][100-:3] == 3'b001;
						Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executeStore[i] = stUpdate[i] && isStore[i];
						Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedStoreData[i] = stPipeReg[i][55-:32];
						Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedStoreVectorData[i] = 1'sb0;
						Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedStoreAddr[i] = stPipeReg[i][21-:22];
						Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedStoreCondEnabled[i] = stPipeReg[i][89];
						Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedStoreRegValid[i] = stPipeReg[i][88];
						Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedStoreMemAccessMode[i] = stIqData[i][76-:3];
						Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadQueuePtrByStore[i] = stIqData[i][61-:4];
						Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedStoreQueuePtrByStore[i] = stIqData[i][57-:4];
						stRecordData[i][51-:6] = stPipeReg[i][141-:6];
						stRecordData[i][37-:12] = stPipeReg[i][127-:12];
						stRecordData[i][25-:7] = stPipeReg[i][115-:7];
						stRecordData[i][18-:19] = stPipeReg[i][108-:19];
						stRecordData[i][41-:4] = stIqData[i][41-:4];
						stRecordData[i][45-:4] = stIqData[i][45-:4];
						stRecordData[i][100-:49] = stPipeReg[i][190-:49];
						stRecordData[i][53] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						stRecordData[i][52-:CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
						stRegValid[i] = stPipeReg[i][88];
						stNextStage[i][92] = stRegValid[i];
						stNextStage[i][87-:32] = stPipeReg[i][87-:32];
						stNextStage[i][55-:2] = stPipeReg[i][23-:2];
						stNextStage[i][53-:22] = stPipeReg[i][21-:22];
						stNextStage[i][31-:32] = stPipeReg[i][55-:32];
						stNextStage[i][149] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						stNextStage[i][150] = isStore[i];
						stNextStage[i][148] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						stNextStage[i][147] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						stNextStage[i][146] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						stNextStage[i][145-:7] = stIqData[i][25-:7];
						stNextStage[i][138-:6] = stIqData[i][51-:6];
						stNextStage[i][132-:4] = stIqData[i][45-:4];
						stNextStage[i][128-:4] = stIqData[i][41-:4];
						stNextStage[i][124-:32] = stIqData[i][18-:19];
						if (!stUpdate[i] || (stUpdate[i] && !stRegValid[i]))
							stNextStage[i][91-:4] = 4'b0000;
						else if (isStore[i] && Core.loadStoreUnitIF.conflict[i]) begin
							stNextStage[i][91-:4] = 4'b0011;
							memAccessOrderViolation[i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						end
						else
							stNextStage[i][91-:4] = 4'b0001;
						if (|{stNextStage[i][91-:4] == 4'b0001, stNextStage[i][91-:4] == 4'b0011})
							if (isStore[i])
								if (stPipeReg[i][23-:2] == 2'b10)
									stNextStage[i][91-:4] = 4'b1011;
								else if (OpFormatTypes_IsMisalignedAddress(stPipeReg[i][87-:32], stIqData[i][75-:2]))
									stNextStage[i][91-:4] = 4'b1010;
						stNextStage[i][151] = (((stall || clear) || Core.mtStageIF.rst) || stFlush[i] ? BasicTypes_FALSE : stPipeReg[i][191]);
					end
			end
			reg flush [0:1];
			reg [303:0] nextStage;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_SUBSET_BIT_WIDTH = 5;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM = 8;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 3;
			function automatic [4:0] ToIndexSubsetPartFromFullAddrInMTStage;
				input reg [31:0] addr;
				ToIndexSubsetPartFromFullAddrInMTStage = addr[7:CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.memAccessOrderViolation = memAccessOrderViolation;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_261
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							Core.schedulerIF.memRecordEntry[i] = ldUpdate[i] && !ldRegValid[i];
							Core.schedulerIF.memRecordData[i] = ldRecordData[i];
							Core.schedulerIF.memRecordAddrHit[i] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.mshrAddrHit[i];
							Core.schedulerIF.memRecordAddrSubset[i] = ToIndexSubsetPartFromFullAddrInMTStage(ldPipeReg[i][87-:32]);
							nextStage[(1 - i) * 152+:152] = ldNextStage[i];
							flush[i] = ldFlush[i];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_262
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							Core.schedulerIF.memRecordEntry[i + BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_LANE_BEGIN] = stUpdate[i] && !stRegValid[i];
							Core.schedulerIF.memRecordData[i + BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_LANE_BEGIN] = stRecordData[i];
							Core.schedulerIF.memRecordAddrHit[i + BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_LANE_BEGIN] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							Core.schedulerIF.memRecordAddrSubset[i + BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_LANE_BEGIN] = ToIndexSubsetPartFromFullAddrInMTStage(stPipeReg[i][87-:32]);
							nextStage[(1 - (i + BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_LANE_BEGIN)) * 152+:152] = stNextStage[i];
							flush[i + BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_LANE_BEGIN] = stFlush[i];
						end
				end
				Core.mtStageIF.nextStage = nextStage;
			end
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_263
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
					Core.hwCounterIF.loadMiss[i] = ((ldUpdate[i] && isLoad[i]) && ldPipeReg[i][88]) && !Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcReadHit[i];
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : maStage
			localparam BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_ADDR_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM = 64;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM = BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM;
			localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_WIDTH = 22;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH = 20;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM = 64;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			reg [303:0] pipeReg;
			always @(posedge Core.maStageIF.clk)
				if (Core.maStageIF.rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_264
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						pipeReg[((1 - i) * 152) + 151] <= 1'sb0;
				end
				else if (!Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[1])
					pipeReg <= Core.mtStageIF.nextStage;
			reg isStore [0:1];
			reg isLoad [0:1];
			reg isCSR [0:1];
			reg isDiv [0:1];
			reg isMul [0:1];
			reg valid [0:1];
			reg update [0:1];
			reg regValid [0:1];
			reg stall;
			reg clear;
			reg flush [0:1];
			reg [247:0] nextStage;
			reg [32:0] dataOut [0:1];
			localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			reg [32:0] ldDataOut [0:0];
			localparam BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			reg [32:0] stDataOut [0:0];
			localparam BasicTypes_VEC_WIDTH = 128;
			wire [128:0] vecDataOut [0:1];
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_LANE_BEGIN = BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			function automatic PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector;
				input reg detectRange;
				input reg [5:0] headPtr;
				input reg [5:0] tailPtr;
				input reg [5:0] opPtr;
				if (!detectRange)
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr >= headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else if (detectRange && (tailPtr < headPtr)) begin
					if ((opPtr >= headPtr) && (opPtr > tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else if ((opPtr < headPtr) && (opPtr < tailPtr))
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					else
						PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else
					PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin
				stall = Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[1];
				clear = Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[0];
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_265
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							valid[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 152) + 151];
							flush[i] = PipelineTypes_SelectiveFlushDetector(Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeHeadPtr, Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeTailPtr, pipeReg[((1 - i) * 152) + 138-:6]);
							isStore[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 152) + 150];
							isLoad[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 152) + 149];
							isCSR[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 152) + 148];
							isDiv[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 152) + 147];
							isMul[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 152) + 146];
							update[i] = ((pipeReg[((1 - i) * 152) + 151] && !stall) && !clear) && !flush[i];
							regValid[i] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 152) + 92];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_266
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							if (i == 0)
								Core.ioUnitIF.ioReadAddrIn = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 152) + 53-:22];
							if (isLoad[i]) begin
								if ((i == 0) && (pipeReg[((1 - i) * 152) + 55-:2] == 2'b01))
									ldDataOut[i][31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] = Core.ioUnitIF.ioReadDataOut;
								else
									ldDataOut[i][31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadData[i * 32+:32];
							end
							else if (isCSR[i])
								ldDataOut[i][31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 152) + 31-:32];
							else
								ldDataOut[i][31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 152) + 87-:32];
							ldDataOut[i][32] = regValid[i];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_267
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							stDataOut[i][31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
							stDataOut[i][32] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_268
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						dataOut[i] = ldDataOut[i];
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_269
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						dataOut[i + BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_LANE_BEGIN] = stDataOut[i];
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_270
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_MEM_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 124) + 32-:33] = dataOut[i];
							Core.bypassNetworkIF.memDstRegDataOut[i] = dataOut[i];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 124) + 44-:7] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 152) + 145-:7];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 124) + 122-:6] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 152) + 138-:6];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 124) + 116-:4] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 152) + 132-:4];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 124) + 112-:4] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 152) + 128-:4];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 124) + 37-:4] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 152) + 91-:4];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 124) + 108-:32] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 152) + 124-:32];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 124) + 76-:32] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 152) + 87-:32];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 124) + 33] = pipeReg[((1 - i) * 152) + 150];
							nextStage[((1 - i) * 124) + 123] = (((stall || clear) || Core.maStageIF.rst) || flush[i] ? BasicTypes_FALSE : pipeReg[((1 - i) * 152) + 151]);
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_271
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						;
				end
				Core.maStageIF.nextStage = nextStage;
			end
			genvar i;
		end
		if (1) begin : loadStoreUnit
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM = 8;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 3;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BIT_WIDTH = 64;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_WIDTH = 22;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH = 20;
			function automatic [31:0] ShiftCacheLineData;
				input reg [63:0] srcLine;
				input reg [21:0] addr;
				reg [31:0] data;
				begin
					data = srcLine >> (addr[2:0] * 8);
					ShiftCacheLineData = data;
				end
			endfunction
			localparam BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH = 8;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_BYTE_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_BYTE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 2;
			function automatic [31:0] ShiftForwardedData;
				input reg [31:0] srcLine;
				input reg [21:0] addr;
				reg [31:0] data;
				begin
					data = srcLine >> (addr[1:0] * 8);
					ShiftForwardedData = data;
				end
			endfunction
			function automatic [31:0] ExtendLoadData;
				input reg [31:0] loadData;
				input reg [2:0] mode;
				case (mode[1-:2])
					2'b00: ExtendLoadData = (mode[2] ? {{24 {loadData[7]}}, loadData[7:0]} : {{24 {1'b0}}, loadData[7:0]});
					2'b01: ExtendLoadData = (mode[2] ? {{16 {loadData[15]}}, loadData[15:0]} : {{16 {1'b0}}, loadData[15:0]});
					default: ExtendLoadData = loadData;
				endcase
			endfunction
			localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			reg storeLoadForwardedReg [0:0];
			reg [31:0] forwardedLoadDataReg [0:0];
			reg mshrReadHitReg [0:0];
			reg [63:0] mshrReadDataReg [0:0];
			localparam BasicTypes_ADDR_WIDTH = 32;
			reg [31:0] loadAddrReg [0:0];
			reg [2:0] loadMemAccessSizeReg [0:0];
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			always @(posedge Core.loadStoreUnitIF.clk) begin : sv2v_autoblock_272
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
					if (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.rst) begin
						storeLoadForwardedReg[i] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
						forwardedLoadDataReg[i] <= 1'sb0;
						mshrReadHitReg[i] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
						mshrReadDataReg[i] <= 1'sb0;
						loadAddrReg[i] <= 1'sb0;
						loadMemAccessSizeReg[i] <= 2'b00;
					end
					else if (!Core.ctrlIF.backEnd[1]) begin
						storeLoadForwardedReg[i] <= Core.loadStoreUnitIF.storeLoadForwarded[i];
						forwardedLoadDataReg[i] <= Core.loadStoreUnitIF.forwardedLoadData[i * 32+:32];
						mshrReadHitReg[i] <= Core.loadStoreUnitIF.mshrReadHit[i];
						mshrReadDataReg[i] <= Core.loadStoreUnitIF.mshrReadData[i];
						loadAddrReg[i] <= Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadAddr[i];
						loadMemAccessSizeReg[i] <= Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadMemAccessMode[i];
					end
			end
			reg [31:0] loadLSQ_BlockData [0:0];
			reg [31:0] shiftedLoadData [0:0];
			reg [31:0] extendedLoadData [0:0];
			reg [31:0] executedLoadData;
			localparam DebugTypes_HARDWARE_COUNTER_TAG = 5'b11111;
			localparam DebugTypes_HARDWARE_COUNTER_TAG_BIT_SIZE = 5;
			localparam DebugTypes_HARDWARE_COUNTER_TAG_MSB = 31;
			localparam DebugTypes_HARDWARE_COUNTER_TAG_LSB = 27;
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_BYTE_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_BYTE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 2;
			localparam DebugTypes_HARDWARE_COUNTER_TYPE_LSB = BasicTypes_DATA_BYTE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE;
			localparam DebugTypes_HARDWARE_COUNTER_TYPE_BIT_SIZE = 3;
			localparam DebugTypes_HARDWARE_COUNTER_TYPE_MSB = 4;
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_273
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							executedLoadData[i * BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
							Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadVectorData[i] = 0;
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_274
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							loadLSQ_BlockData[i] = (storeLoadForwardedReg[i] ? forwardedLoadDataReg[i] : (mshrReadHitReg[i] ? mshrReadDataReg[i] : Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcReadData[i]));
							Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadVectorData[i] = loadLSQ_BlockData[i];
							shiftedLoadData[i] = (storeLoadForwardedReg[i] ? ShiftForwardedData(forwardedLoadDataReg[i], loadAddrReg[i]) : (mshrReadHitReg[i] ? ShiftCacheLineData(mshrReadDataReg[i], loadAddrReg[i]) : ShiftCacheLineData(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcReadData[i], loadAddrReg[i])));
							extendedLoadData[i] = ExtendLoadData(shiftedLoadData[i], loadMemAccessSizeReg[i]);
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_275
								reg [2:0] sv2v_tmp_cast;
								sv2v_tmp_cast = loadAddrReg[i][DebugTypes_HARDWARE_COUNTER_TYPE_MSB:DebugTypes_HARDWARE_COUNTER_TYPE_LSB];
								Core.hwCounterIF.hardwareCounterType[i] = sv2v_tmp_cast;
							end
							if (loadAddrReg[i][DebugTypes_HARDWARE_COUNTER_TAG_MSB:DebugTypes_HARDWARE_COUNTER_TAG_LSB] == DebugTypes_HARDWARE_COUNTER_TAG)
								executedLoadData[i * BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] = Core.hwCounterIF.hardwareCounterData[i];
							else
								executedLoadData[i * BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] = extendedLoadData[i];
						end
				end
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadData = executedLoadData;
			end
			always @(*) Core.loadStoreUnitIF.allocatable = (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.loadQueueAllocatable && Core.loadStoreUnitIF.storeQueueAllocatable) && !Core.loadStoreUnitIF.busyInRecovery;
		end
		if (1) begin : loadQueue
			reg reset;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			wire [3:0] headPtr;
			wire [3:0] tailPtr;
			reg push;
			localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;
			reg [1:0] pushCount;
			wire [LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH:0] curCount;
			localparam BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH = 2;
			SetTailMultiWidthQueuePointer #(
				.SIZE(LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM),
				.INITIAL_HEAD_PTR(0),
				.INITIAL_TAIL_PTR(0),
				.INITIAL_COUNT(0),
				.PUSH_WIDTH(BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH),
				.POP_WIDTH(BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH)
			) loadQueuePointer(
				.clk(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.clk),
				.rst(reset),
				.pop(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.releaseLoadQueue),
				.popCount(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.releaseLoadQueueEntryNum),
				.push(push),
				.pushCount(pushCount),
				.setTail(Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase),
				.setTailPtr(Core.recoveryManagerIF.loadQueueRecoveryTailPtr),
				.count(curCount),
				.headPtr(headPtr),
				.tailPtr(tailPtr)
			);
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			always @(*) begin
				pushCount = 0;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_276
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							if ((tailPtr + pushCount) < LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM)
								Core.loadStoreUnitIF.allocatedLoadQueuePtr[i] = tailPtr + pushCount;
							else
								Core.loadStoreUnitIF.allocatedLoadQueuePtr[i] = (tailPtr + pushCount) - LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM;
							pushCount = pushCount + Core.loadStoreUnitIF.allocateLoadQueue[i];
						end
				end
				push = pushCount > 0;
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.loadQueueAllocatable = (curCount <= 13 ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.loadQueueHeadPtr = headPtr;
			end
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_WIDTH = 22;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ADDR_MSB = MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_WIDTH;
			localparam BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH = 8;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_BYTE_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_BYTE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_WORD_WIDTH = 1;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			reg [41:0] loadQueue [0:15];
			localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			reg [3:0] executedLoadQueuePtrByLoad [0:0];
			reg [19:0] executedLoadAddr [0:0];
			reg [0:0] executedLoadWordRE [0:0];
			reg executedLoadRegValid [0:0];
			always @(posedge Core.loadStoreUnitIF.clk)
				if (reset) begin : sv2v_autoblock_277
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)
						loadQueue[i][40] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else begin
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_278
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							if (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executeLoad[i]) begin
								loadQueue[executedLoadQueuePtrByLoad[i]][41] <= executedLoadRegValid[i];
								loadQueue[executedLoadQueuePtrByLoad[i]][40] <= BasicTypes_TRUE;
								loadQueue[executedLoadQueuePtrByLoad[i]][39-:20] <= executedLoadAddr[i];
								loadQueue[executedLoadQueuePtrByLoad[i]][19-:1] <= executedLoadWordRE[i];
								loadQueue[executedLoadQueuePtrByLoad[i]][18-:MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH] <= Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadPC[i];
							end
					end
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_279
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							if (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.allocateLoadQueue[i])
								loadQueue[Core.loadStoreUnitIF.allocatedLoadQueuePtr[i]][40] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
					end
				end
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH = 20;
			function automatic [19:0] LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToBlockAddr;
				input reg [21:0] addr;
				LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToBlockAddr = addr[21:LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_BYTE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE];
			endfunction
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_BYTE_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_BYTE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 2;
			function automatic signed [31:0] LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_SelectBits;
				input reg signed [31:0] data;
				input reg signed [31:0] offset;
				input reg signed [31:0] width;
				reg signed [31:0] ret;
				begin
					ret = 0;
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_280
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < width; i = i + 1)
							ret[i] = data[i + offset];
					end
					LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_SelectBits = ret;
				end
			endfunction
			function automatic [0:0] LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToBlockWordEnable;
				input reg [21:0] addr;
				input reg [2:0] mode;
				reg [0:0] wordEnable;
				begin
					case (mode[1-:2])
						2'b11: wordEnable = 1'sb1;
						default: wordEnable = 1;
					endcase
					LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToBlockWordEnable = wordEnable << LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_SelectBits(addr, BasicTypes_DATA_BYTE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE, 0);
				end
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_281
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
					begin
						executedLoadRegValid[i] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadRegValid[i];
						executedLoadQueuePtrByLoad[i] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadQueuePtrByLoad[i];
						executedLoadAddr[i] = LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToBlockAddr(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadAddr[i]);
						executedLoadWordRE[i] = LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToBlockWordEnable(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadAddr[i], Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadMemAccessMode[i]);
					end
			end
			localparam BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			reg [3:0] executedLoadQueuePtrByStore [0:0];
			reg [15:0] addrMatch [0:0];
			wire [3:0] pickedPtr [0:0];
			wire picked [0:0];
			genvar i;
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1) begin : genblk1
				CircularRangePicker #(.ENTRY_NUM(LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM)) picker(
					.headPtr(executedLoadQueuePtrByStore[i]),
					.tailPtr(tailPtr),
					.request(addrMatch[i]),
					.grantPtr(pickedPtr[i]),
					.picked(picked[i])
				);
			end
			reg violation [0:0];
			reg [19:0] executedStoreAddr [0:0];
			reg [0:0] executedStoreWordWE [0:0];
			reg [18:0] conflictLoadPC;
			function automatic [LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH:0] LoadQueuePtrToAge;
				input reg [3:0] ptr;
				input reg [3:0] head;
				reg [LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH:0] age;
				begin
					age = ptr;
					if (ptr < head)
						LoadQueuePtrToAge = age + LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM;
					else
						LoadQueuePtrToAge = age;
				end
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_282
				reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;
				_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
				reset = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.rst;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_283
					reg signed [31:0] si;
					for (si = 0; si < BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH; si = si + 1)
						begin
							violation[si] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							conflictLoadPC[si * MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH+:MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
							executedStoreAddr[si] = LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToBlockAddr(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedStoreAddr[si]);
							executedStoreWordWE[si] = LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToBlockWordEnable(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedStoreAddr[si], Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedStoreMemAccessMode[si]);
							executedLoadQueuePtrByStore[si] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadQueuePtrByStore[si];
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_284
					reg signed [31:0] si;
					for (si = 0; si < BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH; si = si + 1)
						begin : sv2v_autoblock_285
							reg signed [31:0] lqe;
							for (lqe = 0; lqe < LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; lqe = lqe + 1)
								addrMatch[si][lqe] = ((loadQueue[lqe][40] && loadQueue[lqe][41]) && (loadQueue[lqe][39-:20] == executedStoreAddr[si])) && ((loadQueue[lqe][19-:1] & executedStoreWordWE[si]) != 1'b0);
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_286
					reg signed [31:0] si;
					for (si = 0; si < BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH; si = si + 1)
						if (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executeStore[si]) begin
							violation[si] = picked[si];
							if (picked[si] && (pickedPtr[si] < LoadStoreUnitTypes_LOAD_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM))
								conflictLoadPC[si * MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH+:MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH] = loadQueue[pickedPtr[si]][18-:MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH];
						end
						else
							violation[si] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_287
					reg signed [31:0] si;
					for (si = 0; si < BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH; si = si + 1)
						if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
							_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_288
								reg signed [31:0] li;
								begin : sv2v_autoblock_289
									reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump_1;
									_sv2v_jump_1 = _sv2v_jump;
									for (li = 0; li < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; li = li + 1)
										if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
											_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
											if (!Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executeLoad[li])
												_sv2v_jump = 2'b01;
											if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00) begin
												if (executedLoadAddr[li] != executedStoreAddr[si])
													_sv2v_jump = 2'b01;
												if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00) begin
													if ((executedLoadWordRE[li] & executedStoreWordWE[si]) == 1'b0)
														_sv2v_jump = 2'b01;
													if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00)
														if (LoadQueuePtrToAge(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadQueuePtrByLoad[li], headPtr) >= LoadQueuePtrToAge(executedLoadQueuePtrByStore[si], headPtr)) begin
															violation[si] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
															conflictLoadPC[si * MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH+:MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadPC[li];
														end
												end
											end
										end
									if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
										_sv2v_jump = _sv2v_jump_1;
								end
							end
						end
					if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
						_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
				end
				if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00) begin
					Core.loadStoreUnitIF.conflictLoadPC = conflictLoadPC;
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_290
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							Core.loadStoreUnitIF.conflict[i] = violation[i];
					end
				end
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : storeQueue
			localparam BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH = 8;
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_BYTE_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_BYTE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 2;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_WIDTH = 22;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH = 20;
			task automatic GenerateStoreData;
				output reg [31:0] dataOut;
				input reg [31:0] dataIn;
				input reg [31:0] blockDataIn;
				input reg [21:0] addr;
				input reg [2:0] mode;
				begin
					dataOut = dataIn;
					dataOut = dataOut << (addr[1:0] * BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH);
				end
			endtask
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ADDR_MSB = MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_WIDTH;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_WORD_WIDTH = 1;
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_BYTE_WIDTH = 4;
			reg [26:0] headAddrEntry;
			reg [37:0] headDataEntry;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			reg [3:0] releasedStoreQueuePtr;
			wire [3:0] headPtr;
			wire [3:0] tailPtr;
			localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;
			reg [1:0] pushCount;
			wire [LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH:0] curCount;
			reg push;
			localparam BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH = 2;
			SetTailMultiWidthQueuePointer #(
				.SIZE(LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM),
				.INITIAL_HEAD_PTR(0),
				.INITIAL_TAIL_PTR(0),
				.INITIAL_COUNT(0),
				.PUSH_WIDTH(BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH),
				.POP_WIDTH(BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH)
			) storeQueuePointer(
				.clk(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.clk),
				.rst(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.rst),
				.pop(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.releaseStoreQueueHead),
				.popCount(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.releaseStoreQueueHeadEntryNum),
				.push(push),
				.pushCount(pushCount),
				.setTail(Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase),
				.setTailPtr(Core.recoveryManagerIF.storeQueueRecoveryTailPtr),
				.count(curCount),
				.headPtr(headPtr),
				.tailPtr(tailPtr)
			);
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			always @(*) begin
				pushCount = 0;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_291
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							if ((tailPtr + pushCount) < LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM)
								Core.loadStoreUnitIF.allocatedStoreQueuePtr[i] = tailPtr + pushCount;
							else
								Core.loadStoreUnitIF.allocatedStoreQueuePtr[i] = (tailPtr + pushCount) - LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM;
							pushCount = pushCount + Core.loadStoreUnitIF.allocateStoreQueue[i];
						end
				end
				push = pushCount > 0;
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.storeQueueCount = curCount;
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.storeQueueAllocatable = (curCount <= 13 ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.storeQueueEmpty = curCount == 0;
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.storeQueueHeadPtr = headPtr;
			end
			reg [26:0] storeQueue [15:0];
			localparam BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			reg executeStore [0:0];
			reg [19:0] executedStoreAddr [0:0];
			reg [0:0] executedStoreWordWE [0:0];
			reg [3:0] executedStoreByteWE [0:0];
			reg executedStoreCondEnabled [0:0];
			reg executedStoreRegValid [0:0];
			reg [3:0] executedStoreQueuePtrByStore;
			always @(posedge Core.loadStoreUnitIF.clk)
				if (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_292
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)
						begin
							storeQueue[i][25] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
							storeQueue[i][24-:20] <= 1'sb0;
							storeQueue[i][4-:1] <= 1'sb0;
							storeQueue[i][3-:BasicTypes_DATA_BYTE_WIDTH] <= 1'sb0;
						end
				end
				else begin
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_293
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							if (executeStore[i]) begin
								storeQueue[executedStoreQueuePtrByStore[i * LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]][26] <= executedStoreRegValid[i];
								storeQueue[executedStoreQueuePtrByStore[i * LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]][25] <= executedStoreCondEnabled[i];
								storeQueue[executedStoreQueuePtrByStore[i * LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]][24-:20] <= executedStoreAddr[i];
								storeQueue[executedStoreQueuePtrByStore[i * LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]][4-:1] <= executedStoreWordWE[i];
								storeQueue[executedStoreQueuePtrByStore[i * LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]][3-:BasicTypes_DATA_BYTE_WIDTH] <= executedStoreByteWE[i];
							end
					end
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_294
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							if (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.allocateStoreQueue[i])
								storeQueue[Core.loadStoreUnitIF.allocatedStoreQueuePtr[i]][25] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
					end
				end
			function automatic [19:0] LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToBlockAddr;
				input reg [21:0] addr;
				LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToBlockAddr = addr[21:LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_BYTE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE];
			endfunction
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_BYTE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 2;
			function automatic signed [31:0] LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_SelectBits;
				input reg signed [31:0] data;
				input reg signed [31:0] offset;
				input reg signed [31:0] width;
				reg signed [31:0] ret;
				begin
					ret = 0;
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_295
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < width; i = i + 1)
							ret[i] = data[i + offset];
					end
					LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_SelectBits = ret;
				end
			endfunction
			function automatic [0:0] LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToBlockWordEnable;
				input reg [21:0] addr;
				input reg [2:0] mode;
				reg [0:0] wordEnable;
				begin
					case (mode[1-:2])
						2'b11: wordEnable = 1'sb1;
						default: wordEnable = 1;
					endcase
					LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToBlockWordEnable = wordEnable << LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_SelectBits(addr, BasicTypes_DATA_BYTE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE, 0);
				end
			endfunction
			function automatic [3:0] LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToWordByteEnable;
				input reg [21:0] addr;
				input reg [2:0] mode;
				reg [3:0] byteEnable;
				begin
					case (mode[1-:2])
						2'b00: byteEnable = 'b1;
						2'b01: byteEnable = 'b11;
						default: byteEnable = 1'sb1;
					endcase
					LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToWordByteEnable = byteEnable << addr[1:0];
				end
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_296
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
					begin
						executeStore[i] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executeStore[i];
						executedStoreAddr[i] = LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToBlockAddr(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedStoreAddr[i]);
						executedStoreCondEnabled[i] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedStoreCondEnabled[i];
						executedStoreRegValid[i] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedStoreRegValid[i];
						executedStoreQueuePtrByStore[i * LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedStoreQueuePtrByStore[i];
						executedStoreWordWE[i] = LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToBlockWordEnable(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedStoreAddr[i], Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedStoreMemAccessMode[i]);
						executedStoreByteWE[i] = LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToWordByteEnable(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedStoreAddr[i], Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedStoreMemAccessMode[i]);
					end
			end
			localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			reg [37:0] forwardedDataEntry [0:0];
			reg [0:0] executedLoadWordRE [0:0];
			reg [3:0] executedLoadByteRE [0:0];
			reg [15:0] addrMatch [0:0];
			wire [3:0] pickedPtr [0:0];
			wire picked [0:0];
			reg [3:0] executedStoreQueuePtrByLoad [0:0];
			genvar i;
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1) begin : genblk1
				CircularRangePicker #(.ENTRY_NUM(LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM)) picker(
					.headPtr(headPtr),
					.tailPtr(executedStoreQueuePtrByLoad[i]),
					.request(addrMatch[i]),
					.grantPtr(pickedPtr[i]),
					.picked(picked[i])
				);
			end
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_297
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
					executedStoreQueuePtrByLoad[i] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedStoreQueuePtrByLoad[i];
			end
			reg [7:0] sqReadPtr;
			wire [75:0] sqReadData;
			reg [0:0] sqWE;
			reg [37:0] sqWriteData;
			wire [31:0] sqWriteStoreData [0:0];
			DistributedMultiPortRAM #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM),
				.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(38),
				.READ_NUM(2),
				.WRITE_NUM(BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH)
			) storeQueueData(
				.clk(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.clk),
				.we(sqWE),
				.wa(executedStoreQueuePtrByStore),
				.wv(sqWriteData),
				.ra(sqReadPtr),
				.rv(sqReadData)
			);
			always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_298
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
					begin
						GenerateStoreData(sqWriteStoreData[i], Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedStoreData[i], Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedStoreVectorData[i], Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedStoreAddr[i], Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedStoreMemAccessMode[i]);
						sqWE[i] = executeStore[i];
						sqWriteData[(i * 38) + 36-:32] = sqWriteStoreData[i];
						sqWriteData[(i * 38) + 37] = executedStoreCondEnabled[i];
						sqWriteData[(i * 38) + 4-:1] = executedStoreWordWE[i];
						sqWriteData[(i * 38) + 3-:BasicTypes_DATA_BYTE_WIDTH] = executedStoreByteWE[i];
					end
			end
			reg [0:0] storeLoadForwarded;
			reg [31:0] forwardedLoadData;
			reg [0:0] forwardMiss;
			always @(*) begin
				releasedStoreQueuePtr = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.retiredStoreQueuePtr;
				sqReadPtr[0+:LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = releasedStoreQueuePtr;
				headDataEntry = sqReadData[0+:38];
				headAddrEntry = storeQueue[releasedStoreQueuePtr];
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.retiredStoreLSQ_BlockAddr = headAddrEntry[24-:20];
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.retiredStoreData = headDataEntry[36-:32];
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.retiredStoreCondEnabled = headDataEntry[37];
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.retiredStoreWordWE = headDataEntry[4-:1];
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.retiredStoreByteWE = headDataEntry[3-:BasicTypes_DATA_BYTE_WIDTH];
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.storeQueueHeadPtr = headPtr;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_299
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							sqReadPtr[(1 - i) * LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = (pickedPtr[i] >= LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM ? pickedPtr[i] - LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM : pickedPtr[i]);
							forwardedDataEntry[i] = sqReadData[(1 - i) * 38+:38];
							executedLoadWordRE[i] = LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToBlockWordEnable(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadAddr[i], Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadMemAccessMode[i]);
							executedLoadByteRE[i] = LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToWordByteEnable(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadAddr[i], Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadMemAccessMode[i]);
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_300
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin : sv2v_autoblock_301
							reg signed [31:0] j;
							for (j = 0; j < LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; j = j + 1)
								addrMatch[i][j] = ((storeQueue[j][25] && (storeQueue[j][24-:20] == LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToBlockAddr(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executedLoadAddr[i]))) && ((storeQueue[j][4-:1] & executedLoadWordRE[i]) != 1'b0)) && ((storeQueue[j][3-:BasicTypes_DATA_BYTE_WIDTH] & executedLoadByteRE[i]) != {4 {1'sb0}});
						end
				end
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_302
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							if (pickedPtr[i] < LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM) begin
								forwardMiss[i] = (!storeQueue[pickedPtr[i]][26] || ((~forwardedDataEntry[i][4-:1] & executedLoadWordRE[i]) != 1'b0)) || ((~forwardedDataEntry[i][3-:BasicTypes_DATA_BYTE_WIDTH] & executedLoadByteRE[i]) != {4 {1'sb0}});
								forwardedLoadData[i * LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_WIDTH+:LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_WIDTH] = forwardedDataEntry[i][36-:32];
							end
							else begin
								forwardMiss[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
								forwardedLoadData[i * LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_WIDTH+:LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
							end
							storeLoadForwarded[i] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.executeLoad[i] && picked[i];
						end
				end
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.forwardedLoadData = forwardedLoadData;
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.forwardMiss = forwardMiss;
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.storeLoadForwarded = storeLoadForwarded;
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : storeCommitter
			reg phase;
			reg nextPhase;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
			reg [LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH:0] unfinishedStoreNum;
			reg [LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH:0] nextUnfinishedStoreNum;
			always @(posedge Core.loadStoreUnitIF.clk)
				if (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.rst) begin
					phase <= 1'd0;
					unfinishedStoreNum <= 1'sb0;
				end
				else begin
					phase <= nextPhase;
					unfinishedStoreNum <= nextUnfinishedStoreNum;
				end
			always @(*)
				if (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.rst)
					nextPhase = 1'd0;
				else if (Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase)
					nextPhase = 1'd1;
				else if (phase == 1'd1)
					nextPhase = 1'd0;
				else
					nextPhase = phase;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM = 2;
			reg [4:0] portMSHRPhase [0:1];
			reg portStoreHasAllocatedMSHR;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 1;
			reg [0:0] portStoreMSHRID;
			always @(*) begin
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_303
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; i = i + 1)
						portMSHRPhase[i] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.mshrPhase[i];
				end
				portStoreHasAllocatedMSHR = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.storeHasAllocatedMSHR[0];
				portStoreMSHRID = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.storeMSHRID[0];
			end
			reg stallStoreTagStage;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_WIDTH = 22;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ADDR_MSB = MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_WIDTH;
			localparam BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH = 8;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_BYTE_WIDTH = 4;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_BYTE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_WORD_WIDTH = 1;
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_BYTE_WIDTH = 4;
			reg [60:0] tagStagePipeReg;
			reg [60:0] nextTagStagePipeReg;
			reg [60:0] dataStagePipeReg;
			reg [60:0] nextDataStagePipeReg;
			reg headStoreHasAllocatedMSHRPipeReg;
			reg [0:0] storeMSHRID;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			always @(posedge Core.loadStoreUnitIF.clk)
				if (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.rst) begin
					tagStagePipeReg <= 1'sb0;
					dataStagePipeReg <= 1'sb0;
					headStoreHasAllocatedMSHRPipeReg <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
					storeMSHRID <= 1'sb0;
				end
				else begin
					if (!stallStoreTagStage)
						tagStagePipeReg <= nextTagStagePipeReg;
					dataStagePipeReg <= nextDataStagePipeReg;
					if (!stallStoreTagStage)
						headStoreHasAllocatedMSHRPipeReg <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
					else if (!headStoreHasAllocatedMSHRPipeReg)
						headStoreHasAllocatedMSHRPipeReg <= portStoreHasAllocatedMSHR;
					if (portStoreHasAllocatedMSHR)
						storeMSHRID <= portStoreMSHRID;
				end
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM = 8;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 3;
			function automatic signed [31:0] LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_SelectBits;
				input reg signed [31:0] data;
				input reg signed [31:0] offset;
				input reg signed [31:0] width;
				reg signed [31:0] ret;
				begin
					ret = 0;
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_304
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < width; i = i + 1)
							ret[i] = data[i + offset];
					end
					LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_SelectBits = ret;
				end
			endfunction
			function automatic [7:0] GenerateDCacheWriteEnable;
				input reg [0:0] wordWE;
				input reg [3:0] byteWE;
				input reg [19:0] blockAddr;
				reg [7:0] ret;
				reg [3:0] we;
				begin
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_305
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_WORD_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							if (wordWE[i])
								we[i * BasicTypes_DATA_BYTE_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_DATA_BYTE_WIDTH] = byteWE;
							else
								we[i * BasicTypes_DATA_BYTE_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_DATA_BYTE_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
					end
					ret = we;
					ret = ret << (LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_BYTE_WIDTH * LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_SelectBits(blockAddr, 0, 1));
					GenerateDCacheWriteEnable = ret;
				end
			endfunction
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BIT_WIDTH = 64;
			function automatic [63:0] GenerateDCacheLine;
				input reg [31:0] data;
				reg [63:0] line;
				begin
					line = 1'sb0;
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_306
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < 2; i = i + 1)
							line[i * LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_WIDTH+:LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_WIDTH] = data;
					end
					GenerateDCacheLine = line;
				end
			endfunction
			reg dcWriteReq;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH = 20;
			reg [21:0] dcWriteAddr;
			reg [63:0] dcWriteData;
			reg dcWriteUncachable;
			reg isIO;
			reg [7:0] dcWriteByteWE;
			reg isUncachable;
			reg [3:0] retiredStoreQueuePtr;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			localparam LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ADDR_UPPER_PADDING_BIT_SIZE = 0;
			function automatic [21:0] LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToFullAddrFromBlockAddr;
				input reg [19:0] blockAddr;
				LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToFullAddrFromBlockAddr = {{LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ADDR_UPPER_PADDING_BIT_SIZE {1'b0}}, blockAddr, {LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_BYTE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE {1'b0}}};
			endfunction
			function automatic [21:0] LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToFullPhyAddrFromBlockAddrAndWordWE;
				input reg [19:0] blockAddr;
				input reg [0:0] wordWE;
				reg [21:0] ret;
				reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;
				begin
					_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
					ret = {blockAddr, {LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_BYTE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE {1'b0}}};
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_307
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_WORD_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
								_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
								if (wordWE[i]) begin
									LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToFullPhyAddrFromBlockAddrAndWordWE = ret + (i * BasicTypes_DATA_BYTE_WIDTH);
									_sv2v_jump = 2'b11;
								end
							end
						if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
							_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
					end
					if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00) begin
						LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToFullPhyAddrFromBlockAddrAndWordWE = ret;
						_sv2v_jump = 2'b11;
					end
				end
			endfunction
			function automatic MemoryMapTypes_IsPhyAddrIO;
				input reg [21:0] phyAddr;
				MemoryMapTypes_IsPhyAddrIO = phyAddr[20];
			endfunction
			function automatic MemoryMapTypes_IsPhyAddrUncachable;
				input reg [21:0] phyAddr;
				MemoryMapTypes_IsPhyAddrUncachable = phyAddr[21];
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin
				retiredStoreQueuePtr = (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.storeQueueHeadPtr + (tagStagePipeReg[60] ? 1 : 0)) + (dataStagePipeReg[60] ? 1 : 0);
				if (retiredStoreQueuePtr >= LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM)
					retiredStoreQueuePtr = retiredStoreQueuePtr - LoadStoreUnitTypes_STORE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM;
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.retiredStoreQueuePtr = retiredStoreQueuePtr;
				nextUnfinishedStoreNum = unfinishedStoreNum;
				if (Core.loadStoreUnitIF.commitStore)
					nextUnfinishedStoreNum = nextUnfinishedStoreNum + Core.loadStoreUnitIF.commitStoreNum;
				isIO = MemoryMapTypes_IsPhyAddrIO(LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToFullPhyAddrFromBlockAddrAndWordWE(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.retiredStoreLSQ_BlockAddr, Core.loadStoreUnitIF.retiredStoreWordWE));
				isUncachable = MemoryMapTypes_IsPhyAddrUncachable(LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToFullPhyAddrFromBlockAddrAndWordWE(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.retiredStoreLSQ_BlockAddr, Core.loadStoreUnitIF.retiredStoreWordWE));
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.busyInRecovery = phase == 1'd1;
				if (unfinishedStoreNum == 0) begin
					nextTagStagePipeReg = 1'sb0;
					nextTagStagePipeReg[60] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					dcWriteReq = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else begin
					nextTagStagePipeReg[1] = isIO;
					nextTagStagePipeReg[0] = isUncachable;
					if (!Core.loadStoreUnitIF.retiredStoreCondEnabled || isIO) begin
						dcWriteReq = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						nextTagStagePipeReg[60] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					end
					else begin
						dcWriteReq = !stallStoreTagStage;
						nextTagStagePipeReg[60] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcWriteReqAck;
					end
					nextTagStagePipeReg[59] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.retiredStoreCondEnabled;
					nextTagStagePipeReg[26-:20] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.retiredStoreLSQ_BlockAddr;
					nextTagStagePipeReg[58-:32] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.retiredStoreData;
					nextTagStagePipeReg[6-:1] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.retiredStoreWordWE;
					nextTagStagePipeReg[5-:4] = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.retiredStoreByteWE;
					if (!stallStoreTagStage && nextTagStagePipeReg[60])
						nextUnfinishedStoreNum = nextUnfinishedStoreNum - 1;
				end
				if (stallStoreTagStage) begin
					if (headStoreHasAllocatedMSHRPipeReg)
						dcWriteReq = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					else
						dcWriteReq = tagStagePipeReg[59];
					dcWriteAddr = LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToFullAddrFromBlockAddr(tagStagePipeReg[26-:20]);
					dcWriteData = GenerateDCacheLine(tagStagePipeReg[58-:32]);
					dcWriteByteWE = GenerateDCacheWriteEnable(tagStagePipeReg[6-:1], tagStagePipeReg[5-:4], tagStagePipeReg[26-:20]);
					dcWriteUncachable = tagStagePipeReg[0];
				end
				else begin
					dcWriteAddr = LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToFullAddrFromBlockAddr(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.retiredStoreLSQ_BlockAddr);
					dcWriteData = GenerateDCacheLine(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.retiredStoreData);
					dcWriteByteWE = GenerateDCacheWriteEnable(Core.loadStoreUnitIF.retiredStoreWordWE, Core.loadStoreUnitIF.retiredStoreByteWE, Core.loadStoreUnitIF.retiredStoreLSQ_BlockAddr);
					dcWriteUncachable = isUncachable;
				end
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcWriteReq = dcWriteReq;
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcWriteData = dcWriteData;
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcWriteByteWE = dcWriteByteWE;
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcWriteAddr = dcWriteAddr;
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcWriteUncachable = dcWriteUncachable;
			end
			always @(*) begin
				if (tagStagePipeReg[60]) begin
					if (!tagStagePipeReg[59] || tagStagePipeReg[1])
						stallStoreTagStage = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					else if (headStoreHasAllocatedMSHRPipeReg) begin
						if (portMSHRPhase[storeMSHRID] > 5'd17)
							stallStoreTagStage = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						else
							stallStoreTagStage = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					end
					else
						stallStoreTagStage = !Core.loadStoreUnitIF.dcWriteHit;
				end
				else
					stallStoreTagStage = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				nextDataStagePipeReg = tagStagePipeReg;
				if (stallStoreTagStage)
					nextDataStagePipeReg[60] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			end
			reg releaseStoreQueueHead;
			localparam BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 1;
			reg [BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE:0] releaseStoreQueueHeadEntryNum;
			function automatic [31:0] LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToScalarWordDataFromBlockData;
				input reg [31:0] data;
				input reg [0:0] wordWE;
				reg [31:0] ret;
				reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;
				begin
					_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
					ret = data[31:0];
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_308
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						for (i = 0; i < LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_BLOCK_WORD_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
							if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
								_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
								if (wordWE[i]) begin
									LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToScalarWordDataFromBlockData = data >> (i * BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH);
									_sv2v_jump = 2'b11;
								end
							end
						if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
							_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
					end
					if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00) begin
						LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToScalarWordDataFromBlockData = ret;
						_sv2v_jump = 2'b11;
					end
				end
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin
				Core.ioUnitIF.ioWE = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				Core.ioUnitIF.ioWriteDataIn = LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToScalarWordDataFromBlockData(dataStagePipeReg[58-:32], dataStagePipeReg[6-:1]);
				Core.ioUnitIF.ioWriteAddrIn = LoadStoreUnitTypes_LSQ_ToFullPhyAddrFromBlockAddrAndWordWE(dataStagePipeReg[26-:20], dataStagePipeReg[6-:1]);
				if (dataStagePipeReg[60]) begin
					releaseStoreQueueHeadEntryNum = 1;
					releaseStoreQueueHead = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					if (dataStagePipeReg[59] && dataStagePipeReg[1])
						Core.ioUnitIF.ioWE = BasicTypes_TRUE;
				end
				else begin
					releaseStoreQueueHeadEntryNum = 0;
					releaseStoreQueueHead = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.releaseStoreQueueHeadEntryNum = releaseStoreQueueHeadEntryNum;
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.releaseStoreQueueHead = releaseStoreQueueHead;
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : dCache
			localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_NUM = BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_NUM = 1;
			localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_PORT_NUM = 2;
			reg hit [0:1];
			reg missReq [0:1];
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_WIDTH = 22;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH = 20;
			reg [21:0] missAddr [0:1];
			reg missIsUncachable [0:1];
			if (1) begin : port
				wire clk;
				wire rst;
				wire rstStart;
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_ARRAY_PORT_NUM = 2;
				reg tagArrayWE [0:1];
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH = 9;
				reg [8:0] tagArrayIndexIn [0:1];
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM = 8;
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 3;
				localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_WIDTH = 22;
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_TAG_BIT_WIDTH = 10;
				reg [9:0] tagArrayDataIn [0:1];
				reg tagArrayValidIn [0:1];
				reg [19:0] tagArrayDataOut;
				reg [0:1] tagArrayValidOut;
				reg dataArrayWE [0:1];
				reg [8:0] dataArrayIndexIn [0:1];
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BIT_WIDTH = 64;
				reg [127:0] dataArrayDataIn;
				reg [127:0] dataArrayDataOut;
				reg [15:0] dataArrayByteWE_In;
				reg dataArrayDirtyIn [0:1];
				reg [0:1] dataArrayDirtyOut;
				localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_NUM = BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH;
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_NUM = 1;
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_PORT_NUM = 2;
				reg lsuCacheReq [0:1];
				reg [0:1] lsuCacheGrt;
				reg [97:0] lsuMuxIn [0:1];
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM = 2;
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 1;
				reg [79:0] lsuMuxTagOut [0:1];
				reg [64:0] lsuMuxDataOut [0:1];
				reg mshrCacheReq [0:1];
				reg mshrCacheGrt [0:1];
				reg [97:0] mshrCacheMuxIn [0:1];
				reg [79:0] mshrCacheMuxTagOut [0:1];
				reg [64:0] mshrCacheMuxDataOut [0:1];
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_MUX_PORT_NUM = 4;
				reg [3:0] cacheArrayInSel;
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_ARRAY_PORT_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 1;
				reg [3:0] cacheArrayOutSel;
				reg mshrMemReq [0:1];
				reg mshrMemGrt [0:1];
				localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH = 20;
				reg [86:0] mshrMemMuxIn [0:1];
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_MEM_ACCESS_SERIAL_BIT_SIZE = 2;
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_MEM_WRITE_SERIAL_BIT_SIZE = 1;
				reg [3:0] mshrMemMuxOut [0:1];
				reg [0:0] memInSel;
				reg [21:0] memAddr;
				reg [63:0] memData;
				reg memValid;
				reg memWE;
				reg memReqAck;
				reg [1:0] memSerial;
				reg [66:0] memAccessResult;
				reg [0:0] memWSerial;
				reg [1:0] memAccessResponse;
				reg initMSHR [0:1];
				reg [21:0] initMSHR_Addr [0:1];
				reg mshrValid [0:1];
				reg [21:0] mshrAddr [0:1];
				reg [4:0] mshrPhase [0:1];
				reg [63:0] mshrData [0:1];
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_SUBSET_BIT_WIDTH = 5;
				reg [4:0] mshrAddrSubset [0:1];
				reg mshrCanBeInvalid [0:1];
				reg isAllocatedByStore [0:1];
				reg isUncachable [0:1];
				reg makeMSHRCanBeInvalidByMemoryRegisterReadStage [0:1];
				reg makeMSHRCanBeInvalidByMemoryTagAccessStage [0:1];
				reg makeMSHRCanBeInvalidByReplayQueue [0:1];
				localparam BasicTypes_VEC_WIDTH = 128;
				reg [127:0] storedLineData;
				reg [7:0] storedLineByteWE;
				reg [0:1] lsuCacheGrtReg;
				reg dcFlushing;
				reg dcFlushReqAck;
				reg dcFlushComplete;
				reg dcFlushReq;
				reg flushComplete;
				reg mshrFlushComplete;
			end
			assign port.clk = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.clk;
			assign port.rst = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.rst;
			assign port.rstStart = Core.loadStoreUnitIF.rstStart;
			if (1) begin : controller
				reg [1:0] regPhase;
				reg [1:0] nextPhase;
				reg dcFlushReqAck;
				reg dcFlushComplete;
				reg mshrBusy;
				reg loadStoreBusy;
				always @(posedge Core.dCache.port.clk)
					if (Core.dCache.port.rst)
						regPhase <= 2'd0;
					else
						regPhase <= nextPhase;
				localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
				localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
				localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_READ_PORT_NUM = BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH;
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_WRITE_PORT_NUM = 1;
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_PORT_NUM = 2;
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM = 2;
				always @(*) begin
					nextPhase = regPhase;
					dcFlushReqAck = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					dcFlushComplete = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					mshrBusy = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					loadStoreBusy = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					case (regPhase)
						default: nextPhase = 2'd0;
						2'd0: begin
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_309
								reg signed [31:0] i;
								for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_MSHR_NUM; i = i + 1)
									if (Core.dCache.port.mshrPhase[i] != 5'd0)
										mshrBusy = BasicTypes_TRUE;
							end
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_310
								reg signed [31:0] i;
								for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LSU_PORT_NUM; i = i + 1)
									if (Core.dCache.port.lsuCacheGrtReg[i] || Core.dCache.port.lsuCacheGrt[i])
										loadStoreBusy = BasicTypes_TRUE;
							end
							dcFlushReqAck = !mshrBusy;
							if (Core.dCache.port.dcFlushReq && dcFlushReqAck)
								nextPhase = 2'd1;
						end
						2'd1:
							if (Core.dCache.port.mshrFlushComplete)
								nextPhase = 2'd2;
						2'd2: begin
							dcFlushComplete = BasicTypes_TRUE;
							if (Core.dCache.port.flushComplete)
								nextPhase = 2'd0;
						end
					endcase
					Core.dCache.port.dcFlushReqAck = dcFlushReqAck;
					Core.dCache.port.dcFlushComplete = dcFlushComplete;
					Core.dCache.port.dcFlushing = regPhase == 2'd1;
				end
			end
			if (1) begin : array
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_ARRAY_PORT_NUM = 2;
				reg [0:1] dataArrayWE;
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM = 8;
				reg [0:1] dataArrayByteWE [0:7];
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH = 9;
				reg [17:0] dataArrayIndex;
				localparam BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH = 8;
				reg [15:0] dataArrayIn [0:7];
				wire [15:0] dataArrayOut [0:7];
				reg [0:1] dataArrayDirtyIn;
				wire [0:1] dataArrayDirtyOut;
				reg [15:0] dataArrayByteWE_Tmp;
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BIT_WIDTH = 64;
				reg [127:0] dataArrayInTmp;
				reg [127:0] dataArrayOutTmp;
				reg [0:1] tagArrayWE;
				reg [17:0] tagArrayIndex;
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 3;
				localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_WIDTH = 22;
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_TAG_BIT_WIDTH = 10;
				reg [21:0] tagArrayIn;
				wire [21:0] tagArrayOut;
				reg [8:0] rstIndex;
				always @(posedge Core.dCache.port.clk)
					if (Core.dCache.port.rstStart)
						rstIndex <= 1'sb0;
					else
						rstIndex <= rstIndex + 1;
				genvar i;
				localparam CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_NUM = 512;
				for (i = 0; i < CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM; i = i + 1) begin : genblk1
					BlockTrueDualPortRAM #(
						.ENTRY_NUM(CacheSystemTypes_DCACHE_INDEX_NUM),
						.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH)
					) dataArray(
						.clk(Core.dCache.port.clk),
						.we(dataArrayByteWE[i]),
						.rwa(dataArrayIndex),
						.wv(dataArrayIn[i]),
						.rv(dataArrayOut[i])
