										commit[i] = (|{execState[(1 - i) * 4+:4] == 4'b0010, execState[(1 - i) * 4+:4] == 4'b1000, execState[(1 - i) * 4+:4] == 4'b1001, execState[(1 - i) * 4+:4] == 4'b1010, execState[(1 - i) * 4+:4] == 4'b1011, execState[(1 - i) * 4+:4] == 4'b1100, execState[(1 - i) * 4+:4] == 4'b1101} || unableToStartRecovery ? BasicTypes_FALSE : BasicTypes_TRUE);
									else
										commit[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
								end
								else
									commit[i] = (i < finishedInsnRange ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
							end
							else
								commit[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					end
				end
			endtask
			always @(*) begin
				alReadData = Core.activeListIF.readData;
				execState = Core.activeListIF.headExecState;
				phase = Core.recoveryManagerIF.phase;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_411
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						begin
							last[i] = alReadData[((1 - i) * 47) + 17];
							isBranch[i] = alReadData[((1 - i) * 47) + 19];
							isStore[i] = alReadData[((1 - i) * 47) + 20];
						end
				end
				DecideCommit(commit, toRecoveryPhase, recoveryOpIndex, refetchType, recoveryCause, phase == 2'd0, Core.activeListIF.validEntryNum, execState, isBranch, isStore, last, Core.recoveryManagerIF.unableToStartRecovery);
				commitNum = 0;
				commitLoadNum = 0;
				commitStoreNum = 0;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_412
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						if (commit[i]) begin
							commitNum = commitNum + 1;
							if (alReadData[((1 - i) * 47) + 21])
								commitLoadNum = commitLoadNum + 1;
							if (alReadData[((1 - i) * 47) + 20])
								commitStoreNum = commitStoreNum + 1;
						end
				end
				Core.renameLogicIF.commit = commit[0];
				Core.renameLogicIF.commitNum = commitNum;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_413
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						if (commit[i]) begin
							Core.renameLogicIF.retRMT_WriteReg[i] = alReadData[((1 - i) * 47) + 22];
							Core.renameLogicIF.retRMT_WriteReg_PhyRegNum[i] = alReadData[((1 - i) * 47) + 15-:6];
							Core.renameLogicIF.retRMT_WriteReg_LogRegNum[i] = alReadData[((1 - i) * 47) + 27-:5];
						end
						else begin
							Core.renameLogicIF.retRMT_WriteReg[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							Core.renameLogicIF.retRMT_WriteReg_PhyRegNum[i] = 0;
							Core.renameLogicIF.retRMT_WriteReg_LogRegNum[i] = 0;
						end
				end
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.releaseLoadQueue = commit[0];
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.releaseLoadQueueEntryNum = commitLoadNum;
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.commitStore = commit[0];
				Core.loadStoreUnitIF.commitStoreNum = commitStoreNum;
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.exceptionDetectedInCommitStage = toRecoveryPhase;
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.refetchTypeFromCommitStage = refetchType;
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.recoveryOpIndex = recoveryOpIndex;
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.recoveryCauseFromCommitStage = recoveryCause;
				lastCommittedPC = prevLastCommittedPC;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_414
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						if (commit[i])
							lastCommittedPC = alReadData[((1 - i) * 47) + 46-:19];
				end
				Core.csrUnitIF.commitNum = commitNum;
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_415
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						Core.debugIF.lastCommittedPC = lastCommittedPC;
				end
				Core.hwCounterIF.commitNum = commitNum;
				Core.hwCounterIF.refetchThisPC = toRecoveryPhase && (refetchType == 3'b000);
				Core.hwCounterIF.refetchNextPC = toRecoveryPhase && |{refetchType == 3'b001, refetchType == 3'b010};
				Core.hwCounterIF.refetchBrTarget = toRecoveryPhase && (refetchType == 3'b011);
			end
			genvar i;
		end
		if (1) begin : recoveryManager
			localparam BasicTypes_ADDR_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM = 64;
			localparam SchedulerTypes_ACTIVE_LIST_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
			reg [118:0] regState;
			reg [118:0] nextState;
			reg toRecoveryPhase;
			reg toCommitPhase;
			reg refetchFromCSR;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			reg [18:0] recoveredPC;
			reg [5:0] exceptionOpPtr;
			reg exceptionDetected;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			always @(posedge Core.recoveryManagerIF.clk)
				if (!Core.recoveryManagerIF.rst)
					regState <= nextState;
				else begin
					regState[118-:2] <= 2'd0;
					regState[15-:6] <= 1'sb0;
					regState[9-:6] <= 1'sb0;
					regState[3] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
					regState[2-:3] <= 3'b000;
					regState[116] <= 1'sb0;
					regState[115-:32] <= 1'sb0;
					regState[83-:32] <= 1'sb0;
					regState[51-:4] <= 4'b0000;
					regState[47-:32] <= 1'sb0;
				end
			localparam BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH = 8;
			localparam BasicTypes_INSN_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH = 4;
			function automatic [18:0] MemoryMapTypes_ToPC_FromAddr;
				input reg [31:0] addr;
				MemoryMapTypes_ToPC_FromAddr = {addr[31], addr[17:0]};
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin
				toRecoveryPhase = Core.recoveryManagerIF.exceptionDetectedInCommitStage || Core.recoveryManagerIF.exceptionDetectedInRwStage;
				if (toRecoveryPhase)
					nextState[3] = Core.recoveryManagerIF.exceptionDetectedInRwStage;
				else
					nextState[3] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				toCommitPhase = (regState[118-:2] == 2'd2) && !(Core.recoveryManagerIF.renameLogicRecoveryRMT || Core.recoveryManagerIF.issueQueueReturnIndex);
				nextState[2-:3] = (Core.recoveryManagerIF.exceptionDetectedInCommitStage ? Core.recoveryManagerIF.refetchTypeFromCommitStage : Core.recoveryManagerIF.refetchTypeFromRwStage);
				nextState[51-:4] = Core.recoveryManagerIF.recoveryCauseFromCommitStage;
				nextState[47-:32] = Core.recoveryManagerIF.faultingDataAddr;
				nextState[116] = Core.recoveryManagerIF.exceptionDetectedInCommitStage;
				nextState[115-:32] = Core.recoveryManagerIF.recoveredPC_FromRwStage;
				nextState[83-:32] = Core.recoveryManagerIF.recoveredPC_FromCommitStage;
				refetchFromCSR = |{regState[2-:3] == 3'b100, regState[2-:3] == 3'b101};
				Core.csrUnitIF.triggerExcpt = (regState[118-:2] == 2'd1) && refetchFromCSR;
				Core.csrUnitIF.excptCauseAddr = MemoryMapTypes_ToPC_FromAddr(regState[83-:32]);
				Core.csrUnitIF.excptCause = regState[51-:4];
				Core.csrUnitIF.excptCauseDataAddr = regState[47-:32];
				if (regState[118-:2] == 2'd1) begin
					if (refetchFromCSR)
						recoveredPC = MemoryMapTypes_ToPC_FromAddr(Core.csrUnitIF.excptTargetAddr);
					else if (regState[2-:3] == 3'b000)
						recoveredPC = (regState[116] ? MemoryMapTypes_ToPC_FromAddr(regState[83-:32]) : MemoryMapTypes_ToPC_FromAddr(regState[115-:32]));
					else if (|{regState[2-:3] == 3'b001, regState[2-:3] == 3'b010})
						recoveredPC = (regState[116] ? MemoryMapTypes_ToPC_FromAddr(regState[83-:32]) + BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH : MemoryMapTypes_ToPC_FromAddr(regState[115-:32]) + BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH);
					else
						recoveredPC = (regState[116] ? MemoryMapTypes_ToPC_FromAddr(regState[83-:32]) : MemoryMapTypes_ToPC_FromAddr(regState[115-:32]));
				end
				else
					recoveredPC = 1'sb0;
				if (Core.recoveryManagerIF.rst)
					nextState[118-:2] = 2'd0;
				else if (regState[118-:2] == 2'd0)
					nextState[118-:2] = (toRecoveryPhase ? 2'd1 : 2'd0);
				else if (regState[118-:2] == 2'd1)
					nextState[118-:2] = 2'd2;
				else
					nextState[118-:2] = (toCommitPhase ? 2'd0 : regState[118-:2]);
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.phase = regState[118-:2];
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.recoveredPC_FromRwCommit = recoveredPC;
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.toCommitPhase = toCommitPhase;
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.toRecoveryPhase = regState[118-:2] == 2'd1;
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.recoveryFromRwStage = regState[3];
				Core.ctrlIF.cmStageFlushUpper = regState[118-:2] == 2'd1;
				exceptionDetected = Core.recoveryManagerIF.exceptionDetectedInCommitStage || Core.recoveryManagerIF.exceptionDetectedInRwStage;
				exceptionOpPtr = Core.activeListIF.exceptionOpPtr;
				nextState[15-:6] = (|{nextState[2-:3] == 3'b000, nextState[2-:3] == 3'b101} ? exceptionOpPtr : exceptionOpPtr + 1);
				nextState[9-:6] = Core.activeListIF.detectedFlushRangeTailPtr;
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.loadQueueRecoveryTailPtr = Core.activeListIF.loadQueueRecoveryTailPtr;
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.storeQueueRecoveryTailPtr = (regState[2-:3] == 3'b010 ? Core.activeListIF.storeQueueRecoveryTailPtr + 1 : Core.activeListIF.storeQueueRecoveryTailPtr);
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeHeadPtr = regState[15-:6];
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.flushRangeTailPtr = regState[9-:6];
				Core.recoveryManagerIF.unableToStartRecovery = ((((regState[118-:2] != 2'd0) || Core.recoveryManagerIF.renameLogicRecoveryRMT) || Core.recoveryManagerIF.issueQueueReturnIndex) || Core.recoveryManagerIF.replayQueueFlushedOpExist) || Core.recoveryManagerIF.wakeupPipelineRegFlushedOpExist;
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : csrUnit
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_CAUSE_INTERRUPT_CODE_WIDTH = 5;
			reg [319:0] csrReg;
			reg [319:0] csrNext;
			reg [31:0] rv;
			reg [31:0] wv;
			reg [31:0] mcycle;
			localparam BasicTypes_ADDR_WIDTH = 32;
			wire [31:0] jumpTarget;
			localparam BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH = 2;
			localparam BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 1;
			reg [BasicTypes_COMMIT_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE:0] regCommitNum;
			localparam BasicTypes_RSD_EXTERNAL_INTERRUPT_CODE_WIDTH = 5;
			reg [4:0] externalInterruptCodeReg;
			always @(posedge Core.csrUnitIF.clk)
				if (Core.csrUnitIF.rst) begin
					csrReg <= 1'sb0;
					regCommitNum <= 1'sb0;
					externalInterruptCodeReg <= 1'sb0;
				end
				else begin
					csrReg <= csrNext;
					regCommitNum <= Core.csrUnitIF.commitNum;
					externalInterruptCodeReg <= Core.csrUnitIF.externalInterruptCode;
				end
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			localparam [1:0] CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_MTVEC_BASE_PADDING = 2'b00;
			localparam CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MCAUSE = 12'h342;
			localparam CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MCYCLE = 12'hb00;
			localparam CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MEPC = 12'h341;
			localparam CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MIE = 12'h304;
			localparam CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MINSTRET = 12'hb02;
			localparam CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MIP = 12'h344;
			localparam CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MSCRATCH = 12'h340;
			localparam CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MSTATUS = 12'h300;
			localparam CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MTVAL = 12'h343;
			localparam CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MTVEC = 12'h305;
			function automatic [4:0] CSR_UnitTypes_ToTrapCodeFromExecState;
				input reg [3:0] state;
				case (state)
					4'b0100: CSR_UnitTypes_ToTrapCodeFromExecState = 5'd11;
					4'b0101: CSR_UnitTypes_ToTrapCodeFromExecState = 5'd3;
					4'b1000: CSR_UnitTypes_ToTrapCodeFromExecState = 5'd4;
					4'b1001: CSR_UnitTypes_ToTrapCodeFromExecState = 5'd5;
					4'b1010: CSR_UnitTypes_ToTrapCodeFromExecState = 5'd6;
					4'b1011: CSR_UnitTypes_ToTrapCodeFromExecState = 5'd7;
					4'b1100: CSR_UnitTypes_ToTrapCodeFromExecState = 5'd2;
					4'b1101: CSR_UnitTypes_ToTrapCodeFromExecState = 5'd1;
					4'b1110: CSR_UnitTypes_ToTrapCodeFromExecState = 5'd0;
					default: CSR_UnitTypes_ToTrapCodeFromExecState = 5'd14;
				endcase
			endfunction
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_TAG = 13;
			function automatic [31:0] MemoryMapTypes_ToAddrFromPC;
				input reg [18:0] pc;
				MemoryMapTypes_ToAddrFromPC = {pc[18], {MemoryMapTypes_PC_TAG {1'b0}}, pc[17:0]};
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin
				mcycle = csrReg[63-:32];
				case (Core.csrUnitIF.csrNumber)
					CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MSTATUS: rv = csrReg[319-:32];
					CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MIP: rv = csrReg[287-:32];
					CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MIE: rv = csrReg[255-:32];
					CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MCAUSE: rv = csrReg[223-:32];
					CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MTVEC: rv = csrReg[191-:32];
					CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MTVAL: rv = csrReg[159-:32];
					CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MEPC: rv = csrReg[127-:32];
					CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MSCRATCH: rv = csrReg[95-:32];
					CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MCYCLE: rv = csrReg[63-:32];
					CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MINSTRET: rv = csrReg[31-:32];
					default: rv = 1'sb0;
				endcase
				csrNext = csrReg;
				csrNext[63-:32] = csrNext[63-:32] + 1;
				csrNext[31-:32] = csrNext[31-:32] + regCommitNum;
				wv = 1'sb0;
				if (Core.csrUnitIF.triggerInterrupt) begin
					csrNext[295] = csrNext[291];
					csrNext[291] = 0;
					csrNext[127-:32] = MemoryMapTypes_ToAddrFromPC(Core.csrUnitIF.interruptRetAddr);
					csrNext[159-:32] = MemoryMapTypes_ToAddrFromPC(Core.csrUnitIF.interruptRetAddr);
					csrNext[223] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					csrNext[196-:CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_CAUSE_INTERRUPT_CODE_WIDTH] = Core.csrUnitIF.interruptCode;
				end
				else if (Core.csrUnitIF.triggerExcpt) begin
					if (Core.csrUnitIF.excptCause == 4'b0110)
						csrNext[291] = csrNext[295];
					else begin
						csrNext[295] = csrNext[291];
						csrNext[291] = 0;
						csrNext[127-:32] = MemoryMapTypes_ToAddrFromPC(Core.csrUnitIF.excptCauseAddr);
						csrNext[159-:32] = Core.csrUnitIF.excptCauseDataAddr;
						csrNext[223] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
						csrNext[196-:5] = CSR_UnitTypes_ToTrapCodeFromExecState(Core.csrUnitIF.excptCause);
					end
				end
				else if (Core.csrUnitIF.csrWE) begin
					case (Core.csrUnitIF.csrCode)
						2'b01: wv = Core.csrUnitIF.csrWriteIn;
						2'b10: wv = rv | Core.csrUnitIF.csrWriteIn;
						2'b11: wv = rv & ~Core.csrUnitIF.csrWriteIn;
						default: wv = Core.csrUnitIF.csrWriteIn;
					endcase
					case (Core.csrUnitIF.csrNumber)
						CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MSTATUS: csrNext[319-:32] = wv;
						CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MIE: csrNext[255-:32] = wv;
						CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MCAUSE: csrNext[223-:32] = wv;
						CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MTVEC: csrNext[191-:32] = wv;
						CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MTVAL: csrNext[159-:32] = wv;
						CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MEPC: csrNext[127-:32] = wv;
						CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MSCRATCH: csrNext[95-:32] = wv;
						CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MCYCLE: csrNext[63-:32] = wv;
						CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_NUM_MINSTRET: csrNext[31-:32] = wv;
						default: wv = 1'sb0;
					endcase
				end
				csrNext[263] = Core.csrUnitIF.reqTimerInterrupt;
				csrNext[267] = Core.csrUnitIF.reqExternalInterrupt;
				Core.csrUnitIF.externalInterruptCodeInCSR = externalInterruptCodeReg;
				Core.csrUnitIF.csrReadOut = rv;
				if (Core.csrUnitIF.excptCause == 4'b0110)
					Core.csrUnitIF.excptTargetAddr = csrReg[127-:32];
				else
					Core.csrUnitIF.excptTargetAddr = {csrReg[191-:30], CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_MTVEC_BASE_PADDING};
				Core.csrUnitIF.csrWholeOut = csrReg;
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : cacheFlushManager
			reg icFlushReq;
			reg dcFlushReq;
			reg [1:0] regPhase;
			reg [1:0] nextPhase;
			reg regIcFlushComplete;
			reg nextIcFlushComplete;
			reg regDcFlushComplete;
			reg nextDcFlushComplete;
			reg cacheFlushComplete;
			always @(posedge Core.cacheFlushManagerIF.clk)
				if (Core.cacheFlushManagerIF.rst)
					regPhase <= 2'd0;
				else
					regPhase <= nextPhase;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			always @(*) begin
				nextPhase = regPhase;
				nextIcFlushComplete = regIcFlushComplete;
				nextDcFlushComplete = regDcFlushComplete;
				icFlushReq = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				dcFlushReq = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				cacheFlushComplete = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				case (regPhase)
					default: nextPhase = 2'd0;
					2'd0:
						if (Core.cacheFlushManagerIF.cacheFlushReq)
							nextPhase = 2'd1;
					2'd1:
						if (Core.cacheSystemIF.icFlushReqAck && Core.cacheSystemIF.dcFlushReqAck) begin
							icFlushReq = BasicTypes_TRUE;
							dcFlushReq = BasicTypes_TRUE;
							nextIcFlushComplete = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							nextDcFlushComplete = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							nextPhase = 2'd2;
						end
					2'd2: begin
						if (Core.cacheSystemIF.icFlushComplete)
							nextIcFlushComplete = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						if (Core.cacheSystemIF.dcFlushComplete)
							nextDcFlushComplete = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						if ((nextIcFlushComplete || regIcFlushComplete) && (nextDcFlushComplete || regDcFlushComplete))
							nextPhase = 2'd3;
					end
					2'd3: begin
						cacheFlushComplete = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						if (Core.cacheFlushManagerIF.cacheFlushReq)
							nextPhase = 2'd0;
					end
				endcase
				Core.cacheSystemIF.icFlushReq = icFlushReq;
				Core.cacheSystemIF.dcFlushReq = dcFlushReq;
				Core.cacheSystemIF.flushComplete = cacheFlushComplete;
				Core.cacheFlushManagerIF.cacheFlushComplete = cacheFlushComplete;
			end
			always @(posedge Core.cacheFlushManagerIF.clk)
				if (Core.cacheFlushManagerIF.rst) begin
					regIcFlushComplete <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
					regDcFlushComplete <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
				end
				else begin
					regIcFlushComplete <= nextIcFlushComplete;
					regDcFlushComplete <= nextDcFlushComplete;
				end
		end
		if (1) begin : interruptCtrl
			reg reqInterrupt;
			reg triggerInterrupt;
			reg reqTimerInterrupt;
			reg reqExternalInterrupt;
			localparam CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_CAUSE_INTERRUPT_CODE_WIDTH = 5;
			reg [4:0] interruptCode;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
			reg [18:0] interruptTargetAddr;
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
			reg [319:0] csrReg;
			localparam BasicTypes_RSD_EXTERNAL_INTERRUPT_CODE_WIDTH = 5;
			reg [4:0] interruptCodeConv;
			localparam [1:0] CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_MTVEC_BASE_PADDING = 2'b00;
			localparam BasicTypes_ADDR_WIDTH = 32;
			function automatic [18:0] MemoryMapTypes_ToPC_FromAddr;
				input reg [31:0] addr;
				MemoryMapTypes_ToPC_FromAddr = {addr[31], addr[17:0]};
			endfunction
			always @(*) begin
				csrReg = Core.csrUnitIF.csrWholeOut;
				reqTimerInterrupt = csrReg[231] && csrReg[263];
				reqExternalInterrupt = csrReg[235] && csrReg[267];
				reqInterrupt = csrReg[291] && (reqTimerInterrupt || reqExternalInterrupt);
				interruptCodeConv[4-:BasicTypes_RSD_EXTERNAL_INTERRUPT_CODE_WIDTH] = Core.csrUnitIF.externalInterruptCodeInCSR;
				if (reqTimerInterrupt) begin : sv2v_autoblock_416
					reg [4:0] sv2v_tmp_cast;
					sv2v_tmp_cast = 7;
					interruptCode = sv2v_tmp_cast;
				end
				else
					interruptCode = interruptCodeConv[4-:CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_CAUSE_INTERRUPT_CODE_WIDTH];
				Core.ctrlIF.npStageSendBubbleLowerForInterrupt = reqInterrupt;
				triggerInterrupt = (Core.ctrlIF.wholePipelineEmpty && !Core.recoveryManagerIF.unableToStartRecovery) && reqInterrupt;
				Core.csrUnitIF.triggerInterrupt = triggerInterrupt;
				Core.csrUnitIF.interruptRetAddr = Core.npStageIF.pcOut;
				Core.csrUnitIF.interruptCode = interruptCode;
				interruptTargetAddr = MemoryMapTypes_ToPC_FromAddr({(csrReg[161-:2] == 2'd1 ? csrReg[191-:30] + interruptCode : csrReg[191-:30]), CSR_UnitTypes_CSR_MTVEC_BASE_PADDING});
				Core.npStageIF.interruptAddrWE = triggerInterrupt;
				Core.npStageIF.interruptAddrIn = interruptTargetAddr;
			end
		end
		if (1) begin : ioUnit
			localparam IO_UnitTypes_TIMER_REGISTER_WIDTH = 64;
			localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
			reg [127:0] tmReg;
			reg [127:0] tmNext;
			always @(posedge Core.ioUnitIF.clk)
				if (Core.ioUnitIF.rst)
					tmReg <= 1'sb0;
				else
					tmReg <= tmNext;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_WIDTH = 22;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_RAW_ADDR_WIDTH = 20;
			reg [19:0] phyRawReadAddr;
			reg [19:0] phyRawWriteAddr;
			localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
			localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_TIMER_BASE = 20'h00000;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_TIMER_CMP_HI = 12;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_TIMER_CMP_LOW = 8;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_TIMER_HI = 4;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_TIMER_LOW = 0;
			always @(*) begin
				phyRawReadAddr = Core.ioUnitIF.ioReadAddrIn[19-:20];
				phyRawWriteAddr = Core.ioUnitIF.ioWriteAddrIn[19-:20];
				tmNext = tmReg;
				tmNext[127-:IO_UnitTypes_TIMER_REGISTER_WIDTH] = tmNext[127-:IO_UnitTypes_TIMER_REGISTER_WIDTH] + 1;
				Core.csrUnitIF.reqTimerInterrupt = (tmNext[127-:64] >= tmNext[63-:64] ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
				if (Core.ioUnitIF.ioWE)
					if (phyRawWriteAddr == MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_TIMER_LOW)
						tmNext[95-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] = Core.ioUnitIF.ioWriteDataIn;
					else if (phyRawWriteAddr == MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_TIMER_HI)
						tmNext[127-:32] = Core.ioUnitIF.ioWriteDataIn;
					else if (phyRawWriteAddr == MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_TIMER_CMP_LOW)
						tmNext[31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] = Core.ioUnitIF.ioWriteDataIn;
					else if (phyRawWriteAddr == MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_TIMER_CMP_HI)
						tmNext[63-:32] = Core.ioUnitIF.ioWriteDataIn;
				if (phyRawReadAddr == MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_TIMER_LOW)
					Core.ioUnitIF.ioReadDataOut = tmReg[95-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH];
				else if (phyRawReadAddr == MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_TIMER_HI)
					Core.ioUnitIF.ioReadDataOut = tmReg[127-:32];
				else if (phyRawReadAddr == MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_TIMER_CMP_LOW)
					Core.ioUnitIF.ioReadDataOut = tmReg[31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH];
				else
					Core.ioUnitIF.ioReadDataOut = tmReg[63-:32];
			end
			localparam IO_UnitTypes_SERIAL_OUTPUT_WIDTH = 32;
			localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_SERIAL_OUTPUT = 20'h02000;
			always @(*) begin
				Core.ioUnitIF.serialWE = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				Core.ioUnitIF.serialWriteDataOut = Core.ioUnitIF.ioWriteDataIn[31:0];
				if (Core.ioUnitIF.ioWE && (phyRawWriteAddr == MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_SERIAL_OUTPUT))
					Core.ioUnitIF.serialWE = BasicTypes_TRUE;
			end
		end
	endgenerate
endmodule
module MicroOpPicker (
	req,
	serialize,
	picked,
	pickedIndex,
	next
);
	localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
	localparam BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;
	localparam MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM = 3;
	localparam MicroOpTypes_ALL_DECODED_MICRO_OP_WIDTH = 6;
	input wire [5:0] req;
	input wire [5:0] serialize;
	output reg [0:1] picked;
	localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 1;
	localparam BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE;
	localparam MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_INDEX_BITS = 2;
	localparam MicroOpTypes_ALL_DECODED_MICRO_OP_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 3;
	output reg [5:0] pickedIndex;
	output reg [5:0] next;
	reg clear;
	reg sent;
	reg [5:0] cur;
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
	always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
		reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;
		_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
		clear = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		sent = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		cur = req;
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_2
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
				if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
					_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
					picked[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					pickedIndex[(1 - i) * MicroOpTypes_ALL_DECODED_MICRO_OP_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE+:MicroOpTypes_ALL_DECODED_MICRO_OP_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE] = 0;
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_3
						reg signed [31:0] mn;
						begin : sv2v_autoblock_4
							reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump_1;
							_sv2v_jump_1 = _sv2v_jump;
							for (mn = 0; mn < MicroOpTypes_ALL_DECODED_MICRO_OP_WIDTH; mn = mn + 1)
								if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
									_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
									if (cur[mn] && !clear) begin
										if (serialize[mn])
											clear = BasicTypes_TRUE;
										if (clear && sent)
											_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
										if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00) begin
											sent = BasicTypes_TRUE;
											picked[i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
											pickedIndex[(1 - i) * MicroOpTypes_ALL_DECODED_MICRO_OP_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE+:MicroOpTypes_ALL_DECODED_MICRO_OP_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE] = mn;
											cur[mn] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
											_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
										end
									end
								end
							if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
								_sv2v_jump = _sv2v_jump_1;
						end
					end
				end
			if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
				_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
		end
		if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00)
			next = cur;
	end
endmodule
module RenameStageSerializer (
	clk,
	rst,
	stall,
	clear,
	activeListEmpty,
	storeQueueEmpty,
	opInfo,
	valid,
	serialize
);
	input wire clk;
	input wire rst;
	input wire stall;
	input wire clear;
	input wire activeListEmpty;
	input wire storeQueueEmpty;
	localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
	localparam BasicTypes_RENAME_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;
	localparam MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_INDEX_BITS = 2;
	localparam BasicTypes_LREG_NUM = 32;
	localparam BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 5;
	localparam OpFormatTypes_BR_DISP_WIDTH = 20;
	localparam BasicTypes_RISCV_SHIFTER_WIDTH = 30;
	localparam OpFormatTypes_CSR_IMM_WIDTH = 5;
	input wire [141:0] opInfo;
	input wire [1:0] valid;
	output reg serialize;
	genvar i;
	reg [1:0] regPhase;
	reg [1:0] nextPhase;
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (rst)
			regPhase <= 2'd0;
		else if (!stall)
			regPhase <= nextPhase;
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
	always @(*) begin
		serialize = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		nextPhase = 2'd0;
		if (clear)
			nextPhase = 2'd0;
		if (regPhase == 2'd0) begin
			if (opInfo[0] && valid[0])
				if (opInfo[47]) begin
					if (!activeListEmpty || !storeQueueEmpty) begin
						serialize = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						nextPhase = 2'd0;
					end
					else
						nextPhase = 2'd2;
				end
				else if (!activeListEmpty) begin
					serialize = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					nextPhase = 2'd0;
				end
				else
					nextPhase = 2'd2;
		end
		else if (!activeListEmpty || !storeQueueEmpty) begin
			serialize = BasicTypes_TRUE;
			nextPhase = 2'd2;
		end
		else
			nextPhase = 2'd0;
	end
endmodule
module BypassCtrlStage (
	clk,
	rst,
	ctrl,
	in,
	out
);
	input wire clk;
	input wire rst;
	input wire [1:0] ctrl;
	localparam BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM = 64;
	localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM = BasicTypes_PSCALAR_NUM;
	localparam BasicTypes_PREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 6;
	input wire [6:0] in;
	output wire [6:0] out;
	reg [6:0] body;
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (rst || ctrl[0]) begin
			body[6-:6] <= 0;
			body[0] <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
		end
		else if (ctrl[1])
			body <= body;
		else
			body <= in;
	assign out = body;
endmodule
module BypassStage (
	clk,
	rst,
	ctrl,
	in,
	out
);
	input wire clk;
	input wire rst;
	input wire [1:0] ctrl;
	localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
	input wire [32:0] in;
	output wire [32:0] out;
	reg [32:0] body;
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (rst || ctrl[0]) begin
			body[31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] <= 0;
			body[32] <= 0;
		end
		else if (ctrl[1])
			body <= body;
		else
			body <= in;
	assign out = body;
endmodule
module VectorBypassStage (
	clk,
	rst,
	ctrl,
	in,
	out
);
	input wire clk;
	input wire rst;
	input wire [1:0] ctrl;
	localparam BasicTypes_VEC_WIDTH = 128;
	input wire [128:0] in;
	output wire [128:0] out;
	reg [128:0] body;
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (rst || ctrl[0]) begin
			body[127-:BasicTypes_VEC_WIDTH] <= 0;
			body[128] <= 0;
		end
		else if (ctrl[1])
			body <= body;
		else
			body <= in;
	assign out = body;
endmodule
module BitCounter (
	fuOpA_In,
	dataOut
);
	localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
	input wire [31:0] fuOpA_In;
	output reg [31:0] dataOut;
	reg [31:0] count;
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
	always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
		reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;
		_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_2
			reg [31:0] _sv2v_value_on_break;
			for (count = 0; count < BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH; count = count + 1)
				if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
					_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
					if (fuOpA_In[(BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH - count) - 1] == BasicTypes_TRUE)
						_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
					_sv2v_value_on_break = count;
				end
			if (!(_sv2v_jump < 2'b10))
				count = _sv2v_value_on_break;
			if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
				_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
		end
		if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00)
			dataOut = count;
	end
endmodule
module PipelinedBitCounter (
	clk,
	rst,
	stall,
	fuOpA_In,
	dataOut
);
	parameter PIPELINE_DEPTH = 3;
	input clk;
	input rst;
	input stall;
	localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
	input wire [31:0] fuOpA_In;
	output reg [31:0] dataOut;
	reg [31:0] pipeReg [0:PIPELINE_DEPTH - 2];
	reg [31:0] count;
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
	always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
		reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;
		_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_2
			reg [31:0] _sv2v_value_on_break;
			for (count = 0; count < BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH; count = count + 1)
				if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
					_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
					if (fuOpA_In[(BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH - count) - 1] == BasicTypes_TRUE)
						_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
					_sv2v_value_on_break = count;
				end
			if (!(_sv2v_jump < 2'b10))
				count = _sv2v_value_on_break;
			if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
				_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
		end
		if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00)
			dataOut = pipeReg[PIPELINE_DEPTH - 2];
	end
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_3
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < (PIPELINE_DEPTH - 1); i = i + 1)
				pipeReg[i] <= 1'sb0;
		end
		else if (stall) begin : sv2v_autoblock_4
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < (PIPELINE_DEPTH - 1); i = i + 1)
				pipeReg[i] <= pipeReg[i];
		end
		else begin
			pipeReg[0] <= count;
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_5
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 1; i < (PIPELINE_DEPTH - 1); i = i + 1)
					pipeReg[i] <= pipeReg[i - 1];
			end
		end
endmodule
module IntAdder (
	dst,
	overflowOut,
	srcA,
	invA,
	srcB,
	invB,
	carryIn
);
	localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
	output reg [32:0] dst;
	output reg overflowOut;
	input wire [31:0] srcA;
	input wire invA;
	input wire [31:0] srcB;
	input wire invB;
	input wire carryIn;
	function automatic [31:0] Inv;
		input reg [31:0] src;
		input reg inv;
		Inv = (inv ? ~src : src);
	endfunction
	reg [31:0] tmpA;
	reg [31:0] tmpB;
	always @(*) begin
		tmpA = Inv(srcA, invA);
		tmpB = Inv(srcB, invB);
		dst = (tmpA + tmpB) + carryIn;
		overflowOut = (dst[31] ^ tmpA[31]) && (dst[31] ^ tmpB[31]);
	end
endmodule
module IntALU (
	aluDataOut,
	aluCode,
	fuOpA_In,
	fuOpB_In
);
	localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
	output reg [31:0] aluDataOut;
	input wire [3:0] aluCode;
	input wire [31:0] fuOpA_In;
	input wire [31:0] fuOpB_In;
	reg [32:0] opDst;
	reg [31:0] opA;
	reg [31:0] opB;
	reg signed [31:0] signedOpA;
	reg signed [31:0] signedOpB;
	wire [32:0] adderDst;
	reg [31:0] adderInA;
	reg adderInvInA;
	reg [31:0] adderInB;
	reg adderInvInB;
	reg adderInCarry;
	wire adderOutOverflow;
	IntAdder adder(
		.dst(adderDst),
		.srcA(adderInA),
		.invA(adderInvInA),
		.srcB(adderInB),
		.invB(adderInvInB),
		.carryIn(adderInCarry),
		.overflowOut(adderOutOverflow)
	);
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
	always @(*) begin
		opA = fuOpA_In;
		opB = fuOpB_In;
		case (aluCode)
			default: begin
				adderInA = 1'sb0;
				adderInvInA = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				adderInB = 1'sb0;
				adderInvInB = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				adderInCarry = 0;
				opDst = {1'b0, opA & opB};
			end
			4'b0100: begin
				adderInA = 1'sb0;
				adderInvInA = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				adderInB = 1'sb0;
				adderInvInB = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				adderInCarry = 0;
				opDst = {1'b0, opA ^ opB};
			end
			4'b0001: begin
				adderInA = opA;
				adderInvInA = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				adderInB = opB;
				adderInvInB = BasicTypes_TRUE;
				adderInCarry = 1;
				opDst = adderDst;
			end
			4'b0000: begin
				adderInA = opA;
				adderInvInA = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				adderInB = opB;
				adderInvInB = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				adderInCarry = 0;
				opDst = adderDst;
			end
			4'b0010: begin
				signedOpA = opA;
				signedOpB = opB;
				adderInA = 1'sb0;
				adderInvInA = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				adderInB = 1'sb0;
				adderInvInB = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				adderInCarry = 0;
				opDst = {1'b0, {31'h00000000, signedOpA < signedOpB}};
			end
			4'b0011: begin
				adderInA = 1'sb0;
				adderInvInA = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				adderInB = 1'sb0;
				adderInvInB = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				adderInCarry = 0;
				opDst = {1'b0, {31'h00000000, opA < opB}};
			end
			4'b0110: begin
				adderInA = 1'sb0;
				adderInvInA = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				adderInB = 1'sb0;
				adderInvInB = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				adderInCarry = 0;
				opDst = {1'b0, opA | opB};
			end
		endcase
		aluDataOut = opDst[31-:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH];
	end
endmodule
module OrgShifter (
	shiftOperandType,
	shiftType,
	immShiftAmount,
	regShiftAmount,
	dataIn,
	carryIn,
	dataOut,
	carryOut
);
	input wire shiftOperandType;
	input wire [1:0] shiftType;
	localparam BasicTypes_SHIFT_AMOUNT_BIT_SIZE = 5;
	input wire [4:0] immShiftAmount;
	input wire [4:0] regShiftAmount;
	localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
	input wire [31:0] dataIn;
	input wire carryIn;
	output reg [31:0] dataOut;
	output reg carryOut;
	function automatic [32:0] OpImmShift;
		input reg [1:0] shiftType;
		input reg [4:0] shiftAmount;
		input reg [31:0] dataIn;
		input reg carryIn;
		reg [BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH:0] shiftTmp;
		reg [32:0] result;
		reg [31:0] dataOut;
		reg carryOut;
		begin
			case (shiftType)
				default:
					if (shiftAmount == 0) begin
						dataOut = dataIn;
						carryOut = carryIn;
					end
					else begin
						shiftTmp = dataIn << shiftAmount;
						dataOut = shiftTmp[31:0];
						carryOut = shiftTmp[BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH];
					end
				2'b01:
					if (shiftAmount == 0) begin
						dataOut = 0;
						carryOut = dataIn[31];
					end
					else begin
						shiftTmp = {dataIn, 1'b0} >> shiftAmount;
						dataOut = shiftTmp[BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH:1];
						carryOut = shiftTmp[0];
					end
				2'b10:
					if (shiftAmount == 0) begin
						if (dataIn[31] == 0) begin
							dataOut = 0;
							carryOut = dataIn[31];
						end
						else begin
							dataOut = 32'hffffffff;
							carryOut = dataIn[31];
						end
					end
					else begin
						shiftTmp = {{BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH {dataIn[31]}}, dataIn, 1'b0} >> shiftAmount;
						dataOut = shiftTmp[BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH:1];
						carryOut = shiftTmp[0];
					end
				2'b11:
					if (shiftAmount == 0) begin
						dataOut = {carryIn, dataIn[31:1]};
						carryOut = dataIn[0];
					end
					else begin
						shiftTmp = {dataIn, dataIn, 1'b0} >> shiftAmount;
						dataOut = shiftTmp[BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH:1];
						carryOut = shiftTmp[0];
					end
			endcase
			result[32-:32] = dataOut;
			result[0] = carryOut;
			OpImmShift = result;
		end
	endfunction
	function automatic [32:0] OpRegShift;
		input reg [1:0] shiftType;
		input reg [4:0] shiftAmount;
		input reg [31:0] dataIn;
		input reg [4:0] shiftIn;
		input reg carryIn;
		reg [BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH:0] shiftTmp;
		reg [31:0] dataOut;
		reg carryOut;
		reg [32:0] result;
		begin
			case (shiftType)
				default:
					if (shiftIn == 0) begin
						dataOut = dataIn;
						carryOut = carryIn;
					end
					else begin
						shiftTmp = dataIn << shiftIn;
						dataOut = shiftTmp[31:0];
						carryOut = shiftTmp[BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH];
					end
				2'b01:
					if (shiftIn == 0) begin
						dataOut = 0;
						carryOut = dataIn[31];
					end
					else begin
						shiftTmp = {dataIn, 1'b0} >> shiftIn;
						dataOut = shiftTmp[BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH:1];
						carryOut = shiftTmp[0];
					end
				2'b10:
					if (shiftIn == 0) begin
						if (dataIn[31] == 0) begin
							dataOut = 0;
							carryOut = dataIn[31];
						end
						else begin
							dataOut = 32'hffffffff;
							carryOut = dataIn[31];
						end
					end
					else begin
						shiftTmp = {{BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH {dataIn[31]}}, dataIn, 1'b0} >> shiftAmount;
						dataOut = shiftTmp[BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH:1];
						carryOut = shiftTmp[0];
					end
				2'b11:
					if (shiftIn == 0) begin
						dataOut = {carryIn, dataIn[31:1]};
						carryOut = dataIn[0];
					end
					else begin
						shiftTmp = {dataIn, dataIn, 1'b0} >> shiftIn;
						dataOut = shiftTmp[BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH:1];
						carryOut = shiftTmp[0];
					end
			endcase
			result[32-:32] = dataOut;
			result[0] = carryOut;
			OpRegShift = result;
		end
	endfunction
	reg [32:0] shiftResult;
	always @(*) begin
		case (shiftType)
			default: shiftResult = OpImmShift(shiftType, immShiftAmount, dataIn, carryIn);
			1'b1: shiftResult = OpRegShift(shiftType, immShiftAmount, dataIn, regShiftAmount, carryIn);
		endcase
		dataOut = shiftResult[32-:32];
		carryOut = shiftResult[0];
	end
endmodule
module Shifter (
	shiftOperandType,
	shiftType,
	immShiftAmount,
	regShiftAmount,
	dataIn,
	carryIn,
	dataOut,
	carryOut
);
	input wire shiftOperandType;
	input wire [1:0] shiftType;
	localparam BasicTypes_SHIFT_AMOUNT_BIT_SIZE = 5;
	input wire [4:0] immShiftAmount;
	input wire [4:0] regShiftAmount;
	localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
	input wire [31:0] dataIn;
	input wire carryIn;
	output reg [31:0] dataOut;
	output reg carryOut;
	reg [4:0] shiftAmount;
	reg [65:0] unifiedShiftTmp;
	reg [31:0] unifiedShiftHighIn;
	reg [31:0] unifiedShiftLowIn;
	reg [33:0] unifiedShiftOut;
	reg [BasicTypes_SHIFT_AMOUNT_BIT_SIZE:0] unifiedShiftAmount;
	reg isShiftZero;
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
	always @(*) begin
		case (shiftOperandType)
			default: shiftAmount = immShiftAmount;
			1'b1: shiftAmount = regShiftAmount;
		endcase
		isShiftZero = (shiftAmount == 0 ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
		case (shiftType)
			2'b00: begin
				unifiedShiftAmount = BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH - shiftAmount;
				unifiedShiftHighIn = dataIn;
				unifiedShiftLowIn = 0;
			end
			2'b01: begin
				unifiedShiftAmount = shiftAmount;
				unifiedShiftHighIn = 0;
				unifiedShiftLowIn = dataIn;
			end
			2'b10: begin
				unifiedShiftAmount = shiftAmount;
				unifiedShiftHighIn = {BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH {dataIn[31]}};
				unifiedShiftLowIn = dataIn;
			end
			2'b11: begin
				unifiedShiftAmount = shiftAmount;
				unifiedShiftHighIn = {dataIn[31:1], (isShiftZero ? carryIn : dataIn[0])};
				unifiedShiftLowIn = dataIn;
			end
		endcase
		unifiedShiftTmp = {carryIn, unifiedShiftHighIn, unifiedShiftLowIn, 1'b0};
		case (unifiedShiftAmount[5:4])
			2'h3: unifiedShiftTmp = 49'hxxxxxxxxxxxxx;
			2'h2: unifiedShiftTmp = {15'hxxxx, unifiedShiftTmp[65:32]};
			2'h1: unifiedShiftTmp = unifiedShiftTmp[64:16];
			2'h0: unifiedShiftTmp = unifiedShiftTmp[48:0];
		endcase
		case (unifiedShiftAmount[3:2])
			2'h3: unifiedShiftTmp = unifiedShiftTmp[48:12];
			2'h2: unifiedShiftTmp = unifiedShiftTmp[44:8];
			2'h1: unifiedShiftTmp = unifiedShiftTmp[40:4];
			2'h0: unifiedShiftTmp = unifiedShiftTmp[36:0];
		endcase
		case (unifiedShiftAmount[1:0])
			2'h3: unifiedShiftTmp = unifiedShiftTmp[36:3];
			2'h2: unifiedShiftTmp = unifiedShiftTmp[35:2];
			2'h1: unifiedShiftTmp = unifiedShiftTmp[34:1];
			2'h0: unifiedShiftTmp = unifiedShiftTmp[33:0];
		endcase
		unifiedShiftOut = unifiedShiftTmp;
		dataOut = unifiedShiftOut[BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH:1];
		case (shiftType)
			2'b00: carryOut = unifiedShiftOut[33];
			default: carryOut = unifiedShiftOut[0];
		endcase
	end
endmodule
module MultiplierUnit (
	fuOpA_In,
	fuOpB_In,
	getUpper,
	dataOut
);
	localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
	input wire [31:0] fuOpA_In;
	input wire [31:0] fuOpB_In;
	input wire getUpper;
	output reg [31:0] dataOut;
	wire [63:0] mulResult;
	Multiplier #(.BIT_WIDTH(BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH)) mul(
		.srcA(fuOpA_In),
		.srcB(fuOpB_In),
		.dst(mulResult)
	);
	always @(*)
		if (getUpper)
			dataOut = mulResult[63:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH];
		else
			dataOut = mulResult[31:0];
endmodule
module PipelinedMultiplierUnit (
	clk,
	stall,
	fuOpA_In,
	fuOpB_In,
	getUpper,
	mulCode,
	dataOut
);
	parameter BIT_WIDTH = 32;
	parameter PIPELINE_DEPTH = 3;
	input wire clk;
	input wire stall;
	localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
	input wire [31:0] fuOpA_In;
	input wire [31:0] fuOpB_In;
	input wire getUpper;
	input wire [1:0] mulCode;
	output reg [31:0] dataOut;
	reg [4:0] pipeReg [0:PIPELINE_DEPTH - 2];
	wire [63:0] mulResult;
	wire signed [31:0] fuOpA_signed;
	wire signed [31:0] fuOpB_signed;
	wire fuOpA_sign;
	wire fuOpB_sign;
	reg signA;
	reg signB;
	reg [31:0] srcA;
	reg [31:0] srcB;
	PipelinedMultiplier #(
		.BIT_WIDTH(BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH),
		.PIPELINE_DEPTH(PIPELINE_DEPTH)
	) mul(
		.clk(clk),
		.stall(stall),
		.srcA(srcA),
		.srcB(srcB),
		.signA(signA),
		.signB(signB),
		.dst(mulResult)
	);
	always @(*) begin
		if (mulCode == 2'b11)
			signA = 1'sb0;
		else
			signA = 1'sb1;
		if ((mulCode == 2'b11) || (mulCode == 2'b10))
			signB = 1'sb0;
		else
			signB = 1'sb1;
		srcA = fuOpA_In;
		srcB = fuOpB_In;
	end
	always @(*)
		if (pipeReg[PIPELINE_DEPTH - 2][4])
			dataOut = mulResult[63:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH];
		else
			dataOut = mulResult[31:0];
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (stall) begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < (PIPELINE_DEPTH - 1); i = i + 1)
				pipeReg[i] <= pipeReg[i];
		end
		else begin
			pipeReg[0][4] <= getUpper;
			pipeReg[0][3-:2] <= mulCode;
			pipeReg[0][1] <= fuOpA_sign;
			pipeReg[0][0] <= fuOpB_sign;
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_2
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 1; i < (PIPELINE_DEPTH - 1); i = i + 1)
					pipeReg[i] <= pipeReg[i - 1];
			end
		end
endmodule
module DividerUnit (
	clk,
	rst,
	req,
	fuOpA_In,
	fuOpB_In,
	divCode,
	finished,
	dataOut
);
	input wire clk;
	input wire rst;
	input wire req;
	localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
	input wire [31:0] fuOpA_In;
	input wire [31:0] fuOpB_In;
	input wire [1:0] divCode;
	output wire finished;
	output reg [31:0] dataOut;
	reg regIsSigned;
	reg nextIsSigned;
	reg [31:0] regDividend;
	reg [31:0] nextDividend;
	reg [31:0] regDivisor;
	reg [31:0] nextDivisor;
	reg [1:0] regDivCode;
	reg [1:0] nextDivCode;
	wire [31:0] quotient;
	wire [31:0] remainder;
	QuickDivider div(
		.clk(clk),
		.rst(rst),
		.req(req),
		.dividend(nextDividend),
		.divisor(nextDivisor),
		.isSigned(nextIsSigned),
		.finished(finished),
		.quotient(quotient),
		.remainder(remainder)
	);
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (rst) begin
			regIsSigned <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
			regDividend <= 1'sb0;
			regDivisor <= 1'sb0;
			regDivCode <= 2'b00;
		end
		else begin
			regIsSigned <= nextIsSigned;
			regDividend <= nextDividend;
			regDivisor <= nextDivisor;
			regDivCode <= nextDivCode;
		end
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
	always @(*) begin
		if (req) begin
			if ((divCode == 2'b01) || (divCode == 2'b11))
				nextIsSigned = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			else
				nextIsSigned = BasicTypes_TRUE;
			nextDividend = fuOpA_In;
			nextDivisor = fuOpB_In;
			nextDivCode = divCode;
		end
		else begin
			nextIsSigned = regIsSigned;
			nextDividend = regDividend;
			nextDivisor = regDivisor;
			nextDivCode = regDivCode;
		end
		if ((regDivCode == 2'b00) || (regDivCode == 2'b01))
			dataOut = quotient;
		else
			dataOut = remainder;
	end
endmodule
module PipelinedDividerUnit (
	clk,
	stall,
	req,
	fuOpA_In,
	fuOpB_In,
	divCode,
	finished,
	dataOut
);
	parameter BIT_WIDTH = 32;
	parameter PIPELINE_DEPTH = 3;
	input wire clk;
	input wire stall;
	input wire req;
	localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
	input wire [31:0] fuOpA_In;
	input wire [31:0] fuOpB_In;
	input wire [1:0] divCode;
	output reg finished;
	output reg [31:0] dataOut;
	reg [3:0] pipeReg [0:PIPELINE_DEPTH - 2];
	reg [3:0] nextReg;
	wire [31:0] quotient;
	wire [31:0] remainder;
	reg isSigned;
	reg [31:0] dividend;
	reg [31:0] divisor;
	PipelinedRefDivider #(
		.BIT_WIDTH(BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH),
		.PIPELINE_DEPTH(PIPELINE_DEPTH)
	) div(
		.clk(clk),
		.stall(stall),
		.dividend(dividend),
		.divisor(divisor),
		.isSigned(isSigned),
		.quotient(quotient),
		.remainder(remainder)
	);
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (!stall) begin
			pipeReg[0] <= nextReg;
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 1; i < (PIPELINE_DEPTH - 1); i = i + 1)
					pipeReg[i] <= pipeReg[i - 1];
			end
		end
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
	always @(*) begin
		if ((divCode == 2'b01) || (divCode == 2'b11))
			isSigned = BasicTypes_TRUE;
		else
			isSigned = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		dividend = fuOpA_In;
		divisor = fuOpB_In;
		nextReg[2] = isSigned;
		nextReg[1-:2] = divCode;
		nextReg[3] = req;
		if (pipeReg[PIPELINE_DEPTH - 2][3]) begin
			if ((pipeReg[PIPELINE_DEPTH - 2][1-:2] == 2'b00) || (pipeReg[PIPELINE_DEPTH - 2][1-:2] == 2'b01))
				dataOut = quotient;
			else
				dataOut = remainder;
			finished = BasicTypes_TRUE;
		end
		else begin
			dataOut = 1'sb0;
			finished = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		end
	end
endmodule
module PipelinedVectorAdder (
	clk,
	stall,
	fuOpA_In,
	fuOpB_In,
	dataOut
);
	parameter PIPELINE_DEPTH = 3;
	input wire clk;
	input wire stall;
	localparam BasicTypes_VEC_WIDTH = 128;
	input wire [127:0] fuOpA_In;
	input wire [127:0] fuOpB_In;
	output reg [127:0] dataOut;
	reg [127:0] pipeReg [0:PIPELINE_DEPTH - 2];
	localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
	localparam BasicTypes_VEC_WORD_WIDTH = 4;
	reg [127:0] srcA;
	reg [127:0] srcB;
	reg [127:0] dst;
	always @(*) begin
		srcA = fuOpA_In;
		srcB = fuOpB_In;
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_VEC_WORD_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
				dst[i * BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] = srcA[i * BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH] + srcB[i * BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH];
		end
		dataOut = pipeReg[PIPELINE_DEPTH - 2];
	end
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (stall) begin : sv2v_autoblock_2
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < (PIPELINE_DEPTH - 1); i = i + 1)
				pipeReg[i] <= pipeReg[i];
		end
		else begin
			pipeReg[0] <= dst;
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_3
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 1; i < (PIPELINE_DEPTH - 1); i = i + 1)
					pipeReg[i] <= pipeReg[i - 1];
			end
		end
endmodule
module PipelinedVectorMultiplier (
	clk,
	stall,
	fuOpA_In,
	fuOpB_In,
	getUpper,
	mulCode,
	dataOut
);
	parameter PIPELINE_DEPTH = 3;
	input wire clk;
	input wire stall;
	localparam BasicTypes_VEC_WIDTH = 128;
	input wire [127:0] fuOpA_In;
	input wire [127:0] fuOpB_In;
	input wire getUpper;
	input wire [1:0] mulCode;
	output reg [127:0] dataOut;
	localparam BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH = 32;
	localparam BasicTypes_VEC_WORD_WIDTH = 4;
	reg [127:0] srcA;
	reg [127:0] srcB;
	wire [127:0] dst;
	genvar i;
	generate
		for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_VEC_WORD_WIDTH; i = i + 1) begin : mulUnit
			PipelinedMultiplierUnit #(
				.BIT_WIDTH(BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH),
				.PIPELINE_DEPTH(PIPELINE_DEPTH)
			) mulUnit(
				.clk(clk),
				.stall(stall),
				.fuOpA_In(srcA[i * BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH]),
				.fuOpB_In(srcB[i * BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH]),
				.getUpper(getUpper),
				.mulCode(mulCode),
				.dataOut(dst[i * BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH+:BasicTypes_DATA_WIDTH])
			);
		end
	endgenerate
	always @(*) begin
		srcA = fuOpA_In;
		srcB = fuOpB_In;
		dataOut = dst;
	end
endmodule
module Decoder (
	insn,
	illegalPC,
	microOps,
	insnInfo
);
	localparam BasicTypes_INSN_WIDTH = 32;
	input wire [31:0] insn;
	input wire illegalPC;
	localparam MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM = 3;
	localparam MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_INDEX_BITS = 2;
	localparam BasicTypes_LREG_NUM = 32;
	localparam BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 5;
	localparam OpFormatTypes_BR_DISP_WIDTH = 20;
	localparam BasicTypes_RISCV_SHIFTER_WIDTH = 30;
	localparam OpFormatTypes_CSR_IMM_WIDTH = 5;
	output wire [212:0] microOps;
	output reg [4:0] insnInfo;
	reg [31:0] isf;
	reg [6:0] rv32mFunct7;
	reg undefined;
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
	task automatic EmitInvalidOp;
		output reg [70:0] op;
		begin
			op = 1'sb0;
			op[4] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		end
	endtask
	function automatic [70:0] ModifyMicroOp;
		input reg [70:0] src;
		input reg [1:0] mid;
		input reg split;
		input reg last;
		reg [70:0] op;
		begin
			op = src;
			op[2-:2] = mid;
			op[5] = split;
			op[3] = last;
			ModifyMicroOp = op;
		end
	endfunction
	function automatic [19:0] OpFormatTypes_GetBranchDisplacement;
		input reg [31:0] isfBr;
		OpFormatTypes_GetBranchDisplacement = {{9 {isfBr[31]}}, isfBr[7], isfBr[30-:6], isfBr[11-:4]};
	endfunction
	task automatic OpFormatTypes_RISCV_DecodeBrFunct3;
		output reg [2:0] condCode;
		input reg [2:0] funct3;
		case (funct3)
			3'b000: condCode = 3'b000;
			3'b001: condCode = 3'b001;
			3'b100: condCode = 3'b100;
			3'b101: condCode = 3'b101;
			3'b110: condCode = 3'b110;
			3'b111: condCode = 3'b111;
			default: condCode = 3'b010;
		endcase
	endtask
	function automatic [2:0] sv2v_cast_3;
		input reg [2:0] inp;
		sv2v_cast_3 = inp;
	endfunction
	task automatic RISCV_EmitBranch;
		output reg [70:0] opInfo;
		input reg [31:0] isf;
		reg [31:0] isfR;
		reg [2:0] brFunct3;
		reg [2:0] condCode;
		begin
			isfR = isf;
			brFunct3 = sv2v_cast_3(isfR[14-:3]);
			opInfo[62-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 0;
			opInfo[57-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = isfR[19-:5];
			opInfo[52-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = isfR[24-:5];
			opInfo[8] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[12-:2] = 2'b00;
			opInfo[10-:2] = 2'b00;
			opInfo[67-:2] = 2'b00;
			opInfo[65-:3] = 3'b010;
			OpFormatTypes_RISCV_DecodeBrFunct3(condCode, brFunct3);
			opInfo[70-:3] = condCode;
			opInfo[32-:OpFormatTypes_BR_DISP_WIDTH] = OpFormatTypes_GetBranchDisplacement(isfR);
			opInfo[47-:15] = 1'sb0;
			opInfo[6] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[7] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
		end
	endtask
	task automatic RISCV_DecodeBranch;
		output reg [212:0] microOps;
		output reg [4:0] insnInfo;
		input reg [31:0] isf;
		reg [70:0] brOp;
		reg [1:0] mid;
		begin
			RISCV_EmitBranch(brOp, isf);
			mid = 0;
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; i = i + 1)
					EmitInvalidOp(microOps[i * 71+:71]);
			end
			microOps[71+:71] = ModifyMicroOp(brOp, mid, BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE);
			insnInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
			insnInfo[3] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[1] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
			insnInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		end
	endtask
	localparam BasicTypes_LSCALAR_NUM = 32;
	localparam BasicTypes_LSCALAR_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 5;
	localparam MicroOpTypes_ZERO_REGISTER = 5'h00;
	task automatic OpFormatTypes_RISCV_DecodeComplexOpFunct3;
		output reg [1:0] mulCode;
		output reg [1:0] divCode;
		input reg [2:0] funct3;
		case (funct3)
			3'b000: begin
				mulCode = 2'b00;
				divCode = 2'b00;
			end
			3'b001: begin
				mulCode = 2'b01;
				divCode = 2'b00;
			end
			3'b010: begin
				mulCode = 2'b10;
				divCode = 2'b00;
			end
			3'b011: begin
				mulCode = 2'b11;
				divCode = 2'b00;
			end
			3'b100: begin
				mulCode = 2'b00;
				divCode = 2'b00;
			end
			3'b101: begin
				mulCode = 2'b00;
				divCode = 2'b01;
			end
			3'b110: begin
				mulCode = 2'b00;
				divCode = 2'b10;
			end
			3'b111: begin
				mulCode = 2'b00;
				divCode = 2'b11;
			end
			default: begin
				mulCode = 2'b00;
				divCode = 2'b00;
			end
		endcase
	endtask
	function automatic [6:0] sv2v_cast_7;
		input reg [6:0] inp;
		sv2v_cast_7 = inp;
	endfunction
	task automatic RISCV_EmitComplexOp;
		output reg [70:0] opInfo;
		input reg [31:0] isf;
		input reg [4:0] srcRegNumA;
		input reg [4:0] srcRegNumB;
		input reg [4:0] dstRegNum;
		input reg unsupported;
		reg [31:0] isfR;
		reg [2:0] opFunct3;
		reg [6:0] opFunct7;
		reg [2:0] rv32mFunct3;
		reg [6:0] rv32mFunct7;
		reg isMul;
		reg [1:0] mulCode;
		reg [1:0] divCode;
		begin
			isfR = isf;
			opFunct3 = sv2v_cast_3(isfR[14-:3]);
			opFunct7 = sv2v_cast_7(isfR[31-:7]);
			rv32mFunct3 = sv2v_cast_3(isfR[14-:3]);
			rv32mFunct7 = sv2v_cast_7(isfR[31-:7]);
			isMul = rv32mFunct3 < 3'b100;
			opInfo[62-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = dstRegNum;
			opInfo[57-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = srcRegNumA;
			opInfo[52-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = srcRegNumB;
			opInfo[47] = rv32mFunct3 > 3'b000;
			OpFormatTypes_RISCV_DecodeComplexOpFunct3(mulCode, divCode, rv32mFunct3);
			opInfo[46-:2] = mulCode;
			opInfo[44-:2] = divCode;
			opInfo[42-:16] = 1'sb0;
			opInfo[26-:14] = 1'sb0;
			opInfo[8] = (dstRegNum != MicroOpTypes_ZERO_REGISTER ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
			opInfo[12-:2] = 2'b00;
			opInfo[10-:2] = 2'b00;
			opInfo[67-:2] = 2'b01;
			opInfo[65-:3] = (isMul ? 3'b000 : 3'b001);
			opInfo[70-:3] = 3'b010;
			opInfo[6] = unsupported;
			opInfo[7] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
		end
	endtask
	task automatic RISCV_DecodeComplexOp;
		output reg [212:0] microOps;
		output reg [4:0] insnInfo;
		input reg [31:0] isf;
		reg [70:0] complexOp;
		reg [1:0] mid;
		begin
			RISCV_EmitComplexOp(complexOp, isf, isf[19-:5], isf[24-:5], isf[11-:5], BasicTypes_FALSE);
			mid = 0;
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_2
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; i = i + 1)
					EmitInvalidOp(microOps[i * 71+:71]);
			end
			microOps[71+:71] = ModifyMicroOp(complexOp, mid, BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE);
			mid = mid + 1;
			insnInfo[4] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[3] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[1] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		end
	endtask
	task automatic RISCV_EmitIllegalOp;
		output reg [70:0] opInfo;
		input reg illegalPC;
		reg [31:0] isfSystem;
		reg [2:0] opFunct3;
		reg [11:0] opFunct12;
		reg [49:0] systemOp;
		reg undefined;
		begin
			systemOp[49-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
			systemOp[44-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
			systemOp[39-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
			systemOp[31] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
			systemOp[30-:19] = 1'sb0;
			systemOp[11-:12] = 1'sb0;
			undefined = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			systemOp[34-:3] = (illegalPC ? 3'b100 : 3'b011);
			opInfo[67-:2] = 2'b10;
			opInfo[65-:3] = 3'b110;
			opInfo[62-:50] = systemOp;
			opInfo[8] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[12-:2] = 2'b01;
			opInfo[10-:2] = 2'b01;
			opInfo[70-:3] = 3'b010;
			opInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
			opInfo[6] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[7] = undefined;
			opInfo[0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
		end
	endtask
	task automatic RISCV_DecodeIllegal;
		output reg [212:0] microOps;
		output reg [4:0] insnInfo;
		input reg [31:0] isf;
		input illegalPC;
		reg [70:0] opInfo;
		reg [1:0] mid;
		begin
			RISCV_EmitIllegalOp(opInfo, illegalPC);
			mid = 0;
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_3
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; i = i + 1)
					EmitInvalidOp(microOps[i * 71+:71]);
			end
			microOps[71+:71] = ModifyMicroOp(opInfo, mid, BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE);
			insnInfo[4] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[3] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[1] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
		end
	endtask
	localparam MicroOpTypes_LINK_REGISTER = 5'h01;
	function automatic [19:0] OpFormatTypes_GetJAL_Target;
		input reg [31:0] isfJAL;
		OpFormatTypes_GetJAL_Target = {isfJAL[31], isfJAL[19-:8], isfJAL[20], isfJAL[30-:10]};
	endfunction
	task automatic RISCV_EmitJAL;
		output reg [70:0] opInfo;
		input reg [31:0] isf;
		reg [31:0] isfU;
		begin
			isfU = isf;
			opInfo[62-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = isfU[11-:5];
			opInfo[57-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
			opInfo[52-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
			opInfo[8] = (isfU[11-:5] != MicroOpTypes_ZERO_REGISTER ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
			opInfo[12-:2] = 2'b10;
			opInfo[10-:2] = 2'b01;
			opInfo[67-:2] = 2'b00;
			opInfo[65-:3] = 3'b010;
			opInfo[70-:3] = 3'b010;
			opInfo[32-:OpFormatTypes_BR_DISP_WIDTH] = OpFormatTypes_GetJAL_Target(isf);
			opInfo[47-:15] = 0;
			opInfo[6] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[7] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
		end
	endtask
	task automatic RISCV_DecodeJAL;
		output reg [212:0] microOps;
		output reg [4:0] insnInfo;
		input reg [31:0] isf;
		reg [70:0] brOp;
		reg [1:0] mid;
		begin
			RISCV_EmitJAL(brOp, isf);
			mid = 0;
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_4
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; i = i + 1)
					EmitInvalidOp(microOps[i * 71+:71]);
			end
			microOps[71+:71] = ModifyMicroOp(brOp, mid, BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE);
			insnInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
			insnInfo[3] = (brOp[8] && (brOp[62-:5] == 5'h01) ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
			insnInfo[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[1] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
			insnInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		end
	endtask
	function automatic [19:0] OpFormatTypes_GetJALR_Target;
		input reg [31:0] isfJALR;
		OpFormatTypes_GetJALR_Target = {{8 {isfJALR[31]}}, isfJALR[31-:12]};
	endfunction
	task automatic RISCV_EmitJALR;
		output reg [70:0] opInfo;
		input reg [31:0] isf;
		reg [31:0] isfI;
		begin
			isfI = isf;
			opInfo[62-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = isfI[11-:5];
			opInfo[57-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = isfI[19-:5];
			opInfo[52-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 0;
			opInfo[8] = (isfI[11-:5] != MicroOpTypes_ZERO_REGISTER ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
			opInfo[12-:2] = 2'b00;
			opInfo[10-:2] = 2'b01;
			opInfo[67-:2] = 2'b00;
			opInfo[65-:3] = 3'b011;
			opInfo[70-:3] = 3'b010;
			opInfo[32-:OpFormatTypes_BR_DISP_WIDTH] = OpFormatTypes_GetJALR_Target(isfI);
			opInfo[47-:15] = 1'sb0;
			opInfo[6] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[7] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
		end
	endtask
	task automatic RISCV_DecodeJALR;
		output reg [212:0] microOps;
		output reg [4:0] insnInfo;
		input reg [31:0] isf;
		reg [70:0] brOp;
		reg [1:0] mid;
		begin
			RISCV_EmitJALR(brOp, isf);
			mid = 0;
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_5
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; i = i + 1)
					EmitInvalidOp(microOps[i * 71+:71]);
			end
			microOps[71+:71] = ModifyMicroOp(brOp, mid, BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE);
			insnInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
			insnInfo[3] = (brOp[8] && (brOp[62-:5] == 5'h01) ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
			insnInfo[2] = ((brOp[57-:5] == 5'h01) && (brOp[62-:5] == 5'h00) ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
			insnInfo[1] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		end
	endtask
	task automatic OpFormatTypes_RISCV_DecodeMemAccessMode;
		output reg [2:0] memAccessMode;
		input reg [2:0] funct3;
		case (funct3)
			3'b000: begin
				memAccessMode[2] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
				memAccessMode[1-:2] = 2'b00;
			end
			3'b001: begin
				memAccessMode[2] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
				memAccessMode[1-:2] = 2'b01;
			end
			3'b010: begin
				memAccessMode[2] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
				memAccessMode[1-:2] = 2'b10;
			end
			3'b100: begin
				memAccessMode[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				memAccessMode[1-:2] = 2'b00;
			end
			3'b101: begin
				memAccessMode[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				memAccessMode[1-:2] = 2'b01;
			end
			default: begin
				memAccessMode[2] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
				memAccessMode[1-:2] = 2'b00;
			end
		endcase
	endtask
	task automatic RISCV_EmitMemOp;
		output reg [70:0] opInfo;
		input reg [31:0] isf;
		reg [31:0] isfS;
		reg [31:0] isfI;
		reg [2:0] memFunct3;
		reg isLoad;
		reg [2:0] memAccessMode;
		begin
			isfS = isf;
			isfI = isf;
			memFunct3 = sv2v_cast_3(isfS[14-:3]);
			isLoad = isfI[6-:7] == 7'b0000011;
			opInfo[62-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = (isLoad ? isfI[11-:5] : {5 {1'sb0}});
			opInfo[57-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = isfI[19-:5];
			opInfo[52-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = (isLoad ? {5 {1'sb0}} : isfS[24-:5]);
			opInfo[42-:8] = 1'sb0;
			opInfo[34-:10] = 1'sb0;
			opInfo[8] = (isfI[11-:5] != MicroOpTypes_ZERO_REGISTER) & isLoad;
			opInfo[12-:2] = 2'b00;
			opInfo[10-:2] = (isLoad ? 2'b01 : 2'b00);
			opInfo[67-:2] = 2'b10;
			opInfo[65-:3] = (isLoad ? 3'b000 : 3'b001);
			opInfo[70-:3] = 3'b010;
			opInfo[24-:12] = (isLoad ? isfI[31-:12] : {isfS[31-:7], isfS[11-:5]});
			opInfo[47] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
			opInfo[46] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
			OpFormatTypes_RISCV_DecodeMemAccessMode(memAccessMode, memFunct3);
			opInfo[45-:3] = memAccessMode;
			opInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
			opInfo[6] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[7] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		end
	endtask
	task automatic RISCV_DecodeMemOp;
		output reg [212:0] microOps;
		output reg [4:0] insnInfo;
		input reg [31:0] isf;
		reg [70:0] memOp;
		reg [1:0] mid;
		begin
			RISCV_EmitMemOp(memOp, isf);
			mid = 0;
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_6
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; i = i + 1)
					EmitInvalidOp(microOps[i * 71+:71]);
			end
			microOps[71+:71] = ModifyMicroOp(memOp, mid, BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE);
			insnInfo[4] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[3] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[1] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		end
	endtask
	task automatic RISCV_EmitMiscMemOp;
		output reg [70:0] opInfo;
		input reg [31:0] isf;
		reg [31:0] isfMiscMem;
		reg [2:0] opFunct3;
		reg [49:0] miscMemOp;
		begin
			isfMiscMem = isf;
			opFunct3 = sv2v_cast_3(isfMiscMem[14-:3]);
			miscMemOp[49-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
			miscMemOp[44-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
			miscMemOp[39-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
			miscMemOp[32-:15] = 1'sb0;
			miscMemOp[17-:18] = 1'sb0;
			if (opFunct3 == 3'b000) begin
				miscMemOp[34] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
				miscMemOp[33] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			end
			else if (opFunct3 == 3'b001) begin
				miscMemOp[34] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
				miscMemOp[33] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
			end
			else begin
				miscMemOp[34] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				miscMemOp[33] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			end
			opInfo[62-:50] = miscMemOp;
			opInfo[8] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[12-:2] = 2'b01;
			opInfo[10-:2] = 2'b01;
			opInfo[67-:2] = 2'b10;
			opInfo[65-:3] = 3'b101;
			opInfo[70-:3] = 3'b010;
			opInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
			opInfo[6] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			if (miscMemOp[34])
				opInfo[7] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			else
				opInfo[7] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
			opInfo[0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
		end
	endtask
	task automatic RISCV_DecodeMiscMem;
		output reg [212:0] microOps;
		output reg [4:0] insnInfo;
		input reg [31:0] isf;
		reg [70:0] miscMemOp;
		reg [1:0] mid;
		begin
			RISCV_EmitMiscMemOp(miscMemOp, isf);
			mid = 0;
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_7
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; i = i + 1)
					EmitInvalidOp(microOps[i * 71+:71]);
			end
			microOps[71+:71] = ModifyMicroOp(miscMemOp, mid, BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE);
			insnInfo[4] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[3] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[1] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
		end
	endtask
	task automatic OpFormatTypes_RISCV_DecodeOpFunct3;
		output reg [3:0] aluCode;
		input reg [2:0] funct3;
		input reg [6:0] funct7;
		case (funct3)
			3'b000:
				if (funct7 == 7'b0000000)
					aluCode = 4'b0000;
				else
					aluCode = 4'b0001;
			3'b010: aluCode = 4'b0010;
			3'b011: aluCode = 4'b0011;
			3'b100: aluCode = 4'b0100;
			3'b110: aluCode = 4'b0110;
			3'b111: aluCode = 4'b0111;
			3'b001: aluCode = 4'b0111;
			3'b101: aluCode = 4'b0111;
			default: aluCode = 4'b0111;
		endcase
	endtask
	task automatic RISCV_EmitOp;
		output reg [70:0] opInfo;
		input reg [31:0] isf;
		input reg [4:0] srcRegNumA;
		input reg [4:0] srcRegNumB;
		input reg [4:0] dstRegNum;
		input reg unsupported;
		reg [31:0] isfR;
		reg [2:0] opFunct3;
		reg [6:0] opFunct7;
		reg [6:0] shiftFunct7;
		reg [29:0] intOperandImmShift;
		reg isShift;
		reg [3:0] aluCode;
		begin
			isfR = isf;
			opFunct3 = sv2v_cast_3(isfR[14-:3]);
			opFunct7 = sv2v_cast_7(isfR[31-:7]);
			shiftFunct7 = sv2v_cast_7(isfR[31-:7]);
			isShift = (opFunct3 == 3'b001) || (opFunct3 == 3'b101);
			opInfo[62-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = dstRegNum;
			opInfo[57-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = srcRegNumA;
			opInfo[52-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = srcRegNumB;
			opInfo[43] = 1'b1;
			intOperandImmShift[29-:5] = (isShift ? isfR[24-:5] : {5 {1'sb0}});
			intOperandImmShift[24-:2] = (opFunct3 == 3'b001 ? 2'b00 : ((opFunct3 == 3'b101) && (shiftFunct7 == 7'b0000000) ? 2'b01 : ((opFunct3 == 3'b101) && (shiftFunct7 == 7'b0100000) ? 2'b10 : 2'b11)));
			intOperandImmShift[22] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
			intOperandImmShift[21-:20] = 1'sb0;
			intOperandImmShift[1-:2] = 2'b00;
			opInfo[42-:BasicTypes_RISCV_SHIFTER_WIDTH] = intOperandImmShift;
			OpFormatTypes_RISCV_DecodeOpFunct3(aluCode, opFunct3, opFunct7);
			opInfo[47-:4] = aluCode;
			opInfo[8] = (dstRegNum != MicroOpTypes_ZERO_REGISTER ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
			opInfo[12-:2] = 2'b00;
			opInfo[10-:2] = 2'b00;
			opInfo[67-:2] = 2'b00;
			opInfo[65-:3] = (isShift ? 3'b001 : 3'b000);
			opInfo[70-:3] = 3'b010;
			opInfo[6] = unsupported;
			opInfo[7] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
		end
	endtask
	task automatic RISCV_DecodeOp;
		output reg [212:0] microOps;
		output reg [4:0] insnInfo;
		input reg [31:0] isf;
		reg [70:0] intOp;
		reg [70:0] shiftOp;
		reg [70:0] rijOp;
		reg [70:0] selectOp;
		reg [1:0] mid;
		begin
			RISCV_EmitOp(intOp, isf, isf[19-:5], isf[24-:5], isf[11-:5], BasicTypes_FALSE);
			mid = 0;
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_8
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; i = i + 1)
					EmitInvalidOp(microOps[i * 71+:71]);
			end
			microOps[71+:71] = ModifyMicroOp(intOp, mid, BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE);
			mid = mid + 1;
			insnInfo[4] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[3] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[1] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		end
	endtask
	function automatic [19:0] OpFormatTypes_I_TypeImmExtention;
		input reg [31:0] isfI;
		OpFormatTypes_I_TypeImmExtention = {{8 {isfI[31]}}, isfI[31-:12]};
	endfunction
	task automatic OpFormatTypes_RISCV_DecodeOpImmFunct3;
		output reg [3:0] aluCode;
		input reg [2:0] funct3;
		case (funct3)
			3'b000: aluCode = 4'b0000;
			3'b010: aluCode = 4'b0010;
			3'b011: aluCode = 4'b0011;
			3'b100: aluCode = 4'b0100;
			3'b110: aluCode = 4'b0110;
			3'b111: aluCode = 4'b0111;
			3'b001: aluCode = 4'b0111;
			3'b101: aluCode = 4'b0111;
			default: aluCode = 4'b0111;
		endcase
	endtask
	function automatic [19:0] OpFormatTypes_ShamtExtention;
		input reg [31:0] isfR;
		OpFormatTypes_ShamtExtention = {15'h0000, isfR[24-:5]};
	endfunction
	task automatic RISCV_EmitOpImm;
		output reg [70:0] opInfo;
		input reg [31:0] isf;
		input reg [4:0] srcRegNumA;
		input reg [4:0] srcRegNumB;
		input reg [4:0] dstRegNum;
		input reg unsupported;
		reg [31:0] isfI;
		reg [31:0] isfR;
		reg [2:0] opFunct3;
		reg [6:0] shiftFunct7;
		reg [29:0] intOperandImmShift;
		reg isShift;
		reg [3:0] aluCode;
		begin
			isfI = isf;
			isfR = isf;
			opFunct3 = sv2v_cast_3(isfR[14-:3]);
			shiftFunct7 = sv2v_cast_7(isfR[31-:7]);
			isShift = (opFunct3 == 3'b001) || (opFunct3 == 3'b101);
			opInfo[62-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = dstRegNum;
			opInfo[57-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = srcRegNumA;
			opInfo[52-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = srcRegNumB;
			opInfo[43] = 1'b0;
			intOperandImmShift[29-:5] = (isShift ? isfR[24-:5] : {5 {1'sb0}});
			intOperandImmShift[24-:2] = (opFunct3 == 3'b001 ? 2'b00 : ((opFunct3 == 3'b101) && (shiftFunct7 == 7'b0000000) ? 2'b01 : ((opFunct3 == 3'b101) && (shiftFunct7 == 7'b0100000) ? 2'b10 : 2'b11)));
			intOperandImmShift[22] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			intOperandImmShift[21-:20] = (isShift ? OpFormatTypes_ShamtExtention(isfR) : OpFormatTypes_I_TypeImmExtention(isfI));
			intOperandImmShift[1-:2] = 2'b01;
			opInfo[42-:BasicTypes_RISCV_SHIFTER_WIDTH] = intOperandImmShift;
			OpFormatTypes_RISCV_DecodeOpImmFunct3(aluCode, opFunct3);
			opInfo[47-:4] = aluCode;
			opInfo[8] = (dstRegNum != MicroOpTypes_ZERO_REGISTER ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
			opInfo[12-:2] = 2'b00;
			opInfo[10-:2] = 2'b01;
			opInfo[67-:2] = 2'b00;
			opInfo[65-:3] = (isShift ? 3'b001 : 3'b000);
			opInfo[70-:3] = 3'b010;
			opInfo[6] = unsupported;
			opInfo[7] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
		end
	endtask
	task automatic RISCV_DecodeOpImm;
		output reg [212:0] microOps;
		output reg [4:0] insnInfo;
		input reg [31:0] isf;
		reg [70:0] intOp;
		reg [70:0] shiftOp;
		reg [70:0] rijOp;
		reg [70:0] selectOp;
		reg [1:0] mid;
		begin
			RISCV_EmitOpImm(intOp, isf, isf[19-:5], 0, isf[11-:5], BasicTypes_FALSE);
			mid = 0;
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_9
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; i = i + 1)
					EmitInvalidOp(microOps[i * 71+:71]);
			end
			microOps[71+:71] = ModifyMicroOp(intOp, mid, BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE);
			mid = mid + 1;
			insnInfo[4] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[3] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[1] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		end
	endtask
	task automatic RISCV_EmitCSR_Op;
		output reg [70:0] opInfo;
		input reg [31:0] isf;
		reg [31:0] isfSystem;
		reg [2:0] opFunct3;
		reg [49:0] memOp;
		begin
			isfSystem = isf;
			opFunct3 = sv2v_cast_3(isfSystem[14-:3]);
			memOp[49-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = isfSystem[11-:5];
			memOp[44-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = isfSystem[19-:5];
			memOp[39-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
			memOp[21-:10] = 1'sb0;
			memOp[34] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			memOp[33] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			memOp[32-:3] = 1'sb0;
			case (opFunct3)
				default: memOp[23-:2] = 2'b01;
				3'b001, 3'b101: memOp[23-:2] = 2'b01;
				3'b010, 3'b110: memOp[23-:2] = 2'b10;
				3'b011, 3'b111: memOp[23-:2] = 2'b11;
			endcase
			memOp[11-:12] = isfSystem[31-:12];
			memOp[24] = (|{opFunct3 == 3'b001, opFunct3 == 3'b010, opFunct3 == 3'b011} ? BasicTypes_FALSE : BasicTypes_TRUE);
			memOp[29-:5] = isfSystem[19-:5];
			opInfo[62-:50] = memOp;
			opInfo[8] = (isfSystem[11-:5] != MicroOpTypes_ZERO_REGISTER ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
			opInfo[12-:2] = 2'b00;
			opInfo[10-:2] = 2'b01;
			opInfo[67-:2] = 2'b10;
			opInfo[65-:3] = 3'b100;
			opInfo[70-:3] = 3'b010;
			opInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
			opInfo[6] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[7] = (opFunct3 == 3'b100 ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
			opInfo[0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
		end
	endtask
	function automatic [11:0] sv2v_cast_12;
		input reg [11:0] inp;
		sv2v_cast_12 = inp;
	endfunction
	task automatic RISCV_EmitSystemOp;
		output reg [70:0] opInfo;
		input reg [31:0] isf;
		reg [31:0] isfSystem;
		reg [2:0] opFunct3;
		reg [11:0] opFunct12;
		reg [49:0] systemOp;
		reg undefined;
		begin
			isfSystem = isf;
			opFunct3 = sv2v_cast_3(isfSystem[14-:3]);
			opFunct12 = sv2v_cast_12(isfSystem[31-:12]);
			systemOp[49-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
			systemOp[44-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
			systemOp[39-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = 1'sb0;
			systemOp[31] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
			systemOp[30-:19] = 1'sb0;
			systemOp[11-:12] = 1'sb0;
			undefined = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			if (opFunct12 == 12'b000100000101) begin
				opInfo[67-:2] = 2'b00;
				opInfo[65-:3] = 3'b000;
				systemOp[34-:3] = 3'b001;
			end
			else begin
				case (sv2v_cast_12(isfSystem[31-:12]))
					12'b000000000000: systemOp[34-:3] = 3'b000;
					12'b000000000001: systemOp[34-:3] = 3'b001;
					12'b001100000010: systemOp[34-:3] = 3'b010;
					default: begin
						systemOp[34-:3] = 3'b001;
						undefined = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					end
				endcase
				opInfo[67-:2] = 2'b10;
				opInfo[65-:3] = 3'b110;
			end
			opInfo[62-:50] = systemOp;
			opInfo[8] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[12-:2] = 2'b01;
			opInfo[10-:2] = 2'b01;
			opInfo[70-:3] = 3'b010;
			opInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
			opInfo[6] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[7] = undefined;
			opInfo[0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
		end
	endtask
	task automatic RISCV_DecodeSystem;
		output reg [212:0] microOps;
		output reg [4:0] insnInfo;
		input reg [31:0] isf;
		reg [70:0] opInfo;
		reg [1:0] mid;
		begin
			if (sv2v_cast_3(isf[14-:3]) == 3'b000)
				RISCV_EmitSystemOp(opInfo, isf);
			else
				RISCV_EmitCSR_Op(opInfo, isf);
			mid = 0;
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_10
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; i = i + 1)
					EmitInvalidOp(microOps[i * 71+:71]);
			end
			microOps[71+:71] = ModifyMicroOp(opInfo, mid, BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE);
			insnInfo[4] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[3] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[1] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
		end
	endtask
	task automatic RISCV_EmitUTypeInst;
		output reg [70:0] opInfo;
		input reg [31:0] isf;
		input reg [4:0] srcRegNumA;
		input reg [4:0] srcRegNumB;
		input reg [4:0] dstRegNum;
		input reg unsupported;
		reg [31:0] isfU;
		reg [29:0] intOperandImmShift;
		begin
			isfU = isf;
			opInfo[62-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = dstRegNum;
			opInfo[57-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = srcRegNumA;
			opInfo[52-:BasicTypes_LREG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = srcRegNumB;
			opInfo[43] = 1'b1;
			intOperandImmShift[29-:5] = 1'sb0;
			intOperandImmShift[24-:2] = 2'b11;
			intOperandImmShift[22] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
			intOperandImmShift[21-:20] = isfU[31-:20];
			intOperandImmShift[1-:2] = 2'b11;
			opInfo[42-:BasicTypes_RISCV_SHIFTER_WIDTH] = intOperandImmShift;
			opInfo[47-:4] = 4'b0000;
			opInfo[8] = (dstRegNum != MicroOpTypes_ZERO_REGISTER ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
			opInfo[12-:2] = (isf[6-:7] == 7'b0010111 ? 2'b10 : 2'b00);
			opInfo[10-:2] = 2'b01;
			opInfo[67-:2] = 2'b00;
			opInfo[65-:3] = 3'b000;
			opInfo[70-:3] = 3'b010;
			opInfo[6] = unsupported;
			opInfo[7] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			opInfo[4] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
		end
	endtask
	task automatic RISCV_DecodeUTypeInst;
		output reg [212:0] microOps;
		output reg [4:0] insnInfo;
		input reg [31:0] isf;
		reg [70:0] intOp;
		reg [1:0] mid;
		begin
			RISCV_EmitUTypeInst(intOp, isf, 0, 0, isf[11-:5], BasicTypes_FALSE);
			mid = 0;
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_11
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; i = i + 1)
					EmitInvalidOp(microOps[i * 71+:71]);
			end
			microOps[71+:71] = ModifyMicroOp(intOp, mid, BasicTypes_FALSE, BasicTypes_TRUE);
			mid = mid + 1;
			insnInfo[4] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[3] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[1] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			insnInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		end
	endtask
	always @(*) begin
		isf = insn;
		rv32mFunct7 = sv2v_cast_7(isf[31-:7]);
		insnInfo[4] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		insnInfo[3] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		insnInfo[2] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		insnInfo[1] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		insnInfo[0] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		case (isf[6-:7])
			7'b0000011, 7'b0100011:
				RISCV_DecodeMemOp(microOps, insnInfo, insn);
			7'b1100011:
				RISCV_DecodeBranch(microOps, insnInfo, insn);
			7'b1100111:
				RISCV_DecodeJALR(microOps, insnInfo, insn);
			7'b1101111:
				RISCV_DecodeJAL(microOps, insnInfo, insn);
			7'b0010011:
				RISCV_DecodeOpImm(microOps, insnInfo, insn);
			7'b0110011:
				if (rv32mFunct7 == 7'b0000001)
					RISCV_DecodeComplexOp(microOps, insnInfo, insn);
				else
					RISCV_DecodeOp(microOps, insnInfo, insn);
			7'b0010111, 7'b0110111:
				RISCV_DecodeUTypeInst(microOps, insnInfo, insn);
			7'b0001111:
				RISCV_DecodeMiscMem(microOps, insnInfo, insn);
			7'b1110011:
				RISCV_DecodeSystem(microOps, insnInfo, insn);
			default:
				RISCV_DecodeIllegal(microOps, insnInfo, insn, illegalPC);
		endcase
		undefined = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_12
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_MAX_NUM; i = i + 1)
				if (microOps[(i * 71) + 7] || microOps[(i * 71) + 6])
					undefined = BasicTypes_TRUE;
		end
		if (undefined || illegalPC)
			RISCV_DecodeIllegal(microOps, insnInfo, insn, illegalPC);
	end
endmodule
module DecodedBranchResolver (
	clk,
	rst,
	stall,
	decodeComplete,
	insnValidIn,
	isf,
	brPredIn,
	pc,
	insnInfo,
	insnValidOut,
	insnFlushed,
	flush,
	brPredOut,
	recoveredPC
);
	input wire clk;
	input wire rst;
	input wire stall;
	input wire decodeComplete;
	localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
	localparam BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;
	input wire [0:1] insnValidIn;
	input wire [63:0] isf;
	localparam FetchUnitTypes_BRANCH_GLOBAL_HISTORY_BIT_WIDTH = 10;
	localparam FetchUnitTypes_PHT_ENTRY_WIDTH = 2;
	localparam MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH = 19;
	input wire [63:0] brPredIn;
	input wire [37:0] pc;
	input wire [9:0] insnInfo;
	output reg [0:1] insnValidOut;
	output reg [0:1] insnFlushed;
	output reg flush;
	output reg [63:0] brPredOut;
	output reg [18:0] recoveredPC;
	parameter RAS_ENTRY_NUM = 4;
	reg [18:0] ras [0:RAS_ENTRY_NUM - 1];
	reg [18:0] nextRAS;
	reg pushRAS;
	reg popRAS;
	reg [$clog2(RAS_ENTRY_NUM) - 1:0] rasPtr;
	reg [$clog2(RAS_ENTRY_NUM) - 1:0] nextRAS_Ptr;
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (rst) begin
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < RAS_ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)
					ras[i] <= 1'sb0;
			end
			rasPtr <= 1'sb0;
		end
		else if (!stall && decodeComplete) begin
			if (pushRAS)
				ras[nextRAS_Ptr] <= nextRAS;
			rasPtr <= nextRAS_Ptr;
		end
	reg [18:0] decodedPC [0:1];
	reg [18:0] nextPC [0:1];
	reg [31:0] isfU [0:1];
	reg addrCheck;
	reg addrIncorrect;
	localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = 1;
	localparam BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH_BIT_SIZE;
	reg [0:0] addrCheckLane;
	reg [1:0] brTargetType [0:1];
	reg addrMismatch [0:1];
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
	localparam BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH = 8;
	localparam BasicTypes_INSN_WIDTH = 32;
	localparam BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH = 4;
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
	localparam BasicTypes_ADDR_WIDTH = 32;
	localparam OpFormatTypes_BR_DISP_WIDTH = 20;
	function automatic [31:0] OpFormatTypes_ExtendBranchDisplacement;
		input reg [19:0] brDisp;
		OpFormatTypes_ExtendBranchDisplacement = {{11 {brDisp[19]}}, brDisp, 1'b0};
	endfunction
	function automatic [19:0] OpFormatTypes_GetBranchDisplacement;
		input reg [31:0] isfBr;
		OpFormatTypes_GetBranchDisplacement = {{9 {isfBr[31]}}, isfBr[7], isfBr[30-:6], isfBr[11-:4]};
	endfunction
	function automatic [19:0] OpFormatTypes_GetJAL_Target;
		input reg [31:0] isfJAL;
		OpFormatTypes_GetJAL_Target = {isfJAL[31], isfJAL[19-:8], isfJAL[20], isfJAL[30-:10]};
	endfunction
	always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_2
		reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;
		_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
		flush = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		recoveredPC = 1'sb0;
		pushRAS = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		popRAS = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		nextRAS = 0;
		nextRAS_Ptr = rasPtr;
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_3
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
				begin
					insnValidOut[i] = insnValidIn[i];
					insnFlushed[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					brPredOut[(1 - i) * 32+:32] = brPredIn[i * 32+:32];
					isfU[i] = isf[i * 32+:32];
				end
		end
		addrCheck = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		addrIncorrect = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		addrCheckLane = 1'sb0;
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_4
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
				if (insnInfo[(i * 5) + 1])
					brTargetType[i] = 2'd1;
				else if (insnInfo[(i * 5) + 4])
					brTargetType[i] = 2'd2;
				else if (insnInfo[i * 5])
					brTargetType[i] = 2'd3;
				else
					brTargetType[i] = 2'd0;
		end
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_5
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
				if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
					_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
					if (!insnValidIn[i])
						_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
					if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00)
						if (!insnInfo[(i * 5) + 4] && brPredIn[(i * 32) + 12]) begin
							addrCheckLane = i;
							addrCheck = BasicTypes_TRUE;
							addrIncorrect = BasicTypes_TRUE;
							_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
						end
						else if ((brTargetType[i] == 2'd1) && ((isfU[i][6-:7] == 7'b1101111) || brPredIn[(i * 32) + 12])) begin
							pushRAS = insnInfo[(i * 5) + 3];
							addrCheckLane = i;
							addrCheck = BasicTypes_TRUE;
							_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
						end
						else if (brTargetType[i] == 2'd2) begin
							pushRAS = insnInfo[(i * 5) + 3];
							popRAS = insnInfo[(i * 5) + 2];
							if (popRAS) begin
								addrCheckLane = i;
								addrCheck = BasicTypes_TRUE;
								_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
							end
							else if (pushRAS) begin
								addrCheckLane = i;
								_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
							end
						end
						else if (brTargetType[i] == 2'd3) begin
							addrCheckLane = i;
							addrCheck = BasicTypes_TRUE;
							addrIncorrect = BasicTypes_TRUE;
							_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
						end
				end
			if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
				_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
		end
		if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00) begin
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_6
				reg signed [31:0] i;
