				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
					begin
						nextPC[i] = pc[(1 - i) * MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH+:MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH] + BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH;
						if (brTargetType[i] == 2'd1) begin
							if (isfU[i][6-:7] == 7'b1101111)
								decodedPC[i] = pc[(1 - i) * MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH+:MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH] + OpFormatTypes_ExtendBranchDisplacement(OpFormatTypes_GetJAL_Target(isfU[i]));
							else
								decodedPC[i] = pc[(1 - i) * MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH+:MemoryMapTypes_PC_WIDTH] + OpFormatTypes_ExtendBranchDisplacement(OpFormatTypes_GetBranchDisplacement(isfU[i]));
						end
						else if (brTargetType[i] == 2'd2)
							decodedPC[i] = ras[rasPtr];
						else
							decodedPC[i] = nextPC[i];
					end
			end
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_7
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
					addrMismatch[i] = brPredIn[(i * 32) + 31-:19] != decodedPC[i];
			end
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_8
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
					if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
						_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
						if (((addrMismatch[i] && addrCheck) || addrIncorrect) && (addrCheckLane == i)) begin
							flush = BasicTypes_TRUE;
							brPredOut[((1 - i) * 32) + 31-:19] = decodedPC[i];
							brPredOut[((1 - i) * 32) + 12] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
							_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
						end
					end
				if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
					_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
			end
			if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00) begin
				recoveredPC = decodedPC[addrCheckLane];
				nextRAS = nextPC[addrCheckLane];
				if (pushRAS)
					nextRAS_Ptr = rasPtr + 1;
				else if (popRAS)
					nextRAS_Ptr = rasPtr - 1;
				else
					nextRAS_Ptr = rasPtr;
				if (flush) begin : sv2v_autoblock_9
					reg signed [31:0] i;
					for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DECODE_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
						if (i > addrCheckLane) begin
							insnValidOut[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							insnFlushed[i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						end
				end
			end
		end
	end
endmodule
module ReadyBitTable (
	clk,
	rst,
	rstStart,
	stall,
	wakeup,
	wakeupDstValid,
	wakeupDstRegNum,
	dispatch,
	dispatchedDstValid,
	dispatchedDstRegNum,
	dispatchedSrcValid,
	dispatchedSrcRegNum,
	dispatchedSrcReady
);
	parameter SRC_OP_NUM = 2;
	parameter REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 5;
	parameter ENTRY_NUM = 32;
	input wire clk;
	input wire rst;
	input wire rstStart;
	input wire stall;
	localparam BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
	localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
	localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
	localparam SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH = 4;
	input wire [0:3] wakeup;
	input wire [0:3] wakeupDstValid;
	input wire [(4 * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH) - 1:0] wakeupDstRegNum;
	localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
	localparam BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;
	input wire [0:1] dispatch;
	input wire [0:1] dispatchedDstValid;
	input wire [(2 * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH) - 1:0] dispatchedDstRegNum;
	input wire [(2 * SRC_OP_NUM) - 1:0] dispatchedSrcValid;
	input wire [((2 * SRC_OP_NUM) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH) - 1:0] dispatchedSrcRegNum;
	output reg [(2 * SRC_OP_NUM) - 1:0] dispatchedSrcReady;
	localparam READY_WRITE_NUM = 6;
	localparam READY_READ_NUM = BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH * SRC_OP_NUM;
	reg [0:5] readyWE;
	reg [0:5] readyWV;
	reg [(6 * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH) - 1:0] readyWA;
	wire [0:READY_READ_NUM - 1] readyRV;
	reg [(READY_READ_NUM * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH) - 1:0] readyRA;
	DistributedMultiPortRAM #(
		.ENTRY_NUM(1 << REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH),
		.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(1),
		.READ_NUM(READY_READ_NUM),
		.WRITE_NUM(READY_WRITE_NUM)
	) radyBitTable(
		.clk(clk),
		.we(readyWE),
		.wa(readyWA),
		.wv(readyWV),
		.ra(readyRA),
		.rv(readyRV)
	);
	reg [REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] resetIndex;
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (rstStart)
			resetIndex <= 0;
		else
			resetIndex <= resetIndex + 1;
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
	always @(*) begin
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
				begin
					readyWA[(5 - i) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = wakeupDstRegNum[(3 - i) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
					readyWV[i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					readyWE[i] = !stall && (wakeup[i] && wakeupDstValid[i]);
				end
		end
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_2
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
				begin
					readyWA[(5 - (i + SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH)) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = dispatchedDstRegNum[(1 - i) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
					readyWV[i + SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					readyWE[i + SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH] = dispatch[i] && dispatchedDstValid[i];
				end
		end
		if (rst) begin
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_3
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < READY_WRITE_NUM; i = i + 1)
					readyWE[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
			end
			readyWA[5 * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = resetIndex;
			readyWV[0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
			readyWE[0] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
		end
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_4
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_5
					reg signed [31:0] j;
					for (j = 0; j < SRC_OP_NUM; j = j + 1)
						begin
							readyRA[((READY_READ_NUM - 1) - ((i * SRC_OP_NUM) + j)) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] = dispatchedSrcRegNum[(((1 - i) * SRC_OP_NUM) + ((SRC_OP_NUM - 1) - j)) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
							if (dispatchedSrcValid[((1 - i) * SRC_OP_NUM) + ((SRC_OP_NUM - 1) - j)] == BasicTypes_FALSE)
								dispatchedSrcReady[((1 - i) * SRC_OP_NUM) + ((SRC_OP_NUM - 1) - j)] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
							else begin
								dispatchedSrcReady[((1 - i) * SRC_OP_NUM) + ((SRC_OP_NUM - 1) - j)] = readyRV[(i * SRC_OP_NUM) + j];
								begin : sv2v_autoblock_6
									reg signed [31:0] k;
									for (k = 0; k < SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH; k = k + 1)
										if ((wakeup[k] && wakeupDstValid[k]) && (wakeupDstRegNum[(3 - k) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] == dispatchedSrcRegNum[(((1 - i) * SRC_OP_NUM) + ((SRC_OP_NUM - 1) - j)) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]))
											dispatchedSrcReady[((1 - i) * SRC_OP_NUM) + ((SRC_OP_NUM - 1) - j)] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
								end
								begin : sv2v_autoblock_7
									reg signed [31:0] k;
									for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)
										if ((dispatch[k] && dispatchedDstValid[k]) && (dispatchedSrcRegNum[(((1 - i) * SRC_OP_NUM) + ((SRC_OP_NUM - 1) - j)) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] == dispatchedDstRegNum[(1 - k) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]))
											dispatchedSrcReady[((1 - i) * SRC_OP_NUM) + ((SRC_OP_NUM - 1) - j)] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
								end
							end
						end
				end
		end
	end
endmodule
module SourceCAM (
	clk,
	rst,
	stall,
	dispatch,
	dispatchPtr,
	dispatchedSrcRegNum,
	dispatchedSrcReady,
	wakeup,
	wakeupDstValid,
	wakeupDstRegNum,
	opReady
);
	parameter SRC_OP_NUM = 1;
	parameter REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 1;
	input wire clk;
	input wire rst;
	input wire stall;
	localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
	localparam BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;
	input wire [0:1] dispatch;
	localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
	localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
	input wire [7:0] dispatchPtr;
	input wire [((2 * SRC_OP_NUM) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH) - 1:0] dispatchedSrcRegNum;
	input wire [(2 * SRC_OP_NUM) - 1:0] dispatchedSrcReady;
	localparam BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
	localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
	localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
	localparam SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH = 4;
	input wire [0:3] wakeup;
	input wire [0:3] wakeupDstValid;
	input wire [(4 * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH) - 1:0] wakeupDstRegNum;
	output reg [15:0] opReady;
	reg srcReady [0:15][0:SRC_OP_NUM - 1];
	reg nextSrcReady [0:15][0:SRC_OP_NUM - 1];
	reg [REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] srcRegNum [0:15][0:SRC_OP_NUM - 1];
	reg match [0:15][0:SRC_OP_NUM - 1];
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
	always @(posedge clk) begin
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_2
					reg signed [31:0] j;
					for (j = 0; j < SRC_OP_NUM; j = j + 1)
						srcReady[i][j] <= (rst ? BasicTypes_FALSE : nextSrcReady[i][j]);
				end
		end
		if (rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_3
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_4
					reg signed [31:0] j;
					for (j = 0; j < SRC_OP_NUM; j = j + 1)
						srcRegNum[i][j] <= 0;
				end
		end
		else begin : sv2v_autoblock_5
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
				if (dispatch[i]) begin : sv2v_autoblock_6
					reg signed [31:0] j;
					for (j = 0; j < SRC_OP_NUM; j = j + 1)
						srcRegNum[dispatchPtr[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]][j] <= dispatchedSrcRegNum[(((1 - i) * SRC_OP_NUM) + ((SRC_OP_NUM - 1) - j)) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH];
				end
		end
	end
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
	always @(*) begin
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_7
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_8
					reg signed [31:0] j;
					for (j = 0; j < SRC_OP_NUM; j = j + 1)
						begin
							match[i][j] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_9
								reg signed [31:0] k;
								for (k = 0; k < SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH; k = k + 1)
									if (wakeup[k] && wakeupDstValid[k])
										match[i][j] = match[i][j] || (wakeupDstRegNum[(3 - k) * REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:REG_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] == srcRegNum[i][j]);
							end
						end
				end
		end
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_10
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)
				begin
					opReady[i] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_11
						reg signed [31:0] j;
						for (j = 0; j < SRC_OP_NUM; j = j + 1)
							opReady[i] = opReady[i] && (match[i][j] || srcReady[i][j]);
					end
				end
		end
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_12
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_13
					reg signed [31:0] j;
					for (j = 0; j < SRC_OP_NUM; j = j + 1)
						if (stall)
							nextSrcReady[i][j] = srcReady[i][j];
						else
							nextSrcReady[i][j] = srcReady[i][j] || match[i][j];
				end
		end
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_14
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_15
					reg signed [31:0] j;
					for (j = 0; j < SRC_OP_NUM; j = j + 1)
						if (dispatch[i])
							nextSrcReady[dispatchPtr[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]][j] = dispatchedSrcReady[((1 - i) * SRC_OP_NUM) + ((SRC_OP_NUM - 1) - j)];
				end
		end
	end
endmodule
module ProducerMatrix (
	clk,
	stall,
	dispatch,
	dispatchedSrcRegReady,
	dispatchedSrcRegPtr,
	dependStoreBitVector,
	dispatchPtr,
	wakeupDstVector,
	opReady
);
	input wire clk;
	input wire stall;
	localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
	localparam BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH = BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH;
	input wire [0:1] dispatch;
	localparam MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_SOURCE_REG_NUM = 2;
	localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_SRC_REG_NUM = MicroOpTypes_MICRO_OP_SOURCE_REG_NUM;
	input wire [3:0] dispatchedSrcRegReady;
	localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM = 16;
	localparam SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 4;
	input wire [15:0] dispatchedSrcRegPtr;
	input [31:0] dependStoreBitVector;
	input wire [7:0] dispatchPtr;
	localparam BasicTypes_STORE_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
	localparam BasicTypes_COMPLEX_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
	localparam BasicTypes_INT_ISSUE_WIDTH = 2;
	localparam BasicTypes_LOAD_ISSUE_WIDTH = 1;
	localparam SchedulerTypes_WAKEUP_WIDTH = 4;
	input wire [79:0] wakeupDstVector;
	output reg [0:15] opReady;
	reg [255:0] nextMatrix;
	reg [255:0] matrix;
	always @(posedge clk) matrix <= nextMatrix;
	reg [31:0] dispatchVector;
	reg [15:0] wakeupVector;
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
	always @(*) begin
		nextMatrix = matrix;
		wakeupVector = 1'sb0;
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
			reg signed [31:0] w;
			for (w = 0; w < 5; w = w + 1)
				wakeupVector = wakeupVector | wakeupDstVector[(4 - w) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM];
		end
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_2
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_3
					reg signed [31:0] j;
					for (j = 0; j < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; j = j + 1)
						nextMatrix[(i * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM) + j] = (wakeupVector[j] ? BasicTypes_FALSE : nextMatrix[(i * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM) + j]);
				end
		end
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_4
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)
				opReady[i] = !(|nextMatrix[i * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM]);
		end
		if (stall)
			nextMatrix = matrix;
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_5
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
				begin
					dispatchVector[i * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM] = 1'sb0;
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_6
						reg signed [31:0] j;
						for (j = 0; j < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_SRC_REG_NUM; j = j + 1)
							if (!dispatchedSrcRegReady[((1 - i) * 2) + (1 - j)])
								dispatchVector[(i * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM) + dispatchedSrcRegPtr[((i * 2) + j) * 4+:4]] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					end
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_7
						reg signed [31:0] j;
						for (j = 0; j < SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM; j = j + 1)
							if (dependStoreBitVector[((1 - i) * 16) + j])
								dispatchVector[(i * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM) + j] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
					end
				end
		end
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_8
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_DISPATCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
				if (dispatch[i])
					nextMatrix[dispatchPtr[(1 - i) * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM] = dispatchVector[i * SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM+:SchedulerTypes_ISSUE_QUEUE_ENTRY_NUM];
		end
	end
endmodule
module ICacheArray (
	clk,
	rst,
	rstStart,
	we,
	writeIndex,
	writeTag,
	writeLineData,
	readIndex,
	readTag,
	readLineData,
	hit,
	valid
);
	input wire clk;
	input wire rst;
	input wire rstStart;
	input wire we;
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_WAY_NUM = 2;
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH = 8;
	input wire [7:0] writeIndex;
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM = 8;
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = 3;
	localparam MemoryMapTypes_PHY_ADDR_WIDTH = 22;
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_TAG_BIT_WIDTH = 11;
	input wire [10:0] writeTag;
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_LINE_BIT_WIDTH = 64;
	input wire [63:0] writeLineData;
	input wire [7:0] readIndex;
	input wire [10:0] readTag;
	output reg [63:0] readLineData;
	output reg hit;
	output reg valid;
	reg weWay;
	reg [7:0] rstIndex;
	reg [7:0] writeWayIndex;
	wire [75:0] readWayData;
	reg [75:0] writeWayData;
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_INDEX_NUM = 256;
	BlockDualPortRAM #(
		.ENTRY_NUM(CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_INDEX_NUM),
		.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(76)
	) tagValidArray(
		.clk(clk),
		.we(weWay),
		.wa(writeWayIndex),
		.wv(writeWayData),
		.ra(readIndex),
		.rv(readWayData)
	);
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
	always @(*) begin
		if (rst) begin
			weWay = BasicTypes_TRUE;
			writeWayIndex = rstIndex;
			writeWayData[11] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		end
		else begin
			weWay = we;
			writeWayIndex = writeIndex;
			writeWayData[11] = BasicTypes_TRUE;
		end
		writeWayData[10-:CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_TAG_BIT_WIDTH] = writeTag;
		writeWayData[75-:64] = writeLineData;
		readLineData = readWayData[75-:64];
		valid = readWayData[11];
		hit = valid && (readWayData[10-:CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_TAG_BIT_WIDTH] == readTag);
	end
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (rstStart)
			rstIndex <= 1'sb0;
		else
			rstIndex <= rstIndex + 1;
endmodule
module ICacheNRUStateArray (
	clk,
	rst,
	rstStart,
	writeIndex,
	writeWay,
	writeHit,
	writeNRUState,
	readIndex,
	readNRUState,
	rstIndex
);
	input wire clk;
	input wire rst;
	input wire rstStart;
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_WAY_NUM = 2;
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_INDEX_BIT_WIDTH = 8;
	input wire [7:0] writeIndex;
	input wire [0:0] writeWay;
	input wire writeHit;
	input wire [1:0] writeNRUState;
	input wire [7:0] readIndex;
	output wire [1:0] readNRUState;
	output reg [7:0] rstIndex;
	reg we;
	reg [7:0] writeNRUStateIndex;
	reg [1:0] writeNRUStateData;
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_INDEX_NUM = 256;
	BlockDualPortRAM #(
		.ENTRY_NUM(CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_INDEX_NUM),
		.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_WAY_NUM)
	) nruStateArray(
		.clk(clk),
		.we(we),
		.wa(writeNRUStateIndex),
		.wv(writeNRUStateData),
		.ra(readIndex),
		.rv(readNRUState)
	);
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
	always @(*)
		if (rst) begin
			we = BasicTypes_TRUE;
			writeNRUStateIndex = rstIndex;
			writeNRUStateData = 1'sb0;
		end
		else begin
			we = writeHit;
			writeNRUStateIndex = writeIndex;
			writeNRUStateData = writeNRUState;
		end
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (rstStart)
			rstIndex <= 1'sb0;
		else
			rstIndex <= rstIndex + 1;
endmodule
module ICacheHitLogic (
	hitIn,
	headWordPtr,
	hitOut
);
	input wire hitIn;
	localparam BasicTypes_BYTE_WIDTH = 8;
	localparam BasicTypes_INSN_WIDTH = 32;
	localparam BasicTypes_INSN_BYTE_WIDTH = 4;
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_LINE_BYTE_NUM = 8;
	localparam CacheSystemTypes_ICACHE_LINE_INSN_NUM = 2;
	input wire [0:0] headWordPtr;
	localparam BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH = 2;
	output reg [0:1] hitOut;
	reg [1:0] wordPtr [0:1];
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
	always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
		reg signed [31:0] i;
		for (i = 0; i < BasicTypes_FETCH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
			begin
				wordPtr[i] = headWordPtr + i;
				if (wordPtr[i][1])
					hitOut[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
				else
					hitOut[i] = hitIn;
			end
	end
endmodule
module VictimWaySelecter (
	valid,
	victimCandidateWayPtr,
	victimWayPtr
);
	parameter WAY_NUM = 2;
	input wire [WAY_NUM - 1:0] valid;
	input wire [$clog2(WAY_NUM) - 1:0] victimCandidateWayPtr;
	output reg [$clog2(WAY_NUM) - 1:0] victimWayPtr;
	reg existInvalidWay;
	reg [$clog2(WAY_NUM) - 1:0] invalidWayPtr;
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
	always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
		reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;
		_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
		existInvalidWay = BasicTypes_FALSE;
		invalidWayPtr = 0;
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_2
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < WAY_NUM; i = i + 1)
				if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
					_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
					if (!valid[i]) begin
						existInvalidWay = BasicTypes_TRUE;
						invalidWayPtr = i;
						_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
					end
				end
			if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
				_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
		end
		if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00)
			victimWayPtr = (existInvalidWay ? invalidWayPtr : victimCandidateWayPtr);
	end
endmodule
module ResetController (
	clk,
	rstTrigger,
	locked,
	rst,
	rstStart
);
	parameter CYCLE_OF_RESET_SEQUENCE = 10000;
	input wire clk;
	input wire rstTrigger;
	input wire locked;
	output reg rst;
	output reg rstStart;
	reg [$clog2(CYCLE_OF_RESET_SEQUENCE):0] count;
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
	always @(posedge clk or posedge rstTrigger)
		if (rstTrigger || !locked) begin
			count <= 0;
			rst <= BasicTypes_TRUE;
		end
		else if (rst) begin
			count <= count + 1;
			rst <= (count >= CYCLE_OF_RESET_SEQUENCE ? BasicTypes_FALSE : BasicTypes_TRUE);
		end
		else begin
			count <= 0;
			rst <= BasicTypes_FALSE;
		end
	always @(*) rstStart = (rst && (count == 0) ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
endmodule
module FlipFlop (
	out,
	in,
	clk,
	rst
);
	parameter FF_WIDTH = 32;
	parameter RESET_VALUE = 0;
	output wire [FF_WIDTH - 1:0] out;
	input wire [FF_WIDTH - 1:0] in;
	input wire clk;
	input wire rst;
	reg [FF_WIDTH - 1:0] body;
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (rst == 1)
			body <= RESET_VALUE;
		else
			body <= in;
	assign out = body;
endmodule
module FlipFlopWE (
	out,
	in,
	we,
	clk,
	rst
);
	parameter FF_WIDTH = 32;
	parameter RESET_VALUE = 0;
	output wire [FF_WIDTH - 1:0] out;
	input wire [FF_WIDTH - 1:0] in;
	input wire we;
	input wire clk;
	input wire rst;
	reg [FF_WIDTH - 1:0] body;
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (rst == 1)
			body <= RESET_VALUE;
		else if (we)
			body <= in;
		else
			body <= body;
	assign out = body;
endmodule
module FreeList (
	clk,
	rst,
	push,
	pop,
	pushedData,
	full,
	empty,
	headData
);
	parameter SIZE = 1;
	parameter ENTRY_WIDTH = 1;
	input wire clk;
	input wire rst;
	input wire push;
	input wire pop;
	input wire [ENTRY_WIDTH - 1:0] pushedData;
	output wire full;
	output wire empty;
	output reg [ENTRY_WIDTH - 1:0] headData;
	localparam BIT_WIDTH = $clog2(SIZE);
	wire [BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] headPtr;
	wire [BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] tailPtr;
	QueuePointer #(
		.SIZE(SIZE),
		.INITIAL_HEAD_PTR(0),
		.INITIAL_TAIL_PTR(0),
		.INITIAL_COUNT(SIZE)
	) pointer(
		.clk(clk),
		.rst(rst),
		.push(push),
		.pop(pop),
		.full(full),
		.empty(empty),
		.headPtr(headPtr),
		.tailPtr(tailPtr)
	);
	reg [ENTRY_WIDTH - 1:0] freeList [SIZE - 1:0];
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < SIZE; i = i + 1)
				freeList[i] <= i;
		end
		else if (push)
			freeList[tailPtr] <= pushedData;
	always @(*) headData = freeList[headPtr];
endmodule
module MultiWidthFreeList (
	clk,
	rst,
	rstStart,
	push,
	pop,
	pushedData,
	count,
	poppedData
);
	parameter SIZE = 16;
	parameter ENTRY_BIT_SIZE = 4;
	parameter PUSH_WIDTH = 4;
	parameter POP_WIDTH = 4;
	parameter INITIAL_LENGTH = SIZE;
	input wire clk;
	input wire rst;
	input wire rstStart;
	input wire [0:PUSH_WIDTH - 1] push;
	input wire [0:POP_WIDTH - 1] pop;
	input wire [(PUSH_WIDTH * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] pushedData;
	output wire [$clog2(SIZE):0] count;
	output reg [(POP_WIDTH * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] poppedData;
	localparam INDEX_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(SIZE);
	wire [INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] headPtr;
	wire [INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] tailPtr;
	reg [$clog2(PUSH_WIDTH):0] pushCount;
	reg [$clog2(POP_WIDTH):0] popCount;
	MultiWidthQueuePointer #(
		.SIZE(SIZE),
		.INITIAL_HEAD_PTR(0),
		.INITIAL_TAIL_PTR(INITIAL_LENGTH),
		.INITIAL_COUNT(INITIAL_LENGTH),
		.PUSH_WIDTH(PUSH_WIDTH),
		.POP_WIDTH(POP_WIDTH)
	) queuePointer(
		.clk(clk),
		.rst(rst),
		.push(pushCount != 0),
		.pop(popCount != 0),
		.pushCount(pushCount),
		.popCount(popCount),
		.headPtr(headPtr),
		.tailPtr(tailPtr),
		.count(count)
	);
	reg [0:PUSH_WIDTH - 1] we;
	reg [(PUSH_WIDTH * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] wv;
	reg [(PUSH_WIDTH * INDEX_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] wa;
	wire [(POP_WIDTH * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] rv;
	reg [(POP_WIDTH * INDEX_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] ra;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] rstIndex;
	DistributedMultiBankRAM #(
		.ENTRY_NUM(SIZE),
		.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(ENTRY_BIT_SIZE),
		.READ_NUM(POP_WIDTH),
		.WRITE_NUM(PUSH_WIDTH)
	) freeList(
		.clk(clk),
		.wa(wa),
		.we(we),
		.wv(wv),
		.ra(ra),
		.rv(rv)
	);
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (rstStart)
			rstIndex <= 0;
		else if (rstIndex >= (SIZE - PUSH_WIDTH))
			rstIndex <= 0;
		else
			rstIndex <= rstIndex + PUSH_WIDTH;
	always @(*) begin
		pushCount = 0;
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < PUSH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
				begin
					if ((tailPtr + pushCount) >= SIZE)
						wa[((PUSH_WIDTH - 1) - i) * INDEX_BIT_SIZE+:INDEX_BIT_SIZE] = (tailPtr + pushCount) - SIZE;
					else
						wa[((PUSH_WIDTH - 1) - i) * INDEX_BIT_SIZE+:INDEX_BIT_SIZE] = tailPtr + pushCount;
					wv[((PUSH_WIDTH - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE] = pushedData[((PUSH_WIDTH - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE];
					we[i] = push[i];
					if (push[i])
						pushCount = pushCount + 1;
				end
		end
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_2
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < POP_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
				if ((headPtr + i) >= SIZE)
					ra[((POP_WIDTH - 1) - i) * INDEX_BIT_SIZE+:INDEX_BIT_SIZE] = (headPtr + i) - SIZE;
				else
					ra[((POP_WIDTH - 1) - i) * INDEX_BIT_SIZE+:INDEX_BIT_SIZE] = headPtr + i;
		end
		popCount = 0;
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_3
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < POP_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
				begin
					poppedData[((POP_WIDTH - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE] = rv[((POP_WIDTH - 1) - popCount) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE];
					if (pop[i])
						popCount = popCount + 1;
				end
		end
		if (rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_4
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < PUSH_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
				begin
					wa[((PUSH_WIDTH - 1) - i) * INDEX_BIT_SIZE+:INDEX_BIT_SIZE] = rstIndex + i;
					wv[((PUSH_WIDTH - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE] = rstIndex + i;
					we[i] = (wa[((PUSH_WIDTH - 1) - i) * INDEX_BIT_SIZE+:INDEX_BIT_SIZE] >= rstIndex) && (wa[((PUSH_WIDTH - 1) - i) * INDEX_BIT_SIZE+:INDEX_BIT_SIZE] < SIZE);
				end
		end
	end
endmodule
module QueuePointer (
	clk,
	rst,
	push,
	pop,
	full,
	empty,
	headPtr,
	tailPtr
);
	parameter SIZE = 1;
	parameter INITIAL_HEAD_PTR = 0;
	parameter INITIAL_TAIL_PTR = 0;
	parameter INITIAL_COUNT = 0;
	input wire clk;
	input wire rst;
	input wire push;
	input wire pop;
	output reg full;
	output reg empty;
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE) - 1:0] headPtr;
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE) - 1:0] tailPtr;
	localparam BIT_WIDTH = $clog2(SIZE);
	reg [BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] regHeadStorage;
	reg [BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] nextHeadStorage;
	reg [BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] regTailStorage;
	reg [BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] nextTailStorage;
	reg [BIT_WIDTH:0] regCount;
	reg [BIT_WIDTH:0] nextCount;
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (rst) begin
			regHeadStorage <= INITIAL_HEAD_PTR;
			regTailStorage <= INITIAL_TAIL_PTR;
			regCount <= INITIAL_COUNT;
		end
		else begin
			regHeadStorage <= nextHeadStorage;
			regTailStorage <= nextTailStorage;
			regCount <= nextCount;
		end
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
	always @(*) begin
		full = (regCount == SIZE ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
		empty = (regCount == 0 ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
		nextHeadStorage = regHeadStorage;
		nextTailStorage = regTailStorage;
		nextCount = regCount;
		if (push) begin
			if (regTailStorage == (SIZE - 1))
				nextTailStorage = 0;
			else
				nextTailStorage = nextTailStorage + 1;
			nextCount = nextCount + 1;
		end
		if (pop) begin
			if (regHeadStorage == (SIZE - 1))
				nextHeadStorage = 0;
			else
				nextHeadStorage = nextHeadStorage + 1;
			nextCount = nextCount - 1;
		end
		headPtr = regHeadStorage;
		tailPtr = regTailStorage;
	end
endmodule
module QueuePointerWithEntryCount (
	clk,
	rst,
	push,
	pop,
	full,
	empty,
	headPtr,
	tailPtr,
	count
);
	parameter SIZE = 1;
	parameter INITIAL_HEAD_PTR = 0;
	parameter INITIAL_TAIL_PTR = 0;
	parameter INITIAL_COUNT = 0;
	input wire clk;
	input wire rst;
	input wire push;
	input wire pop;
	output reg full;
	output reg empty;
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE) - 1:0] headPtr;
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE) - 1:0] tailPtr;
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE):0] count;
	localparam BIT_WIDTH = $clog2(SIZE);
	reg [BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] regHeadStorage;
	reg [BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] nextHeadStorage;
	reg [BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] regTailStorage;
	reg [BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] nextTailStorage;
	reg [BIT_WIDTH:0] regCount;
	reg [BIT_WIDTH:0] nextCount;
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (rst) begin
			regHeadStorage <= INITIAL_HEAD_PTR;
			regTailStorage <= INITIAL_TAIL_PTR;
			regCount <= INITIAL_COUNT;
		end
		else begin
			regHeadStorage <= nextHeadStorage;
			regTailStorage <= nextTailStorage;
			regCount <= nextCount;
		end
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
	always @(*) begin
		full = (regCount == SIZE ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
		empty = (regCount == 0 ? BasicTypes_TRUE : BasicTypes_FALSE);
		nextHeadStorage = regHeadStorage;
		nextTailStorage = regTailStorage;
		nextCount = regCount;
		if (push) begin
			if (regTailStorage == (SIZE - 1))
				nextTailStorage = 0;
			else
				nextTailStorage = nextTailStorage + 1;
			nextCount = nextCount + 1;
		end
		if (pop) begin
			if (regHeadStorage == (SIZE - 1))
				nextHeadStorage = 0;
			else
				nextHeadStorage = nextHeadStorage + 1;
			nextCount = nextCount - 1;
		end
		headPtr = regHeadStorage;
		tailPtr = regTailStorage;
		count = regCount;
	end
endmodule
module MultiWidthQueuePointer (
	clk,
	rst,
	push,
	pop,
	pushCount,
	popCount,
	headPtr,
	tailPtr,
	count
);
	parameter SIZE = 1;
	parameter INITIAL_HEAD_PTR = 0;
	parameter INITIAL_TAIL_PTR = 0;
	parameter INITIAL_COUNT = 0;
	parameter PUSH_WIDTH = 1;
	parameter POP_WIDTH = 1;
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
	parameter ENABLE_SPECIFIED_ENTRY_NUM = BasicTypes_FALSE;
	input wire clk;
	input wire rst;
	input wire push;
	input wire pop;
	input wire [$clog2(PUSH_WIDTH):0] pushCount;
	input wire [$clog2(POP_WIDTH):0] popCount;
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE) - 1:0] headPtr;
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE) - 1:0] tailPtr;
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE):0] count;
	localparam INDEX_BIT_WIDTH = $clog2(SIZE);
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] regHead;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] nextHead;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] regTail;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] nextTail;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH:0] regCount;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH:0] nextCount;
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (rst) begin
			regHead <= INITIAL_HEAD_PTR;
			regTail <= INITIAL_TAIL_PTR;
			regCount <= INITIAL_COUNT;
		end
		else begin
			regHead <= nextHead;
			regTail <= nextTail;
			regCount <= nextCount;
		end
	always @(*) begin
		nextTail = regTail;
		if (push) begin
			nextTail = nextTail + pushCount;
			if (nextTail >= SIZE)
				nextTail = nextTail - SIZE;
		end
	end
	always @(*) begin
		nextHead = regHead;
		if (pop) begin
			nextHead = nextHead + popCount;
			if (nextHead >= SIZE)
				nextHead = nextHead - SIZE;
		end
	end
	always @(*) begin
		nextCount = regCount;
		if (push)
			nextCount = nextCount + pushCount;
		if (pop)
			nextCount = nextCount - popCount;
		headPtr = regHead;
		tailPtr = regTail;
		count = regCount;
	end
endmodule
module SetTailMultiWidthQueuePointer (
	clk,
	rst,
	push,
	pop,
	pushCount,
	popCount,
	setTail,
	setTailPtr,
	headPtr,
	tailPtr,
	count
);
	parameter SIZE = 1;
	parameter INITIAL_HEAD_PTR = 0;
	parameter INITIAL_TAIL_PTR = 0;
	parameter INITIAL_COUNT = 0;
	parameter PUSH_WIDTH = 1;
	parameter POP_WIDTH = 1;
	input wire clk;
	input wire rst;
	input wire push;
	input wire pop;
	input wire [$clog2(PUSH_WIDTH):0] pushCount;
	input wire [$clog2(POP_WIDTH):0] popCount;
	input wire setTail;
	input wire [$clog2(SIZE) - 1:0] setTailPtr;
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE) - 1:0] headPtr;
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE) - 1:0] tailPtr;
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE):0] count;
	localparam INDEX_BIT_WIDTH = $clog2(SIZE);
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] regHead;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] nextHead;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] regTail;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] nextTail;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] roundedSetTailPtr;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH:0] regCount;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH:0] nextCount;
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (rst) begin
			regHead <= INITIAL_HEAD_PTR;
			regTail <= INITIAL_TAIL_PTR;
			regCount <= INITIAL_COUNT;
		end
		else begin
			regHead <= nextHead;
			regTail <= nextTail;
			regCount <= nextCount;
		end
	always @(*) begin
		nextTail = regTail;
		if (push) begin
			nextTail = nextTail + pushCount;
			if (nextTail >= SIZE)
				nextTail = nextTail - SIZE;
		end
		else if (setTail)
			nextTail = roundedSetTailPtr;
	end
	always @(*) begin
		nextHead = regHead;
		if (pop) begin
			nextHead = nextHead + popCount;
			if (nextHead >= SIZE)
				nextHead = nextHead - SIZE;
		end
	end
	always @(*) begin
		nextCount = regCount;
		roundedSetTailPtr = setTailPtr;
		if (push)
			nextCount = nextCount + pushCount;
		else if (setTail) begin
			if (setTailPtr >= SIZE)
				roundedSetTailPtr = setTailPtr - SIZE;
			nextCount = (regHead <= roundedSetTailPtr ? roundedSetTailPtr - regHead : (SIZE + roundedSetTailPtr) - regHead);
		end
		if (pop)
			nextCount = nextCount - popCount;
		headPtr = regHead;
		tailPtr = regTail;
		count = regCount;
	end
endmodule
module BiTailMultiWidthQueuePointer (
	clk,
	rst,
	pushTail,
	popTail,
	popHead,
	pushTailCount,
	popTailCount,
	popHeadCount,
	headPtr,
	tailPtr,
	count
);
	parameter SIZE = 1;
	parameter INITIAL_HEAD_PTR = 0;
	parameter INITIAL_TAIL_PTR = 0;
	parameter INITIAL_COUNT = 0;
	parameter PUSH_WIDTH = 1;
	parameter POP_WIDTH = 1;
	input wire clk;
	input wire rst;
	input wire pushTail;
	input wire popTail;
	input wire popHead;
	input wire [$clog2(PUSH_WIDTH):0] pushTailCount;
	input wire [$clog2(POP_WIDTH):0] popTailCount;
	input wire [$clog2(POP_WIDTH):0] popHeadCount;
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE) - 1:0] headPtr;
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE) - 1:0] tailPtr;
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE):0] count;
	localparam INDEX_BIT_WIDTH = $clog2(SIZE);
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] regHead;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] nextHead;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] regTail;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] nextTail;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH:0] regCount;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH:0] nextCount;
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (rst) begin
			regHead <= INITIAL_HEAD_PTR;
			regTail <= INITIAL_TAIL_PTR;
			regCount <= INITIAL_COUNT;
		end
		else begin
			regHead <= nextHead;
			regTail <= nextTail;
			regCount <= nextCount;
		end
	always @(*) begin
		nextHead = regHead;
		if (popHead) begin
			nextHead = nextHead + popHeadCount;
			if (nextHead >= SIZE)
				nextHead = nextHead - SIZE;
		end
	end
	always @(*) begin
		nextTail = regTail;
		if (pushTail) begin
			nextTail = nextTail + pushTailCount;
			if (nextTail >= SIZE)
				nextTail = nextTail - SIZE;
		end
		else if (popTail) begin
			nextTail = nextTail - popTailCount;
			if (nextTail >= SIZE)
				nextTail = nextTail + SIZE;
		end
	end
	always @(*) begin
		nextCount = regCount;
		if (pushTail)
			nextCount = nextCount + pushTailCount;
		else if (popTail)
			nextCount = nextCount - popTailCount;
		if (popHead)
			nextCount = nextCount - popHeadCount;
		headPtr = regHead;
		tailPtr = regTail;
		count = regCount;
	end
endmodule
module SetTailBiTailMultiWidthQueuePointer (
	clk,
	rst,
	pushTail,
	popTail,
	popHead,
	pushTailCount,
	popTailCount,
	popHeadCount,
	setTail,
	setTailPtr,
	headPtr,
	tailPtr,
	count
);
	parameter SIZE = 1;
	parameter INITIAL_HEAD_PTR = 0;
	parameter INITIAL_TAIL_PTR = 0;
	parameter INITIAL_COUNT = 0;
	parameter PUSH_WIDTH = 1;
	parameter POP_WIDTH = 1;
	input wire clk;
	input wire rst;
	input wire pushTail;
	input wire popTail;
	input wire popHead;
	input wire [$clog2(PUSH_WIDTH):0] pushTailCount;
	input wire [$clog2(POP_WIDTH):0] popTailCount;
	input wire [$clog2(POP_WIDTH):0] popHeadCount;
	input wire setTail;
	input wire [$clog2(SIZE) - 1:0] setTailPtr;
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE) - 1:0] headPtr;
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE) - 1:0] tailPtr;
	output reg [$clog2(SIZE):0] count;
	localparam INDEX_BIT_WIDTH = $clog2(SIZE);
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] regHead;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] nextHead;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] regTail;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] nextTail;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] roundedSetTailPtr;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH:0] regCount;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_WIDTH:0] nextCount;
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (rst) begin
			regHead <= INITIAL_HEAD_PTR;
			regTail <= INITIAL_TAIL_PTR;
			regCount <= INITIAL_COUNT;
		end
		else begin
			regHead <= nextHead;
			regTail <= nextTail;
			regCount <= nextCount;
		end
	always @(*) begin
		nextHead = regHead;
		if (popHead) begin
			nextHead = nextHead + popHeadCount;
			if (nextHead >= SIZE)
				nextHead = nextHead - SIZE;
		end
	end
	always @(*) begin
		nextTail = regTail;
		if (pushTail) begin
			nextTail = nextTail + pushTailCount;
			if (nextTail >= SIZE)
				nextTail = nextTail - SIZE;
		end
		else if (popTail) begin
			nextTail = nextTail - popTailCount;
			if (nextTail >= SIZE)
				nextTail = nextTail + SIZE;
		end
		else if (setTail)
			nextTail = roundedSetTailPtr;
	end
	always @(*) begin
		nextCount = regCount;
		roundedSetTailPtr = setTailPtr;
		if (pushTail)
			nextCount = nextCount + pushTailCount;
		else if (popTail)
			nextCount = nextCount - popTailCount;
		else if (setTail) begin
			if (setTailPtr >= SIZE)
				roundedSetTailPtr = setTailPtr - SIZE;
			nextCount = (regHead <= roundedSetTailPtr ? roundedSetTailPtr - regHead : (SIZE + roundedSetTailPtr) - regHead);
		end
		if (popHead)
			nextCount = nextCount - popHeadCount;
		headPtr = regHead;
		tailPtr = regTail;
		count = regCount;
	end
endmodule
module BlockDualPortRAM (
	clk,
	we,
	wa,
	wv,
	ra,
	rv
);
	parameter ENTRY_NUM = 128;
	parameter ENTRY_BIT_SIZE = 64;
	input wire clk;
	input wire we;
	input wire [$clog2(ENTRY_NUM) - 1:0] wa;
	input wire [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] wv;
	input wire [$clog2(ENTRY_NUM) - 1:0] ra;
	output reg [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] rv;
	localparam INDEX_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(ENTRY_NUM);
	reg [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] array [0:ENTRY_NUM - 1];
	always @(posedge clk) begin
		rv <= array[ra];
		if (we)
			array[wa] <= wv;
	end
endmodule
module BlockTrueDualPortRAM (
	clk,
	we,
	rwa,
	wv,
	rv
);
	parameter ENTRY_NUM = 16;
	parameter ENTRY_BIT_SIZE = 1;
	parameter PORT_NUM = 2;
	input wire clk;
	input wire [0:PORT_NUM - 1] we;
	input wire [(PORT_NUM * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) - 1:0] rwa;
	input wire [(PORT_NUM * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] wv;
	output reg [(PORT_NUM * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] rv;
	localparam INDEX_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(ENTRY_NUM);
	reg [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] array [0:ENTRY_NUM - 1];
	always @(posedge clk) begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
		reg signed [31:0] i;
		for (i = 0; i < PORT_NUM; i = i + 1)
			begin
				rv[((PORT_NUM - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE] <= array[rwa[((PORT_NUM - 1) - i) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)]];
				if (we[i])
					array[rwa[((PORT_NUM - 1) - i) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)]] <= wv[((PORT_NUM - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE];
			end
	end
	genvar j;
	generate
		for (j = 0; j < PORT_NUM; j = j + 1) begin : genblk1
			genvar i;
		end
	endgenerate
	genvar i;
endmodule
module BlockMultiPortRAM (
	clk,
	we,
	wa,
	wv,
	ra,
	rv
);
	parameter ENTRY_NUM = 4;
	parameter ENTRY_BIT_SIZE = 8;
	parameter READ_NUM = 2;
	parameter WRITE_NUM = 2;
	input wire clk;
	input wire [0:WRITE_NUM - 1] we;
	input wire [(WRITE_NUM * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) - 1:0] wa;
	input wire [(WRITE_NUM * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] wv;
	input wire [(READ_NUM * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) - 1:0] ra;
	output wire [(READ_NUM * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] rv;
	localparam INDEX_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(ENTRY_NUM);
	generate
		if (WRITE_NUM > 1) begin : genblk1
			localparam WRITE_NUM_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(WRITE_NUM);
			reg [WRITE_NUM_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] select [0:ENTRY_NUM - 1];
			wire [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] rvBank [0:READ_NUM - 1][0:WRITE_NUM - 1];
			reg [0:WRITE_NUM - 1] weReg;
			reg [(WRITE_NUM * INDEX_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] waReg;
			reg [(READ_NUM * INDEX_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] raReg;
			always @(posedge clk) begin
				raReg <= ra;
				weReg <= we;
				waReg <= wa;
			end
			genvar j;
			for (j = 0; j < WRITE_NUM; j = j + 1) begin : genblk1
				genvar i;
				for (i = 0; i < READ_NUM; i = i + 1) begin : genblk1
					BlockDualPortRAM #(
						.ENTRY_NUM(ENTRY_NUM),
						.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(ENTRY_BIT_SIZE)
					) rBank(
						.clk(clk),
						.we(we[j]),
						.wa(wa[((WRITE_NUM - 1) - j) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)]),
						.wv(wv[((WRITE_NUM - 1) - j) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE]),
						.ra(ra[((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)]),
						.rv(rvBank[i][j])
					);
				end
			end
			genvar i;
			for (i = 0; i < READ_NUM; i = i + 1) begin : genblk2
				assign rv[((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE] = rvBank[i][select[raReg[((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * INDEX_BIT_SIZE+:INDEX_BIT_SIZE]]];
			end
			for (j = 0; j < WRITE_NUM; j = j + 1) begin : genblk3
				always @(posedge clk)
					if (weReg[j])
						select[waReg[((WRITE_NUM - 1) - j) * INDEX_BIT_SIZE+:INDEX_BIT_SIZE]] <= j;
			end
		end
		else begin : genblk1
			genvar i;
			for (i = 0; i < READ_NUM; i = i + 1) begin : genblk1
				BlockDualPortRAM #(
					.ENTRY_NUM(ENTRY_NUM),
					.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(ENTRY_BIT_SIZE)
				) rBank(
					.clk(clk),
					.we(we[0]),
					.wa(wa[(WRITE_NUM - 1) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)]),
					.wv(wv[(WRITE_NUM - 1) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE]),
					.ra(ra[((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)]),
					.rv(rv[((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE])
				);
			end
		end
	endgenerate
	genvar i;
endmodule
module InitializedBlockRAM (
	clk,
	we,
	wa,
	wv,
	ra,
	rv
);
	parameter ENTRY_NUM = 128;
	parameter ENTRY_BIT_SIZE = 64;
	parameter INIT_HEX_FILE = "code.hex";
	input wire clk;
	input wire we;
	input wire [$clog2(ENTRY_NUM) - 1:0] wa;
	input wire [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] wv;
	input wire [$clog2(ENTRY_NUM) - 1:0] ra;
	output wire [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] rv;
	localparam HEX_FILE_ENTRY_BIT_SIZE = 128;
	generate
		if (ENTRY_BIT_SIZE <= HEX_FILE_ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) begin : body
			InitializedBlockRAM_ForNarrowRequest #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(ENTRY_NUM),
				.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(ENTRY_BIT_SIZE),
				.INIT_HEX_FILE(INIT_HEX_FILE)
			) ram(
				.clk(clk),
				.we(we),
				.wa(wa),
				.wv(wv),
				.ra(ra),
				.rv(rv)
			);
		end
		else begin : body
			InitializedBlockRAM_ForWideRequest #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(ENTRY_NUM),
				.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(ENTRY_BIT_SIZE),
				.INIT_HEX_FILE(INIT_HEX_FILE)
			) ram(
				.clk(clk),
				.we(we),
				.wa(wa),
				.wv(wv),
				.ra(ra),
				.rv(rv)
			);
		end
	endgenerate
endmodule
module InitializedBlockRAM_ForNarrowRequest (
	clk,
	we,
	wa,
	wv,
	ra,
	rv
);
	parameter ENTRY_NUM = 128;
	parameter ENTRY_BIT_SIZE = 64;
	parameter INIT_HEX_FILE = "code.hex";
	input wire clk;
	input wire we;
	input wire [$clog2(ENTRY_NUM) - 1:0] wa;
	input wire [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] wv;
	input wire [$clog2(ENTRY_NUM) - 1:0] ra;
	output reg [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] rv;
	localparam INDEX_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(ENTRY_NUM);
	localparam ENTRY_BIT_WIDTH = $clog2(ENTRY_BIT_SIZE);
	localparam HEX_FILE_ENTRY_BIT_SIZE = 128;
	localparam HEX_FILE_ENTRY_BIT_WIDTH = 7;
	localparam HEX_FILE_INDEX_BIT_SIZE = (INDEX_BIT_SIZE + ENTRY_BIT_WIDTH) - HEX_FILE_ENTRY_BIT_WIDTH;
	localparam HEX_BLOCK_OFFSET_INDEX = (INDEX_BIT_SIZE > HEX_FILE_INDEX_BIT_SIZE ? INDEX_BIT_SIZE - HEX_FILE_INDEX_BIT_SIZE : 1);
	generate
		if (!(HEX_FILE_ENTRY_BIT_WIDTH <= ENTRY_BIT_SIZE)) begin : genblk1
			RSD_STATIC_ASSERT_FAILED non_existing_module();
		end
	endgenerate
	function automatic [HEX_BLOCK_OFFSET_INDEX - 1:0] GetBlockOffsetFromAddress;
		input reg [INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] addr;
		if (INDEX_BIT_SIZE > HEX_FILE_INDEX_BIT_SIZE)
			GetBlockOffsetFromAddress = addr[HEX_BLOCK_OFFSET_INDEX - 1:0];
		else
			GetBlockOffsetFromAddress = 1'sb0;
	endfunction
	function automatic [HEX_FILE_INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] GetHexArrayIndexFromAddress;
		input reg [INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] addr;
		if (INDEX_BIT_SIZE > HEX_FILE_INDEX_BIT_SIZE)
			GetHexArrayIndexFromAddress = addr[INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:HEX_BLOCK_OFFSET_INDEX];
		else
			GetHexArrayIndexFromAddress = addr;
	endfunction
	localparam HEX_FILE_ARRAY_ENTRY_NUM = 1 << HEX_FILE_INDEX_BIT_SIZE;
	reg [127:0] array [0:HEX_FILE_ARRAY_ENTRY_NUM - 1];
	reg [INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] raReg;
	reg [HEX_FILE_INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] hexFileRA;
	reg [127:0] hexFileRV;
	reg [HEX_BLOCK_OFFSET_INDEX - 1:0] hexFileRAOffset;
	reg [HEX_FILE_INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] hexFileWA;
	reg [127:0] hexFileWV;
	reg [HEX_BLOCK_OFFSET_INDEX - 1:0] hexFileWAOffset;
	reg [127:0] tmpWriteEntry;
	reg [127:0] dummyRV;
	always @(posedge clk) begin
		if (we)
			array[hexFileWA] <= hexFileWV;
		raReg <= ra;
		dummyRV <= hexFileRV;
	end
	always @(*) begin
		hexFileRA = GetHexArrayIndexFromAddress(raReg);
		hexFileRAOffset = GetBlockOffsetFromAddress(raReg);
		hexFileRV = array[hexFileRA];
		rv = hexFileRV[hexFileRAOffset * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE];
	end
	always @(*) begin
		hexFileWA = GetHexArrayIndexFromAddress(wa);
		hexFileWAOffset = GetBlockOffsetFromAddress(wa);
		tmpWriteEntry = array[hexFileWA];
		tmpWriteEntry[hexFileWAOffset * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE] = wv;
		hexFileWV = tmpWriteEntry;
	end
	initial if (INIT_HEX_FILE != "")
		$readmemh(INIT_HEX_FILE, array);
	else
		$display("INIT_HEX_FILE in InitializedBlockRAM is not specified, so no file is read.");
endmodule
module InitializedBlockRAM_ForWideRequest (
	clk,
	we,
	wa,
	wv,
	ra,
	rv
);
	parameter ENTRY_NUM = 128;
	parameter ENTRY_BIT_SIZE = 64;
	parameter INIT_HEX_FILE = "code.hex";
	input wire clk;
	input wire we;
	input wire [$clog2(ENTRY_NUM) - 1:0] wa;
	input wire [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] wv;
	input wire [$clog2(ENTRY_NUM) - 1:0] ra;
	output reg [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] rv;
	localparam INDEX_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(ENTRY_NUM);
	localparam ENTRY_BIT_WIDTH = $clog2(ENTRY_BIT_SIZE);
	localparam HEX_FILE_ENTRY_BIT_SIZE = 128;
	localparam HEX_FILE_ENTRY_BIT_WIDTH = 7;
	localparam HEX_FILE_INDEX_BIT_SIZE = (INDEX_BIT_SIZE + ENTRY_BIT_WIDTH) - HEX_FILE_ENTRY_BIT_WIDTH;
	localparam DIFF_BIT_WIDTH = (HEX_FILE_INDEX_BIT_SIZE > INDEX_BIT_SIZE ? HEX_FILE_INDEX_BIT_SIZE - INDEX_BIT_SIZE : 1);
	localparam HEX_BLOCK_FILL_NUM = (HEX_FILE_INDEX_BIT_SIZE > INDEX_BIT_SIZE ? 1 << DIFF_BIT_WIDTH : 1);
	generate
		if (!(HEX_FILE_ENTRY_BIT_WIDTH <= ENTRY_BIT_WIDTH)) begin : genblk1
			RSD_STATIC_ASSERT_FAILED non_existing_module();
		end
	endgenerate
	function automatic [HEX_FILE_INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] GetHexArrayIndexFromAddress;
		input reg [INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] addr;
		input reg [DIFF_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] count;
		if (HEX_FILE_INDEX_BIT_SIZE > INDEX_BIT_SIZE)
			GetHexArrayIndexFromAddress = {addr, count};
		else
			GetHexArrayIndexFromAddress = addr;
	endfunction
	localparam HEX_FILE_ARRAY_ENTRY_NUM = 1 << HEX_FILE_INDEX_BIT_SIZE;
	reg [127:0] array [0:HEX_FILE_ARRAY_ENTRY_NUM - 1];
	reg [INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] raReg;
	reg [HEX_FILE_INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] hexFileRA [0:HEX_BLOCK_FILL_NUM - 1];
	reg [127:0] hexFileRV [0:HEX_BLOCK_FILL_NUM - 1];
	reg [HEX_FILE_INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] hexFileWA [0:HEX_BLOCK_FILL_NUM - 1];
	reg [127:0] hexFileWV [0:HEX_BLOCK_FILL_NUM - 1];
	always @(posedge clk) begin
		if (we) begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < HEX_BLOCK_FILL_NUM; i = i + 1)
				array[hexFileWA[i]] <= hexFileWV[i];
		end
		raReg <= ra;
	end
	always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_2
		reg signed [31:0] i;
		for (i = 0; i < HEX_BLOCK_FILL_NUM; i = i + 1)
			begin
				hexFileRA[i] = GetHexArrayIndexFromAddress(raReg, i);
				hexFileRV[i] = array[hexFileRA[i]];
				rv[HEX_FILE_ENTRY_BIT_SIZE * i+:HEX_FILE_ENTRY_BIT_SIZE] = hexFileRV[i];
			end
	end
	always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_3
		reg signed [31:0] i;
		for (i = 0; i < HEX_BLOCK_FILL_NUM; i = i + 1)
			begin
				hexFileWA[i] = GetHexArrayIndexFromAddress(wa, i);
				hexFileWV[i] = wv[HEX_FILE_ENTRY_BIT_SIZE * i+:HEX_FILE_ENTRY_BIT_SIZE];
			end
	end
	initial if (INIT_HEX_FILE != "")
		$readmemh(INIT_HEX_FILE, array);
	else
		$display("INIT_HEX_FILE in InitializedBlockRAM is not specified, so no file is read.");
endmodule
module DistributedSinglePortRAM (
	clk,
	we,
	rwa,
	wv,
	rv
);
	parameter ENTRY_NUM = 128;
	parameter ENTRY_BIT_SIZE = 64;
	input wire clk;
	input wire we;
	input wire [$clog2(ENTRY_NUM) - 1:0] rwa;
	input wire [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] wv;
	output reg [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] rv;
	localparam INDEX_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(ENTRY_NUM);
	reg [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] array [0:ENTRY_NUM - 1];
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (we)
			array[rwa] <= wv;
	always @(*) rv = array[rwa];
endmodule
module DistributedDualPortRAM (
	clk,
	we,
	wa,
	wv,
	ra,
	rv
);
	parameter ENTRY_NUM = 128;
	parameter ENTRY_BIT_SIZE = 64;
	input wire clk;
	input wire we;
	input wire [$clog2(ENTRY_NUM) - 1:0] wa;
	input wire [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] wv;
	input wire [$clog2(ENTRY_NUM) - 1:0] ra;
	output reg [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] rv;
	localparam INDEX_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(ENTRY_NUM);
	reg [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] array [0:ENTRY_NUM - 1];
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (we)
			array[wa] <= wv;
	always @(*) rv = array[ra];
endmodule
module RegisterDualPortRAM (
	clk,
	we,
	wa,
	wv,
	ra,
	rv
);
	parameter ENTRY_NUM = 128;
	parameter ENTRY_BIT_SIZE = 64;
	input wire clk;
	input wire we;
	input wire [$clog2(ENTRY_NUM) - 1:0] wa;
	input wire [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] wv;
	input wire [$clog2(ENTRY_NUM) - 1:0] ra;
	output reg [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] rv;
	localparam INDEX_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(ENTRY_NUM);
	reg [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] array [0:ENTRY_NUM - 1];
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (we)
			array[wa] <= wv;
	always @(*) rv = array[ra];
endmodule
module DistributedSharedMultiPortRAM (
	clk,
	we,
	rwa,
	wv,
	ra,
	rv
);
	parameter ENTRY_NUM = 128;
	parameter ENTRY_BIT_SIZE = 64;
	parameter READ_NUM = 4;
	input wire clk;
	input wire we;
	input wire [$clog2(ENTRY_NUM) - 1:0] rwa;
	input wire [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] wv;
	input wire [(0 >= (READ_NUM - 2) ? ((3 - READ_NUM) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) + (((READ_NUM - 2) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) - 1) : ((READ_NUM - 1) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) - 1):(0 >= (READ_NUM - 2) ? (READ_NUM - 2) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM) : 0)] ra;
	output reg [(READ_NUM * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] rv;
	localparam INDEX_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(ENTRY_NUM);
	reg [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] array [0:ENTRY_NUM - 1];
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (we)
			array[rwa] <= wv;
	always @(*) begin
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < (READ_NUM - 1); i = i + 1)
				rv[((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE] = array[ra[(0 >= (READ_NUM - 2) ? i : (READ_NUM - 2) - i) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)]];
		end
		rv[((READ_NUM - 1) - (READ_NUM - 1)) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE] = array[rwa];
	end
	genvar i;
endmodule
module LVT_DistributedMultiPortRAM (
	clk,
	we,
	wa,
	wv,
	ra,
	rv
);
	parameter ENTRY_NUM = 64;
	parameter ENTRY_BIT_SIZE = 63;
	parameter READ_NUM = 3;
	parameter WRITE_NUM = 3;
	input wire clk;
	input wire [0:WRITE_NUM - 1] we;
	input wire [(WRITE_NUM * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) - 1:0] wa;
	input wire [(WRITE_NUM * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] wv;
	input wire [(READ_NUM * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) - 1:0] ra;
	output wire [(READ_NUM * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] rv;
	localparam INDEX_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(ENTRY_NUM);
	localparam WRITE_NUM_BIT_SIZE = (WRITE_NUM == 1 ? 1 : $clog2(WRITE_NUM));
	generate
		if (WRITE_NUM > 1) begin : genblk1
			wire [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] rvBank [0:READ_NUM - 1][0:WRITE_NUM - 1];
			wire [(WRITE_NUM * WRITE_NUM_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] lvi;
			wire [(READ_NUM * WRITE_NUM_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] lvo;
			XOR_DistributedMultiPortRAM #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(ENTRY_NUM),
				.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(WRITE_NUM_BIT_SIZE),
				.READ_NUM(READ_NUM),
				.WRITE_NUM(WRITE_NUM)
			) lvt(
				.clk(clk),
				.we(we),
				.wa(wa),
				.wv(lvi),
				.ra(ra),
				.rv(lvo)
			);
			genvar j;
			for (j = 0; j < WRITE_NUM; j = j + 1) begin : genblk1
				genvar i;
				for (i = 0; i < READ_NUM; i = i + 1) begin : genblk1
					DistributedDualPortRAM #(
						.ENTRY_NUM(ENTRY_NUM),
						.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(ENTRY_BIT_SIZE)
					) rBank(
						.clk(clk),
						.we(we[j]),
						.wa(wa[((WRITE_NUM - 1) - j) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)]),
						.wv(wv[((WRITE_NUM - 1) - j) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE]),
						.ra(ra[((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)]),
						.rv(rvBank[i][j])
					);
				end
			end
			genvar i;
			for (i = 0; i < READ_NUM; i = i + 1) begin : genblk2
				assign rv[((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE] = rvBank[i][lvo[((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * WRITE_NUM_BIT_SIZE+:WRITE_NUM_BIT_SIZE]];
			end
			for (i = 0; i < WRITE_NUM; i = i + 1) begin : genblk3
				assign lvi[((WRITE_NUM - 1) - i) * WRITE_NUM_BIT_SIZE+:WRITE_NUM_BIT_SIZE] = i;
			end
		end
		else begin : genblk1
			genvar i;
			for (i = 0; i < READ_NUM; i = i + 1) begin : genblk1
				DistributedDualPortRAM #(
					.ENTRY_NUM(ENTRY_NUM),
					.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(ENTRY_BIT_SIZE)
				) rBank(
					.clk(clk),
					.we(we[0]),
					.wa(wa[(WRITE_NUM - 1) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)]),
					.wv(wv[(WRITE_NUM - 1) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE]),
					.ra(ra[((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)]),
					.rv(rv[((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE])
				);
			end
		end
	endgenerate
endmodule
module XOR_DistributedMultiPortRAM (
	clk,
	we,
	wa,
	wv,
	ra,
	rv
);
	parameter ENTRY_NUM = 32;
	parameter ENTRY_BIT_SIZE = 2;
	parameter READ_NUM = 8;
	parameter WRITE_NUM = 2;
	input wire clk;
	input wire [0:WRITE_NUM - 1] we;
	input wire [(WRITE_NUM * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) - 1:0] wa;
	input wire [(WRITE_NUM * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] wv;
	input wire [(READ_NUM * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) - 1:0] ra;
	output reg [(READ_NUM * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] rv;
	localparam INDEX_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(ENTRY_NUM);
	localparam WRITE_NUM_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(WRITE_NUM);
	reg [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] rwbWriteValue [0:WRITE_NUM - 1];
	reg [INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] wbReadAddr [0:WRITE_NUM - 1];
	wire [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] wbReadValue [0:WRITE_NUM - 1][0:WRITE_NUM - 1];
	reg [INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] rbReadAddr [0:READ_NUM - 1];
	wire [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] rbReadValue [0:WRITE_NUM - 1][0:READ_NUM - 1];
	genvar j;
	generate
		for (j = 0; j < WRITE_NUM; j = j + 1) begin : wj
			genvar i;
			for (i = 0; i < WRITE_NUM; i = i + 1) begin : wi
				if (j != i) begin : genblk1
					DistributedDualPortRAM #(
						.ENTRY_NUM(ENTRY_NUM),
						.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(ENTRY_BIT_SIZE)
					) wBank(
						.clk(clk),
						.we(we[j]),
						.wa(wa[((WRITE_NUM - 1) - j) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)]),
						.wv(rwbWriteValue[j]),
						.ra(wbReadAddr[i]),
						.rv(wbReadValue[j][i])
					);
				end
			end
		end
		for (j = 0; j < WRITE_NUM; j = j + 1) begin : rj
			genvar i;
			for (i = 0; i < READ_NUM; i = i + 1) begin : ri
				DistributedDualPortRAM #(
					.ENTRY_NUM(ENTRY_NUM),
					.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(ENTRY_BIT_SIZE)
				) rBank(
					.clk(clk),
					.we(we[j]),
					.wa(wa[((WRITE_NUM - 1) - j) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)]),
					.wv(rwbWriteValue[j]),
					.ra(rbReadAddr[i]),
					.rv(rbReadValue[j][i])
				);
			end
		end
	endgenerate
	always @(*) begin
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
			reg signed [31:0] j;
			for (j = 0; j < WRITE_NUM; j = j + 1)
				wbReadAddr[j] = wa[((WRITE_NUM - 1) - j) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)];
		end
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_2
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < WRITE_NUM; i = i + 1)
				begin
					rwbWriteValue[i] = wv[((WRITE_NUM - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE];
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_3
						reg signed [31:0] j;
						for (j = 0; j < WRITE_NUM; j = j + 1)
							if (i != j)
								rwbWriteValue[i] = rwbWriteValue[i] ^ wbReadValue[j][i];
					end
				end
		end
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_4
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < READ_NUM; i = i + 1)
				rbReadAddr[i] = ra[((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)];
		end
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_5
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < READ_NUM; i = i + 1)
				begin
					rv[((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE] = 0;
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_6
						reg signed [31:0] j;
						for (j = 0; j < WRITE_NUM; j = j + 1)
							rv[((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE] = rv[((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE] ^ rbReadValue[j][i];
					end
				end
		end
	end
	generate
		for (j = 0; j < WRITE_NUM; j = j + 1) begin : genblk3
			genvar i;
		end
	endgenerate
endmodule
module DistributedMultiPortRAM (
	clk,
	we,
	wa,
	wv,
	ra,
	rv
);
	parameter ENTRY_NUM = 64;
	parameter ENTRY_BIT_SIZE = 63;
	parameter READ_NUM = 3;
	parameter WRITE_NUM = 3;
	input wire clk;
	input wire [0:WRITE_NUM - 1] we;
	input wire [(WRITE_NUM * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) - 1:0] wa;
	input wire [(WRITE_NUM * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] wv;
	input wire [(READ_NUM * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) - 1:0] ra;
	output wire [(READ_NUM * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] rv;
	localparam INDEX_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(ENTRY_NUM);
	generate
		if ((((READ_NUM < 2) && WRITE_NUM) && (ENTRY_BIT_SIZE < 8)) || ((INDEX_BIT_SIZE <= 4) && (ENTRY_BIT_SIZE > 64))) begin : genblk1
			XOR_DistributedMultiPortRAM #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(ENTRY_NUM),
				.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(ENTRY_BIT_SIZE),
				.READ_NUM(READ_NUM),
				.WRITE_NUM(WRITE_NUM)
			) body(
				.clk(clk),
				.we(we),
				.wa(wa),
				.wv(wv),
				.ra(ra),
				.rv(rv)
			);
		end
		else begin : genblk1
			LVT_DistributedMultiPortRAM #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(ENTRY_NUM),
				.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(ENTRY_BIT_SIZE),
				.READ_NUM(READ_NUM),
				.WRITE_NUM(WRITE_NUM)
			) body(
				.clk(clk),
				.we(we),
				.wa(wa),
				.wv(wv),
				.ra(ra),
				.rv(rv)
			);
		end
	endgenerate
endmodule
module RegisterMultiPortRAM (
	clk,
	we,
	wa,
	wv,
	ra,
	rv
);
	parameter ENTRY_NUM = 64;
	parameter ENTRY_BIT_SIZE = 2;
	parameter READ_NUM = 8;
	parameter WRITE_NUM = 4;
	input wire clk;
	input wire [0:WRITE_NUM - 1] we;
	input wire [(WRITE_NUM * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) - 1:0] wa;
	input wire [(WRITE_NUM * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] wv;
	input wire [(READ_NUM * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) - 1:0] ra;
	output reg [(READ_NUM * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] rv;
	localparam INDEX_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(ENTRY_NUM);
	reg [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] array [0:ENTRY_NUM - 1];
	genvar i;
	generate
		for (i = 0; i < WRITE_NUM; i = i + 1) begin : genblk1
			always @(posedge clk)
				if (we[i])
					array[wa[((WRITE_NUM - 1) - i) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)]] <= wv[((WRITE_NUM - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE];
		end
	endgenerate
	always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
		reg signed [31:0] i;
		for (i = 0; i < READ_NUM; i = i + 1)
			rv[((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE] = array[ra[((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)]];
	end
endmodule
module DistributedMultiBankRAM (
	clk,
	we,
	wa,
	wv,
	ra,
	rv
);
	parameter ENTRY_NUM = 64;
	parameter ENTRY_BIT_SIZE = 63;
	parameter READ_NUM = 4;
	parameter WRITE_NUM = 2;
	input wire clk;
	input wire [0:WRITE_NUM - 1] we;
	input wire [(WRITE_NUM * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) - 1:0] wa;
	input wire [(WRITE_NUM * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] wv;
	input wire [(READ_NUM * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) - 1:0] ra;
	output wire [(READ_NUM * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] rv;
	localparam INDEX_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(ENTRY_NUM);
	localparam BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = $clog2((READ_NUM > WRITE_NUM ? READ_NUM : WRITE_NUM));
	localparam BANK_NUM = 1 << BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH;
	generate
		if (BANK_NUM >= 2) begin : genblk1
			DistributedMultiBankRAM_ForGE2Banks #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(ENTRY_NUM),
				.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(ENTRY_BIT_SIZE),
				.READ_NUM(READ_NUM),
				.WRITE_NUM(WRITE_NUM)
			) rBank(
				.clk(clk),
				.we(we),
				.wa(wa),
				.wv(wv),
				.ra(ra),
				.rv(rv)
			);
		end
		else begin : genblk1
			DistributedDualPortRAM #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(ENTRY_NUM),
				.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(ENTRY_BIT_SIZE)
			) rBank(
				.clk(clk),
				.we(we[0]),
				.wa(wa[(WRITE_NUM - 1) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)]),
				.wv(wv[(WRITE_NUM - 1) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE]),
				.ra(ra[(READ_NUM - 1) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)]),
				.rv(rv[(READ_NUM - 1) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE])
			);
		end
	endgenerate
endmodule
module DistributedMultiBankRAM_ForGE2Banks (
	clk,
	we,
	wa,
	wv,
	ra,
	rv
);
	parameter ENTRY_NUM = 64;
	parameter ENTRY_BIT_SIZE = 63;
	parameter READ_NUM = 4;
	parameter WRITE_NUM = 2;
	input wire clk;
	input wire [0:WRITE_NUM - 1] we;
	input wire [(WRITE_NUM * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) - 1:0] wa;
	input wire [(WRITE_NUM * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] wv;
	input wire [(READ_NUM * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) - 1:0] ra;
	output reg [(READ_NUM * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] rv;
	localparam INDEX_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(ENTRY_NUM);
	localparam BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = $clog2((READ_NUM > WRITE_NUM ? READ_NUM : WRITE_NUM));
	localparam BANK_NUM = 1 << BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH;
	localparam BANK_INDEX_BIT_SIZE = INDEX_BIT_SIZE - BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] waBank [0:BANK_NUM - 1];
	reg [INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] raBank [0:BANK_NUM - 1];
	wire [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] rvBank [0:BANK_NUM - 1];
	reg [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] wvBank [0:BANK_NUM - 1];
	reg weBank [0:BANK_NUM - 1];
	generate
		if ((ENTRY_NUM % BANK_NUM) != 0) begin : genblk1
			RSD_STATIC_ASSERT_FAILED non_existing_module();
		end
	endgenerate
	genvar i;
	generate
		for (i = 0; i < BANK_NUM; i = i + 1) begin : genblk2
			if (ENTRY_BIT_SIZE < 8) begin : genblk1
				RegisterDualPortRAM #(
					.ENTRY_NUM(ENTRY_NUM / BANK_NUM),
					.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(ENTRY_BIT_SIZE)
				) rBank(
					.clk(clk),
					.we(weBank[i]),
					.wa(waBank[i][INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]),
					.wv(wvBank[i]),
					.ra(raBank[i][INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]),
					.rv(rvBank[i])
				);
			end
			else begin : genblk1
				DistributedDualPortRAM #(
					.ENTRY_NUM(ENTRY_NUM / BANK_NUM),
					.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(ENTRY_BIT_SIZE)
				) rBank(
					.clk(clk),
					.we(weBank[i]),
					.wa(waBank[i][INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]),
					.wv(wvBank[i]),
					.ra(raBank[i][INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]),
					.rv(rvBank[i])
				);
			end
		end
	endgenerate
	always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
		reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;
		_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_2
			reg signed [31:0] b;
			for (b = 0; b < BANK_NUM; b = b + 1)
				if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
					_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
					weBank[b] = 1'sb0;
					waBank[b] = wa[(WRITE_NUM - 1) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)];
					wvBank[b] = wv[(WRITE_NUM - 1) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE];
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_3
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						begin : sv2v_autoblock_4
							reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump_1;
							_sv2v_jump_1 = _sv2v_jump;
							for (i = 0; i < WRITE_NUM; i = i + 1)
								if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
									_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
									if (we[i] && (wa[(((WRITE_NUM - 1) - i) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) + (BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH - 1)-:BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] == b)) begin
										weBank[b] = we[i];
										waBank[b] = wa[((WRITE_NUM - 1) - i) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)];
										wvBank[b] = wv[((WRITE_NUM - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE];
										_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
									end
								end
							if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
								_sv2v_jump = _sv2v_jump_1;
						end
					end
				end
			if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
				_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
		end
		if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00) begin
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_5
				reg signed [31:0] b;
				for (b = 0; b < BANK_NUM; b = b + 1)
					if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
						_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
						raBank[b] = ra[(READ_NUM - 1) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)];
						begin : sv2v_autoblock_6
							reg signed [31:0] i;
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_7
								reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump_1;
								_sv2v_jump_1 = _sv2v_jump;
								for (i = 0; i < READ_NUM; i = i + 1)
									if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
										_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
										if (ra[(((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) + (BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH - 1)-:BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] == b) begin
											raBank[b] = ra[((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)];
											_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
										end
									end
								if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
									_sv2v_jump = _sv2v_jump_1;
							end
						end
					end
				if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
					_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
			end
			if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00) begin : sv2v_autoblock_8
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < READ_NUM; i = i + 1)
					if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
						_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
						rv[((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE] = rvBank[0];
						begin : sv2v_autoblock_9
							reg signed [31:0] b;
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_10
								reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump_1;
								_sv2v_jump_1 = _sv2v_jump;
								for (b = 0; b < BANK_NUM; b = b + 1)
									if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
										_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
										if (ra[(((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) + (BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH - 1)-:BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] == b) begin
											rv[((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE] = rvBank[b];
											_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
										end
									end
								if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
									_sv2v_jump = _sv2v_jump_1;
							end
						end
					end
				if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
					_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
			end
		end
	end
	genvar j;
	generate
		for (j = 0; j < WRITE_NUM; j = j + 1) begin : genblk3
			genvar i;
		end
		for (j = 0; j < READ_NUM; j = j + 1) begin : genblk4
			genvar i;
		end
	endgenerate
endmodule
module BlockMultiBankRAM (
	clk,
	we,
	wa,
	wv,
	ra,
	rv
);
	parameter ENTRY_NUM = 64;
	parameter ENTRY_BIT_SIZE = 63;
	parameter READ_NUM = 2;
	parameter WRITE_NUM = 2;
	input wire clk;
	input wire [0:WRITE_NUM - 1] we;
	input wire [(WRITE_NUM * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) - 1:0] wa;
	input wire [(WRITE_NUM * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] wv;
	input wire [(READ_NUM * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) - 1:0] ra;
	output wire [(READ_NUM * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] rv;
	localparam INDEX_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(ENTRY_NUM);
	localparam BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = $clog2((READ_NUM > WRITE_NUM ? READ_NUM : WRITE_NUM));
	localparam BANK_NUM = 1 << BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH;
	generate
		if (BANK_NUM >= 2) begin : genblk1
			BlockMultiBankRAM_Body #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(ENTRY_NUM),
				.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(ENTRY_BIT_SIZE),
				.READ_NUM(READ_NUM),
				.WRITE_NUM(WRITE_NUM)
			) rBank(
				.clk(clk),
				.we(we),
				.wa(wa),
				.wv(wv),
				.ra(ra),
				.rv(rv)
			);
		end
		else begin : genblk1
			BlockDualPortRAM #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(ENTRY_NUM),
				.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(ENTRY_BIT_SIZE)
			) rBank(
				.clk(clk),
				.we(we[0]),
				.wa(wa[(WRITE_NUM - 1) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)]),
				.wv(wv[(WRITE_NUM - 1) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE]),
				.ra(ra[(READ_NUM - 1) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)]),
				.rv(rv[(READ_NUM - 1) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE])
			);
		end
	endgenerate
endmodule
module BlockMultiBankRAM_Body (
	clk,
	we,
	wa,
	wv,
	ra,
	rv
);
	parameter ENTRY_NUM = 64;
	parameter ENTRY_BIT_SIZE = 63;
	parameter READ_NUM = 2;
	parameter WRITE_NUM = 2;
	input wire clk;
	input wire [0:WRITE_NUM - 1] we;
	input wire [(WRITE_NUM * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) - 1:0] wa;
	input wire [(WRITE_NUM * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] wv;
	input wire [(READ_NUM * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) - 1:0] ra;
	output reg [(READ_NUM * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE) - 1:0] rv;
	localparam INDEX_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(ENTRY_NUM);
	localparam BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH = $clog2((READ_NUM > WRITE_NUM ? READ_NUM : WRITE_NUM));
	localparam BANK_NUM = 1 << BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH;
	localparam BANK_INDEX_BIT_SIZE = INDEX_BIT_SIZE - BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] waBank [0:BANK_NUM - 1];
	reg [INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] raBank [0:BANK_NUM - 1];
	wire [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] rvBank [0:BANK_NUM - 1];
	reg [ENTRY_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] wvBank [0:BANK_NUM - 1];
	reg weBank [0:BANK_NUM - 1];
	reg [INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] raReg [0:BANK_NUM - 1];
	generate
		if ((ENTRY_NUM % BANK_NUM) != 0) begin : genblk1
			RSD_STATIC_ASSERT_FAILED non_existing_module();
		end
	endgenerate
	genvar i;
	generate
		for (i = 0; i < BANK_NUM; i = i + 1) begin : genblk2
			BlockDualPortRAM #(
				.ENTRY_NUM(ENTRY_NUM / BANK_NUM),
				.ENTRY_BIT_SIZE(ENTRY_BIT_SIZE)
			) rBank(
				.clk(clk),
				.we(weBank[i]),
				.wa(waBank[i][INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]),
				.wv(wvBank[i]),
				.ra(raBank[i][INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH]),
				.rv(rvBank[i])
			);
		end
	endgenerate
	always @(posedge clk) begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
		reg signed [31:0] i;
		for (i = 0; i < BANK_NUM; i = i + 1)
			raReg[i] <= ra[((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)];
	end
	always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_2
		reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;
		_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_3
			reg signed [31:0] b;
			for (b = 0; b < BANK_NUM; b = b + 1)
				if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
					_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
					weBank[b] = 1'sb0;
					waBank[b] = wa[(WRITE_NUM - 1) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)];
					wvBank[b] = wv[(WRITE_NUM - 1) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE];
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_4
						reg signed [31:0] i;
						begin : sv2v_autoblock_5
							reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump_1;
							_sv2v_jump_1 = _sv2v_jump;
							for (i = 0; i < WRITE_NUM; i = i + 1)
								if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
									_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
									if (we[i] && (wa[(((WRITE_NUM - 1) - i) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) + (BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH - 1)-:BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] == b)) begin
										weBank[b] = we[i];
										waBank[b] = wa[((WRITE_NUM - 1) - i) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)];
										wvBank[b] = wv[((WRITE_NUM - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE];
										_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
									end
								end
							if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
								_sv2v_jump = _sv2v_jump_1;
						end
					end
				end
			if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
				_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
		end
		if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00) begin
			begin : sv2v_autoblock_6
				reg signed [31:0] b;
				for (b = 0; b < BANK_NUM; b = b + 1)
					if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
						_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
						raBank[b] = ra[(READ_NUM - 1) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)];
						begin : sv2v_autoblock_7
							reg signed [31:0] i;
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_8
								reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump_1;
								_sv2v_jump_1 = _sv2v_jump;
								for (i = 0; i < READ_NUM; i = i + 1)
									if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
										_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
										if (ra[(((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) + (BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH - 1)-:BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH] == b) begin
											raBank[b] = ra[((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)];
											_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
										end
									end
								if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
									_sv2v_jump = _sv2v_jump_1;
							end
						end
					end
				if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
					_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
			end
			if (_sv2v_jump == 2'b00) begin : sv2v_autoblock_9
				reg signed [31:0] i;
				for (i = 0; i < READ_NUM; i = i + 1)
					if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
						_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
						rv[((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE] = rvBank[0];
						begin : sv2v_autoblock_10
							reg signed [31:0] b;
							begin : sv2v_autoblock_11
								reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump_1;
								_sv2v_jump_1 = _sv2v_jump;
								for (b = 0; b < BANK_NUM; b = b + 1)
									if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
										_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
										if (raReg[i][BANK_NUM_BIT_WIDTH - 1:0] == b) begin
											rv[((READ_NUM - 1) - i) * ENTRY_BIT_SIZE+:ENTRY_BIT_SIZE] = rvBank[b];
											_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
										end
									end
								if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
									_sv2v_jump = _sv2v_jump_1;
							end
						end
					end
				if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
					_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
			end
		end
	end
	genvar j;
	generate
		for (j = 0; j < WRITE_NUM; j = j + 1) begin : genblk3
			genvar i;
		end
		for (j = 0; j < READ_NUM; j = j + 1) begin : genblk4
			genvar i;
		end
	endgenerate
endmodule
module LRU_Counter (
	clk,
	rst,
	index,
	access,
	accessWay,
	leastRecentlyAccessedWay
);
	parameter WAY_NUM = 2;
	parameter INDEX_BIT_WIDTH = 1;
	parameter PORT_WIDTH = 1;
	input wire clk;
	input wire rst;
	input wire [(PORT_WIDTH * INDEX_BIT_WIDTH) - 1:0] index;
	input wire [0:PORT_WIDTH - 1] access;
	input wire [(PORT_WIDTH * $clog2(WAY_NUM)) - 1:0] accessWay;
	output reg [(PORT_WIDTH * $clog2(WAY_NUM)) - 1:0] leastRecentlyAccessedWay;
	localparam ENTRY_NUM = 1 << INDEX_BIT_WIDTH;
	reg [(WAY_NUM * $clog2(WAY_NUM)) - 1:0] body [0:ENTRY_NUM - 1];
	reg [(WAY_NUM * $clog2(WAY_NUM)) - 1:0] readEntry [0:PORT_WIDTH - 1];
	reg [(WAY_NUM * $clog2(WAY_NUM)) - 1:0] writeEntry [0:PORT_WIDTH - 1];
	reg we [0:PORT_WIDTH - 1];
	localparam BasicTypes_FALSE = 1'b0;
	localparam BasicTypes_TRUE = 1'b1;
	always @(*) begin
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < PORT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
				begin
					readEntry[i] = body[index[((PORT_WIDTH - 1) - i) * INDEX_BIT_WIDTH+:INDEX_BIT_WIDTH]];
					we[i] = access[i];
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_2
						reg signed [31:0] j;
						for (j = 0; j < i; j = j + 1)
							if ((we[j] == BasicTypes_TRUE) && (index[((PORT_WIDTH - 1) - i) * INDEX_BIT_WIDTH+:INDEX_BIT_WIDTH] == index[((PORT_WIDTH - 1) - j) * INDEX_BIT_WIDTH+:INDEX_BIT_WIDTH]))
								we[i] = BasicTypes_FALSE;
					end
					if (readEntry[i][((WAY_NUM * $clog2(WAY_NUM)) - 1) - (((WAY_NUM * $clog2(WAY_NUM)) - 1) - (accessWay[((PORT_WIDTH - 1) - i) * $clog2(WAY_NUM)+:$clog2(WAY_NUM)] * $clog2(WAY_NUM)))+:$clog2(WAY_NUM)] == (WAY_NUM - 1)) begin : sv2v_autoblock_3
						reg signed [31:0] j;
						for (j = 0; j < WAY_NUM; j = j + 1)
							writeEntry[i][((WAY_NUM * $clog2(WAY_NUM)) - 1) - (((WAY_NUM * $clog2(WAY_NUM)) - 1) - (j * $clog2(WAY_NUM)))+:$clog2(WAY_NUM)] = readEntry[i][((WAY_NUM * $clog2(WAY_NUM)) - 1) - (((WAY_NUM * $clog2(WAY_NUM)) - 1) - (j * $clog2(WAY_NUM)))+:$clog2(WAY_NUM)];
					end
					else begin : sv2v_autoblock_4
						reg signed [31:0] j;
						for (j = 0; j < WAY_NUM; j = j + 1)
							if (j == accessWay[((PORT_WIDTH - 1) - i) * $clog2(WAY_NUM)+:$clog2(WAY_NUM)])
								writeEntry[i][((WAY_NUM * $clog2(WAY_NUM)) - 1) - (((WAY_NUM * $clog2(WAY_NUM)) - 1) - (j * $clog2(WAY_NUM)))+:$clog2(WAY_NUM)] = WAY_NUM - 1;
							else
								writeEntry[i][((WAY_NUM * $clog2(WAY_NUM)) - 1) - (((WAY_NUM * $clog2(WAY_NUM)) - 1) - (j * $clog2(WAY_NUM)))+:$clog2(WAY_NUM)] = (readEntry[i][((WAY_NUM * $clog2(WAY_NUM)) - 1) - (((WAY_NUM * $clog2(WAY_NUM)) - 1) - (j * $clog2(WAY_NUM)))+:$clog2(WAY_NUM)] == 0 ? 0 : readEntry[i][((WAY_NUM * $clog2(WAY_NUM)) - 1) - (((WAY_NUM * $clog2(WAY_NUM)) - 1) - (j * $clog2(WAY_NUM)))+:$clog2(WAY_NUM)] - 1);
					end
				end
		end
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_5
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < PORT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
				begin
					leastRecentlyAccessedWay[((PORT_WIDTH - 1) - i) * $clog2(WAY_NUM)+:$clog2(WAY_NUM)] = 0;
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_6
						reg signed [31:0] j;
						for (j = 0; j < WAY_NUM; j = j + 1)
							if (readEntry[i][((WAY_NUM * $clog2(WAY_NUM)) - 1) - (((WAY_NUM * $clog2(WAY_NUM)) - 1) - (j * $clog2(WAY_NUM)))+:$clog2(WAY_NUM)] == 0)
								leastRecentlyAccessedWay[((PORT_WIDTH - 1) - i) * $clog2(WAY_NUM)+:$clog2(WAY_NUM)] = j;
					end
				end
		end
	end
	always @(posedge clk)
		if (rst) begin : sv2v_autoblock_7
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)
				begin : sv2v_autoblock_8
					reg signed [31:0] j;
					for (j = 0; j < WAY_NUM; j = j + 1)
						body[i][((WAY_NUM * $clog2(WAY_NUM)) - 1) - (((WAY_NUM * $clog2(WAY_NUM)) - 1) - (j * $clog2(WAY_NUM)))+:$clog2(WAY_NUM)] <= 0;
				end
		end
		else begin : sv2v_autoblock_9
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < PORT_WIDTH; i = i + 1)
				if (we[i])
					body[index[((PORT_WIDTH - 1) - i) * INDEX_BIT_WIDTH+:INDEX_BIT_WIDTH]] <= writeEntry[i];
		end
endmodule
module Picker (
	req,
	grant,
	grantPtr,
	granted
);
	parameter ENTRY_NUM = 4;
	parameter GRANT_NUM = 2;
	input wire [ENTRY_NUM - 1:0] req;
	output reg [ENTRY_NUM - 1:0] grant;
	output reg [(GRANT_NUM * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) - 1:0] grantPtr;
	output reg [0:GRANT_NUM - 1] granted;
	localparam INDEX_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(ENTRY_NUM);
	reg [ENTRY_NUM - 1:0] reqTmp;
	always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
		reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;
		_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
		reqTmp = req;
		grant = 1'sb0;
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_2
			reg signed [31:0] p;
			for (p = 0; p < GRANT_NUM; p = p + 1)
				if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
					_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
					granted[p] = 1'sb0;
					grantPtr[((GRANT_NUM - 1) - p) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)] = 1'sb0;
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_3
						reg signed [31:0] e;
						begin : sv2v_autoblock_4
							reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump_1;
							_sv2v_jump_1 = _sv2v_jump;
							for (e = 0; e < ENTRY_NUM; e = e + 1)
								if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
									_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
									if (reqTmp[e]) begin
										grant[e] = 1'sb1;
										granted[p] = 1'sb1;
										grantPtr[((GRANT_NUM - 1) - p) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)] = e;
										reqTmp[e] = 1'sb0;
										_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
									end
								end
							if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
								_sv2v_jump = _sv2v_jump_1;
						end
					end
				end
			if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
				_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
		end
	end
endmodule
module InterleavedPicker (
	req,
	grant,
	grantPtr,
	granted
);
	parameter ENTRY_NUM = 4;
	parameter GRANT_NUM = 2;
	input wire [ENTRY_NUM - 1:0] req;
	output reg [ENTRY_NUM - 1:0] grant;
	output reg [(GRANT_NUM * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)) - 1:0] grantPtr;
	output reg [0:GRANT_NUM - 1] granted;
	localparam INDEX_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(ENTRY_NUM);
	reg [ENTRY_NUM - 1:0] reqTmp;
	always @(*) begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
		reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump;
		_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
		reqTmp = req;
		grant = 1'sb0;
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_2
			reg signed [31:0] p;
			for (p = 0; p < GRANT_NUM; p = p + 1)
				if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
					_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
					granted[p] = 1'sb0;
					grantPtr[((GRANT_NUM - 1) - p) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)] = 0;
					begin : sv2v_autoblock_3
						reg signed [31:0] e;
						begin : sv2v_autoblock_4
							reg [0:1] _sv2v_jump_1;
							_sv2v_jump_1 = _sv2v_jump;
							for (e = p; e < ENTRY_NUM; e = e + GRANT_NUM)
								if (_sv2v_jump < 2'b10) begin
									_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
									if (reqTmp[e]) begin
										grant[e] = 1'sb1;
										granted[p] = 1'sb1;
										grantPtr[((GRANT_NUM - 1) - p) * $clog2(ENTRY_NUM)+:$clog2(ENTRY_NUM)] = e;
										reqTmp[e] = 1'sb0;
										_sv2v_jump = 2'b10;
									end
								end
							if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
								_sv2v_jump = _sv2v_jump_1;
						end
					end
				end
			if (_sv2v_jump != 2'b11)
				_sv2v_jump = 2'b00;
		end
	end
endmodule
module CircularRangePicker (
	headPtr,
	tailPtr,
	request,
	grantPtr,
	picked
);
	parameter ENTRY_NUM = 16;
	input wire [$clog2(ENTRY_NUM) - 1:0] headPtr;
	input wire [$clog2(ENTRY_NUM) - 1:0] tailPtr;
	input wire [ENTRY_NUM - 1:0] request;
	output reg [$clog2(ENTRY_NUM) - 1:0] grantPtr;
	output reg picked;
	localparam INDEX_BIT_SIZE = $clog2(ENTRY_NUM);
	reg [(ENTRY_NUM * 2) - 1:0] rightShifterIn;
	wire [ENTRY_NUM - 1:0] shiftedReq;
	reg [INDEX_BIT_SIZE - 1:0] shiftedGrant;
	generate
		if (ENTRY_NUM == 16) begin : genblk1
			CircularRangePickerRightShifter16 rightShifter(
				.shiftIn(rightShifterIn[30:0]),
				.shiftAmount(tailPtr),
				.shiftOut(shiftedReq)
			);
		end
		else begin : genblk1
			CircularRangePickerRightShifter #(
				.INPUT_WIDTH(ENTRY_NUM * 2),
				.SHIFT_AMOUNT_WIDTH(INDEX_BIT_SIZE),
				.OUTPUT_WIDTH(ENTRY_NUM)
			) rightShifter(
				.shiftIn(rightShifterIn),
				.shiftAmount(tailPtr),
				.shiftOut(shiftedReq)
			);
		end
	endgenerate
	always @(*) begin
		rightShifterIn = {request, request};
		picked = 1'b0;
		shiftedGrant = 0;
		begin : sv2v_autoblock_1
			reg signed [31:0] i;
			for (i = 0; i < ENTRY_NUM; i = i + 1)
				if (shiftedReq[i]) begin
					shiftedGrant = i;
					picked = 1'b1;
				end
		end
		if ((shiftedGrant + tailPtr) < ENTRY_NUM)
			grantPtr = shiftedGrant + tailPtr;
		else
			grantPtr = (shiftedGrant + tailPtr) - ENTRY_NUM;
		if (tailPtr >= headPtr) begin
			if (((ENTRY_NUM + headPtr) - tailPtr) > shiftedGrant)
				picked = 1'b0;
		end
		else if ((headPtr - tailPtr) > shiftedGrant)
			picked = 1'b0;
	end
endmodule
module CircularRangePickerRightShifter16 (
	shiftIn,
	shiftAmount,
	shiftOut
);
	input wire [30:0] shiftIn;
	input wire [3:0] shiftAmount;
	output reg [15:0] shiftOut;
	reg [31:0] shiftTmp;
	always @(*) begin
		shiftTmp = shiftIn;
		case (shiftAmount[3:2])
			2'h3: shiftTmp = shiftTmp[30:12];
			2'h2: shiftTmp = shiftTmp[26:8];
			2'h1: shiftTmp = shiftTmp[22:4];
			default: shiftTmp = shiftTmp[18:0];
		endcase
		case (shiftAmount[1:0])
			2'h3: shiftTmp = shiftTmp[18:3];
			2'h2: shiftTmp = shiftTmp[17:2];
			2'h1: shiftTmp = shiftTmp[16:1];
			default: shiftTmp = shiftTmp[15:0];
		endcase
		shiftOut = shiftTmp;
	end
endmodule
module CircularRangePickerRightShifter (
	shiftIn,
	shiftAmount,
	shiftOut
);
	parameter INPUT_WIDTH = 32;
	parameter SHIFT_AMOUNT_WIDTH = 4;
	parameter OUTPUT_WIDTH = 16;
	input wire [INPUT_WIDTH - 1:0] shiftIn;
	input wire [SHIFT_AMOUNT_WIDTH - 1:0] shiftAmount;
